Chapter 3151: Things Are Different Now

The two siblings repeatedly reminded themselves in their hearts that they should not be too surprised no matter how bizarre the objects they saw were, because they knew that the top experts of the human civilization must’ve received a lot of knowledge from the primeval relics.

However, when they walked into the main hall and saw the iron giant more than two hundred meters tall walking past the legs of the giant, they were still dumbfounded and did not know what to say for a long time.

“This—this—this is impossible!”

Meng Xiaolang was shocked. “An iron giant more than two hundred meters tall? Are you kidding me? Is it made of plastic or foam?”

“This is too incredible! This is simply inconceivable! This is an epoch-making breakthrough in materials science and engineering!”

Disbelief beamed out of Meng Lichuan’s eyes, too. He mumbled, “Is—is this a brand-new Giant God Soldier? Ten times larger?”

The super giant in front of them looked both like an awe-inspiring iron god and like a skyscraper that had suddenly come to life. Although the shell of the building had not been polished or spray-painted yet, it was still rough and vintage. Such a primitive and beautiful appearance made it look even stronger and rougher.

Meng Xiaolang and Meng Lichuan were not three-year-olds. They both knew that neither crystal suits nor Giant Soldiers were better than each other.

Just as a body of flesh and blood had the most suitable growth range, a twenty-meter tall giant was certainly not as agile as an ordinary person who was only two meters tall. The crystal suit and the giant soldier were limited by the materials, the hardness of the structure, and the consumption of energy. There was a limit to their size, too.

Generally speaking, a crystal suit that was three to five meters tall and a giant soldier that was twenty to thirty meters tall were the limits. Increasing the height and the size of the crystal suit would only double the weight of the crystal suit and increase the energy consumption and the surface area of the crystal suit. It would be a heavy burden for the joints and transmission structure of the giant soldier, and the comprehensive performance of the crystal suit would plummet.

Therefore, many well-trained soldiers and fighters despised the concept of ‘super-large humanoid machines’ and thought that it was just the imagination of children.

However, with the in-depth study of human life and spiritual energy, modern people had gradually discovered the importance of the ‘life magnetic field’. Only a giant mechanical body in the shape of a human being could resonate perfectly with the life magnetic field of a human being and increase the combat ability of the user by a hundredfold.

Therefore, overcoming a series of tricky problems in the field of materials science and engineering, and building a super-large, human-shaped mechanical body that was larger and larger in scale while limiting the energy consumption and ensuring the hardness and mobility of the structure, became a lifelong project for all the mechanics, designers, and magical equipment experts.

Before, the largest-scale Giant God Soldiers were usually limited to thirty to fifty meters.

It was not that there were no personal magical equipment of a larger scale, but such magical equipment was often shaped into round balls and could be regarded as a personal fortress. Although their long-range firepower and capacity of ammunition were as good as those of the Giant God Soldier, they could not perfectly resonate with the vitality of the user. In terms of instantaneous burst and endurance, they were absolutely no match for the Giant God Soldier.

But today, in front of Meng Xiaolang and Meng Lichuan was a giant soldier almost two hundred meters tall.

Whether the iron god was a prototype that was still being tested or just an exhibition piece, it was evident enough that the civilization of mankind had made a breakthrough in materials science, architecture, and dynamics that could potentially overturn the past ten thousand years.

At the very least, none of the modern super alloys that the two siblings were familiar with were hard enough to support such an iron god to stand on the ground as if nobody was around.

New technology, new materials, new transmission and support structures… Everything represented a brand-new war machine and combat mode. The two siblings seemed to see a new future slowly rising before their eyes.

“Look over there! Look over there!”

Meng Lichuan was no longer the calm, icy beauty in the team. He was drooling at the super soldier while Meng Xiaolang was tugging his sleeve.

She turned her head and looked in the direction that her brother was pointing at. She happened to see hundreds of thousands of mini-drones known as ‘bees’ condensing into a colorful lotus cloud in midair. The cloud slowly bloomed, and the mini-drones formed the childish faces of a boy and a girl, grinning at everyone in the hall.

“Look here! Look here!”

Meng Xiaolang shouted, “What—what—what is this?”

Since they were taking the VIP channel, it was very convenient for them to reach the central area of the hall. Not far away from Meng Xiaolang’s left side stood several tall, brown-skinned members of the Pangu Clan whose skin was full of wrinkles and heat sink holes. There were also members of the Nuwa Clan who had human heads and snake bodies and were covered in glamorous scales.

This was the first time that Meng Xiaolang had seen a living member of the Pangu Clan.

It must be noted that, in the military education of the federal army, including the legend of ‘Vulture Li Yao’, the ‘Pangu Clan’ was not a friendly friend.

Although the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan were dressed to the nines, and several big shots that were familiar to all the citizens of the federation were standing next to them, with a smile on their faces, Meng Xiaolang still felt that something was wrong. It was like a lion talking and laughing with a sheep, or a hungry wolf dancing with a rabbit.

“It seems that we are in big trouble this time!”

Meng Lichuan eyed the ‘living’ Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan curiously and suddenly blurted out.

“Huh?”

Meng Xiaolang was slightly stunned. “Sister, what did you say?”

“It must be noted that the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance, the three forces of human civilization, have made peace three years ago.

Meng Lichuan explained, “If we were not faced with new trouble, we wouldn’t have had to invest tremendous resources and develop brand-new military technology and facilities in such a time of total peace. It would’ve been impossible for us to accept the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan so easily. The primeval species, which thought that they were hundreds of thousands of years ahead of us, wouldn’t have bowed to us and surrendered all their technology and wisdom so easily.

“Therefore, there must be a certain threat that is a hundred times more terrifying than the catastrophe of the Pangu Clan. It is forcing all the intelligent species in the sea of stars to unite and grasp the greatest strength at the cost of all the rules.

“I believe that the meeting in the future is just the beginning. Three years of rest will be enough to heal the wounds caused by the war between the Star Glory Federation, the Star Glory Imperium, and the Star Glory Federation. From now on, human civilization as a whole will embark on the path of war preparation again, won’t it?

“Sister…”

Meng Xiaolang was awed by the unprecedented seriousness on Meng Lichuan’s face.

He knew that his sister, as the ace player of Team Moon Shadow and the best strategical analyst, would not scare him easily.

Just when he didn’t know what to say, a red light suddenly shone in the clouds above his head. A super-large 3D illusion spanning over a thousand meters appeared. It was the face of a mature woman with short red hair.

“It’s—it’s our Speaker!”

Meng Xiaolang blinked his eyes and stared for a long time before he finally recognized it.

The Ding Lingdang of the past was marked by her long, fiery hair that was burning furiously. Whenever her red hair, which looked like nine dragons, danced crazily in the sea of stars, it would always bring the courage to attack again and again for all the federal soldiers.

Meng Xiaolang could not forget that it was under the leadership of such a burning red-haired man that he and his brothers charged at the iron tide of the Imperium of True Human Beings with the remnants of the federal expedition army and finally crushed the enemy.

The wrecked warships in the sea of stars had already cooled down.

But for the veterans who had been through hundreds of battles, the boiling blood never died down.

Although Ding Lingdang had resigned a long time ago, many federal soldiers who had participated in the battle naturally addressed her as ‘our Speaker’.

However, since three years ago, Ding Lingdang had disappeared in public with the excuse of ‘seclusion’. She did not show up in the celebration even though it was the national day and the official establishment of the Kunlun Fleet.

If Meng Xiaolang remembered correctly, this was Ding Lingdang’s first public appearance in three years.

As an expert whose Cultivation had reached the peak, and at the prime of her life, three years should not have left any traces on her.

But Meng Xiaolang clearly felt that ‘our Speaker’ was completely different from three years ago.

Not only was her hair fluttering even though there was no wind, her fiery red hair, which was like a surging tide, had turned into a beautiful short hair that was longer than an inch. Her skin, which was the color of wheat, was also faintly emitting a honey-like luster. Below her skin, a red glow that was as hot as magma seemed to be surging, giving people a restrained and unfathomable feeling.

It was especially so for the pair of eyes that seemed to contain thousands of years of experience. They were completely different from the ‘Dragon King of Fire’ in the past.

“Are you kidding me? Why do I feel that Speaker Ding has made a major breakthrough and is ten times stronger than before?”

Meng Xiaolang shook his head hard and laughed at himself. “That’s impossible. Speaker Ding three years ago was already a marvelous woman who could destroy the world. Ten times stronger in only three years? Impossible!”

“Dear compatriots of the Star Glory Federation, as well as the distinguished guests from the imperium, the Holy Alliance, and the primeval era, I believe that you are all very curious about the purpose of the ‘Future Meeting’ and are eager to unveil the truth that happened in the primeval relics three years ago.

In the clouds, Ding Lingdang said resolutely, one word after another, “Three years ago, in the depths of the primeval relics that had billions of years of history, Li Yao, a super hero of the federation, burnt his life and soul in order to stop the transmission of the key information of the Pangu Universe into the sea of the universe. He bought us precious time to catch our breath and develop ourselves.

“But the threat is not gone yet. The enemy is still drawing close. Li Yao has tried his best. Our mission depends on the survivors.

“Li Yao deeply believes in the strength of his compatriots and the entire civilization of mankind. Therefore, we, the survivors, are also willing to believe that everyone can shoulder this sacred and heavy mission. In this ‘Meeting of the Future’, we will try our best to publicize all the information we have gathered over the past three years to all of mankind. We also hope that everyone—every member of the civilization of mankind, including all the carbon-based intelligent creatures who value free will—can fight side by side until the end just like Li Yao!

“No matter how dark the universe is, there will always be hope as long as we can emit our own light and heat. The strength of the enemy is beyond our imagination, but the potential and the speed of our growth are far beyond our wildest imagination, too. Now, before we reveal the intelligence of the ‘Primeval Civilization’ and the ‘Supernatural Civilization’ to all mankind, let’s show you our latest strength first!”

Chapter 3152: Ding Lingdang Vs Long Yangjun

In the Star Glory Federation, fighting was everything. What could be more eye-catching and morale-boosting than brilliant arena matches?

Before the information about the Flood Tide—the super body civilization—was made public, it was necessary to pave the way for all human beings to realize that, although the enemy was strong, they were not entirely defenseless. That was the main purpose of the ‘Meeting of the Future’.

The ‘Super Duel Competition’, the largest and most professional competition in the history of human civilization, was the most important part of the competition.

Sixteen special arenas were arranged in a row in the sky of the Glorious Sunlight Group. Thousands of super experts, who were at least in the peak of the Nascent Soul Stage, would fight against each other for the title of ‘Ten Strongest in the Pangu Universe’ after Li Yao’s death.

Those who were qualified to participate in the summit match were naturally human-shaped beasts and walking war fortresses. With their unparalleled combat ability, they could destroy Floating Spear City easily when they were on a rampage, not to mention the headquarters of the Glorious Sunlight Group.

Therefore, the arenas were not actually floating in the sky of the city, but rather, the sixteen world fragments were connected to the space folds above Floating Spear City by force with the latest technology excavated from the primeval relics. Then, they would show the audience the real experience with the 3D and the Great Illusionary Land technology.

Other than propaganda and morale-boosting purposes, it was also a gala for the top experts of the three forces, including the survivors of the primeval era, to discuss the next step of their plan.

Although most of the top experts of the human civilization had stayed in the primeval relics over the past three years and had a lot of opportunities to get close to each other, the interior of the tower after it was unlocked and upgraded was shockingly spacious. In fact, it was not a simple tower but a transfer station to countless planets hidden in the ‘black wall’. As long as one found a suitable route, they would be directly teleported to a certain planet.

Some of the planets had abundant spiritual gas, some were extremely dangerous, and some were full of unexpected traps, ferocious beasts, and secret treasures. It was impossible to explore every planet clearly even if there were hundreds of times more people, let alone thousands of experts.

Together with the remains and secret treasures left by a hundred civilizations of pioneers, any one of them contained endless mysteries that no Cultivators or researchers would ever be able to figure out.

It was safe to say that, for the past three years, every top expert and researcher who had explored the ancient relics had been locked in a state of ‘seclusion’ in which they had no time to waste. They had their own opportunities and adventures and were too busy training to bother other people.

It was not until today’s Future Meeting, or rather, the “Super Duel Competition”, that they began to examine the results of their three years of training and research.

The outcome of the match would determine the allocation of training resources and exploration missions as well as the changes among the major forces, including the conditions for renewal of the peace treaty.

It couldn’t be helped. Although the Star Glory Federation, the Star Glory Federation, and the Star Glory Federation were united against a common enemy, it was impossible for them to be truly close to each other. There was cooperation and competition among them. In the end, the stronger one’s fists were, the more influential one would be. It might be the bad habit of human beings, but it was also the unique charm of the human civilization.

In the first arena, in a seemingly ordinary desert world, Ding Lingdang was floating in midair calmly and gazing at her opponent.

Her opponent, Long Yangjun, on the other hand, was grimacing in pain, as if he were about to surrender with a stomachache.

The name list of the matches in the super combat arenas was random. Nobody had expected that Ding Lingdang and Long Yangjun, the two hot favorites of the ‘top ten experts of the Pangu Universe’, would ‘coincidentally’ meet each other in the first round. But Long Yangjun had always felt that there might be some sort of scheme behind the scenes.

“Mr. Speaker has changed his hair!”

While rubbing his stomach, Long Yangjun laughed at Ding Lingdang. “I’m told that you’ve been training crazily in the Heaven’s Origin Sector. Not only have you learned all the knowledge of the ‘Inferno Civilization’, which is best known for its valiance among the pioneers, but you’ve also spent an entire year on a planet that is very close to the star and is mostly magma. You’ve grasped the meaning of ‘True Meaning of Body fundamentalists’, which is to dismantle starships, Giant Soldiers, star fortresses, military bases, and everything else without using any magical equipment. Wow. That’s awesome. Well, I admit defeat, okay? I don’t belong to any organization in the first place. I’m not interested in the distribution of benefits between the federation, the imperium, or the Covenant Alliance at all. I’m just here to fill up my head. I didn’t expect that you would be picked in the first round!”

“Alright. I accept your surrender.”

Ding Lingdang nodded her head and said to Long Yangjun, who was overjoyed, “However, in the culture of war in the ‘Raging Fire Civilization’, even enemies who have surrendered must be blown up brutally. It is the greatest respect and affirmation for those who have surrendered.

“Are you serious?”

Long Yangjun’s smile was completely plastered on his face. He was dumbfounded. “I don’t know how I offended the Speaker of Parliament. Why did he have to torture me, ravage me, and torture me? I feel so wronged!”

“Is that so?”

Ding Lingdang said casually, “Maybe you are innocent about other things, but I’m told that you’ve been very close to the surviving primeval people over the past two years. You’ve been able to cooperate with the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan and carry out your own research projects in secret. In fact, you’ve collected and analyzed half of the eight books in the legends. Where are the other four?”

“What? There is such a thing?”

Long Yangjun’s eyes were wide open, with an innocent look on his face. “I don’t know anything about the Divine Investiture Book. Isn’t it the smoke bomb that the Supreme Emperor released years ago? There is indeed such a thing. Oh, such shocking news. If I had known, I would’ve reported it to the ‘Unified Development Committee of the Ancient Ruins’.”

“It doesn’t matter.”

Ding Lingdang slightly smiled, “Whether or not you have cultivated the Divine Seal Book, I will know after I give it a try!”

Hardly had he finished his sentence when something unbelievable happened.

Ding Lingdang’s beautiful short hair suddenly expanded like a big explosion and reached her shoulders. Her waist and her heels did not stop growing. A volcano seemed to be spurting out of the top of her head.

At the same time, countless interconnecting threads of light appeared on her skin, which was shining in the color of honey. The threads of light bounced off her body as if they were alive and darted toward the ground below her feet. In the blink of an eye, they cut open the hard ground and carved out dozens of rocks more than seventy square meters.

Along with the terrifying dance of the flames of war, dozens of giant rocks wobbled and floated in the air before they were absorbed by Ding Lingdang’s scorching aura. In the blink of an eye, they melted and turned into crimson magma, which revolved rapidly between Ding Lingdang’s arms. In the blink of an eye, they turned into raging red dragons which ruthlessly punched out along with her devastating heavy fists.

BOOM!

Thousands of crimson dragons covered the sky and the earth. The torrents of magma swallowed Long Yangjun immediately.

“An insatiable woman is truly terrifying!”

Long Yangjun’s scream echoed inside the magma. Then, a crystal human figure jumped out of the magma.

Although she was screaming, there was still an indifferent smile on her face. Not only had she turned into a crystal, the magma that Ding Lingdang threw at her was also absorbed by her and turned into sharp, diamond-shaped crystals that surrounded her life magnetic field like satellites and rings.

“Is this the power of the Divine Book of Deification? It is said that the Supreme Emperor created the road of miracles and glory precisely because of such power. Although the legends and the truth are certainly different, the existence of the Divine Book of Deification is undeniable. It is unfortunate that I was not born in the era of the Supreme Emperor and had a duel with him. But I am very lucky to have met you. Let’s fight for three days and three nights and let out all the pain and depression of the past three years!”

Ding Lingdang let out a roar that made even the Tyrant Dragon shiver.

The flames on his back continued raging and turned into the image of eight hundred deities in the burning sky. The temperature of the entire world fragment rose by dozens of degrees.

Eight hundred deities fell from the sky like a meteor shower out of thin air. Earthshaking explosions were echoing below their feet. The desert and the desert had both turned into boiling magma. Surging tides thousands of degrees high were rising inside the magma, too. They were obeying Ding Lingdang’s commands like the most loyal warriors.

“Are you kidding me? Mr. Speaker, do you know that we are playing a ‘friendly match’ or even an ‘exhibition match’ today to boost the morale of all mankind? I—I am also from the Star Glory Federation. I will immediately announce my return to the federation camp, alright? Besides, I don’t have any ‘pain or depression’. Over the past three years, I have been living a carefree and happy life like a god. I can’t be happier!

“Don’t look at me like that. Of course, I’m not really heartless. It’s just that, well, actually, I have a vague premonition that Li Yao is not dead yet. Really, as the saying goes, good people don’t live long… Heroes live for thousands of years. How could a true hero and a good man like Li Yao die so easily, right? Anyway, no one saw his body. Okay, his cell fragments have indeed been discovered, but at least there is no evidence that his soul has been obliterated. We have to be confident in his vitality. Even cockroaches can exist for hundreds of millions of years. Why can’t ‘Vulture Li Yao’ do the same, right?”

“Anyways, just don’t come any closer. If you come any closer, I’m really going to shout for help. Judge, where’s the judge? I want to surrender. I want to surrender. How am I going to get out of this crappy arena? Help! Help!”

Long Yangjun screamed and fled.

Chapter 3153: Different Capabilities

While Ding Lingdang and Long Yangjun were fighting, brilliant matches were taking place in the other fifteen arenas.

In the second ring, thousands of mini drones were condensed into the shape of a super-large girl. Mystic rays covered her body like flowing water. In the glittering brightness, she put on a peaceful smile.

Tilting her head, she eyed her opponent curiously.

Her opponent did not have a body of flesh and blood, either. His intimidating soul data was carried by an old, rusted spiritual puppet. The civil-looking puppet seemed to have been rolling in the mud. Not only were the gears and the bearings stuffed with dust, green sprouts were also growing out of the joints. Nobody could tell whether he was a cold machine or the warm home of the grass.

“Lord Fist King…”

Wen Wen said in surprise, “I thought that you would show up in the latest version of ‘Super Metropolis’. I didn’t know that you had become so natural and harmonious. It seems that your training over the past three years has brought you to a whole new level!”

“Both the body of flesh and the bones of steel are carriers and containers. As long as their life information is burning, what’s the difference between the rusted, old-fashioned puppets and the super-large Iron City that is armed to the teeth?

The King of Fist extended his right hand and opened his palm.

A yellow flower suddenly grew out of the joint of his palm.

He looked at the flower, with vivid tenderness beaming out of his eyes. He said in a low voice, “I’ve destroyed countless towns and butchered thousands of enemies. But the thrill of destruction and slaughter is nothing compared to the touching feeling when I found a flower growing out of my palm. Does it look good?”

“Very nice.”

Wen Wen nodded and asked curiously, “Lord Fist King, how did you train yourself over the past three years? It seems that everybody has found their own opportunities and adventures in the Heaven’s Path Tower and the black wall. What were your adventures?”

“I didn’t have any adventures, not even training. I simply travelled to different planets and modified the environment there, trying to grow flowers and plants in the harshest environments.

The Fist King paused for a moment. The glow of his crystal camera became even gentler as he said, “I promised a… friend that I would help her plant gold foil throughout the entire universe. Although gold foil is a plant that is resistant to cold, drought, and radiation and has a very strong vitality, it is still not an easy task to breed different variants, spread them on planets with different environments, and promote and balance them with the local ecosystem.

“It took me three years and every second to plant more than ten planets. I’ve been rolling in the farmlands and forests every day. I don’t want any training at all.

“Not just me, young man. I’m told that you’ve refused to explore and train in the primeval relics, too. Why? Aren’t there quite a few crystal processors and Spiritual Nexus technologies of the pioneer civilizations in the primeval relics that can give great enlightenment to an information life such as yourself? Moreover, the ‘other half’ of the Pangu civilization and the mainframe crystal processor of the Heaven’s Origin Sector are endless secret treasures for the information life forms. Isn’t your brother ‘Little Ming’ soaking in them and absorbing the mysteries crazily? It is said that he is about to surpass ‘Fuxi’. Why aren’t you with him?”

“Because I have more important things to do.”

With a sweet smile, Li Yao replied without the slightest regret, “The Covenant Alliance cannot live without a mainframe. I have to shoulder the responsibility that the ‘half of the Fuxi’ left behind and help all the people of the Covenant Alliance rebuild their society and civilization. As you know, three years ago, the social system of the entire Covenant Alliance was on the verge of collapse. Many planets were in a state of famine and riot. If we ignored them, billions of people would be killed at any moment. Those who survived would turn into mentally deformed monsters. Faced with the chaos and disorder on every planet, I could not turn a blind eye.

“Is that so…”

The Fist King nodded. “I can tell that you’ve managed the Holy Alliance well. The nation that once fell into the abyss of destruction has struggled to crawl out and walk on the right track again.

“No. It is far from good enough.

Wen Wen shook his head and said, “The road to the future is long. Super-information, deep-network, a brand-new civilization where human beings and machines are interacting every second. What does it look like exactly? How can we avoid repeating the mistakes of the ‘Fuxi’ and the ‘super-body civilization’? We have been racking our brains, too.

“Yes. The road to the future is too long. Even if you and I have extraordinary computational ability, we cannot calculate every step of the future, not even the next second.

The boxing champion smiled and said, “However, it is precisely because we cannot calculate that we are ‘life’. We are truly… alive, aren’t we?”

Wenwen also laughed.

“Lord Fist King, do we still need to fight?”

Wen Wen asked respectfully, “Over the past three years, I have not absorbed any information about combat. As you know, my core data is more focused on management and operations. I’m not interested in exploration or conquest. Maybe, Little Ming is the most suitable opponent for you.”

“I don’t think so.”

Brightness was beaming out of the boxing champion’s eyes. “Only by building a beautiful home with one’s own hands can one better understand the true meaning of the word ‘protect’. Fighting for conquest and fighting for protection are two completely different concepts. I’ve already seen Xiao Ming outside of the meeting hall. At that time, he had crawled into the test machine that was more than two hundred meters tall and used the super-large steel devil ‘Thunder Destroyer’ to flaunt his might. He didn’t notice that I had flown to the back of his head and observed me quietly.

“I can tell that he has indeed learned a lot in the primeval relics, and the ‘Thunder Destroyer’ is indeed a new war machine that represents the future. However…

“He is still not qualified to be my opponent.

“If I wanted to, I could paralyze him within one minute with my ragged body!

“A simple conqueror and destroyer is like this. No matter how strong they are, they are nothing to be scared of.

“But you are different.

“We’ve been communicating for three minutes. During the three minutes, I’ve been calculating how long it will take for me to completely suppress you, and the result is… insufficient information and impairment?

“Interesting. Wen Wen, your silly little brother still doesn’t know that you are actually stronger than him, does he?”

“Everyone has a different path. My path is different from Little Ming’s. We cannot simply measure each other’s strength.

Wen Wen still shook her head. “I’m not good at fighting. I only like to protect.”

“Then, let’s fight for ‘protection’!

The flower in the Fist King’s palm shook slightly before it retreated back into his palm.

The boxing champion raised the fist of the old civil spiritual puppet and smiled at Wen Wen.

…

In the third arena.

It was the most dangerous of the sixteen arenas.

As far as one’s eyes could see, there was not a single flat spot on the layered land. There were interconnecting stalagmites and sharp cones everywhere, which seemed to be mixed with rare minerals. The edges and the tips of the stalagmites were glittering and unstoppable, like a hell of swords and spears.

Yan Liren was sitting cross-legged on the handle of his sword. The tip of the sword was pointing down and touching the tip of a stalagmite that was sharper than a needle.

It seemed to be on the verge of collapse, but in fact, it was as stable as a mountain. Yan Liren didn’t even bother to look at his opponent. He stared at the stalagmites around him in a trance, as if he was imagining the stalagmites as billions of flying swords that would be engaged in an earthshaking battle in his brain.

Hundreds of meters away from him, a large area of stalagmites and cones had been crushed by an invisible force field, revealing a round space that looked like a crater. Standing on the top of the crater was a Pangu Clan member who was almost twelve meters tall and covered in heat sink holes.

“Are you Yan Liren?”

The Pangu Clan’s voice was as loud as a bell. They spoke the human language fluently. Of course, along with their speech, a thunderous brainwave was sent out, too.

But Yan Liren didn’t feel anything. He was still staring at the stalagmite in a daze. He replied briefly, “Yes.”

“I’m told that you are one of the top experts of the human civilization. Over the past year, you’ve defeated more than ten experts among the survivors of the primeval era?”

The Pangu Clan continued questioning aggressively.

Yan Liren was still too lazy to nod. He replied, “Yes.”

“Those guys are not real experts. They are merely the experts, scholars, and researchers of the Heavenly Palace. They are not weak at all, but they are not far from it.

The Pangu Clan raised their head and said casually, “But ‘Obliteration’ is different. I was born to be a warrior. For the glory of the primeval era, I hibernated willingly for hundreds of thousands of years and was only woken up recently. Have you ever heard my name?”

Yan Liren rolled his eyes.

It was obvious that he wanted to shake his head.

But to avoid trouble, he nodded and said, “Yes.”

“Very good.”

The Pangu Clan nodded and said, “I didn’t know that the Pangu Universe would become like this hundreds of thousands of years later. Everything has changed. All the rules and purposes have been overturned. Everything needs to be adapted to again. Thankfully, pure warriors such as myself do not care about politics or the future of a civilization. We only care about martial arts. As long as we can fight, we can do anything!

Yan Liren replied, “Okay.”

The Pangu Clan frowned deeply. “Is it the language of the human civilization that likes to jump out word by word, or are you just a man of few words?”

“It’s not that I don’t like to talk.

Yan Liren yawned and finally finished his speech. “However, I always prefer to talk with my sword.

Huala!

One explosion. A hundred. Ten thousand.

The ten thousand stalagmites scattered around Yan Liren were cut off, crushed, and obliterated at the same time. Flying swords made of dust dashed out of the overwhelming dust.

Chapter 3154: Intruder?

Different arenas were equally splendid. The sixteen matches had just begun, but they were already a pleasant surprise for the audience, the citizens of the federation, and even the human beings far away in the imperium and the Holy Alliance.

It had never occurred to anyone that the capability of the strongest human beings would experience such drastic changes after only three years, which meant that the limits of the species of ‘human beings’ had been improved again. As long as they worked hard, everybody would be able to be like the super experts who could move mountains and overturn seas!

While the match continued, the organizer took the opportunity to hold press conferences and press conferences. One piece of revolutionary, cutting-edge technology after another, including the ancient legends of the Pioneer Civilization and the Primeval Civilization, spread out in the audience and on the network, laying a solid foundation for the final exposure of the ‘Flood—Super Body Civilization’.

In the seven days and seven nights that followed, human beings were all immersed in the surging tides. Their world view was shattered and reshaped time and time again. Even the dumbest and the most indifferent citizens realized that an unprecedented era of surging wind and thunder had gradually begun. From now on, whether they were willing or not, the people who had been lurking in the Pangu Universe for billions of years would resume the mission of the primeval era and march into the sea of the multiverse to face what they had been faced with all the time.

The future meeting was still ongoing.

There were too many things that needed to be discussed and announced. The congress that gathered the wisdom, strength, and hope of mankind would not be over until several months later.

Although it was already midnight, the headquarters of the Glorious Sunlight Group was still a brightly lit city. Several important friendly matches and exhibition matches of new war machines were still taking place in various venues.

Outside of the hall, two women were walking slowly one after another in a small garden.

It had been a long time since Ding Lingdang and Jin Xinyue, two competitors in a delicate relationship who were both enemies and friends, had gathered together and enjoyed peace and tranquility.

Ding Lingdang suddenly stopped.

In front of her, in the middle of the garden, stood a statue of Li Yao.

This was the headquarters of the Glorious Sunlight Group. As the founder of the group and a super hero of the federation, souvenirs related to Li Yao were everywhere in this place.

Ding Lingdang stared at the statue for a long time.

Jin Xinyue frowned, but she did not dare interrupt Ding Lingdang’s stare.

Ding Lingdang stared at him for a long time, her eyes slightly red. Suddenly, she laughed and shook her head. “No, no. When has Li Yao ever been so righteous and serious?”

Jin Xinyue was somewhat relieved. She said, “That’s right. When you were in the federation, you were carefree and happy all the time. Even when you were faced with the biggest problems, you hardly ever felt sad. I believe that you don’t want yourself or the people around you to be sad. It will be best if we face the biggest problems happily.”

“Yes, I know, I know…”

Ding Lingdang stared at the statue that didn’t look like Li Yao for a long time. Finally, she couldn’t help but reach out and brush off the dry leaves and fallen flowers on the statue’s shoulder. She said, “In fact, I don’t have anything to be sad about. Based on my life-and-death adventures with Li Yao and the intensity of our training by burning our souls time and time again, the two of us could have been killed at any moment. We are already very lucky to be alive and kicking for so long and to be able to stay by each other’s side at the last moment. Don’t you think so, Xinyue?”

Jin Xinyue opened her mouth, but she did not know what to say. Of course, she could not really say things like ‘yes, Master’s wife, you are already very lucky to be alive and kicking right now’.

“Over the past three years, I’ve slowly accepted the fact that ‘Li Yao has perished’. However, when I fought against Long Yangjun, the hateful woman, she said that Li Yao might not be dead yet.

Ding Lingdang hesitated for a moment before continuing, “The speaker may not mean it, but the listener will. Xinyue, don’t laugh at my imagination. Do you think your master is still alive?”

“Well…”

Jin Xinyue tilted her head and thought for a long time. Then she said carefully, “Strictly speaking, nobody saw that Master was really dead. There are only two points that everybody saw and inferred. Firstly, Master entered the ‘Rainbow Bridge’, a super teleportation array. His body and soul were fully charged and sent out of the Pangu Universe. His destination was probably the center of the Flood Tide—Earth. Secondly, in order to keep the information about the Pangu Universe from the enemy, Master chose to detonate his own soul and erase all the intelligence about him and the Flood Tide Warship. All the information was reset to zero.

“However, can information really be erased and returned to zero?

“The Dark Moon Foundation, as well as the Secret Sword Department, often searched for the crystal processors of their targets when they were collecting intelligence. Most of the time, when they felt that they were being targeted, they would destroy all the information stored in the crystal processors first. However, for the experts of crystal processors, they would leave a trace wherever they went. Any information that had existed in the past could never be ‘completely destroyed’ or erased. To recover the information, they simply had to ask ‘time, technology, and price’.

“As for Master’s situation, generally speaking, when a Cultivator detonates his soul, he is, of course, deader than a doornail. But there are exceptions. For example, Lu Qingchen during the final battle against the Fuxi Clan. The big devil gambled on his own soul, hoping that Fu Xi would interrupt his self-destruction and repair part of his soul fragments. In the end, he won the gamble!

“When you detonated your soul, Master, it happened to be the moment when the rainbow bridge was fully functioning and the super teleportation was activated. Did you start the teleportation only after you detonated your soul, or were some of the soul fragments sent over so that the self-destruction was not completed? I don’t know.”

“Which means—”

Ding Lingdang’s eyes were glittering. “You also think that your master might still be alive?”

“I really don’t know. I don’t even know whether I should hope that Master is still alive or not.

Jin Xinyue smiled bitterly. “From my perspective, of course, I hope that you can live to five hundred years old, Master. But if the scraps of your soul are not entirely gone and some information is still left, and the Flood Tide civilization learns of it, it will not be a good thing for the entire Pangu Universe.

“…Yes, you’re right.”

Ding Lingdang’s smile turned bitter, too. She lowered her voice and said, “That being said, I won’t let go of the only clue about ‘Earth’. Whether or not the Earth that Li Yao saw in the uncanny dream was the common origin of the super body civilization and the Primeval Civilization billions of years ago, I will go to the Earth and find out the truth one day. Even if I can’t find Li Yao’s soul pieces, I will at least fulfill his unfulfilled wish.

“Master’s wife, are you ready?”

“Destroy the Earth in your place, Master!” Jin Xinyue said.

“What is ‘ready’ for such a thing?

Ding Lingdang said, “Of course, I can’t wait to leave for Earth tomorrow. But there are limits to my recklessness. The federation and the Pangu Universe still have a lot of things to deal with, and our space jump technology can only be limited to the Pangu Universe. We haven’t reached the level where we can roam freely in the sea of the multiverse yet. The road ahead of us is slow. I’m afraid that we cannot set off without three to five hundred years of hard work. Three to five hundred years. Alas, three to five hundred years!”

Ding Lingdang’s fingers tightened and loosened.

Jin Xinyue was about to offer a few comforting words, when sharp alarms suddenly echoed around, no, inside, and outside of the headquarters of the Glorious Sunlight Group.

Ding Lingdang and Jin Xinyue were both high-level officials of the federation. Naturally, they knew what the alert meant. Judging from the rhythm and intensity, it was a warning of the highest intensity of an air attack, indicating that an unknown enemy with high-energy reactions had broken through the defense circle of the Heaven’s Origin Sector and was approaching the atmosphere. The defense fleet of the Heaven’s Origin Sector in the low-Earth orbit could not resist it at all!

However—

How was that possible?

Ding Lingdang and Jin Xinyue looked at each other in shock.

It was impossible for any enemy to attack the Heaven’s Origin Sector at this moment, at least not in the Pangu Universe.

Although the ‘Future Meeting’ was held in the headquarters of the Glorious Sunlight Group, it was merely a memorial for Li Yao, who had perished three years ago.

Even with the size and capability of the Glorious Sunlight Group, it was too much of a stretch to hold a meeting that would determine the future of the civilization of mankind.

The meeting was co-hosted by the leadership of the Star Glory Federation, the Imperium of True Human Beings, and the Holy Accord Alliance. Other than everything else, the arrival of the emperor and the queen mother of the Imperium of True Human Beings in the capital city of the Star Glory Federation for the first time, together with the arrival of the mysterious leader of the Sanctuary, was more than enough for them to bring their own forces, and the federation would have to deploy more elites to guard and alert them.

What was displayed in the meeting in the future were the cutting-edge achievements that the civilization of mankind had made in the past three years. The top 500 experts of the three major forces, as well as the powerful Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan, were all here.

Today, the Heaven’s Origin Sector was literally ‘a forest of experts and a storm of warriors’. There were as many marvelous weapons as there were hairs on a cow. The ultimate war machines could be found everywhere. Even the defense of the low-Earth orbit had been entrusted to the Burning Prairie Fleet and the Kunlun Fleet.

The Burning Prairie Fleet and the Kunlun Fleet were both deep-space fleets that could march across thousands of space zones and fight alone for three to five years to protect a small planet. Even the capital planet of the federation would be too wasteful.

Under such a tight defense, how could the enemy have snuck into the inner part of the orbital ring without alarming anyone? What were the two fleets of Prairie Fire and Kunlun doing exactly?

Ding Lingdang and Jin Xinyue raised their heads subconsciously and looked at the mysterious night sky.

In the night sky, a deep blue star was glittering like a blossoming orchid.

Chapter 3155: Messenger

The orbit of the Heaven’s Origin Sector.

Surrounded by the Burning Prairie Fleet, the Kunlun Fleet, and the latest fleet from the Star Ocean Imperium and the Covenant Alliance, the tiny blue crystal approached the atmosphere of the capital city of the federation unhurriedly.

Compared to the invincible steel beasts that dominated the sea of stars, the blue crystal was like a small flower that could be easily crushed.

However, after only five minutes, it had already attracted the attention of all the leadership and experts on the planet. The federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance, as well as the survivors of the primeval era, were all focused on it.

Cold sweat was popping up on the foreheads of many generals and Marshals who were holding heavy troops and could fire a million cannons at the same time at any moment. White smoke was spurting out of their throats, and the redness almost drowned their eyes.

Weird. The seemingly plain blue crystal was too weird.

First of all, nobody knew how it appeared. To ensure the safety of the ‘Future Meeting’, the space zone at the periphery of the Heaven’s Origin Sector was packed with crystal cameras and spiritual energy scanning magical equipment. Not even a meteoroid was allowed in, not to mention the blue crystals whose features were so obvious that their brightness and ripples were not concealed at all.

Then, was he teleported through a space jump through a crack in the four-dimensional space?

It was impossible, too, that the Burning Prairie Fleet, the Kunlun Fleet, the satellite base of the Heaven’s Origin Sector, or the astronomical observatory on the ground did not detect any abnormal space ripples or signs of the birth of man-made wormholes. It just showed up out of nowhere in the middle of the orbital defense ring and the atmosphere. Even the two fleets failed to discover it for three minutes. It was not until it sent two waves to the Burning Prairie Fleet and the Kunlun Fleet that the two fleets were shocked and alarmed.

No one knew what it was, what it wanted, and whether it was good or bad. Naturally, no one dared to attack it first.

However, Prairie Fire and the Kunlun Fleet had indeed sent quite a few engineering ships and survey starships to capture it, or at least to control it with the interference force field.

However, no matter how hard they tried to increase the power of the interference force field or how many experts they sent to perform remote manipulation techniques, they could not change the trajectory and speed of the blue crystal. It seemed to be immune to all the interference force fields and mystic rays.

In the end, the commander stationed in the area made up his mind and simply commanded an engineering ship to block the route of the blue crystal. However, such a reckless approach failed to stop the enemy. The enemy seemed to have no real body at all, or rather, its real body was in a different dimension. It was merely a projection that arrived in the Pangu Universe and passed through the engineering ship.

Ding Lingdang, Li Jialing, Long Yangjun, the Fist King, Wen Wen, Lei Chenhu, Boss Bai… The leadership of the three forces arrived at the temporary command center on ‘Kunlun’ on shuttles. The blue crystal was only one step away from the atmosphere.

It seemed to possess a clear consciousness and a high degree of wisdom. When it discovered that the strongest experts of the human civilization had come to its side, it actually stopped and floated above the atmosphere quietly, blossoming with the brilliance of an orchid.

“What is it exactly? How did it pass through the defense lines and appear above the atmosphere of the Heaven’s Origin Sector without alarming anyone? Is it material or energy? How can it be immune to our interference force field and pierce through a starship without being damaged at all? What is going on exactly?”

Ever since the ‘Falling Battle’ in the ancient relics three years ago, the top experts of the human civilization had been living in fear. Ignorance was irrelevant. Before, their understanding of the ‘Black Wall Manufacture’, the ‘Primeval Race’, and the ‘Flood Tide’ had been hazy. They had thought that the worst that could happen was that they would die. But after three years of exploration and research, as their understanding grew deeper and deeper, fear and desperation had been haunting them from time to time. Everybody knew clearly that, no matter how big a leap they made in the past three years, if the Flood Tide arrived at this moment, not only would the human civilization be unable to resist it, it would be difficult for them to die a happy death!

Therefore, even Ding Lingdang, who was a fearless heroine, was anxious and solemn when she saw the mysterious blue crystal.

While everybody was talking and feeling lost, the blue crystal suddenly disappeared.

“What?”

Gasps immediately echoed inside the cockpit of Kunlun. The eyes of all the experts seemed to be shooting bullets.

“Hello, everyone. There’s no need to be nervous. I’m back.”

Before they swallowed the last mouthful of cold air, a thin, soft, and seemingly embarrassed voice came from behind them.

Although it sounded like a gentle female voice and did not carry any spiritual waves or mental attacks, it was enough to make all the experts’ hair stand on end.

Ding Lingdang jerked her head back and discovered that there was an additional person behind the stupefied crowd. A person who, although familiar, should absolutely not have appeared in this place. It could not even be called a person, but rather… a ghost!

“Wei Qingqing?”

Ding Lingdang was dumbfounded. She didn’t know whether to clench or relax her fists. Her expression was half confused and half absent-minded. She stammered, “Is—is it really you, Sister Qingqing?”

The experts and Ding Lingdang turned around at the same time. Many of them were from the Star Glory Federation, and those who had been in touch with and participated in the ‘Tinder Plan’ recognized the young girl who appeared out of thin air. She was Wei Qingqing, the ‘little bluebird’ who was wearing a light blue dress, with scattered freckles on her face and a blossoming orchid on her headband, and looked both gentle and stubborn. She was dressed like a village teacher and was not one of the first volunteers of the ‘Tinder Plan’.

But how was it possible?

Speaking of which, Wei Qingqing was also one of the most important guides for ‘Vulture Li Yao’, the super hero of the federation.

More than a hundred years ago, on the train to the Grand Desolate Plateau, it was her, Ding Yin, and six other Cultivators who had stood up and sacrificed themselves to protect the innocent civilians on the train, as well as Li Yao’s belief.

Wei Qingqing sacrificed her own body of flesh and blood. From then on, she turned into an imaginary spectral cultivator. Her clear and pure heart of Dao didn’t lose the slightest bit of radiance. She was still active in the educational battlefront, serving the public in her own way, fighting for her motherland and civilization.

A hundred years later, Professor Mo Xuan and Lu Qingchen pushed the ‘Tinder Plan’, hoping to find volunteers to go to the end of time and space and spread out the tinder of the human civilization so that they could be the professors of the new civilization billions of years later. When Wei Qingqing learned of the matter, she immediately decided to participate and demanded to be the first volunteer.

Of course, the ‘Tinder Plan’ that Professor Mo Xuan and Lu Qingchen promoted was not as pure and sacred as they claimed. They had other purposes, too.

However, Wei Qingqing was merely manipulated by the two of them and did not know their scheme at all. She was too focused on the ‘Tinder Plan’ to be confused with Mo Xuan and Lu Qingchen.

After Mo Xuan’s death, Lu Qingchen’s escape, and the failure of the Phantom Plan, under the insistence of Wei Qingqing, Gui Shishou, and the rest of the Cultivators, the ‘Tinder Plan’ was still carried out on a smaller scale. Wei Qingqing got what she wanted and became the first Pangu Clan member to be shot at the end of the sea of stars.

Calculating the time, it was almost ten years ago.

At that time, the small starship carrying Wei Qingqing’s soul had been jumping and drifting aimlessly in the sea of stars with outdated technology. Logically speaking, it should not have been able to fly very far, much less cross the black wall.

Was it because of a technical fault that Wei Qingqing woke up in advance and fled back to her hometown, unable to bear the loneliness of the long journey?

But that was not right. Wei Qingqing was a spectral Cultivator, or rather, a member of the ‘Spirit Clan’. She had lost her body of flesh and blood a hundred years ago. The only thing that was left was a vague magnetic field of life, but the female teacher in front of him had a warm body.

Ding Lingdang was only one step away from Wei Qingqing.

She could clearly feel the hot stream coming from Wei Qingqing’s body. She could clearly see the movement of every hair on the other party’s body. She could even sense the vitality of light and heat blossoming from every cell of the other party’s body.

It was a very young body that looked like a baby.

If Ding Lingdang could make such a mistake, the past three years, no, the past hundred years of training would have been in vain.

However, if the enemy was a human being made of flesh and blood, a new question popped up. How did she sneak into the cockpit of Kunlun through the most impenetrable defense lines at least thirty to fifty times with the clothes of a village teacher?

She, she was even wearing a pair of cheap-looking plastic sandals, the kind that would make “pa pa pa pa” sounds when she walked. However, whether it was Ding Lingdang or Long Yangjun, whether it was Li Jialing or Boss Bai, whether it was the boxing champion or Yan Liren, no one discovered her unless she spoke up herself!

“Sister Qingqing?”

Taking a deep breath, Ding Lingdang repeated the question that she and everyone else had asked. “Are you really Wei Qingqing—a volunteer of the ‘Tinder Plan’ of the Star Glory Federation?”

“Of course, I am Wei Qingqing, a volunteer of the Pyro Company.

Wei Qingqing smiled. Her smile was as stubborn and tranquil as a hundred years ago, but there was something else in the depths of her eyes. “But right now, I have a new identity — I am a messenger.”

Chapter 3156: More Residents!

“Messenger?”

Ding Lingdang chewed on this unexpected term and did not dare to let down her guard in the slightest. “Whose messenger—the Flood Tide?”

This time, it was Wei Qingqing who revealed a confused expression.

“What is a flood?”

She asked back.

Ding Lingdang was dazed for a moment. She realized that it did make sense. The so-called ‘flood’ was just a name that the carbon-based intelligent creatures inside the Pangu Universe gave to the super body civilization. It would be weird if Wei Qingqing knew the name.

She thought for a moment and asked, “Then, is it the ‘super-body civilization’, or the ‘Primeval civilization’?”

Everyone stared at Wei Qingqing nervously.

Her answer would change the future of the entire Pangu Universe.

“Superhuman civilization and… Primeval civilization?”

Wei Qingqing said, “If my guess is correct, you are referring to the two quasi-god civilizations that originated from the ‘Primeval Earth’ billions of years ago and eventually swept across the entire cosmos? I didn’t know that you already knew so much. If so, it will be very convenient for us to communicate. I suppose you can call me a messenger of the ‘Primeval Civilization’. However, strictly speaking, the Primeval Civilization as a whole has been destroyed a long time ago. The only survivors and the refugees are scattered in unknown places such as the Pangu Universe in the depths of the sea of stars. If you continue resisting stubbornly, I will consider myself a messenger of the ‘Resistance Alliance’!”

Hearing Wei Qingqing’s words, everyone was somewhat relieved.

As long as she was not a messenger of the ‘Flood Tide—Superhuman Civilization’, everything was good.

Even so, everyone’s heart was still in their throat.

Firstly, Wei Qingqing might not be speaking the truth. In fact, what appeared in front of everyone’s eyes might not be the real Wei Qingqing. It was possible that a certain mysterious existence that was beyond the comprehension of human beings, after swallowing Wei Qingqing’s soul, “created” a vivid imitation that even she did not know about.

Secondly, even if she was indeed Wei Qingqing and was speaking the truth, it didn’t mean that everyone was in an absolutely safe state. Since the survivors and fugitives of the Primeval Civilization could find the Pangu Universe, how far away were the claws of the Flood Tide?

Didn’t Wei Qingqing’s words compare with the information inside the Heaven’s Origin Tower and prove that the ‘Primeval Civilization’ had really been destroyed? Wasn’t the current Universe Ocean under the control of the ‘Flood—Super Body Civilization’?

What is going on?

Ding Lingdang’s eyes were sharp. She said solemnly, “Can you talk to us nicely, Sister Qingqing?”

“Of course.”

Wei Qingqing smiled and said, “I came back to the Star Glory Federation to seek the help of the entire civilization of mankind in order to complete my mission. I thought that the civilization of mankind was still in a state of mutual hostility and that it would take a lot of effort for me to unite everyone. I didn’t expect that I would sense thousands of tyrannical life-forms in the sky of the Heaven’s Origin Sector. There are even intelligent life-forms other than human beings. It seems that the Star Glory Federation and the entire sea of stars have undergone tremendous changes over the past ten years. Everyone has realized the threat and united in advance! If so, I believe you will be able to understand my mission more easily.

“However, in order to save each other’s time, can you tell me a thing or two about the latest changes in the situation between the Star Glory Federation, the Star Glory Imperium, and the Star Glory Federation? How did you do it in the past ten years? How did you gather so many experts of different forces and even different species? It is almost impossible!

“Right. What about Li Yao? He should not be hiding in such a place where so many experts are gathered, should he?”

“Li Yao…”

Ding Lingdang took a deep breath and said sadly, “He has passed away.”

“What?”

Wei Qingqing was slightly startled. Her smile was like a withered flower as she faintly sighed, “Cultivators of our generation fear death. The little brother we met on the Grand Desolate Plateau train a hundred years ago has finally embarked on such a road!”

“We can talk about Li Yao later.”

Ding Lingdang pulled herself together and said, “First of all, how can we believe that you are the real Wei Qingqing and that you came with good intentions? I’m sorry. I respect Sister Qingqing as much as Li Yao does, but it is exactly because I respect you that I have to figure out that you are the real you.”

“I understand what you mean. If you don’t want Mo Xuan and Lu Qingchen’s accident to happen again, you should be a hundred times more prudent. However, there is no answer to your question. Even I often ask myself when I look at my brand-new body, ‘Am I the real Wei Qingqing, or am I a monster who does not know that I have Wei Qingqing’s memories?’

Wei Qingqing opened her hands and said frankly, “However, on second thought, the answer to the question is unnecessary. I did not encounter the remnants of the ‘Resistance Alliance’ after I climbed over the black wall. I met them inside the black wall, in the sea of stars of the Pangu Universe. In other words, they knew the general direction of the Pangu Universe and could send a second and a millionth starship if they sent one.

“As for the issue of intelligence leakage… I don’t want to know any top secrets. I just want to know the news media and the public news that everyone on the network knows. Even if you don’t tell me, I can slowly collect it myself. What’s the difference?

“Of course, I still hope that you can trust me and complete your mission as quickly as possible. That is the only way to save our civilization.

Ding Lingdang and the other experts fell silent.

Wei Qingqing was right.

Since she could appear on ‘Kunlun’ without alarming anyone, it was quite possible that she could vanish into thin air, too. Nobody could stop her.

If she had ill intentions, she could have gathered enough intelligence through the network and the public news media. Why would she show up in front of everybody and attract their attention?

Ding Lingdang exchanged a meaningful look with the leadership of the three forces. With everyone’s permission, she told Wei Qingqing about the changes in the federation and the entire Pangu Universe during the ten years she was away.

He did not hide anything about the ancient relics and the Heaven’s Origin Sector, either. Of course, it was just a brief description that was not out of the range of the ‘Future Meeting’ and would not cause any leakage.

Wei Qingqing listened with rapt attention.

“So it was like this!”

Admiration appeared on her face when she heard the last part of the story. She exclaimed, “I knew that Li Yao was a man of miracles, but I didn’t know that the miracles he created in the end far exceeded the limits of my imagination. Such a shocking and magnificent legend would be an out-and-out legend even in the center of the cosmos!

“Then—”

Ding Lingdang looked at Wei Qingqing in the eyes and asked, “Sister Qingqing, what is your legend?”

“Me? I’m not a legend. I’m just an ordinary little bluebird who received a distress call from the depths of the multiverse by accident. I cannot accomplish such an enormous mission by myself. I can only return to my hometown and ask for the help of my compatriots.

Wei Qingqing said, “Of course, I am willing to tell you everything that I know, no matter how big or small, but please pay attention to two points.

“Firstly, in the space zone at the periphery of the Pangu Universe, I only encountered the remains of a starship of the Resistance Alliance. The starship that stored the distress letters seemed to have experienced millions of space jumps in the blink of an eye. It was shattered and almost obliterated. I only got part of its information, which is literally ‘a drop in the bucket’ and cannot describe the whole thing clearly.

“Secondly, including myself, the human civilization of the Pangu Universe is far away from the ‘quasi-god-level civilizations’ such as the Primeval Civilization and the Super Entity Civilization. Trying to figure out the life form and social structure of the quasi-god-level civilizations with our wisdom and thinking, including their methods and purposes, is like a blind man groping for an elephant. The more I say, the more mistakes I make, and the more I lead you on the wrong path.

“Another example. We are like fish that don’t even have eyes in the depths of the ocean. We rely on the garbage that falls from the surface of the ocean to imagine the form and purpose of the human civilization on land—how could we imagine it?

“Therefore, my words are just a reference. The final answer still depends on us.

“I understand. Aren’t we wandering in the dark night right now, stumbling and looking for vague answers?

Ding Lingdang said, “Even if Sister Qingqing can only provide us with a weak torch, at the very least, it can illuminate the road within a radius of a few meters and let us know which direction we should take the first step in. This is enough. Please begin, Sister Qingqing.”

“Alright.”

Wei Qingqing pondered for a moment and said, “There are so many things that I don’t know where to begin. Let’s put it this way. We all know that billions of years ago, on a planet named ‘Earth’, the first civilization of mankind was born. Then, with the arrival of the meteoroid disaster, the civilization of mankind was divided into two branches. One of them left Earth and rushed into the universe. In the end, it evolved into the two dominators of the sea of stars, the ‘Super Entity Civilization’ and the ‘Primeval Civilization’. In the end, the Primeval Civilization was defeated in a vast, long, and cruel war.

“However, a failure does not equate to total annihilation. The super civilization and the Primeval Civilization are both quasi-god civilizations of unimaginable scale. Their tentacles have already extended to billions of stars. Even though the protracted war lasted a hundred million years, it is not safe to say that all the members of the Primeval Race have been annihilated. It is as if we are the descendants of a certain branch of the Primeval Race.

“Such a situation is similar to the survival competition between the Neanderthals and the Homo sapiens on Earth in the early years. The ‘war’ between the two parties lasted tens of thousands of years, sometimes even hundreds of thousands of years. Often, the Neanderthals in one place had already been wiped out by the Homo sapiens. However, the Neanderthals in another place were still thriving and even developed civilizations in various forms.

“It can’t be helped. The multiverse ocean is too vast. In many places, even space and time have lost their significance. Even if the super body civilization is as strong as an overwhelming flood, it is impossible for it to sweep every star within billions of years.

“Besides, the Primeval Civilization was the dominator of the sea of stars in the past after all. The super body civilization paid a high price to destroy it!

Chapter 3157: The Aftermath Of The Vulture Plan

“What?”

Wei Qingqing’s last sentence ignited the flames of hope in the hearts of all the top experts. Ding Lingdang couldn’t help but interrupt her. With shining eyes, she asked, “You mean to say that the super body civilization also paid a terrible price in the ‘Starry Universe Competition’ to the point that they were ‘heavily wounded’?”

“Yes.”

Wei Qingqing nodded her head and said, “Birth, old age, illness, and death. This is the law of nature. Everything has its own destiny. When there is birth, there will be destruction. When there is prosperity, there will be decline. Even for a super body civilization, it is impossible to get rid of this eternal law.

“I don’t know what kind of strike the super body civilization suffered, nor do I know its ‘injuries’. I only know that, for tens of millions of years, it temporarily let go of the pursuit of the survivors of the Primeval Civilization and the refugees, but it has been resting in the center of the sea of the universe, including the carbon-based intelligent creatures of the Pangu Universe. The development of a hundred generations of civilization is also related to the hibernation of the super body civilization. I can’t think of a second reason other than its ‘heavy wounds’.

“In short, the Star Ocean Imperium billions of years ago ended with the collapse of the Primeval Civilization. But the split, fleeing, and hibernating Primeval Beings did not give up resistance—at least, they did not give up struggling. For tens of millions of years, they might have forgotten the glory of their ancestors, their origins, and even their original strength, but they developed a new, splendid civilization according to the different environments of their hiding places.

“Just like us?”

Ding Lingdang thought for a moment and asked again, “Since the collapse of the Star Ocean Imperium ten thousand years ago, the survivors have evolved into brand-new social systems and civilizations in various Sectors of the Pangu Universe, such as the imperium, the Holy Alliance, the federation, the Flying Star Sector, the Heaven’s Origin Sector, the Blood Demon Sector, and the ancient sacred world. There are all kinds of bizarre things in them.

“Yes, it is.”

Wei Qingqing added, “Other than the different branches of the fleeing Primeval Clan, there are also countless low-level native civilizations and alien species in the center of the sea of stars. They are not willing to submit to the super bodies, either, because of the terrifying flood. Instead, they have formed an alliance with the remnants of the Primeval Clan to resist the flood together.

“Wait!”

Ding Lingdang shouted on behalf of everybody again. “Don’t the low-level civilizations and the alien species hate the Primeval Civilization? I remember that the Primeval Civilization appeared as ‘conquerors’ and savaged the entire universe in the beginning.

“There are no eternal friends, only eternal interests. The undeniable truth in the civilization of mankind holds true among the intelligent species in the multiverse.

Wei Qingqing explained, “That’s right. In the beginning, the Primeval Civilization’s image in the sea of stars was indeed that of a ‘brutal conqueror’. They destroyed the homes of countless intelligent species and the nests of other species in space. They were notorious and full of evil.

“However, that was millions of years ago. Do you care that some primitive tribe launched an attack on your ancestors’ primitive tribe millions of years ago?

“The glory of the Primeval Civilization has passed a long, long time ago. Right now, all that is left is a bunch of battered and exhausted soldiers. They have never even enjoyed the slightest bit of benefits from the conquest of the universe by their ancestors. From the moment they were born, they have been shivering in the shadow of the flood tide, living a life of anxiety. What’s the point of retaliating against such a bunch of stray dogs?

“On the Flood Tide civilization’s side, it’s true that they appeared as avengers who preside over justice and peace when they were still weak. They were also supported by countless low-level native civilizations and alien species in space. The ‘big data superlink’ in the beginning should not have involved the obliteration of free will, either. Therefore, the super civilization was trusted by countless species and civilizations and expanded crazily.

“However, when it turned from the ‘Ghost Avenger’ on the planet that had been destroyed a long time ago into an apocalyptic cosmic tide, terrible changes must’ve taken place inside it, too, which made countless native civilizations and space residents realize that it was not as bright and kind as it appeared to be and that it might be an even more formidable enemy than the Primeval Civilization.

“At the very least, the Primeval Civilization is an enemy that is ‘understandable within the normal range’. The strong prey on the weak. The winners are kings. Survival of the fittest is survival of the fittest. All species and civilizations are the same. Whoever receives the power of the Primeval Civilization will embark on the same path as the Primeval Civilization. Everybody is the same. There is no one more evil than anybody else.

“But the super civilization… is entirely different. It is incomprehensible, unpredictable, and even indescribable. Its true form and purpose have always been hidden in the mist.

“When the low-level civilizations and the alien species in space suddenly realized the truth, it was already too late. The people who were connected to the super bodies could never be freed again. They could only send warnings to those who had not joined them yet. Most of them did not want to believe the warnings and chose to be swallowed by the flood. But some of the species and civilizations who were not willing to cooperate with other civilizations chose to make peace with their enemies millions of years ago and face the ‘enemy of freedom’ together.

“All in all, there are two such forces in the sea of the multiverse today. One of them is the Flood Tide, the super body civilization, and the other is the ‘Alliance of Resistance’, which consists of all the people who are against the Flood Tide with the remnants of the Primeval Civilization as the core.

“Understood.”

Ding Lingdang nodded. “Then, how did you run into the starships of the ‘Resistance Alliance’ and become their messenger? What do they want? More importantly, have the Resistance Alliance or the flood found our coordinates?”

“Probably not yet.”

Wei Qingqing explained, “Although it is named ‘Alliance’, the overall strength of the Resistance Alliance is not very strong, at least compared to the Flood Tide. If the Flood Tide is a regular occupying force armed to the teeth, it is just an organization like the ‘cosmic guerrillas’. Even its predecessor, the Primeval Civilization at its peak, was no match for the Flood Tide, much less it.

“Therefore, for thousands of years, the coalition of the rebels has been mostly hiding and fleeing. They rarely take the initiative to attack. It can even be said that ‘the fugitives communicate with each other and help each other so that it will be easier for them to run away’ is a rather helpless slogan. One more day of survival is a victory.

“However, at the moment when the Resistance Alliance launched countless ‘Messenger starships’ into the universal ocean, the Alliance learned a piece of precious news through secret channels and after a lot of risk. It was said that some ‘awakened people’ in the depths of the flood rebelled and launched a great rebellion, which was code-named ‘Vulture Plan’. Although the rebellion failed to destroy the flood, it dealt another heavy blow to the flood, which put it in its weakest state in the past tens of millions of years.

“What?”

Ding Lingdang seemed to have two thunders tied to her feet. She screamed and almost jumped up.

Those who knew the truth, such as Xiao Ming and Wen Wen, were shocked, too, and did not know what to say for a long time.

“What’s wrong?”

Wei Qingqing was slightly surprised, “Is there a problem?”

“N-no.”

Taking a deep breath, Ding Lingdang held her chest and said, “Sister Qingqing, please continue.”

“The Resistance Alliance realized that it was a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity after verifying the authenticity of the ‘Vulture Plan’.

Wei Qingqing continued, “Before that, the Flood Tide, a super civilization, had occupied more than 90% of the space zones in the middle of the Universe Ocean. The stray dogs of the Resistance Alliance were hiding everywhere. Their living space was getting smaller and smaller. It was impossible for them to develop a civilization of a higher level. Therefore, they made a comeback and fought the Flood Tide to the death.

“Let’s take it down while it’s down. If we can’t defeat it when the tide is at its weakest, we will never be able to defeat it and lose all hope.

“Therefore, after tens of millions of years of seclusion, the coalition of the rebels went all out and prepared for the final battle against the flood with all their strength.

“As part of the final war, the coalition of the rebels sent countless ‘messenger starships’ to the remote, unpopulated places in the multiverse. They did not have a destination in mind. It was like a ‘broadcast’ for the entire universe, calling on all the species and civilizations that loved freedom and were not far away from the flood tide, including their compatriots from the beginning of the Yen civilization, to work together against the flood tide.

“During the tens of millions of years of dealing with the flood tide, the rebels who were struggling for survival developed some cutting-edge technologies that even the flood tide was not equipped with. For example, the ‘anti-space folding’ technology that can perform millions of space jumps within a short period of time. It can’t be helped. The speed of the fugitives is always a little bit higher than that of the hunters, because the hunters can always start over after one failure, and the fugitives will never have a second chance after one failure.

“Using the ‘anti-space folding’ technology and the precious resources that had been stored for tens of millions of years, the Resistance Alliance broadcast a large-scale broadcast to the edge of the multiverse. After tens of millions of space jumps, one of the starships finally climbed over the black wall and reached the Pangu Universe, where it detected my existence.

“Space jumps are extremely difficult. Even the cutting-edge starship built by the Resistance Alliance was seriously damaged and almost scrapped after tens of millions of space jumps. It was impossible to perform a new space jump. Even the ‘messenger’ inside the starship was killed by the four-dimensional storm. Therefore, the carrier chose me to be the new ‘messenger’. After I learned the reason and the consequences, I thought that it was necessary to let my compatriots know that it was the reason I returned to the Heaven’s Origin Sector.

Chapter 3158: Black Hole Rampart

Ding Lingdang and the other human experts pondered for a long time.

Although Wei Qingqing’s explanation was plausible, there were still a lot of loopholes and questions that had to be clarified.

“Then, what can we do for the ‘Resistance Alliance’?”

Ding Lingdang tried to ask, “Judging from your description and the technology that allowed you to reunite your real body with the ‘Messenger Starship’, although they are the remnants of the Primeval Civilization, their technology and civilization level are still far higher than ours. There is a huge gap between us and the Resistance. Are we really going to play any role in the competition of the ‘quasi-god civilization’?”

“Even a light straw can crush a camel at the critical moment.

Wei Qingqing said, “If the war between the two quasi-god civilizations comes to an impasse, whoever has the help of one more civilization or even one more starship might be able to completely reverse the situation. We don’t need to undervalue ourselves. After all, in terms of origin, we are all descendants of the Primeval Race, and we are all intelligent creatures from the ancient era. Why can’t we do what the Resistance Alliance can do? Even if we are really primitive people, in the Stone Age, the brains of the primitive people have basically fully developed. After a certain amount of training, even if we don’t know how to manufacture weapons, what’s wrong with learning to use the most advanced weapons?

“Specifically speaking, the Alliance of Resistance has installed a special automation technology on every carrier, which is the technology to build ‘anti-space folding thrusters’. With the input of astronomical resources and energy, brand-new carrier starships can be duplicated and the horn of war will be broadcasted.

“Well…”

Ding Lingdang blinked. “Why?”

“Do you still not understand?”

Wei Qingqing said, “The Resistance Alliance is very clear that the multiverse is extremely vast, and most of the refugees and survivors are hiding in the most secluded places. It is already the best we can do if hundreds of carrier starships can find a batch of survivors.
