“It is impossible to locate all the survivors with the carrier starships that the Alliance of Residents built themselves. Only with the help of the survivors themselves can the crucial information be spread to every corner of the Universe Ocean.

“If you still don’t understand, you might want to think about viruses. In fact, in the eyes of the flood tide, those of us who are disobedient and unwilling to join the flood tide are like viruses in the universe in the first place. It doesn’t matter if a messenger starship is destroyed. As long as it disseminates the technology to replicate itself, it is possible that ten or even a hundred messenger starships will be created and wake up countless people who have fallen into depravity, calling for everyone to stand up against it.

“I see.”

Ding Lingdang thought for a moment and realized what was going on. She exclaimed, “The Resistance Alliance does not care about the leakage of information at all. They are really going to fight the flood!”

“Yes. The Resistance Alliance has been hiding for tens of millions of years. All the roads are dead ends. None of the options are valid. We can only turn around and fight to the death.

Wei Qingqing said, “It doesn’t matter whether or not the information is leaked, whether or not some messenger starships are captured by the tide, and whether or not the technology of the Resistance Alliance is analyzed, because the Resistance Alliance has made up their mind to concentrate all their strength and attack the tide. They just want more help from the alliance before the attack begins.

“So—”

Ding Lingdang said, “In fact, you are not the only ‘messenger’. Are you hoping to turn everyone in the Pangu Universe into a messenger of the Resistance Alliance?”

“Yes.”

Wei Qingqing did not deny it. “Although the Messenger starships of the Resistance Alliance employ advanced automation technology and can be repaired and duplicated on their own, there is no way that they can be built without sufficient energy and resources. Only with the full support of the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance can we build ten or even a hundred Messenger starships.

“Besides, the carrier is not unmanned. It is best to carry a volunteer in order to complete the mission of ‘information relay’.

“If… we really build more messenger starships, where are we going to find more allies?

Ding Lingdang asked, “Are they running around like headless flies?”

“Based on the past experience of the Resistance Alliance, the survivors can be quite concentrated. When there are survivors in a certain universe, it often means that there are other survivors in the universe nearby. It is quite understandable, too, because we are all from the same fleet and have been separated during the long journey in the sea of stars. Of course, we are not far away from each other.

Wei Qingqing explained, “Moreover, many low-level native civilizations and alien species in space, such as the ‘Void Hunters’ who are adept at sailing in the sea of stars, have brought a lot of astronomical parameters at the edge of the multiverse. They can roughly infer the situation of certain universes and whether or not they are suitable for the survival of carbon-based intelligent creatures. Therefore, the search of the messenger starship is not entirely based on luck. There are certain patterns that we can follow. As long as we continue the work of the messenger starship, we will be able to search one universe after another. Just like in the beginning, you and Li Yao have gradually discovered the principle of ‘Crystal Sector, Heaven Ring Sector, Nether World, Tree Sea Sector, Ancient Sacred World’.

“Of course, there are dangers, and the risk factor is very high. Therefore, I am only going to publicize the information and propose suggestions. The final choice will still be decided by you.

“In addition, the Resistance Alliance has stored the approximate coordinates of the headquarters of their starship. If we can fully analyze the ‘anti-space folding technology’ stored inside the ‘Messenger starship’ one day, we will be able to reach the headquarters of the Resistance Alliance and join them. It will be a small gift, or a test, that they throw to us!

“After all, with the recruitment and preparation of the Resistance Alliance, the location of their headquarters must be no secret. The flood tide must’ve known it long ago. There is no point in keeping it a secret.

While Wei Qingqing was talking, her hands blossomed like orchids. A series of astronomical parameters and a chaotic black swirl floated out of her palms.

It was the ‘darkest’ thing that Ding Lingdang had ever seen. It almost swallowed the entire Kunlun.

“Ah!”

Right then, noises suddenly echoed behind them.

When Ding Lingdang turned around, she found that many experts and scholars of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan were greatly shocked.

“Sea of black holes! Sea of black holes!”

Sharp brainwaves from the experts of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan gathered into a new term.

“What do you mean?”

Ding Lingdang frowned.

After the explanation of an astronomer of the Nuwa Clan, the experts of the human race learned that, when the astronomers of the primeval era were studying in the Heavenly Palace, they had inferred from the astronomical information that had leaked into the flood tide that there was a very distant place beyond the Pangu Universe, perhaps the center of the cosmos. There was an area where the gravity was unimaginably high. Therefore, it was named ‘Giant Attractor’.

There were a lot of different theories about the origin of the ‘giant source’. Many primeval astronomers proposed their own hypotheses. One of the more popular theories was that there was an area where countless black holes congregated near the center of the Universe Ocean. Hundreds of thousands of black holes, if not millions, congregated there and formed a sea of black holes!

It was just the speculation of the primeval astronomers. With the current technology and exploration ability of the Pangu Universe, it was impossible for them to be supported by any evidence.

However, Wei Qingqing’s appearance should be the coordinates of the headquarters of the Resistance Alliance, which happened to be the theoretical location of the ocean of black holes. How could the astronomers not be shocked?

“It’s true that the headquarters of the Resistance Alliance is in the middle of a dense black hole. They have built an indestructible defense line with the black hole. It is known as the ‘black hole barrier’.

Wei Qingqing said, “This is also the last trump card that the Alliance of Resistance is confident to stand against the scourge of the flood. At this moment, billions of survivors and refugees are turning into ‘Awakened Ones’ and ‘Resistance’ and marching to the Black Hole Sea to form an army to resist the flood!

Building thousands of black holes into his own fortress!

Such blood-freezing technology was completely beyond everyone’s imagination.

Even though they had witnessed countless bizarre and unbelievable cutting-edge technologies in the primeval relics and the Heaven’s Path Tower over the past three years, the top experts of mankind and the primeval civilization still looked at each other in silence when they were faced with the techniques of a Quasi God civilization.

“We need to think about it carefully.

Ding Lingdang mumbled.

“Of course.”

With a bitter smile, Wei Qingqing said, “Indeed, I should think about it carefully. It’s exactly because I can’t figure out the truth, the risks, and the benefits of the whole thing that I came back and handed over the biggest problem to you.”

…

The top experts of mankind and the primeval experts thought for three days and three nights and still could not figure out one percent of the whole thing.

In the meantime, Ding Lingdang had been in touch with Wei Qingqing countless times, trying to find out whether or not she was telling the truth by asking her to open the letter first.

Wei Qingqing was very cooperative. She handed everything over to the ‘Joint Development Committee of the Heavenly Palace’, which was the decision-making agency of the federation, the imperium, the Holy Alliance, and the ‘primeval’.

However, the more he knew, the more confused and confused he became.

However, based on her intuition, Ding Lingdang had already believed that Wei Qingqing was herself and that she was speaking the truth. The personal friendship between the two quickly heated up. Ding Lingdang took the opportunity to throw some more personal questions to Wei Qingqing regarding… Li Yao.

It was Wei Qingqing’s third night back in the federation.

Of course, there was no such thing as day and night in the endless sea of stars. In the observation corridor at the head of Kunlun, the stars were glittering like a drizzle inside the crystal cabin that was as pure as nonexistent.

Chapter 3159: Reincarnation Prison

“I’m sorry, Sister Qingqing. As for your proposal, the committee will have to study and discuss it. I’m afraid it won’t be so easy to make a decision. It might take a year and a half before a decision is made.

Ding Lingdang said somewhat embarrassedly, “But you don’t need to be in such a hurry, because the research related to ‘super teleportation’ and ‘anti-space folding’ have been carried out as fast as possible.

The research on the super teleportation technology did not start today but three years ago, when Gu Wuxin almost turned into a rainbow. To this day, the best experts of mankind and the survivors of the primeval era had a profound understanding about the nature and form of the ‘Rainbow Bridge’. The ‘anti-space folding’ technology brought by Wei Qingqing could be verified and answered by each other, thereby accelerating mankind’s understanding of the mysteries of space.

“It doesn’t matter. I understand—even I believe that the whole thing is not as simple as it seems. Maybe there is a great scheme behind it.”

Wei Qingqing said, “Everything we know right now is just my side of the story. Even if I didn’t mean to deceive you, who knows whether or not I have been brainwashed by the so-called debris of the ‘Messenger starship’? In fact, there is no such thing as a ‘Messenger starship’ or an alliance of the resistors. Everything is a trap set up by the tides so that all the resistors will walk right into the trap without any trouble. Isn’t it possible?

“Even if the Courier starships and the coalition of the resistors do exist, it does not mean that they have revealed the whole truth to us. In any case, the main body of the coalition of the resistors is the remnants of the Primeval Civilization. Who knows what kind of values the ‘cosmic conquerors’ and the ‘tyrants of the sea of stars’ had, and whether or not they are in line with the tiny civilization of human beings in the Pangu Universe? Maybe, they blew the horn and summoned ‘cannon fodder’ instead of the coalition army. The possibility is very high, too.

“All in all, whether the information that the carrier brought is true or false, what is in front of us is far from a bright road. Our remote and insignificant civilization is destined to endure countless setbacks, tribulations, and tests in order to survive in the endless sea of stars, isn’t it?

Wei Qingqing looked at the stars in the sky, her voice distant and calm.

“…You’re right.”

Ding Lingdang was dazed for a while before she replied, “The bottom line is, we are still too weak. For the weak, any action of the strong can be a scheme or a trap. We have no choice at all.

“Yes. Just like when the Earth was destroyed, the two feeble and terrified fleets of refugees left the mother planet one after another and became the dominators of the sea of stars.

Wei Qingqing said, “The strong change from the weak. If the civilization of mankind, which originated from Earth in the past, can do it, why can’t we, the civilization of mankind from the Pangu Universe? It doesn’t matter whether it is a scheme or a trap. We knew that there were tigers in the mountains, but we still chose to go there. This is our only chance. These words should come from your mouth, not mine.”

Ding Lingdang was silent for a moment.

“Let’s change the topic. I… have some questions for Sister Qingqing.”

Ding Lingdang hesitated.

“Alright.”

Wei Qingqing smiled, “What do you want to know?”

“About Earth…”

Ding Lingdang said, “Of course, we all know that the real Earth was destroyed billions of years ago. But tell me, with the capability of the ‘quasi-god civilization’, is it possible to build a new Earth, including countless ignorant ‘earthlings’, and let them reenact the development of the old Earth civilization?”

“If the intelligence provided by the carrier is correct and the coalition of the resistors can build a ‘black hole fortress’ with the power of billions of black holes, what’s the big deal about building another Earth?

Wei Qingqing said, “Choose a habitable planet of a suitable size and size, simulate the environment of the primeval earth through the modification of the atmosphere and the crust, and then throw in a lot of ‘earthlings’ whose memories have been purged and tampered with, or who were born in the laboratories in the first place, and build one or even a hundred Earths to soothe their homesickness or to simulate the different changes in history. Of course, this is very possible.

“If they are not entities in the three-dimensional universe but the complicated data in the virtual world, it will be even simpler. I believe that, with the technological capability of a civilization at the level of a Quasi God, countless ‘virtual earths’ and billions of ‘virtual earths’ can be created within a moment, right?

“Then—”

Ding Lingdang was a bit nervous. “According to the information you received from the carrier, have you ever heard of such ‘replicating Earth’ or ‘virtual Earth’ in the middle of a flood?”

“I didn’t know that.”

Wei Qingqing said, “But I’m told that there is a very mysterious and terrifying prison in the depths of the flood where all the people who were unwilling to submit to the flood for billions of years are locked.

“What?”

Ding Lingdang was surprised. “What do you mean?”

“Although the flood was unstoppable and swallowed species and civilizations one after another, especially when it defeated the Primeval Civilization and absorbed the thousands of universes that belonged to it, not all the individuals were willing to submit to its tyranny.

Wei Qingqing explained, “The three armies can seize the commander-in-chief position, but ordinary people can’t seize the will. It’s easy to crush a civilization, but it’s difficult to make all the individuals of the civilization obediently submit. For the individuals of the defeated race and civilization, the stronger they are, the more tenacious their will is, the more independent their spirit is, and the more unwilling they are to meld with the souls of thousands of people.

“The flood tide has already captured these people. It is a piece of cake to kill them. However, such a simple and crude approach will not achieve the ultimate purpose of the flood tide. It will even give these people a quick release.

“Therefore, such a prison exists in the depths of the flood. It is known as ‘reincarnation prison’ by the coalition of the rebels. Countless unyielding heroic souls are locked up there, including the ‘Vulture Plan’ that I mentioned three days ago. It is very likely that it is the ‘prison break’ of the heroic souls.

“So it was like this.”

Ding Lingdang stared at Li Yao and thought for a long time. Eventually, she couldn’t help but ask, “Sister Qingqing, let’s say—let’s say—let’s say Li Yao is still alive. Is it possible that his soul is locked in the ‘Reincarnation Prison’?”

“I don’t know.”

Wei Qingqing said, “But such a possibility does exist. Death is not the end. It is just the beginning of the other side of the journey. According to your description, Li Yao’s soul is already strong enough. Even if most of his life information has been erased, it must still be of great research value. Chances are that he is locked in the ‘Reincarnation Prison’.”

“Therefore, we still have a chance, even if it is one in a billion, to rescue him and revive him?”

Ding Lingdang was extremely excited. But on second thought, she was greatly disappointed. “Forget it. Even if such a possibility does exist, the defense of the ‘reincarnation prison’ must be extremely tight. Li Yao’s soul must be the target of high-level surveillance. His every move and ours will be detected by the flood. Our recklessness will only be walking into a trap and getting ourselves killed.”

“Not necessarily.”

Wei Qingqing said, “This is what I told you earlier. To understand the existence of the flood tide with our language and thinking methods is completely different. In fact, whether it is the words ‘civilization’, ‘alliance’, or ‘reincarnation prison’, they were all randomly put together in order to explain it.

“You shouldn’t think of the flood as a civilization billions of times more advanced than that of mankind. Instead, you should think of it as a certain… gargantuan life form that includes the universe. It is a super life form with the ‘universe’ as its body and billions of shining stars as its thought fire. At the very least, it is evolving toward such an unbelievable ‘cosmic life form’.

“And we, the little carbon-based intelligent creatures, are equivalent to… bacteria and viruses.

“For example, you might be able to monitor the outbreak and destruction of a common cold. You might be able to come up with various methods to prevent and eliminate it. You might be able to intervene with the common cold. You might have various methods to protect yourself, but it would be impossible for you to monitor every specific cold virus, right?”

Ding Lingdang was slightly surprised and nodded subconsciously.

“By the same logic, when you find that there is some kind of bacterial infection or imbalance in your body that will affect your health, you can take medicine and IV fluids, you can use the most advanced treatment measures, and you can also activate the immune system inside your body to deal with it.

Wei Qingqing continued, “But you still can’t lock onto every specific bacterium and destroy them one by one… There’s absolutely no need for that, is there?”

“Understood.”

Ding Lingdang said, “I kind of understand what you are trying to say. You are saying that, assuming that the tide is a gargantuan giant, because the giant is too enormous, it is impossible for him to communicate with us ‘bacteria and viruses’ directly, and it is even difficult for him to monitor a specific ‘bacteria and virus’? After billions of years of evolution, he has actually turned into a completely different life form from us and cut off the ability to communicate with us?”

“Yes. Not to mention that the giant was still at the weakest moment of his life. You may imagine him to be a seriously wounded giant who was barely breathing.

Wei Qingqing said, “He is trying his best to treat his disease. Maybe his ‘treatment plan’ will eliminate billions of germs and viruses in the blink of an eye. However, monitoring a specific bacteria and virus? I don’t think it is necessary.”

“I understand now. When a giant swallows all the stars in the universe and the entire universe needs to process the endless information from billions of stars every second, it is indeed difficult to squeeze out the redundant computational ability to pay special attention to an empty soul.

Ding Lingdang mumbled, “However, even if we can break into the ‘Reincarnation Prison’, it will be hundreds of years later. Can Li Yao really wait that long?”

Chapter 3160: Prison Rebellion Frenzy

“I don’t know whether or not Li Yao can wait that long, but I do know that time and space are the same in the sea of the multiverse. They can be twisted, folded, and penetrated.

Wei Qingqing said, “As a carbon-based intelligent life, no matter how much we evolve, the lifespan of human beings has a limit. At most, it can be a few hundred years, or even a thousand years. However, I have heard that many of the heroic spirits locked in the ‘reincarnation prison’ have existed for tens of thousands of years, hundreds of thousands of years, or even millions of years. It is absolutely impossible if it does not involve a certain law of time compression, time distortion, or time freezing.

“On the other hand, different life forms have different feelings about the scale of time. It is impossible for a bacterium to have the same feelings as humans about the passage of time. Similarly, if Li Yao really enters the reincarnation prison and becomes something completely different, perhaps a thousand years of vicissitudes for you and me will be nothing but a short moment for him.

“There is another possibility. If Li Yao’s soul is fully ‘digitalized’ and ‘virtualized’, and the truth of the Reincarnation Prison turns out to be a virtual world independent of the three-dimensional universe, the flow of time must have other mysteries.

“All in all, I believe that, as long as Li Yao’s heroic spirit is not extinguished, and you can persist in fighting until the moment when you break into the reincarnation prison, you will have a chance to meet him again.”

“It’s like this…”

Ding Lingdang couldn’t help but nod her head as she recalled the tens of thousands of years of solidification caused by the “time curvature change” in the depths of the primordial ruins.

“However, I think that, even if Li Yao’s soul still exists, he must’ve erased most of his life information. It’s impossible for him to remember anything that happened in the 40,000 years of Cultivation in the Pangu Universe. He might not even know who he is.

Wei Qingqing said, “If you really want to find him, you have to find a way to send a message to the Reincarnation Prison first to awaken his memories, his will, and his self. I believe that as long as he can remember who he is, he will definitely turn the Reincarnation Prison upside down. You don’t need to worry at all.”

“Send a message to the reincarnation prison?”

Ding Lingdang thought for a moment. “Wouldn’t that be walking right into a trap? Everything Li Yao did was to destroy the information!”

“Times have changed. Of course, I’m not talking about sending a message to the reincarnation prison in the depths of the flood right now. Our current technology is far from enough to do that.

Wei Qingqing said, “Right now, the final battle between the Flood Tide and the Resistance Alliance has not broken out yet. We are still hiding in the darkness. Of course, we cannot leak any information to the outside world.

“However, if we look at the future hundreds or even thousands of years from now, if we find dozens or even hundreds of completely different civilizations near the Pangu Universe one day and reach out to the Alliance of Resistance, and the two quasi-god civilizations start a total war, there will be no need for us to hide ourselves anymore. On the contrary, if we can reach out to the Reincarnation Prison in the depths of the flood and wake up all the heroes there, a ‘Betrayal Frenzy’ that is hundreds of times more intense than the ‘Vulture Plan’ might be the key to victory.

“Look, just as I said earlier, our seemingly remote, desolate, and insignificant civilization is not necessarily unable to determine the future of the multiverse sea. A small dike can be destroyed by an ant’s nest. Who says we can’t become the ants that destroy the flood tide?

“Of course, I’m not in a hurry, but I might as well think about it carefully first. If I really need to send messages to the ‘reincarnation prison’ one day to wake up a heroic spirit such as Li Yao, what kind of technology should I use and how should I do it?”

Wei Qingqing’s words made Ding Lingdang lost in thought for a long, long time.

She remembered that Li Yao had told her about the Earth in her dream.

The Earth in his dream seemed to be inhabited by six billion ordinary people who did not even have the strength to truss a chicken. They were all ignorant creatures who did not know the wonders of spiritual energy, the vastness of the universe, and the true brilliance of life.

In other words, they were never really alive. Li Yao was just one of them.

However, what if the ‘earthlings’ discovered that they were not as ordinary as they thought but heroic spirits who had been sealed in countless failed wars against the tides over billions of years?

If he could wake up the six billion heroic spirits and remind them of their freedom, glory, and destiny, they would all be as strong as Li Yao.

Maybe, it was the key to destroying the flood tide.

“I understand.”

Ding Lingdang’s eyes, which had been confused for three days, turned firm again. She took a long breath and put on a confident smile again. She clenched her fists and said sincerely, “Thank you, Sister Qingqing. I think I know what to do next!”

“There’s no need to thank me. Think more, think more. Don’t believe anything anyone says—including what I just said.”

Wei Qingqing said, “Let me repeat myself. I don’t know whether or not the matter about the messenger starship and the war horn is true. I don’t know whether or not the Resistance Alliance is just a disguise made up by the flood tide to attract the resistors into a trap. I don’t know whether or not I have become a tool of evil and whether or not I will lead our civilization into endless darkness… I don’t know anything about any of this.

“All I know is that the storm that sweeps the sea of stars is here, and we have nowhere to run except to march forward, right?

“Yes.”

Ding Lingdang’s eyes pierced through the transparent cabin window and into the depths of the storm-like stars. She said resolutely, one word after another, “We have nowhere to run to, and we never thought of running away at all. Whether our enemy is the Flood Tide or the Resistance Alliance, whether it is a quasi-god civilization or a true god-level civilization, or an existence that is even higher than a god-level civilization and cannot be described in words, we will fight until the end. We will either die a hero’s death, step on the corpses of our enemies, and climb to the peak of evolution and the peak above it!

“Over the past billions of years, our ancestors have made a hundred percent miracle with only one percent of hope. In the billions of years to come, we will go on like this until the heaven and the earth destroy us or we slaughter through the entire world!

…

Three years had passed since Wei Qingqing returned to the federation.

Over the past three years, the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance, as well as the ‘primeval autonomous space zone’, had changed hundreds of times more violently and rapidly than in the previous three years.

Of all the changes, the most important one for the future of the Pangu Universe was undoubtedly the decision of the ‘Joint Development Committee of the Heavenly Palace’ to activate the ‘Messenger Plan’ and march out of the Pangu Universe.

It was not an impulsive move, but as Ding Lingdang and Wei Qingqing had said, there was no other choice.

Firstly, whether Wei Qingqing was speaking the truth or she herself had been hypnotized by the enemy, at least according to the analysis of the magical equipment experts, the debris of the ‘Messenger starship’ had strong traces of industrial production.

In other words, the messenger starship was not a one-of-a-kind, handmade thing. It was possible that thousands of starships had been built and distributed to the entire cosmos.

Under such circumstances, it would be hilarious if they continued being cowards. Even if the human civilization of the Pangu Universe did not explore the outside world, other carrier starships might find other survivors, or new carrier starships might jump into the Pangu Universe and eventually figure out the coordinates of the Pangu Universe. The pursuers of the Flood Tide might also find the Pangu Universe through the carrier starships.

The ostrich buried its head in the sand. Other than gaining a temporary, illusionary sense of security, it was of no use.

Secondly, if Wei Qingqing was really hypnotized by a hostile civilization, her purpose was to lure the prey into her trap so that the hunting process would be easier. Even if the prey was not fooled, the hunter would still come in person in the near future.

With the scale of the Pangu Universe, it was unlikely that it could develop into a civilization that could compete with a civilization at the level of a Quasi God within several thousand years. Then, what was the difference between being fooled and not being fooled?

Thirdly, it was true that the coalition of the rebels was not a ‘kind civilization’. Maybe they were planning to use the survivors as cannon fodder, but cannon fodder had to be trained to a certain extent, too. Some of them would be granted with high-precision weapons, and some of them would have the opportunity to survive the brutal battlefield. Building their own legends with blood and courage was better than waiting to be killed.

In short, it was necessary for a civilization to communicate with the outside world in order to develop. War was the most advanced form of communication. The human civilization of the Pangu Universe had to fight the war between two quasi-god civilizations.

Of course, neither the federation, the imperium, nor the Covenant Alliance, nor the wise leaders among the survivors of the primeval era, would be foolish enough to follow the Alliance of the Resistance’s rules.

Li Yao’s experience in the ‘ultimate test’ of the Heaven’s Origin Sector told everybody that the acquisition of any kind of strength had an equal price. A gift that fell from the sky and was too advanced was often a sign of the doom of a civilization.

Those who had learned their lesson did not activate the self-recovery and replication functions of the debris of the carrier. Instead, they analyzed the debris, retrieved the technology, and applied it to their starships and teleportation arrays.

The Pangu Civilization boasted advanced technology in space navigation and space jump—advanced enough to cross the black wall.

The technology of the Bifrost Bridge, the super teleportation array that Gu Wuxin had retrieved from the captives of the Flood Tide Legion, had been grasped by the human experts over the past three years, too.

Together with the ‘anti-space folding technology’ contained in the debris of the messenger starship that Wei Qingqing brought, although it was still beyond the understanding of the human civilization, it was not entirely impossible to improve the exploration starship of the Pangu Civilization with the technology of ‘knowing what it is but not knowing why it is so’.

Chapter 3161: Gone

Exploration starships that were refined with such complicated technology could be regarded as upgraded versions of the primeval warships. Their specifications were far from those of the real ‘Messenger starships’.

If the messenger starship was a giant warship that could sail through the ocean, then such an upgraded version of the ancient warship was a sampan that could only cruise offshore at best. It was impossible for the starship to carry them to the ‘blackhole barrier’ at the center of the multiverse. The starship could only explore other universes near the Pangu Universe that were also ‘backwater towns’.

Even so, the leaders of the civilization of mankind and the survivors of the primeval era still decided to make the first step in the exploration of the multiverse ocean with the technology that belonged to them. However shabby the technology was, at least 100% of it belonged to them, and it could be improved in the subsequent voyages and explorations.

After all, they did not intend to get in touch with the Resistance Alliance for now. Instead, they planned to explore the environment near the Pangu Universe first.

Most of the time, the rapid development could solve a lot of problems on its own. Maybe they were hesitating whether or not to be involved in the war between the flood tide and the coalition of the resistors. But after five hundred years, when they grasped more advanced technology and communicated with the dozens, if not hundreds, of universes near the Pangu Universe, there would certainly be smarter, more rational, and more farsighted ways to solve the problems. All the problems would be solved easily.

With such thoughts in mind, the volunteers, or ‘messengers’, who were the first batch to board the ‘upgraded prehistoric exploration starship’ and ready to cross the black wall to explore the space zones, soon selected their names and set off.

…

Grand Desolate War Institution, Heaven’s Origin.

Early in the morning, on the boulevard, a beautiful girl in sportswear was jogging barefoot on the cobblestone path.

Her signature red hair was tightly wrapped in a headscarf painted with a crimson dragon. In addition, the morning mist had yet to completely disperse, so the people exercising in the distance did not recognize her. She was the former Speaker of Parliament of the Federation. Other than ‘Vulture Li Yao’, she was the Grand Desolate War Institution’s most famous alumni, Ding Lingdang.

After a hundred years of development, countless people had gone through the arduous journey of fighting against the heavens and the earth. In the past, sand was flying, sand was flying, and dust was flying. The sky was dark, and the sun and moon were lackluster. The Grand Desolate War Institution had long turned into a fertile, prosperous, and fruitful paradise. The Grand Desolate War Institution had also changed from an institute that was known purely for its Combat Department and Refining Department to a comprehensive, advanced institute that was well-acknowledged in dozens of Sectors.

With the support of the ‘Ultra-Level Federal Heroes’ and the ‘former Speaker of the Federation’, as well as the continuous investments from the super sects such as the Glorious Sunlight Group, the Grand Desolate War Institution’s research results in various cutting-edge and cutting-edge fields were even more eye-catching than those of the established colleges in the ‘Nine Elite Universities of the Federation’, which were almost as good as the ‘Imperium Universities of the Imperium of True Human Beings’, ‘Imperium Colonization Universities’, ‘Imperium Advanced Technological Universities of the Imperium’, and so on. Not only were all the young people of the federation crazy about it, even many citizens of the imperium had come to the place from thousands of kilometers away to seek enlightenment humbly. Since last year, the Grand Desolate War Institution had even become the first academic holy land to receive the people of the Covenant Alliance, the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and other prehistoric species in the entire federation. It could be said to be a place where all the students were taught without discrimination.

Ding Lingdang’s mother was a teacher of the Grand Desolate War Institution. She had grown up in the Grand Desolate War Institution. Although the former Grand Desolate War Institution had been destroyed and the new campus rebuilt from scratch, she still had deep feelings for the place.

Walking on the boulevard, Ding Lingdang couldn’t help but fall into a trance when she saw the greenness, the singing birds and the fragrance of flowers, and the carefree college students sitting in gardens or on the grass with smiles on their faces.

The picture in front of his eyes seemed to overlap with his memories from more than a hundred years ago.

The young man and the young girl leaning against each other and smiling in a low voice seemed to be her and Li Yao in the old days.

At that time, the two of them, who were still a young lecturer and a college student in the Grand Desolate War Institution, were still gritting their teeth and thinking hard about the invasion of the demon race. The world in front of them was just a tiny Heaven’s Origin Sector with, at best, a tiny Blood Demon Planet. The so-called ‘Three Thousand Worlds, Star Ocean Imperium’ was just an imaginary legend that had nothing to do with them or the Star Glory Federation.

They did not see it coming. They did not see it coming at all. In only a hundred years, the world in front of them seemed to have expanded thousands of times, if not millions of times, like a big explosion.

The enemies of the past had turned into allies. The legends of the past had turned into a world-shaking stage for her. Her dreams of the past had become reality bit by bit. Soon, she would grow wings and create an even greater miracle—together with Li Yao.

Slowly, Ding Lingdang stopped.

Everything that had happened a hundred years ago flashed before her eyes. She remembered the first time she met Li Yao. He was just an ignorant, reckless high school student. She had never thought that her life, the fate of the federation, and even the future of the Pangu Universe would be changed by this high school student.

While she was thinking, Ding Lingdang couldn’t help but burst into laughter.

“Li Yao, Li Yao, you’ve created enough miracles and caused enough trouble. This time, please stay on ‘Earth’!”

Ding Lingdang narrowed her eyes and mumbled to herself, “No matter how far away you are, no matter how long it takes, I will find Earth and rescue you!”

The sun rose in the east, dispersing the mist. Quite a few students were already casting suspicious gazes at her from afar, seemingly unable to determine whether or not she was Ding Lingdang.

Ding Lingdang smiled and snapped her fingers.

A streamlined, small shuttle that looked like an arc dart without any gaps immediately appeared above her head, startling the college students.

“Let’s go to the ‘Traveler’ base!”

Ding Lingdang waved her fists at the rising sun.

…

The shore of the East Ocean in the Great Qian Dynasty.

The 3,000 sword cultivators were completely silent. Their longswords had been firmly suppressed within their scabbards. Not even the slightest sound of tigers and dragons could be heard. Everyone’s soul had been seized by a single sword, and that sword was nothing more than an ordinary three-foot-long blade.

It was ‘Sword Addict’, Yan Liren!

The Three Saints, Four Villains, and the leaders and elders of the major sects, who were known as the top ten experts of the Ancient Saints, were all solemn, too. They stood straight and stared at Yan Liren’s hands.

“Watch carefully. I will only use it once.”

Yan Liren was floating in midair, as if he had melded with the clouds and the waves.

Then, he unsheathed his sword.

There was no dazzling brilliance, no earsplitting thunder. The surging ocean seemed to have been frozen by his sword aura, so that everybody could count the number of the waves and the swirls clearly. It was not until one breath later that an immeasurable canyon was opened on the surface of the ocean. The seawater on the two sides of the canyon was as smooth as a mirror, and it was not closed again until it was frozen by an unbelievable force for three to five breaths.

It seemed that Yan Liren’s attack had the effect of stopping time.

“This is the first strike.

Yan Liren said to the dumbfounded crowd, “Next is the second attack.”

He slowly raised his short sword, pointed the tip at the three thousand sword cultivators, and lightly stabbed.

The 3,000 sword cultivators suddenly felt that this sword had pierced towards their eyes at the same time. Their hearts and the web between their thumb and index finger seemed to emit a “Clang” crisp sound from their sheaths. Even though they knew that no matter how terrifying Yan Liren’s sword technique was, it was impossible for him to break the flying swords of 3,000 people, none of them had the courage and ability to draw their swords to verify.

Some of the weaker sword Cultivators really grunted and clutched their chest hard.

“Lastly, the third strike.

Yan Liren waved his long sword to the sky. An unforgettable picture appeared. The sky that was as blue as the ocean was torn apart by his invisible sword aura, too, leaving a black gap that looked like a roaring black dragon that was going to swallow everything on the ground.

“The three strikes just now contain all the sword will that I perceived in the ancient relics. You can learn as much as you want.

Yan Liren sheathed his sword and looked at the mountains and rivers that were both familiar and strange. He sighed and said, “These are my last gifts to the Ancient Saint World!”

Hardly had his voice faded away when a round, small shuttle that looked like bent mercury appeared behind him. Holding a short sword in his arms, Yan Liren stepped on the shuttle and flew higher and higher, until he disappeared through the black gap. Countless ancient Cultivators were dumbfounded when they saw the three remaining sword wills on the surface of the world.

…

Inside the imperial city of Empyrean Terminus in the Imperium of True Human Beings…

“Mother, have you made up your mind?”

Li Jialing asked solemnly, “You must know that neither the space navigation technology of the Pangu civilization nor the anti-space folding technology brought by the ‘messenger starships’ have been fully grasped yet, including the super teleportation array technology left by Gu Wuxin. Many technical difficulties have yet to be overcome. The new generation of super long-range exploration starships based on the technology still have a lot of safety hazards and are extremely unstable. There is no need for you to be the first batch of ‘messengers’ to explore the unpredictable foreign space!

“Yes. I’ve made up my mind.”

Li Linghai looked at his son, who was maturing day by day and was truly as magnificent as a golden lion. He smiled and said, “Emperor, you’ve done very well over the past six years. Your growth has far exceeded my expectations. Right now, you are the real master of the imperium. You don’t need me to tell you what to do anymore.

“Truth be told, you and I, mother and son, are not women who are used to doing nothing and enjoying the rest of their lives. I also have a bunch of loyal and loyal subordinates who have been following me through life and death with me since decades ago. Even if I am willing to relinquish all my power, as long as I stay in the imperium, these people will never be 100% at your disposal.

“Therefore, I’m afraid that this is the best choice for the Immortal Cultivators, isn’t it?”

“There’s no need to worry about me. Don’t forget that I was the first to discover the ancient relics in the past. Maybe I will be as lucky as you during the exploration and make more marvelous discoveries.

Before Li Jialing continued his persuasion, Li Linghai had already leapt backwards into a silver brightness with a relieved smile.

Chapter 3162: Transmigrator Base

Other than Ding Lingdang, Yan Liren, and Li Linghai, Boss Bai, Long Yangjun, Little Ming, and Wei Qingqing, as well as the first batch of messengers, had settled all their affairs in the Pangu Universe and gathered at the ‘transmigrating base’.

Wei Qingqing told everyone that in the multiverse sea, every natural paradise with abundant spiritual energy often had a thin barrier between them and the outside world because of the drastic changes in the concentration of spiritual energy. It was not necessarily a high wall or a stone belt that was visible to the naked eye, but it was certainly different. It was called ‘crystal wall’.

Fearing that everybody would not be able to understand the concept of ‘crystal wall’, she gave two more examples. The first one was that the seawater would be divided into distinct lines because of the different concentration of salt. The color, nutrition, and even the ecosystem inside the lines would be entirely different.

Secondly, the magma that had an extremely high temperature would quickly condense into a shell when it came into contact with the ice that had an extremely low temperature. The shell was essentially a ‘crystal wall’.

The natural paradises that were surrounded by crystal walls were known as sub-universes. For example, the ‘spiritless worlds’ that were not surrounded by crystal walls and did not boast any spiritual energy, such as ‘Earth’, the birthplace of human civilization, were a miracle among miracles. They were not part of the exploration this time.

Since they were going through the ‘crystal walls’ to arrive at a completely different, unpredictable, and mysterious world, they were naturally ‘travelers’, and their base was the ‘traveler base’.

Since the coordinates of the primeval relics were unpredictable, they did not choose to build a base inside the Heavenly Palace. Instead, they listened to the suggestions of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan and chose an abandoned world that seemed to be barren. A million years ago, the abandoned world was the forward base of the first expedition of the Pangu civilization.

There must’ve been a good reason why he chose this place as his starting point to cross the black wall. With the guidance of the survivors of the primeval era and the cooperation of the three major forces of the human civilization, a magnificent new base that looked like the future was established very quickly. Thankfully, Li Yao had chosen communication and cooperation instead of hostility when he met the ‘Full Moon’ girl of the Nuwa Clan. Otherwise, it would’ve been impossible for the human civilization to find the forward base of the Pangu Expeditionary Force in the past.

Looking at the bases and starships that were both primeval and futuristic, Ding Lingdang felt that she was back to the time a hundred years ago when she and Li Yao were preparing to explore the worlds around the Star Glory Federation.

In fact, the ‘Messenger Plan’ right now was exactly the same as the ‘Heavenly Path Plan’ a hundred years ago. They were both meant to explore the wider living space for their civilization and look for more like-minded friends, or at least the kind of people that they could communicate with.

When a civilization was born in communication, it was bound to grow stronger in communication, too. A civilization that lived in seclusion for a long time might be able to take a moment of rest, but it would also put an invisible noose around its neck and cut off the possibility of growth.

Compared to the ‘Heavenly Path Plan’ a hundred years ago, the ‘Messenger Plan’ right now was a completely different one.

When the Heaven’s Path Plan was implemented, the federation only had the Heaven’s Origin Sector, the Blood Demon Sector, and the Flying Star Sector. The fire of war between the three worlds had only just died down. Just like the bloody dawn when the darkness had just dispersed, the starship and the hibernation system that they used to explore the sea of stars were also crude and highly dangerous. Countless explorers had fallen before they saw the dawn of a new world.

But today, the entire base was packed with cutting-edge technologies that even Ding Lingdang had never seen before, and Wei Qingqing had retrieved a lot of precious star maps from the debris of the ‘Messenger starships’. Countless experts and scholars of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan had been busy providing the greatest technical support for the exploration operation.

In fact, the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan had been the best candidates for the exploration. After all, their ancestors had carried out a similar operation a million years ago. Although the exploration was not very far away from the black wall, the scale of the operation was tens of thousands of times larger than that of Operation Messenger.

However, the simplest and most important reason was still that they had to retreat to the back lines and give up on the first wave—they were too large and too heavy.

It was going to be a long journey in the sea of stars. The energy and resources required to increase the size and mass of the starship were astronomical. It must be noted that the latest exploration starship might have to perform tens of thousands of space jumps. The difficulty and danger of the mission would be ten times higher if the mass of the space travelers was increased by one gram.

Therefore, in order to explore a few ‘subuniverse’ where intelligent creatures might exist, human beings that were smaller in size and lighter in mass were more suitable to take the lead.

Passing through the silver, almost transparent crystal pathway, Ding Lingdang met the other ‘travelers’ in the last preparation cabin.

She, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, Yan Liren, Li Linghai, Xiao Ming, Wei Qingqing… Maybe they were her old friends, maybe they were her old enemies, maybe they were in different life forms, and maybe they had different ideas and expectations about the future. But right now, all they could do was smile at each other and sense each other’s beliefs and determination.

“It seems that everybody has made clear where they want to explore!”

Ding Lingdang looked around and smiled at Boss Bai. “Boss Bai, where are you going to start?”

“Here—”

Boss Bai took out a star map from the crystal processor on his wrist without any hesitation. “The battlefield where the expedition army of the Pangu Clan fought the Flood Tide Legion was an earthshaking, epic war. Even after a million years, I believe that countless starship remnants are still floating among the stars. Maybe, there are still some interesting things that can survive and develop with the remnants of the battlefield? I want to check it out first before I make any plans. What about you?”

It is indeed the style of the Pirate Potentate.

Ding Lingdang brought up her own star map, too. “This is my first target. Sister Qingqing said that, according to the astronomical parameters and the ancient intelligence collected by the Resistance Alliance, it is very likely that a furious undercurrent of spiritual energy exists in the sea of stars. If a habitable planet happens to be wherever the undercurrent goes, and if it is selected by the survivors of the Primeval Clan, it will most likely evolve into a universe where the strong reign supreme and the martial arts reign supreme. I want to go there and experience the legendary ‘battle aura’.

“Same as me.”

Xiao Ming rubbed his hands and grinned. “My target is this space zone. Aunt Qingqing said that there are a lot of weird heavy elements in the sea of stars. If the survivors of the Primeval Clan choose this place as a shelter, it is very likely that a deformed ‘mechanical universe’ will be developed in millions of years. It sounds very suitable for me.

While they were talking and laughing, they introduced their first target.

Of course, the intelligence that Wei Qingqing brought was vague. Nobody knew whether or not the so-called ‘Battle Aura Universe’ and ‘Mechanical Universe’ really existed.

But they firmly believed that they were not alone. Their own kind and friends must still exist in the vast universe. Even if they were not ‘battle aura’ or ‘mechanical’, there must be something different about them.

“Hey, wait.”

After everyone finished their introductions, Wei Qingqing suddenly frowned and said, “Why do I feel that we are missing a person?”

“I’m here. Ladies and gentlemen, fellow Cultivators, my friends who have been waiting in front of the Spiritual Nexus and the light beam for a long time, it’s finally my turn. The new legend of the universe, the great sorcerer Han Te, has shown up! Hahahaha!”

After a burst of earsplitting laughter, a crimson cloak that was carved with countless weird runes and out of place in the world of Cultivators flew into the room. The cloak was fluttering even though there was no wind, as if it belonged to a guy who was enjoying himself. Then, the guy suddenly appeared in front of the cloak. “Are you shocked? Are you shivering? Are you dumbfounded? Is this… the power of magic?”

Veins were bulging on the foreheads of Ding Lingdang, Boss Bai, Li Linghai, Long Yangjun, and Yan Liren.

They looked over Han Te’s shoulder at the boxing champion who was walking close slowly.

“Punching King, have you really decided to let the boy explore the ‘magical universe’ in your place?”

Ding Lingdang hesitated. “You must know that, according to the information awakened in your core database, the ‘magical universe’ is the one that is most likely to exist and the one that is most likely to be found. It was your home in your previous life. Do you really not want to go back and take a look?”

“The past is history. Right now, the Pangu Universe is my only home.

The Fist King was still controlling the broken civil puppet that was so rusted that it was hard to tell whether it was made of metal or wood. More wildflowers and gold foils were growing out of the joints. However, the air around him made Xiao Ming, who was carrying the latest combat puppet, unable to look at him in the eyes. “After six years of hard training, Han Te has grasped the mysteries of magic, and he has passed all of your tests to prove that he is strong enough to explore the space zones, hasn’t he?”

“Indeed, but…”

Ding Lingdang frowned. “Are you sure that injecting unparalleled magical mysteries into his brain in only several years will not cause any… permanent damage to his brain?”

“Rest assured. Han Te is the most talented man in the Pangu Universe. He is born to be a sorcerer and not a Cultivator. The magical universe and the magical towers are the best choices for him. I believe that he will find the magical universe and be like a fish in water there.

“Besides,” the Fist King said, “we can’t send all the top experts out. Somebody has to stay and protect the Pangu Universe. Recently, I’ve had a vague feeling that danger beyond our calculation is lurking somewhere in the Pangu Universe.

Chapter 3163: Balance Of Evil

“No danger calculated?”

Ding Lingdang and the other experts all put on solemn expressions. With the talents of both the civilization of magic and the life of information, the boxing champion was among the top five experts of the Pangu Universe or even the top three. His computational ability and deduction ability were unparalleled, too. Nobody dared to underestimate his senses. “What do you mean?”

“Evil. Unspeakable evil.

The Fist King mumbled, “Since I finished my training in the Tower of Heaven three years ago and tried to connect the other half of the mainframe crystal processor of the Heavenly Palace to my core database after hundreds of upgrades, my computational ability has improved by at least ten times. Even so, I can’t describe the full picture of evil clearly. I only have a vague feeling that the darkness of the past hasn’t entirely dispersed yet. One day, it will change the future of the Pangu Universe and the entire cosmos.

“It was like the other half of the ‘Fuxi’ did not calculate the rise of the civilization of mankind and the mutation of Gu Wuxin precisely, but it still responded vaguely. A voice in my core database told me that I should stay and find a balance with the evilness so that I could protect the Pangu Universe. That way, the ‘travelers’ such as yourself could complete your mission better.

Ding Lingdang and Li Linghai looked at each other.

The Pangu Universe was indeed faced with many problems.

The flood tide and the coalition of the resistors were just far-fetched concerns. The attack, defense, and balance of the three forces of the federation, the imperium, the renaissance of the civilization of the pioneers, the rise of the life of information, and so on. If the ‘next-door worries’ were not handled properly, they might indeed lead to ‘unspeakable evil’.

With the Fist King in charge of the Pangu Universe, and with the continuous growth of the new generation such as Li Jialing, Jin Xinyue, and Wen Wen, they were able to focus on the future without worries.

“Special Agent Han—”

The boxing champion patted the arrogant young man’s shoulder neither lightly nor heavily and said solemnly, “Are you ready?”

“Completely, absolutely, 120% ready!”

Han Te bellowed, revealing his arms that had been carved with dense magical runes under the red cloak. “Six years of hard training and inhuman torture. Not just the girls, I’ve only seen my favorite glass three times! Ahhh! After such crazy training, I finally blew up the mysterious gate of the world of magic! Just look at my strong arms. There is no power that can stop me from becoming a ‘Magical Cannon King’—no, a ‘Magical Cannon King’ that rules the sea of stars, commands the universe, and is loved by everyone!”

/ .

“Very good. In that case, I will entrust the sacred mission of exploring and saving the magical universe to you!”

Deep and solemn brilliance beamed out of the crystal camera of the Fist King. He pointed at his head and said in a trustworthy voice, “Believe me. I’ve inferred your future with almost unlimited computational ability. I seem to see a new generation of dominators of the universe rising like the rising sun. Maybe your future will be more promising than anybody could ever imagine. Not only will you rescue the magical universe from decay, you will also be the one who defeats the tides and saves the multiverse ocean, including Earth. You will be the savior!”

“I—I—I think so, too! Really. I don’t know when it started, but I felt that a mysterious force was surging upwards from my abdomen. My heart was beating fast and my blood was boiling! Is this the legendary ‘fortune’?

Han Te was grinning from ear to ear like a drunk baboon. He patted his chest and said, “Rest assured, everybody. Although ‘Vulture Li Yao’, the great hero that everybody admires, is no more, the invincible legend of the new generation has risen. If the story that took place in the 40,000 years of Cultivation is a magnificent, brilliant epic, the ‘prequel’ of the epic has just come to an end. The glamorous legend about me, Han Te, is about to be unveiled. I will make it!”

“…” Ding Lingdang.

“…” Li Linghai.

“…” Boss Bai.

The boxing champion coughed in the back.

“Well, what I meant was—”

Han Te scratched his hair and hurried to explain, “I also believe that my most beloved ‘Old Yao’ is not dead but locked up in some ghost prison or planet. So, you don’t have to worry, because one day, I will blow up the planet with my ‘Magical Cannon’ and rescue Old Yao!”

Ding Lingdang laughed.

Looking around, his smile was purer and his eyes more determined.

“Yes. Although I am Li Yao’s wife, I agree with you on this point.

Ding Lingdang said firmly, one word after another, “A war cannot be fought by a single person. A home cannot be guarded by a single person. The universe cannot be saved by a single person. Li Yao has already completed his mission. No matter how brilliant the fireworks are, there will be a moment when they dim. However, I believe that the sparks he once shot out in the dark universe will eventually attract more latecomers. They will blossom with flames that are millions of times more brilliant than his. In the end, they will ignite the entire sea of stars and illuminate the entire universe.

“It’s time. Let’s go. Fellow Cultivators, let’s illuminate our universe!”

…

Later that day.

In a maintenance room deep inside the transmigrator base.

The boxing champion, who had just finished eight launches and was exhausted, walked into his room like a piece of rotten wood.

But he soon stopped in his tracks.

A ponytailed girl in cargo pants, with pockets all over her body and with all kinds of tools in them and oil stains on her face, was standing in his room and staring at him.

“Miss Liuli, why are you here?”

The crystal camera of the boxing champion was blinking in panic.

“Lord Fist King, why are you still here?”

After six years of immersion, the girl was no longer young and aggressive. “I know that you are eager to explore the mysteries of your origins in the magical universe. Why did you give up the once-in-a-lifetime opportunity and stay here?”

“I—I sensed evil.”

The boxing champion stammered. The iron shell that could resist the bombardment of the main gun of a starship seemed to be unable to resist the burning eyes of the girl. “I have to stay and protect the Pangu Universe. I—”

“Is that all?”

The girl took a step toward him, her eyes shining with unparalleled courage and determination. “Just to protect the Pangu Universe?”

“Yes, yes!”

The boxing champion tapped his head. A few mutated gold foil crawled out of the gaps of his skull. “I suddenly remember that there are some unfinished work in the control room. I—I’m leaving.”

“Don’t go, Fist King!”

The girl shouted at the boxing champion’s somewhat ragged back, “Answer my question. Did you stay just to protect the Pangu Universe? Or do you have something else to protect? Are—are you still willing to protect me like before?”

The gears and bearings all over the boxing champion’s body were squeaking.

The crystal processor was humming like a broken fan.

The simple and feeble puppet seemed to be unable to withstand the overflowing heat from his core database and was about to melt.

“Someone will protect you in my place. A certain man. A handsome, confident man.”

The boxing champion said somewhat clumsily and sadly, “I… am just a machine.”

“I know. But so what? You’ve been like this since we first met. You’ve never changed, and neither have I. I’m already used to the boxing champion!”

The girl gritted her teeth, her fists shivering because of nervousness. “Turn around and look at me, Master Fist King. Tell me the answer. If I say, if I say that I don’t care, that I don’t care about handsome and confident men, and that I don’t like the protection of ‘great sorcerers’, will you still protect me like before?”

The boxing champion did not turn around.

But he stood where he was for a long time, as if an invisible lightning had cut off his logic.

“I… am willing.”

He said in a voice that the girl could not hear.

But the blossoming flowers on his head betrayed him.

“Until forever? Oh, no. Until the day you or the universe is destroyed?”

“No, Miss Liu Li. As long as you exist in the universe, I will never allow myself or the universe to be destroyed!”

…

At the same time, in the Big Red Spot, an ancient giant in the Gemini Nebula…

In the eternal storm, the ocean of lightning was surging. Billions of lightning were interweaving, as if billions of enormous cells were swallowing and changing each other and roaring.

In the middle of the thunderstorm, voices that did not make any sense and could not have come from such a harsh environment echoed.

Whistles.

Yes. It was indeed a high-pitched, weird whistle.

If a Cultivator from the Star Glory Federation came close and heard the whistle-like sounds in person, he would definitely be shocked and shudder.

It was because the echoing whistles had twisted into the melody of the national anthem of the federation.

The source of the whistle was a giant swirl of lightning and plasma.

In the depths of the swirl, lightning wriggled like vipers and formed an extremely ugly face that looked like a human being lying in a sea of lightning with his arms as a pillow.

After the national anthem of the federation was played, the face, or rather, the bean sprout-like ‘person’, suddenly thought of something and smiled silently.

No. No. Calm down. Now is not the time to go out.

Neither human beings nor the survivors of the primeval era had stopped monitoring the ancient giants. They were still very wary of the lightning creatures. However weird and powerful the lightning creatures were, they would not survive long if they left the ancient giants, not to mention conquering the entire Pangu Universe or… the multiverse.

However, the wariness would not last long. After all, they were faced with serious internal and external problems. He was not their number one enemy.

The first batch of ‘travelers’ had already set off smoothly. Who would have thought that he would be able to hack into the Spiritual Nexus through the magical equipment that human beings used to monitor the ancient giants and steal the top-secret information of the transmigrator bases?

For the next five hundred years, their focus should be on the ‘Messenger Plan’. However, even if more ‘subunits’ were discovered, would they be so easy to communicate and integrate with?

As for the Resistance Alliance, were they really the so-called ‘freedom-loving, indomitable fighters who defend their dignity’? He did not believe it at all!

All in all, the Pangu Universe would be torn apart again and sink into unimaginable schemes and darkness. As long as he waited patiently, he and his ‘new friends’ would have many, many opportunities.

Messenger, time traveler, Resistance Alliance, Flood Tide, Reincarnation Prison, Earth…

Such a splendid and interesting universe was too much to bear. He could not help but wonder what the legendary Earth looked like.

“Then, as I said before I left—”

The lightning-like man in the sea of lightning chuckled. “Let’s meet again on Earth, Uncle Li!”

[40,000 Years of Cultivation, Mausoleum of Gods, complete.]

Chapter 3164: New Strange Dream

The tyrannosaur was only one step away from him.

He could clearly see the interconnecting wrinkles on the tyrannosaur’s body. The wrinkles divided the enormous body into the shape of a chessboard. It was not ugly. Instead, it was glittering and translucent like a body of colorful jade.

Illuminated by the sunlight, the jade armor of the tyrannosaur blossomed with a rainbow-like brilliance and rose up like a chimney, before it turned into a surging redness that seemed to be burning furiously and stimulated a certain strength deep inside his heart that had been sleeping for a long time.

Like a nightmare, he could neither move nor shout. He could only stare at the tyrannosaur.

Such a scene seemed to have been repeated countless times.

Every time, he had a vague feeling that the tyrannosaur wanted to say something to him, but the roars were all lost in the wind and sand.

There were even many times when a bottomless canyon appeared between him and the tyrannosaur. They could only look at each other across the river.

But this time, he was so close to the tyrannosaur that he could see the lipstick smeared on its bloody mouth.

A T-rex wearing lipstick?

He was dumbfounded and found it hard to believe. For a long time, he could not understand the science behind the weird picture.

When he gazed at the tyrannosaur, the tyrannosaur gazed back.

The scorching flames spurting out of his nose, which looked like a train tunnel, ignited the fire in the bottom of his heart. He felt restless and eager, as if his other self was about to break out of his chest and jump on the back of the tyrannosaur.

Weirdly enough, the eyes of a tyrannosaur should be dirty and brutal, just like those in all the monster movies.

But the eyes of the tyrannosaur were full of humanity and indescribable emotions and information. His heart was beating faster and faster, and he could not help but move forward…

Then, he was slapped away by the tyrannosaur.

“Sleeping? Wake up and fight!”

The tyrannosaur let out a deafening roar.

…

“Ah!”

Li Yao screamed and jumped up from the bunk bed. His head almost hit the ceiling, and he almost fell to the ground.

Holding the bed, he breathed heavily for a long time, with cold sweat popping out of his pores.

His other brothers in the dormitory were already used to his weird behavior. The eldest brother went to the library, but the second brother was still curled up in bed reading a novel with headphones on as usual. The third brother, on the other hand, was scared awake by him. He yawned and said, “What are you doing? Why are you crying and shouting so early in the morning? Are you having nightmares again?”

“Yes.”

Li Yao looked at his hands. His fingertips were still shivering. There was no telling whether it was because of fear or excitement.

“Which nightmare is it?”

The third brother, Yu Xin, rubbed his eyes and said, “Is it the one where you were ravaged by a female T-rex time and time again, the one where you were exhausted, the one where you wanted to die, the one where you met a ‘good brother in disguise’ and you saw through him but didn’t say anything? The one where you got intimate with her, and one night when you were in a trance, you discovered that the ‘good brother in disguise’ had a monster boa hidden deep below his crotch, or the one where you dreamed that you would give birth to two computers when you were pregnant?”

Li Yao sniffed and ignored Yu Xin’s teasing.

It was not until his hands were no longer shaking that he carefully climbed out of bed and went to the bathroom to wash his face with cold water.

Raising his head, what appeared in the mirror was a plain, ordinary face.

In this ordinary second-rate university in this ordinary second-rate city, there were as many as one could imagine.

Li Yao was in a trance. He felt that his soul had left his body and was wandering in the outside world.

“Is this really me?”

For some reason, a voice popped up from the bottom of his heart. “Who am I? Where am I?”

He was dazed for a long time, as if he would fall asleep again if he were to stand. He fell into a new, unpredictable dream.

The sharp whistle outside the window woke him up.

Looking into the distance through the window, one could see that the entire Jiangnan University Town was drowned in the slanting wind and drizzling rain. Dark clouds covered the light of dawn. The heavens and the earth rippled with a dark mist. Rows upon rows of tall buildings were lit up one after another like lanterns dancing in the wind. Quite a few vigorous and vigorous university students were unafraid of the wind and rain. They had already started their morning exercise. There were also umbrellas blossoming like flowers that surged towards the cafeteria, bringing a sliver of liveliness to the university town that had just awakened.

Smoke was rising in the sky of the cafeteria. The smoke and fire of the human world covered the earth and blew away the nightmare that he had just had.

“You are truly the ‘God of Sleeping’ of 610.”

With a toothbrush in his mouth, Yu Xin walked to the toilet beside Li Yao and peed. “I’ve never seen someone who likes to sleep so much as you. You sleep on the bed for at least twelve hours a day, don’t you? And when you’re in the classroom, you have to continue sleeping for three to five classes, right? Even when you’re not sleeping, you look muddle-headed and absent-minded. Last time, when the department organized a football match, you could even fall asleep on the field. Brother, what kind of technique are you practicing?”

“Training? Techniques?”

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently.

But it was zoomed in the next second.

He had no idea what Yu Xin was talking about.

“And those weird, eccentric dreams.

Yu Xin grinned. “Although everybody dreams in their sleep, I’ve never seen anyone else’s dream as wonderful and exciting as yours. The most important thing is that most people forget the dream completely within three to five seconds when they wake up, but you remember every detail of the dream clearly. Impressive. If I can learn your trick, won’t I be able to remember all the erotic dreams and enjoy them again and again?”

Li Yao did not know what to say.

Ever since the accident five years ago, when he woke up from the three-month coma, he had been having weird dreams. They were not really “nightmares”, but more “weird” than “shock”.

The uncanny dreams were like glittering paint that had penetrated deep into his cerebral cortex and wouldn’t disperse even after three to five days. Sometimes, they would even appear in broad daylight and torment him, making him unable to distinguish between reality and falsehood, reality and dream, life and death.

It was not a technique but more like a curse.

The most direct side effect was that Li Yao was originally a cheerful, warm, and straightforward young man. But every time he had a strange dream, he felt that he had become… sleazy, shameless, and dark.

But Li Yao also knew that among the four brothers in the dormitory, Yu Xin was the one who played the best with him. There was no malice in his jibber-jabber. Those who had not experienced these strange dreams in person would absolutely not know how big of an influence these strange dreams had on people. It could even… change one’s fate.

“Are you really alright?”

Seeing that there was still something wrong with Li Yao’s face, Yu Xin couldn’t help but extend his hand. “Do you have a fever?”

“I’m fine.”

Li Yao took a step back. “Don’t touch me with your hands that you just peed on!”

“That’s good.”

Yu Xin wiped his hands on his pants. He thought for a moment and said, “Brother, take it easy. In fact, it’s not a big deal to have weird dreams, but it doesn’t matter if you wake up. It won’t be good if you fall into them.

“Second Brother likes to read webnovels. I like to read comic books. These are also dreams. Riding the wind, riding the sword, singing a song of chivalry, dominating the world, powerful magic, evil robots, terrifying cosmic tyrants… Modern life is so stressful. Everyone needs a channel to vent. Who hasn’t had similar dreams? However, dreams are just dreams after all. They are not reality and can’t be used as food. You and I are not some rich second-generation heirs. We are about to graduate, so we still have to find a way to eat first before we dream. Dreams are all false. They are lies or self-deception. Dreams can’t affect life. We have to be down-to-earth, right?”

“…Yes.”

Li Yao nodded his head. He returned to his seat, opened a drawer, and took out a small notebook with a password lock. He opened the notebook. Inside the notebook were densely packed words the size of a fly’s head, recording the details of all the strange dreams over the past few years. The notebook had been rubbed so often that the edges were white and yellow.

Li Yao thought for a moment, flipped to the latest page of the notebook, and wrote:

“Early morning, May 19, 2018.

“I dreamed of T-rex again, but she put on lipstick. What does that mean?”

He turned to the first two pages and drew a vivid picture of a tyrannosaur with a ballpoint pen. He thought for a moment and added a few strokes to the bloody mouth of the tyrannosaur, which was sort of a ‘lipstick’.

Then, he looked at the tyrannosaur that had been smeared with lipstick and fell into deep thought again.

“It’s over. It’s over. The child is possessed. He’s completely hopeless!”

Yu Xin lamented behind him.

Li Yao closed the notebook and locked it. Just as he was about to turn around and refute, he was shocked by Yu Xin’s appearance. “What are you doing? Why are you wearing a colorful balaclava? And what’s with the foam and plastic?”

“What ‘Colorful Headgear’? This is a ‘Colorful Aura Cool Suit’. Do you know what cosplay is?”

Yu Xin was wearing a layer of armor made of foam and plastic. He gestured a few body-building movements and said, “Isn’t today the ‘Heavenly Horse Lake International Anime Festival’? I spent half a month’s breakfast money to buy this set. Let’s see, let’s see how this bro looks. How professional, how domineering!”

Li Yao was lost for words. “Didn’t you just teach me not to indulge in messy dreams?”

“How is that the same? Your big brother’s head is very clear. I have a very restrained and bottom line. I absolutely won’t let my imagination get mixed up with my real life. After today, of course, I will go all out in the job fair. I will find a job, make a lot of money, get married, have children, buy a house, and so on. But today, hehehehe. I’ve heard that there will be a lot of beautiful girls and professional cosplay teams from abroad at the Comic Festival. They are all golden-haired, blue-eyed foreigners. I must go and appreciate them.”

Yu Xin rubbed his hands and suddenly thought of something. “Hey, why don’t we go together? It’s like fighting fire with fire. It’s better to think about girls and foreigners than to dream of tyrannosaurs all day long. Chances are that you won’t have those weird dreams when you discover the wonderful world of 2D and cosplay.”

“Is that so?”

Today was the weekend, and Li Yao was not a fan of studying. It was not a big deal.

“Go now!”

On the other hand, Yu Xin’s interest was piqued. “Just based on the fact that you often dream of a giant python sticking out of the crotch of your good brother who is dressed as a woman, I’m certain that you have the potential to develop into the 2D world… Maybe even you didn’t realize that you are actually a big shot in cross-dressing who has been hiding himself very deeply?”

“Get lost!”

Li Yao laughed and cursed, but he still stood up honestly. He washed up and went with Yu Xin.

Passing by the bed of Zhao Kai, the second son of the dormitory, Li Yao patted the bed railing and said, “We’re going to the Comic-Con Festival to see the girls. Are you coming?”

Zhao Kai’s muffled laughter came from under the blanket as he said, “No, you guys go ahead. Just take a few more pictures of the girls and bring them back for our brothers to admire!”

“Let’s go. I don’t know what Zhao Kai has been reading these days, but he’s as fascinated as you are.”

Yu Xin dragged Li Yao out of the dormitory. “There are too many wolves and too little meat. Little girls don’t wait for others. GO GO GO!”

Chapter 3165: Something Big Happened!

“It’s huge!”

Li Yao exclaimed at the ‘Heavenly Horse Lake International Convention and Exhibition Center’.

The convention and exhibition center was located in a newly developed area of Jiang Nan City. It was huge and magnificent. Several silver, bizarre-shaped buildings made people feel as if they were travelling to the future.

Even though the main meeting hall was as large as several football fields, today’s convention and exhibition center was still packed with people from all over the country as well as other related manufacturers and workers.

As far as one’s eyes could reach, there were fans of cartoons and fantasy culture who were dressed in bizarre costumes and pretended to be serious. Like Yu Xin, they were dressed in colorful and bizarre costumes. Some of them were dressed as powerful great magicians; some were dressed as ghastly vampires; some were dressed as glamorous and charming female demons; some were dressed as steel-like robots that were armed to the teeth; and of course, there were also some sword immortals who had local characteristics and were dressed in elegant white robes.

Some of them were swaggering as if there was no one else in the room. They were walking here and there, displaying their delicate homemade clothes. Some of them were even coming up with short plays with plots, attracting the attention of a large number of spectators. They were like-minded and harmonious. Li Yao even saw a young black man who was wearing a homemade “armor”. His hands and back were shining and smoking!

As an ordinary youth of this era, Li Yao had, of course, come in contact with fantasy entertainment products such as cartoons, web novels, and sci-fi movies. He didn’t have any resistance to this. It was just that in the past, when he was plagued by strange dreams, he spent most of his time sleeping or daydreaming. He wasn’t as engrossed as Yu Xin and Zhao Kai. Today, when he arrived at the animation festival, his mood had been infected by so many fans, and he was also interested. He carried a plastic inflatable prop known as the “Sword of Vast Heaven”. He looked here and there. It was quite refreshing.

“Wow, so big!”
