Yu Xin was next to him, too. He was drooling while he looked around lewdly. “Look over there! Look over there! That hammer is so white! No. What I meant was that the sabers are so round! No. Well, you will know when you see them. I really want to suffocate in them!”

Li Yao was somewhat speechless. He said, “Speaking of which, why do we have to stand in this place? This seems to be the entrance of a toilet?”

“You don’t understand. We’re here to see a young lady, but everyone is dressed so gorgeously. Do you know who the young lady is and who the cross-dressing big shot is if you don’t take off your pants? What if you drool for a certain succubus or female demon for a long time, and when you’re in a hurry to pee, you find out that the other party went to the men’s restroom with you? The guy will take it out and it will be even bigger than you. Don’t you think it’s awkward?”

Yu Xin said, “So, of course, I’m going to stand at the entrance of the toilet and figure out which girls are the real girls, so that it will be easier for me to take action later… Don’t look at me like that. I meant to take a picture with them, have a heart-to-heart competition, leave a phone number or a WeChat message. That should be fine, right?”

“Yes.”

Li Yao watched absent-mindedly. Suddenly, his gaze froze and he poked Yu Xin. “There seems to be a musical in the booth over there. Do you know what it is?”

“Where? Where?”

/ .

Yu Xin squinted his eyes and read for a while before saying, “Oh, it’s ‘Legend of the Super Cultivator’. It’s quite a popular online novel. Recently, it seems to have been adapted into a comic and a game. The game manufacturer wants to use the animation festival platform to advertise first. Not only have they arranged musicals and imitation shows, but the author of the novel will also be present to sign the book. He’s the legendary web novelist. If he can get an autograph, Zhao Kai will definitely like it.”

“‘Legend of Super Cultivators’?”

Li Yao subconsciously pressed his temples and felt that his head was aching again.

“Yes. You haven’t seen them? Sword immortals, Cultivators, demons, devils, and so on. I’ve read half of them. Not bad.”

Yu Xin said, “I have to ask Zhao Kai whether or not I should bring an autograph back for him. Hehe. Let him manage my breakfast for a month!”

“Cultivators…”

Li Yao stared at the girls in the booth who were wearing the same clothes as before, singing and dancing, and the scenes where flying swords were flying and shooting mystic rays with various sound and electricity effects. For a moment, he felt that the scene in front of him was blurred again, and a distant world was becoming clearer and clearer.

Right at this moment, a commotion broke out in the surroundings. Quite a few animation fans and cosplayers took out their cell phones, lowered their heads, and started discussing.

Yu Xin had just taken out his phone and was about to contact his roommate when he saw a message pop up. He was also stunned and said in disbelief, “Gilson is dead? No way!”

“Which Gilson?”

Li Yao was baffled.

“It’s Gilson, the one who is known as the ‘Butcher’. He is the author of ‘Blood and Sand’, a world-famous author of serious fantasy literature. He is a real god who is best known for his cruelty and mercilessness to his characters. You don’t know him?”

Yu Xin said, “The novel is named ‘Blood and Sand’. Later, it was adapted into a serial called ‘Song of the Abyss’. It has been quite a hit over the past two years. Many people have been chasing after it. Even if you haven’t read the novel, you should have read a few of the serial, right?”

“Now that you mention it, I remember now. I’ve watched a few episodes of ‘Song of the Abyss’, but I didn’t know that it was named ‘Blood and Sand’. I didn’t know that this Gilson was dead, either.

Fantasy literature, or rather, fantasy entertainment works, were all budding in China. They had just entered the blossoming stage. Even the world-renowned gods might not be known by society, but this was the Comic-Con. Most of the tourists and the audience were fans of the related fields. Naturally, they had a strong reaction to the collapse of the industry.

Li Yao did not have much of a reaction to the death of a foreign fantasy writer, but seeing that many people were talking about it, he asked casually, “What a pity. How did he die?”

“I think it’s a heart attack. The old man isn’t young anymore, and he’s also fat. All day, he’s been killing and killing in the novels. His blood flows like a river, and his head is rolling. Even he can’t stand the stimulation. In the middle of the night, he had a heart attack… It seems so. The specific news hasn’t been released yet. It’s all gossip. Let me look at another website…”

Yu Xin lowered his head and scrolled through his phone. He switched to a forum dedicated to the discussion of fantasy entertainment works. Suddenly, his fingers and his breath came to a halt. His pupils constricted to the minimum, and he looked like he had seen a ghost.

“What’s wrong?”

Li Yao was shocked. “You don’t have such feelings for this Gilson, do you? I remember that you like to read comic books, but you don’t particularly like to read fantasy novels or watch dramas. What are you doing here?”

“That’s impossible.”

Yu Xin mumbled, “Gao Wuyi is dead, too?”

“Huh?”

Li Yao was baffled. “Who is Gao Wuyi?”

“It’s a mangaka from Fusang. His name is ‘Strong Colonization Hunter’. He has been drawing on and off for 20-30 years and killed a lot of readers. I started reading it when I was in primary school. Now that I’m about to graduate from college, it’s impossible for me to finish it. There’s no way that I can ever finish it.”

Yu Xin glanced at his phone quickly. “The news has been confirmed by the police of Fusang. It is not fake news. It seems that he was crushed to death by an out-of-control truck when he went out to play mahjong. He has been crushed into pieces beyond recognition. His hands, in particular, have been crushed into minced meat. Oh, he is doomed!”

Yu Xin heaved a long sigh and slapped his thigh.

It could be seen that, for him, who was an experienced comic fan, the death of the mangaka of Fusang was obviously more heartbreaking than the passing of the western fantasy novelist. However, Li Yao could not tell whether he was crying or laughing, lamenting the death of a genius mangaka, or gritting his teeth and saying “good death”. It was truly strange.

The atmosphere of the Comic-Con Festival was apparently more solemn.

In this era of super-advanced information, even across the ocean, nothing could stop the speed of news.

Very soon, the news of the passing of two masters in different fields, one in the east and the other in the west, who brought endless imagination and happiness, spread to every corner of the convention center. Although it was not enough to extinguish the joyful atmosphere, it at least brought two topics that were not so easy to talk about. Judging from the time, the two masters seemed to have passed away at the same time, no more than an hour before and after. There was no telling whether or not it was the will of heaven, but it was quite a pity.

Although he was in a bad mood, he could not postpone the series of events that he had prepared for the Comic-Con Festival. Soon, with the background music and the background music, the atmosphere was heated again. In the booth of the game ‘Legend of Super Cultivators’, the author of the novel stepped onto the stage and started interacting with hundreds of crazy fans or the official fans.

“Thank you for your support. Without you, there would be no ‘Legend of Super Cultivators’. Thank you very, very much—”

The author of the novel was jumping up and down on stage.

Watching him from afar, Li Yao had an ominous feeling deep inside his aching brain.

Creak. Creak.

He seemed to have heard something. He saw a few screws that were not fixed properly break and fall off.

“No—”

Li Yao cried out involuntarily, yet he was unable to stop everything. He could only watch helplessly as a combination lamp weighing hundreds of pounds fell from the sky above the booth and ruthlessly smashed the author of the novel who was promoting the game in front of countless people.

…

When Li Yao and Yu Xin returned to the dormitory, they looked like two disheveled chickens.

It was no laughing matter that an accident had taken place in the crowded Animation Festival. At that time, order was already in chaos. The two of them had just run out, and one of Yu Xin’s shoes had been knocked off. He had no choice but to buy a pair of slippers.

“Somebody’s dead! Somebody’s dead! Somebody’s dead!”

His excitement didn’t diminish in the slightest. There wasn’t the slightest bit of fear or sorrow in his expression. Instead, he knocked on the bed railing of the second brother of the dormitory, Zhao Kai, and shouted, “Take a guess. Who’s dead? I’m not talking about Gilson or Gao Wuyi. I guarantee you won’t be able to guess!”

“What the heck!”

Zhao Kai stuck his head out from under the covers and pulled out one of his earbuds. Although his tone was dissatisfied, there was still a smile on his face as he said, “Stop fooling around. I’m reading a book. What dead people? What does it have to do with me?”

“What book are you so engrossed in?”

Yu Xin shrieked, “You didn’t know such a big thing happened?”

“I don’t know. I’ve been reading books for the past two days. Hahahaha. I don’t know what the author’s dog brain is thinking. It’s quite interesting. I’ll be damned if I believe him!”

Zhao Kai chuckled, “I sincerely recommend you to take a look. It’s been a long time since I’ve seen such nonsense.”

“Is that so?”

Yu Xin wanted to tell Zhao Kai about the accident at the Comic-Con Festival, but he was attracted by Zhao Kai’s words and said, “What book? Who wrote it? I’ll read it when I’m free.”

“It doesn’t matter whether you flip it or not, but Li Yao is definitely going to flip it. I guarantee you a big surprise.”

Zhao Kai waved his phone at Li Yao and said with a smile, “The title of the book is . The author’s name is—oh, it’s a newcomer. The name is quite awkward to pronounce. It’s something called ‘Master Snail’.”

Chapter 3166: Argument Of A Book

“I was wondering what book it was. So it was this book!”

Before Li Yao could react, Yu Xin pursed his lips and said, “I’ve already skimmed through this damn a while ago. It’s clearly a big poisonous grass that’s both white and watery. The plots are simple, the characters are crude, and the writing is crude. Tsk tsk tsk tsk. The entire article is shoddy. It’s all nonsense. Zhao Kai, your taste hasn’t plummeted so much as to recommend this kind of trash, right?”

Zhao Kai’s face was flushed red from Yu Xin’s words. He spat and said, “Where’s the ‘big poisonous grass’? I think it’s alright. I can still read it alive. As for online literature, I don’t have high requirements for the characters. It’s fine as long as it’s passable. The key is creativity. Do you understand creativity?”

“The requirements are not too high. It’s not that there are no requirements. Even if it’s online literature, there can’t be three ‘dumbfounded’, five ‘racing to the limit’, and seven ‘huge’!”

Yu Xin paused for a moment. “Wait. Let me confirm with you first. This book you were talking about, is it the one with a lot of ‘stunned, soaring to the limit, enormous, and astronomical figures’? I’ve been reading so many books every day that I’ve almost forgotten its name.”

“You are exaggerating!

Zhao Kai also started laughing. He scratched his head and said, “Alright, the words used in this book are a bit monotonous. The writing style is a bit like a high school student’s essay, but there aren’t three to five ‘dumbfounded’ words on each page!”

“Yes, yes.”

Yu Xin said, “Not only will the author be dumbfounded, he will also be dumbfounded!”

“I told you not to bother about the details!

Zhao Kai was somewhat embarrassed by Yu Xin’s rebuttal. He waved his phone and said, “Don’t you think that the writing method of cultivation and technology is very creative?”

“I don’t think so. Cultivators are Cultivators, and technology is technology. Not everything can be mixed together. What kind of creativity is Malatang and pasta?”

Yu Xin shrugged his shoulders and said, “What’s more, even if it’s creative to combine completely unrelated elements, this author simply doesn’t have the ability to mix them, alright? On the surface, maintenance magical equipment, sects going on sale, all these messy things are quite interesting. But if you look at the back, you will know that it’s still the same old wine in the new bottle, the same water in the new bottle, the same medicine in the new bottle. It’s the same Little White acting cool face slapping set. Moreover, it’s written in the most ancient, the crudest way. Don’t glare. I remember there’s a villain in front. What’s his name? What’s his name?”

“Helian Lie?”

Zhao Kai said.

“That’s right. Their class monitor was also the president of the student union. For the sake of a class beauty, he took the initiative to come looking for trouble with the protagonist. In the end, he was ruthlessly beaten up by the protagonist.”

Yu Xin said, “Wow, how many years has it been since I’ve seen such an idiotic villain, such an idiotic plot, and such an idiotic author? At that time, when I saw this Helian Lie being beaten up by the protagonist, I couldn’t help but abandon the book… I reckon the latter part of the story will be similar to this. Acting cool and slapping the face, subduing the class belle and school belle, becoming the king of the universe. In short, there’s no need to waste time on this kind of trash book!”

“Alright, I admit that this book is a little childish in the early stages. I really didn’t have any books to read, so I casually flipped through it.”

Zhao Kai’s face was flushed red. However, what Yu Xin said was reasonable, and his deduction was quite reasonable. He had nothing to say, so he could only say, “But you’ll know after you read it. It’s still alright to use the game as an example. This book follows the path of late-stage development. At worst, you can skip ahead!”

“Jump over?”

Yu Xin asked, “How much?”

“Well…”

Zhao Kai thought for a moment, “In fact, I think we can watch the beginning, but you really don’t have the patience. 400 chapters should be enough, but this way, we won’t be able to see the growth of the protagonist. Maybe it will affect the brilliance later.”

“What? Four hundred chapters? Three thousand words per chapter means more than a million words, big brother!”

Yu Xin didn’t know whether to laugh or cry. “How many words has this stupid book written? What’s there to read if it’s over a million words?”

“Believe me, it is true!”

Zhao Kai’s face was about to turn purple. It could be seen that he sincerely wanted to recommend to his roommate. “The author himself has said that this book will be written very, very long. It will take more than a million words to leave the novice village!”

“More than a million words to get out of the beginner village? Then wouldn’t the entire story be over ten million words? These unscrupulous writers nowadays, in order to cheat money, are really getting more and more unscrupulous!”

Yu Xin said in disdain, “Zhao Kai, do you really not have a conscience to recommend such hydrology and poison to your brothers?”

Zhao Kai was rendered speechless by Yu Xin.

Li Yao watched the fire from the side with a smile.

He didn’t feel that Yu Xin’s words were too sharp and caustic. The way the second brother and the third brother got along was just like this. The second brother was a fan of novels, while the third brother was a fan of comic books. The two often recommended their favorite works to each other. However, their aesthetic tastes were a bit different. Every time they recommended something, the other party would bring a gun and a stick to the head and criticize it. They would make a big fuss and completely forget about it the next day. They would continue to suffer and forget to remember the beating. Once again, they would enthusiastically recommend something to the other party.

There was also a reason for today’s incident. A few days ago, Yu Xin had recommended a comic that was said to be “extremely good” to Zhao Kai. In the end, when Zhao Kai read it, he had criticized it with thousands of holes, angering Yu Xin. Today, it was Zhao Kai’s turn to recommend his beloved novel. How could Yu Xin have any good words to say?

However, Li Yao didn’t care as long as he had his own dreams. He wouldn’t argue with anyone over a novel or a comic.

“Forget it. If you don’t want to see it, then don’t. It’s not like I’m recommending it to you!”

The argument between his two roommates had reached its climax. They were both blushing and foaming at the corners of their mouths. Zhao Kai said angrily, “You deserve to be short of books. I recommended this book to Li Yao!”

“If you can’t find a good book to read, you don’t have to force yourself to swallow a poisonous grass with more than a million words!”

Yu Xin said, “Li Yao, you don’t need to waste your time. Just think about it. A book has over a million words written. The author’s writing style, ideas, and characters are all fixed. The author’s level is all here. How good can the later chapters be? A dog can’t change its habit of eating sh*t. Can this ‘Master Lie Niu’ change his habit of being ‘dumbfounded’ all the time?”

“Uh…”

Zhao Kai was at a loss for words. After breathing heavily for a long time, he looked at Li Yao and said, “Anyways, you must go and take a look. The reason is simple. The protagonist of this book is called ‘Li Yao’!”

“Huh?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment.

Speaking of which, it was not very strange.

After all, the name ‘Li Yao’ was too ordinary. He had met several people with the same name when he was growing up. They were almost the same as ‘Zhang San Li Si’.

Even when he was reading a book in the past, he had encountered a villain named Li Yao in the book. In the end, he was killed by the protagonist after only a few chapters. He had simply laughed it off.

If his name was ‘Ximen Dazhuang’, and the protagonist of the book was also ‘Ximen Dazhuang’, wouldn’t that be weird enough?

However, Zhao Kai was looking at him with such eagerness that it was hard to refuse. He could not bear to embarrass Zhao Kai.

In fact, Li Yao was quite grateful to his three bros in the dormitory, because he had a habit of having strange dreams. Many a time, he would wake up from the dream with a start and cause quite a stir. If it were his dorm mate with a bad temper, they would have long been at loggerheads with each other. In the end, no matter how Zhao Kai and Yu Xin ridiculed him, they didn’t care about it in their daily life and were quite concerned about him. Everyone got along quite well.

Li Yao nodded and said, “Alright. Cultivation and technology sounds very interesting. I’ll check it out later.”

“Yes, you must be patient. The beginning isn’t that bad. You can still continue watching without a brain. Then, there will be a change in the first 100 chapters. The entire theme will sublimate. Then, you have to grit your teeth and persist until 400-500 chapters. Everything will be flat. The more you watch, the more exciting it will be!”

“Alright.”

Li Yao couldn’t help but laugh.

In fact, he also believed in Yu Xin’s theory. Although online novels nowadays were getting longer and longer and 3-5 million words were nothing unusual, a million words was enough to show the skill of the author.

Even Zhao Kai, who liked it so much, didn’t dare to say how good it looked in the beginning and even asked him to grit his teeth and persist. It seemed that this was indeed lacking.

Li Yao did not believe that such a mediocre work would be reborn after a million words.

Unless it was a different author, or the author was suddenly struck by lightning, or the author’s brain mutated, and a few imps crawled into his brain to help him write.

In any case, whether was gold wrapped in dog sh*t or dog sh*t wrapped in dog sh*t was not a big deal. Just now, it was just a joke. If he continued talking, it would hurt the brotherhood.

“Right. Don’t you know?”

Li Yao changed the topic. “Gilson had a heart attack and died. He is the author of ‘Blood and Sand’ and ‘Song of the Abyss’.”

Li Yao didn’t say anything about Gao Wuyi, because he knew that Zhao Kai was not very interested in Fusang Comics. Maybe he didn’t know who Gao Wuyi was. In fact, Li Yao didn’t know why many netizens were celebrating Gao Wuyi’s death.

But he had seen Zhao Kai watching ‘Song of the Abyss’ on his phone. Even if he was not interested, it would still be a piece of news.

As for the online novelist’s death, it was not a big deal in the first place. Now that it was so noisy, nobody really cared.

As expected, Zhao Kai’s interest was piqued. He crawled a bit out from under the covers, “What’s what? Which one? What’s going on?”

“Let me tell you… Such a big fatty… With a crack… Heart attack… He died all of a sudden… He’s even more fragile than the characters in his books!”

Yu Xin had forgotten that the two of them had been arguing with each other just now. Instead, they were telling Zhao Kai what had happened today in great interest.

Seeing that both of them were born in the year of the bear, Li Yao smiled and walked to the balcony at the end of the dormitory.

For some reason, his heart was beating faster and faster.

Chapter 3167: Don'T Continue!

Li Yao was still thinking about what had happened during the day.

Although he was far away from the web novelist at that time and didn’t see the scene clearly, he didn’t know whether or not he was hallucinating, but he seemed to have heard the sound of a chandelier breaking, falling, and the web novelist’s bones breaking.

Later, when the crowd was rioting and everybody was running away, he vaguely saw that the blood on the booth was like a zigzagging snake that was trying to send him a message.

Later, he received news that the web novelist had passed away due to serious injuries.

Modern society had developed a lot of information. Most of the young people had seen a lot of bloody and brutal videos. Their psychological endurance was not that bad.

But Li Yao’s heart was still beating fast. The whole thing was echoing in his head.

Was it… fear?

No. Other than fear, there was confusion. Something inexplicable was flowing inside his brain.

Li Yao thought of the two masters in the fields of fantasy and animation from the East and West—Gilson and Takaya Ibo’s death.

After half a day of fermenting, the two incidents had already caused a huge uproar in the small circle of fans of fantasy literature and animation. All kinds of half-truths and half-false posts were flying everywhere, and netizens were spontaneously mourning and so on.

Logically speaking, Li Yao was not a particularly enthusiastic fan of fantasy literature or comic. There was no need for him to join in the fun.

However, he could not help but watch the news of the passing of the two masters again and again, not letting go of a single photo. He even compared the two news clips with the accident at the Comic Festival.

He didn’t know why he was doing this.

A Japanese cartoonist who loved mahjong, a ruthless master of Western fantasy literature, and a local web novelist… There was not the slightest bit of similarity, nor was there any connection.

Although it was a bit of a coincidence that accidents happened at almost the same time, there were so many authors of fantasy, science fiction, and fantasy literature around the world, as well as a large number of cartoonists and animation producers, as well as ten times the number of workers. It was not strange for accidents to occur.

But Li Yao just felt that…

Through the slanting wind and drizzling rain on the balcony, one could see that the entire Jiangnan University Town was flickering with a dim yellow fire under the illumination of the twilight. Not far away, the construction had just been completed, and the rumbling of a car could be heard coming from the high platform that connected the entire city. The campus was filled with colorful umbrellas, forming a world as gorgeous as a kaleidoscope.

This world seemed to be a bit duller than the previous one.

No. Not now. More precisely, when the burning lamp fell from the sky and hit the web novelist’s head, Li Yao felt that the color of the world had become duller and lackluster.

It was like a world made of 18,000 colors. One color was gone, and only 17,999 colors were left.

Maybe, one day, the entire world would turn black and white, and maybe even black and white would cease to exist. There would only be dead silence and gloom, forever and ever.

“What kind of hysteria am I having?”

Li Yao woke up from his trance and couldn’t help but laugh. He tapped his head hard. “Did you read too many novels or have too many strange dreams? What are you thinking about?”

It was already graduation season.

The students were all busy writing papers, doing their internship, looking for a job, and welcoming the first wave of society.

Li Yao’s grades and family were ordinary. They were not good enough for him to daydream all day long.

He was not particularly ambitious, but he was not so depraved that he would abandon himself to despair. After all, he was just an ordinary person in the world of mortals. He could live however other people lived. As long as it was neither too high nor too low, he could live as long as he had 60 points and 100,000,000 years. Wasn’t that the case for the vast majority of the people in the world?

“Stop thinking about these useless things.”

Li Yao took a deep breath and extended his hands to the outside of the balcony. He caught a handful of cold raindrops and rubbed his hot face hard. “Do you think you can get a good job by daydreaming all day? Starting from tomorrow, I really can’t fall like this. I have to prepare my graduation thesis and participate in the job fair. I have to try to find a job with a bigger development opportunity. Buy a house, buy a car, marry a wife, have children… Speaking of which, it’s really hard to find a job this year. Now that the property prices are so high, I don’t know when I will be able to collect the down payment. Who will marry you if you don’t have a house?”

Li Yao sighed. He felt powerless from the bottom of his heart.

He felt that an invisible membrane was covering his head, making him unable to see the direction, breathe, or exert any strength.

Maybe, this was how real life was like. There was a lot of pressure. Everyone had to do everything they could and be exhausted in order to fight for some breathing space in the steel forest. Only in this way could they complete their duty and mission.

It was better to be down-to-earth. After all, he was no longer at the age where he could dream irresponsibly.

They could not live in absurd dreams forever and never grow up, could they?

Li Yao shook his head hard, trying to get rid of all the messy thoughts—the accident during the day, the death of Master Fantasia in the east and the west, and the tyrannosaur that had been smeared with blood in his dream.

He washed up simply, climbed into his bunk bed, and threw his entire body into the bedroll. His body was extremely tired, but his mind was extremely clear. He resisted the thought of falling asleep. He didn’t know what he would do if he fell asleep and saw the tyrannosaurus rex, who had smeared red lipstick, in his dreams. And if the tyrannosaurus rex invited him to “wake up and fight” again.

Li Yao turned on his phone and prepared to read a few more news and posts.

In the pop-up window, Zhao Kai’s profile picture blinked as he sent Li Yao a link—the web address of “Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation”.

It seemed that Zhao Kai was really excited.

In the beginning, Li Yao didn’t have the mood to read any novels… It was said that from tomorrow onwards, he couldn’t fall so low. He had to work hard, he had to struggle, he had to study hard and improve every day!

However, it was not tomorrow yet.

Li Yao opened the link and was ready to close the webpage at any time.

The title of the book was somewhat exaggerated.

[“This is a ‘Heavenly Wolf’-level crystal warship weighing 390 million tons and manned by 1,523 Refinement Stage Cultivators. The mainframe crystal processor can calculate 90 million telepathic thoughts per second, and its computational ability is equal to that of a Nascent Soul Stage Cultivator. It can suppress a planet instantly!”

Standing in the middle of the endless sea of stars, Li Yao looked at the enormous object in front of him and calculated silently, I need—seven seconds to dismantle this warship into scrap!

In the Cultivation Era of 40,000, an ordinary teenager at the border roared at the stars and dominated the galaxy!

Li Yao was not excited at all when he saw his name appearing in the introduction in such an awe-inspiring way. Instead, he felt both embarrassed and amused.

The introduction was too silly.

What Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators? What computational ability? What mainframe crystal processor? What were they talking about?

Roars of the stars and dominates the universe? The authors nowadays can’t even write a proper book about the armed conflict between two villages, and yet they dare to write about planets, galaxies, and universes? They are really too arrogant.

As for the word ‘hot blood’, according to Li Yao’s limited reading experience, it meant that the author would use a lot of exclamation marks.

Li Yao yawned and scrolled to the top of the page.

Uh, isn’t this a cultivation novel? How did it end up in the science fiction section?

If this “roaring the stars and dominating the galaxy” thing could be considered a science fiction novel, then snails were also awesome.

The title, introduction, and column were enough to make Li Yao lose 90% interest.

However, he was afraid that Zhao Kai would ask him about it tomorrow morning, so he decided to just casually browse through a few chapters.

Li Yao clicked on the main text and opened the first chapter, which was named ‘Magical Equipment Graveyard’.

Magical equipment graveyard?

For some reason, Li Yao’s heartbeat was disrupted.

A needle that was thousands of times thinner than a hair pierced his heart softly.

Wake up! Wake up!

The heroic spirit that had slept for billions of years woke up!

The war had begun. Horns were roaring!

The Universe Ocean needs you. We… need you!

“What the hell? Have you been worrying about graduation and finding a job recently? Did you not sleep well and have a nervous breakdown?”

Li Yao dug his ears hard to clear the annoying noise. Then he rubbed his chest and continued reading.

[ Lake Rusty.

Special Waste Treatment Plant No. 23.

It was also known as the ‘Magical Equipment Graveyard’.

With the continuous development of the cultivation civilization, the magical equipment that used to be high and mighty and could only be driven by cultivators had entered thousands of households, becoming a necessary tool for ordinary people to stay at home, travel, study, and work.

Not only did it bring convenience to his life, it also produced a lot of wasted magical equipment and metal garbage.

Most of the garbage magical equipment still had a lot of spiritual energy left, which could easily lead to radiation pollution. The rune arrays to build the magical equipment were extremely unstable, too, and there was even a risk of explosion. If left alone, the environment could be seriously damaged.

Therefore, in every major city of the federation, there would always be several “special waste treatment centers” that specialized in the disposal of scrap magical equipment.

Special Waste Treatment Plant No. 23, located in the southern suburbs of Floating Spear City, a major cultivation town of the federation…

Li Yao stared at the words for a long time.

It was indeed as Yu Xin had said. The writing was rather… crude. It could only be considered as explaining things clearly. There was not much outstanding about the background setting. It was the usual opening of such a garbage dump. Even though Li Yao did not read many web novels, he had read more than ten similar opening novels. He simply could not understand why these authors were so passionate about the garbage dump. Or was it because they did not use their brains when they wrote and simply “refer” it?

“This is a common, plain, and shoddy web novel. There’s no need to waste any more time.

Li Yao bit his lips hard and warned himself in a high-pitched voice, “Stop watching. There’s no need to keep watching. Stop. Stop. Stop!”

Chapter 3168: : Bizarre Book

However, his fingers and eyes seemed to be manipulated by a mysterious force as he flipped through the pages.

As the characters flickered, a vivid, illusory, and real modern world of cultivation appeared in front of his eyes. He seemed to be able to see the lackluster sky, the red and purple gutter, the rusted magical equipment graveyard, and the countless “garbage bugs” living inside. He could even clearly smell the nauseating yet incomparably familiar smell that was a mix of rot, mold, and blood.

“What—what is going on?”

“Why does this d*mn ‘Magical Equipment Graveyard’ look so familiar? It’s clearly a very pale and weak template for descriptions and routines. It’s no different from the novels I’ve read before. I can’t tell how skilled the author is, but it feels… it feels different!”

“Is it my imagination?

“Fatty Leung… What an ancient name. Why is it that a clear image popped up in my head the moment I heard the name? I’ve never seen the author spend so much time on Fatty Leung!”

Li Yao swallowed hard and opened the second chapter.

The title of the chapter was ‘Hologram Projector’.

Obviously, this was a word coined by the author. There was no such thing in reality.

However, before he started reading, a piece of magical equipment named ‘Hologram Projector’ popped up in Li Yao’s brain. The structural designs and spiritual energy circulation diagrams of hundreds of different light beams appeared, too. Countless complicated structures and circuits interweaved into a mess and almost spurted out through his ears and nose.

His fingers. All ten of his fingers were twitching unconsciously, as if they had been possessed by a talented pianist, as if they would turn into lightning and grey mist at any moment.

“One must always dream.”

Before he turned to the last page of the second chapter, Li Yao mumbled, “What if it does?”

Then, he saw the sentence at the end of the second chapter.

Every word pierced through his soul like a burning bullet.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Earsplitting engine noises came from outside the window.

Ever since the viaduct that ran through the entire city had been built, many racing fans had taken advantage of the dead of night to treat this place as a racing arena. No matter how much they tried to ban it, it was to no avail.

Li Yao’s dormitory building happened to be the closest building to the viaduct in the entire Jiangnan College Town. It was not unusual to hear a loud noise in the middle of the night.

In the past, they could only curse or complain to the school.

But today, the engine noise was particularly loud and ear-piercing, as if it did not come from outside but directly from the depths of Li Yao’s mind.

In a trance, he saw another picture.

He was wearing a maintenance suit that was full of oil stains and driving a high-speed racing car that had been modified by brute force. He was racing on the overpass and on the bank of the river, overtaking one opponent after another and even surpassing himself and time.

In the end, there were brilliant fireworks, boiling blood, and miserable death.

Li Yao’s hands and feet started shaking.

The feeling of holding the steering wheel, stepping on the brake, and stepping on the accelerator was too real. It was so real that he forgot that he was a college student lying in his dormitory. Instead, he was like an underground racing driver sitting in the wreckage of a racing car, furiously burning. The back of his hand and the back of his foot were bulging with thick blue veins. The blue veins were like snakes that had been electrocuted and were swimming crazily below his skin. His skin was stretched to the limit. At any moment, a strange and powerful force was about to break out of this ordinary body and completely change the future of him and the entire world!

Li Yao tapped the screen of his phone with his trembling fingertips.

Chapter 3: “Strange Dream”.

Li Yao was dazed.

The content of the chapter happened to be the story of Li Yao, who lived in the 40,000th century of Cultivation in the Star Glory Federation, or ‘Cultivator Li Yao’, who recalled the past and discovered that he was a time traveler from Earth and an underground racing driver in his previous life.

It was almost identical to what Li Yao had seen a moment ago.

What the hell?

Li Yao curled up. He felt like screaming.

Touching the goosebumps that had expanded to the size of corn kernels, he said in a trembling voice, “What kind of damn book did Zhao Kai recommend? It’s too strange. I can’t read it anymore. I absolutely, absolutely can’t read it anymore… How about I read another chapter? I must have been hallucinating just now. I must have been frightened during the day and had a nervous breakdown. I must be hallucinating and hallucinating. Yes, that’s right. I’ll read another chapter to confirm… Otherwise, how can I sleep at night?”

Li Yao continued clicking.

‘Fiend Blade Peng Hai’, ‘Imitation College Entrance Examination’, ‘Great Dark Age’, ‘Second High School Goddess’.

The familiar yet strange feeling still lingered in his heart, causing him to unknowingly place himself in the role of “Cultivator Li Yao”.

It was quite strange. Although the novel was written in a mediocre style and the characters were few, the plot was still the same as the old practice of cultivation and face-slapping. The combination of cultivation and technology was not very wonderful. On the contrary, it was a bit of a claptrap. In order to attract people’s attention, it pretended to be shocking. But once one read it and really accepted this kind of setting, many parts of the novel felt quite good.

“‘Mad Dog’ Helian Lie?”

Li Yao couldn’t help but laugh as he watched, “This must be the so-called rich second-generation monitor that Zhao Kai and Yu Xin were arguing about. He doesn’t even know that he is the president of the student union, does he? What an old-fashioned way of writing. What a stiff conflict. He simply wants to create contradictions even if there are no contradictions!”

After saying that, he was dazed again.

“What did I just say?”

He was somewhat in disbelief. “I said ‘Mad Dog’ Helian Lie? That’s right, that’s what I said. It’s true that Helian Lie’s nickname is ‘Mad Dog’. I won’t be mistaken!”

He didn’t know why, but the character of Helian Lie in the book left a deep impression on Li Yao. He was the first antagonist to appear in the entire book. Even if he was at the level of a minion, Li Yao wouldn’t have mistaken his nickname.

However, whether it was the first few chapters or the later chapters, from the moment Helian Lie first appeared, the author had never once mentioned the word “Mad Dog”.

No. Not at all.

When he first appeared, the title used by the author was “Crimson Nimbus Second’s First Expert”, Helian Lie.

And after the protagonist “Cultivator Li Yao” and Helian Lie had a conflict, the word to curse the other party was “Bastard”.

Thinking about it, it did make sense. At the very least, in the first few dozen chapters, Helian Lie had the image of a high and mighty noble young master. He had his own demeanor and upbringing and simply didn’t place the protagonist in his eyes. As long as he used one hand, he would be able to blow the protagonist away. How could he reveal such a vicious and vicious image of a rabid dog in front of the protagonist?

Not just the protagonist, even the passers-by and the author wouldn’t give such a character such a weird nickname as ‘Mad Dog’.

The author had always wanted to describe Helian Lie’s arrogance, indifference, and tyranny. He wanted to use this method to increase the pleasure of “Cultivator Li Yao’s” counterattack. Although the author’s description was not very successful due to the restriction of his writing, in any case, Helian Lie had never been reduced to the level of “Mad Dog”.

However, when Li Yao looked at the author’s depiction of ‘Your Noble Son Helian Lie’, a face that had clearly lost everything, was like a trapped beast struggling to survive, looked like a fiendish beast, had scars all over its face, and had a malevolent look on its face. It truly looked like a mad dog whose spine had been broken and reattached.

That’s right, this was the Helian Lie in Li Yao’s imagination. The “Mad Dog” Helian Lie.

“Li Yao, go eat sh*t!”

‘Mad Dog’ Helian Lie’s wild laughter echoed in Li Yao’s mind, as well as the blossoming flower of flames among the dark stars.

It was truly strange. Not only did this kind of ‘Mad Dog’ Helian Lie not incite the slightest bit of anger in him, it even caused the corners of his eyes and nose to feel somewhat sour. A faint feeling of guilt and admiration was born.

“What’s wrong with me?”

Li Yao shuddered. He put down his phone and slapped himself. “I feel ‘guilt and admiration’ when other people ask me to eat sh*t? I’m not that cheap, am I? Am I a psycho?”

Of course, whether it was “Mad Dog Helian Lie” or “Cultivator Li Yao”, they were both characters in the book. They were made up randomly by novelists and had nothing to do with him. Therefore, they did not really ask him to go… to that place.

In short, he didn’t know how to put it, but it still felt weird.

“I understand.”

Li Yao held his phone and pondered for a long time. Finally, he came to a reasonable conclusion. “In fact, I’ve read this ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’ before. It’s very likely that I skimmed through it a few times and left a vague impression on some of the characters and plots in the book. However, I’ve forgotten the title and the author a long time ago. Now that I’ve read it again today, the memory pieces that have been buried for a long time will naturally surface.”

It was not unusual.

Webnovels were not scientific papers. There was no need to read them too carefully. For a veteran fan like Zhao Kai, he could finish reading a large volume of several million words in two to three days. Over the past few years in university, he had read 300-500, 700-800 webnovels, but it was not a lot. Although Li Yao was not as exaggerated as him, he had read over a hundred of these webnovels, and even more so, he had casually flipped through countless of them. For the vast majority of them, even if he had read them, he could forget the title, the author, the characters, and the plot completely. Sometimes, after reading with great interest for a long time, he would remember that he had read this book before. This was a common occurrence.

“So I have read this book before. No wonder most of the plots look so familiar.”

Li Yao nodded heavily. “Yes. That’s right. Everything makes sense now!”

Now that he had seen it, there seemed to be no need to waste his time watching it again.

Tonight, he would strive to go to bed early. Tomorrow, he would wake up early in the morning. He absolutely could not laze around in bed and degenerate. He had to be reborn and become a new person. He had to go to the library to prepare his thesis, and he had to gather information about the job fair. In short, he had to pull himself together, he had to strive forward, he had to be in his prime, he had to be vigorous!

He absolutely could not continue reading these damn webnovels. He had already seen through the hallucinations and auditory hallucinations, as well as the neurasthenia. Even the book reading software might as well be uninstalled. Wasting time and wasting life’s meaningless things. That’s right, let’s do it. Uninstall it. Yes, tomorrow the moment he got up, he would uninstall it. He swore!

Well, since it was about to be uninstalled, why not… read the last chapter again? After all, he was going to be reborn early tomorrow morning. It wouldn’t be too much to watch the last chapter as a memorial, would it?

He swore that he would never read this chapter again. Really. Even if he was bored to the point of crashing into a wall and jumping off a building, he would never read this damn again!

Thinking like this, Li Yao lit up the screen and entered the 47th chapter.

The name of the chapter was ‘Ding Lingdang’.

Chapter 3169: Yesterday Reappears

BOOM!

A kaleidoscope seemed to have exploded in Li Yao’s mind. Countless gorgeous and mottled scenes flooded in like a surging tide. He seemed to see a tall, slender girl with honey-colored skin and a big mouth and ears. She was not very pretty, but she was full of vitality. She walked toward him casually. They cried, laughed, roared, and hugged wantonly…

He also saw two tyrannosaurs rolling on the ancient wasteland as if there were no one else in the world. He saw two weird trees tangling with each other for thousands of years. He saw pairs of wolves, tigers, and leopards rubbing their heads against each other in the hot afternoon. He even saw two planets. Two shooting stars rushed close and crashed into each other brutally. A hundred times, a thousand times, ten thousand times. Hot magma surged out, and the scent of life spread throughout the universe.

“What’s going on?”

Li Yao scratched his crotch and mumbled to himself, “Why do I suddenly feel that my back is sore and my legs are weak? Why do I feel so empty and lonely? It’s like I’ve been robbed of my belongings. My entire body is exhausted and dull… I haven’t had sex in the past two days!”

Li Yao calmed himself down and continued reading.

Very soon, he was attracted by Ding Lingdang, this seemingly unconventional female character.

According to typical novels, such a fierce big sister who could kill a bull with a single punch was unlikely to be the heroine.

But for some reason, Ding Lingdang’s lively and blossoming flame-like image faintly emerged in Li Yao’s mind. This image was shining brilliantly in every brain cell, causing him to be unable to restrain himself from watching. He wanted to know everything about this girl, as well as what exactly had happened between her and “Cultivator Li Yao”.

“This chapter is too short. The author really knows how to write. There’s no content at all. This chapter doesn’t count, doesn’t count. Read another chapter.”

“Sigh, this d*mned chapter ending dog just so happens to be stuck at the critical point of the plot. Isn’t this too shameless? Can’t you let me sleep? Why don’t I finish watching this so-called Devil Flood Dragon Island plot? En, I’ll sleep after watching it!”

/ .

“As expected, this ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ is still the same old-fashioned unconscious, weak, ridiculed, and considered to be trash. But what’s going on? He can’t really be in a relationship with that muscular girl, Ding Lingdang, right? If he really has found that muscular girl, I won’t watch it. I won’t watch it even if I’m beaten to death!”

“Without me knowing, I’ve already read almost a hundred chapters. Why don’t I just make it up? I’ll sleep when I see a hundred chapters. I swear!”

“It’s time. Yes, yes, yes. It’s 11:57 in the middle of the night. Speaking of which, it’s sort of ‘today’. In the last three minutes, watch another chapter. Go to bed at midnight and welcome a brand-new tomorrow with a brand-new appearance!

“What—what the hell is going on? What the hell is going on? Why did it turn from 11:57 a.m. to 2:00 a.m. after only one chapter? What, it’s not one chapter? I’ve already seen 182 chapters? That’s impossible! What the hell!”

“Alright, since it’s already 2:02 p.m., let’s round it up and try to see 200 chapters at 2:30 p.m. Then, delete this book completely. Even the book reading app will be removed. I will be reborn and become a new person!”

“Three o’clock? Why? Oh my god, oh my god, why is this happening?”

“3:30? How come it’s already 3:30? This is depravity. How did I degenerate to this extent? Could it be that I’ve been under too much pressure recently writing a paper and looking for a job, and I’m in desperate need of a channel to soothe my nerves? That’s right. The necessary rest and entertainment are still necessary. One moment of relaxation, and the next moment of relaxation. The path of civil and military. Don’t dwell too much on the matter of the paper and the work. The body is the capital of the revolution. It’s not good to have a screw loose when you’re head over heels. Speaking of which, this author really doesn’t know how to write a love story. Hahahaha. I treat you as a brother, yet you want to sleep with me. I’ve never seen this kind of male and female protagonist. Hahahaha, what’s going on? I feel that my legs are getting weaker and weaker. My lower body is aching. It’s like I’ve been crushed by a train back and forth… I should really lower the frequency in the future.”

“No, I have to sleep. I have to read the last chapter. I have to sleep. I swear…”

Li Yao was in a daze. He rubbed his stomach while he typed on his phone.

It was truly strange. Besides the gimmick of cultivation and technology, the novel was indeed plain and uninteresting. There was nothing extraordinary about it. It was like a standardized product from a large industrial assembly line. One could look at it or not.

The blood-boiling scene on the Grand Desolate War Institution’s train could not get rid of the author’s hot-bloodedness. Instead, it gave the readers goosebumps.

However…

Why did Li Yao find it more and more familiar the more he looked at it? Most of the scenes and pictures were as hazy as memories from a hundred years ago. He felt that he had seen them before, but the more he looked at them, the more he wanted to know what had happened to ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ and his friends.

“Zhao Kai is not lying. is really good!”

Li Yao yawned. “I’m going to take a piss. I’m going to sleep after that. I’m not a human being if I don’t sleep!”

Li Yao fell asleep.

Or rather, he felt that he was asleep.

It was because he was having countless bizarre and bizarre dreams.

But today, the dream was hundreds of times clearer than before. It was so clear that he could not tell whether he was dreaming or sitting at the desk below the bunk bed, reading a web novel named ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’ wholeheartedly.

He saw the T-rex with the lipstick again.

But this time, a queen of the barbarians was riding on the back of the tyrannosaur.

She had sharp eyes, a straight nose, honey-colored lips, and wheat-colored skin. Her entire body exuded an aura of supremacy. She was none other than the character in the novel, Ding Lingdang.

“For so many years, I’ve been looking for you in the endless sea of stars. Starting from the Battle Aura Universe, I’ve killed my way through so many universes, so many worlds, and so many charming havens and terrifying hells.

Ding Lingdang stared at Li Yao. Her voice was deep and full of magnetism, pure and filled with the vicissitudes of life. “Everybody says that you are dead. Your soul and your life information have been completely shattered into nothingness. But I don’t believe it. I know that you are still alive. You must persist in your own way, even if your enemy is Earth or… the entire universe!

“Do you still remember our parting words?

“Like I said, even if you die one day, it doesn’t matter whether you go to the netherworld or the deepest level of hell, because I will demolish the netherworld, blow up the netherworld, burn the deepest level of hell, and rescue you!

“Li Yao, hang in there.

“I’m here!”

Li Yao was suddenly woken up again.

He felt that his fingers and waist had never been so sore in his entire life.

It was like someone had ruthlessly stabbed a red-hot iron needle into his ten fingernails and ruthlessly hammered his waist with a red-hot iron hammer.

Although his body had woken up, his consciousness was still in the swamp of his brain. He sat next to the chair in a daze for ten minutes. It was not until the pale sunlight pierced into his eyes that he realized it was already 9:30 in the morning.

Today was Monday. Although there were not many classes in the graduating class, his roommates had either gone to the library to prepare their papers or to do their internship and recruitment.

The dormitory was extremely quiet. The sound of time passing on the wall clock was so loud that it was unbearable. Li Yao held his breath and thought about what had happened last night.

He seemed to have read a very interesting web novel until the latter half of the night. Then, he got out of bed and went to pee. When he was done, he sat on the chair below the bunk bed and read another chapter, and another chapter, and yet another chapter. There was no telling how many chapters he had read. When his blood was boiling and his heart was surging, he even took out the small notebook in which he had recorded his strange dreams to write and draw. Then, in a daze, he fell asleep on the table…

“Novels are killing people!”

Li Yao scratched his hair, which was as messy as a bird’s nest, hard. He thought to himself in his sleep, “Alright, I’ve fallen again last night. I’ll delete the book later and uninstall the software. Then, I’ll take a nap at noon today. No, I’ll take a nap at noon. I’ll be reborn at ten o’clock at night and be a new person. That’s right. It’s decided then!”

“Speaking of which, why does my waist hurt so much? I can’t even stand up. That’s not right. My underwear is very dry. I really don’t have that!”

Li Yao tilted his head and yawned. He tried his best to recall what he had done after he peed last night. Other than reading, he seemed to be scribbling in his notebook.

Li Yao opened his most private notebook.

Then, he was dazed. His face was pale, as if he had seen a ghost.

He found that a valiant, dominating queen of the barbarians had appeared on the back of the tyrannosaur that had been smeared with red lipstick. Her eyes were so deep that they could pierce through the notebook, the desk, and even the crust.

The queen extended her hand to him, as if she were trying to pull him out of the abyss or an invitation to fight.

Li Yao touched the queen of the barbarians that he had drawn with his trembling fingers. A weird electric current flowed out of his fingertips and was about to detonate his heart.

LUM! LUM! LUM! LUM! LUM! LUM! LUM! LUM!

His heart was beating faster and faster, reaching its limit. He could not help but let out a roar that had been suppressed for countless years.

“Did I draw this?”

Li Yao asked himself in disbelief, “When did my drawing skills become so good? The person I drew looks so similar?”

Flipping to the back, there were still new paintings. Using the ballpoint pen and pen on his desk, he drew random graffiti. With just a few strokes, he outlined cities, buildings, and natural landscapes.

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently. His heart was about to grow arms and legs and jump out of his chest.

Floating Spear City, Magical Equipment Graveyard, Crimson Nimbus Second, Distant Expanse, Grand Desolate War Institution…

Although there were no annotations, Li Yao knew with a single glance that they were all scenes from the novel . There was a drawing of a ballpoint pen depicting the Grand Desolate War Institution’s train. It was even possible to clearly see the soldiers who were fighting, the cultivators who had sacrificed themselves, the screaming passengers, and the “Ghost Face Silver Mosquitoes” that were shrieking in the distance.

Although the strokes were clumsy, one look at them was enough to make one’s blood boil. It was as if one had returned to the Wasteland, the Thunderstorm Zone.

Chapter 3170: Li Yao'S Qilin Arm

Seeing the doodles on the ballpoint pens and pens, Li Yao’s heart was beating fast. He had to cover his chest with his hands to suppress the familiar yet weird feeling in his heart.

Flipping to the back, there were also some sketches of ‘magical equipment’. Most of them were chainswords, vibration sabers, and storm bolters. Not only did they look decent, they were also uniform and harmonious. One could tell that they were of a mature industrial design style that was completely different from the common designs. Moreover, there were also very delicate internal structures that looked like intricate, detailed circuit boards.

That was even weirder.

Li Yao majored in economics. He was very sure that he had never taken any courses in industrial design or circuit design. If the previous ‘novel scenes’ were just graffiti, what were the delicate and rigorous structural designs about?

Li Yao tilted his head and thought for a long time.

“Could it be that I still have potential that even I have not discovered? Could it be that I am a self-taught genius who should have taken the exam for industrial design?”

He continued reading. There were more.

This time, it was a few interconnecting sketches that looked like a 3D labyrinth. Li Yao flipped the notebook over and over for a long time before he came to a sudden realization—it was finally not the characters, scenes, or magical equipment from the novels, but the architectural blueprints and terrain of their dormitory, school building, and even the entire university.

Although it was difficult for Li Yao to determine the precision of the structural designs and the topographic maps, just the complicated lines and the seamless scale were enough to show the artist’s skills. Li Yao didn’t think that he, who had never studied architecture, would have such skills. What was odd was that, be it the school building or the dormitory, the ventilation ducts and the sewer ducts were all highlighted and highlighted by him. Even the winding ventilation ducts and sewer ducts leading to the female toilet had been meticulously drawn by him.

“Is—is there a mistake? Could it be that all of this came from my hands? What kind of person am I exactly? A hidden genius in industrial and architectural design, or a deeply hidden perverted peeping tom?”

Looking at the structural design, Li Yao was dazed for a long time.

What one thinks in the day, one dreams at night. After staying on campus for several years, it was not strange for him to be familiar with the general structure of the school building and dormitory building, but these blueprints did not even leak out the ventilation ducts and sewer pipes hidden between the floors. It was truly too strange!

However, it also explained Li Yao’s other question.

From getting out of bed to suddenly waking up, only three to five hours had passed. Those that could be used to paint without being discovered would take at most one to two hours.

To draw so many detailed, vivid, and seamless scenes and structural designs within one to two hours, no wonder his fingers were aching so much.

Li Yao rubbed his fingers and found a few dents left by him holding the brush too hard. There were also traces of ink between his fingers, which further strengthened his belief. He vaguely recalled that the scenes, magical equipment, and structural designs were indeed drawn by him.

It was probably the darkest time before dawn. His blood was boiling when he read the novel, and he felt that he had nowhere to vent his anger. He felt that his ten fingers were turning into ten Taiyi Lightning Railguns, and countless burning runes were appearing on his palm and the back of his hand. For some reason, he started writing furiously and leaving the pictures behind.

It was obvious.

If he didn’t draw it himself, could it be that someone secretly came to his side in the middle of the night and played pranks on his pen and notebook?

Li Yao knew Zhao Kai and Yu Xin very well. They were not the kind of people who would play pranks like this. Besides, the guys in the dormitory were all the same. They did not have the slightest bit of artistic or design cells. Who could draw such a complicated thing?

Li Yao flipped to a blank page and held the pen again. He really wanted to draw a new one when he was sober. Whatever he drew would do—scenes, magical equipment, architectural blueprints, maps.

Of course, the one he wanted to draw the most was still Ding Lingdang. He wanted to draw ten thousand different Ding Lingdang’s appearances so that he could clearly see who this mysterious girl was.

It was a pity that the burning feeling in his palm was gone. He chewed on the brush for a long time and managed to draw a few sketches, all of which were crooked doodles. He could not find the spirituality from last night anymore.

“What’s this called? A genius from one night ago?”

Li Yao raised his chair and shook it, but he failed to find any clues.

At this moment, he discovered that there was something wrong with his right hand.

He had just picked up a pen and was drawing, but at this moment, he held the pen between his two fingers and unconsciously rotated it. This kind of small game called “Reversing Pen” was something that all the students in high school, middle school, and high school liked to hear. It was a leisure and entertainment sport, a folk sport that everyone knew. Of course, the vast majority of people would at most place the pen between their fingertips and spin it a few times. If they could play one or two fancy moves, they would be considered to be an expert. If they could receive cries of surprise from their companions, then it was the same for Li Yao. Usually, he would fall asleep whenever he had the chance to be in a daze. When would he have the time to practice spinning the pen? To be able to spin it two or three times without dropping it was already his limit.

But right now, his right hand seemed to have been injected with a mysterious spirituality. It seemed to be alive, or maybe it was surrounded by a weird magnetic field. He manipulated the ballpoint pen to jump and dance on his fingertip in a way that he had never seen before. The revolution of the ballpoint pen gave him a soul-stirring feeling and wind-breaking sound. No matter how he moved, the ballpoint pen stuck to his finger.

“How is that possible?”

Li Yao was dumbfounded.

He didn’t know whether or not it was his imagination, but he felt that his ten fingers were getting more and more agile and slender, allowing him to perform some inconceivable movements. He even saw that the ballpoint pen, which had already slipped away from his fingertips, was pulled by an invisible force and once again jumped into his palm, spinning at lightning speeds like a drill.

Besides, the more distracted he was and the less he thought about his fingers, the faster he would spin the pen, and the more glamorous and glamorous his movements would be. When he paid too much attention to it and tried to manipulate his fingers and the ballpoint pen consciously, he would end up being clumsy. The more he tried, the more out of control he became. In the end, the ballpoint pen finally fell to the ground.

The fingertips of his right hand were red from the rubbing, too, as if he had been stung by needles.

Li Yao stared at the pen on the ground as if it were a hibernating viper.

Why were his hands so agile all of a sudden? There were a few students in Li Yao’s class who were particularly fond of spinning pens. Li Yao had also watched a few videos of the so-called masters of spinning pens through them. It was not that he was boasting, but compared to his fancy moves just now, the hands of those masters of spinning pens were no different from chicken feet. They were the kind that had been marinated.

For some reason, Li Yao thought of a word or a description.

‘Master Lie Niu’, the author of ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’, had a rather poor vocabulary and writing skills. Every time he described ‘Cultivator Li Yao’s’ speed of magical equipment maintenance and his amazing skills, he would always describe it as ‘his hands turning into two grey mists’.

Although Li Yao had complained many times when he was reading the book, he had to admit that his hand had indeed turned into a grey mist when he recalled the movement of his pen. If he had been faster, electric arcs might have burst out of the grey mist!

He must’ve been as fast when he was drawing the sceneries, structural designs, and terrain designs. Otherwise, it would’ve been impossible for him to finish half a notebook in only one to two hours.

“It’s a ghost!”

Li Yao was restless. He paced back and forth, only to feel that the small dormitory was gloomy and creepy. He was so scared that he shivered and had goosebumps all over his body.

But no matter how he wracked his brain, he could not figure out what was going on—he had only heard of ghosts hitting the wall and ghosts hitting the bed. He had never heard of ghosts being possessed to teach people how to spin pens!

His hand, his hand…

An unbearable feeling of heat spread from his heart to his entire body, and then from his entire body to his fingertips. He suddenly felt that his ten fingers were itching. It was not the itch of being bitten by mosquitoes in the ordinary sense, but an itch that was a hundred times more unbearable. He wanted to shout. He wanted to tear. He wanted to rub his ten fingers against the roughest bricks. He even wanted to burn his fingertips with fire. But what he wanted to do most was still… dismantle things.

Yes. He suddenly knew what he was going to do.

He was going to dismantle everything that could be dismantled into the most basic components.

It was an irresistible feeling like a fatal lightning.

Before his brain realized what was going on, he had already extended his evil hands toward his desk, breathing heavily. In the blink of an eye, before he was able to see the gestures clearly, all the pens and pens on the desk had turned into the tiniest parts, sorted, and arranged neatly.

Still not satisfied, he trembled as he pulled open the desk. Inside was a broken calculator and a radio for listening tests.

Although the two electronic products were not too complicated, they were still made up of hundreds of components. Moreover, the parts were very close to each other with tiny screws.

Li Yao did not have a screwdriver, but he had dismantled the calculator and the radio within three minutes with just a ballpoint pen. Every component had returned to the state when it had just been manufactured.

Li Yao stared at the two piles of components for a long time before putting the ballpoint pen in his mouth and biting it.

Pa! Pa!

He suddenly slapped himself twice to wake himself up. Then, he closed his eyes and mixed the two piles of components together, making sure that the calculator and the radio were completely mixed up.

“It’s impossible. It doesn’t make any sense. There’s no way to explain the whole thing. How did my hands end up like this? Besides, the structure, weight, materials, dimensions, and other data of every component is swirling inside my brain. My hands. Wait. What are my hands doing?”

Li Yao suddenly opened his eyes and saw that he was putting the button battery and the No.7 battery into a brand-new calculator and radio.

Yes. He was very sure that half a minute had passed. The clock on the wall on the other side of the room had not been completed yet. The pile of components that were so messy that even the creator might not have been able to distinguish them had been neatly restored.

Chapter 3171: Falling Deeper And Deeper

Sunlight slowly rose up from Li Yao’s feet like crimson mist, making him fall into a warm and fuzzy dream.

To this moment, he was still not clear-headed enough to tell whether he was in reality or in a dream.

But the impulsiveness, passion, and strength in the bottom of his heart were fading fast. It was an indescribable feeling, but every man knew that it was like the double cooling of the body and the mind after an outburst of passion.

Ding Lingdang, Floating Spear City, the Grand Desolate War Institution, the Star Glory Federation, the Cultivators, the demon beasts… The pictures that were extremely clear a moment ago were now mottled and hidden in the depths of his brain cells like a receding tide. They had been replaced by memories of the real world.

His hands, which were so light that they seemed to be burning a moment ago, were now as heavy as cold granite. He couldn’t even lift them, much less perform any dazzling movements.

Other than the paintings in the notebook, there was simply no evidence to prove that he had encountered a strange thing. And these paintings… were they really painted in the early hours of this morning? Was it possible that he had remembered wrongly and had drawn them a few days ago?

Li Yao looked at the intact calculator and radio. Had he really dismantled the two electronic products into parts and assembled them again?

Or was it just a nap and a dream?

Li Yao tapped the calculator softly.

A few black numbers immediately jumped out on the LCD screen that was covered in dust.

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently, and he found it hard to breathe.

He remembered clearly that the calculator had been broken for a long time. There was no way to display the number no matter how he pressed it. It was not very valuable in the first place. He was too lazy to fix it and simply threw it to a corner of the desk.

How could it be…

Could it be that the electronic components had malfunctioned because of dampness and were left in a corner by him? The interior was gradually drying up, and naturally, it was repaired?

“It’s decided!”

Li Yao suddenly slapped the table and stood up. “I should brush my teeth and wash up. I should be refreshed and go out for a delicious breakfast. Then… I should come back and continue sleeping!”

“It seems that I’ve been under too much mental pressure over the past few months. I’ve almost become mentally ill. Today, I’m going to take a good rest and restore my former vigor. Starting from tomorrow, I will absolutely not look at these messy things, nor will I dream of nonsense. I will face reality and work hard! I will fight! I will fight!”

The plan was perfect.

But Li Yao went back on his word again.

/ .

One could not blame his weak willpower.

The novel was truly too strange.

Every time, he would remind himself that he would stop reading the last chapter after reading the first one. But his hands and eyes seemed to be controlled by a mysterious force, and his head and heart seemed to be cursed, too. He could not stop reading chapter after chapter.

Li Yao’s entire soul was attracted by the novel. The world of 40,000 years of cultivation had turned into a terrifying black hole. Not only did it consume his energy, it also consumed his time and life. Often, when he raised his head, it was still noon. He lowered his head and read two chapters. When he raised his head again, it was already 7 or 8 o’clock in the evening. He was shocked and was about to close the webpage. In the end, he flipped to a few pages of comments. When he raised his head again, it was already 1:00 p.m.

During this period of time, Li Yao experienced quite a few bursts of passion, a strange state where he was impulsive to the point where he had nowhere to vent. It was absolutely not a phenomenon of overflowing spiritual energy that an ordinary teenager was very familiar with. It could not be solved by moving his hands up and down and enjoying himself. He drew crazily, dismantled, repaired, and assembled crazily. The notebook that was originally used to record dreams was filled with dense characters, scenes, and magical equipment blueprints. There was also the map of the entire Jiangnan City, which Li Yao was currently in. He had even unconsciously devised several escape routes. Even if an entire army was surrounding and hunting him, he could still calmly escape through the underground passageway.

God knows whether or not these maps and escape routes were accurate.

God knows why he was drawing these things!

He could not even tell whether he was reading the novel or he was dreaming that he was reading a novel. Once, when he was engrossed in reading the novel, the veins on the back of his hand protruded out and formed a weird pattern of flames. Then, a tiny flame appeared on his finger and lasted for about half a second!

This must be a dream, right?

Either his eyes were playing tricks on him, or the self-combustion of the human body had been exposed as pseudoscience a long time ago. Were there really such things as special abilities in the world? Hahahaha. It was simply impossible!

Li Yao shook his hands and continued reading.

He had passed the first 400-500 chapters. Just as Zhao Kai had said, the style of the first chapter and the second chapter were completely different. The Flying Star Sector, Iron Plateau, and the Blood Demon Sector behind them, as well as all kinds of new worlds and weird concepts, were all presented to him. He was dazzled and greatly enjoyed.

“Vulture Plan? It sounds very familiar. I seem to have heard it somewhere. Aiya, why are the engines outside so noisy? Speaking of which, what exactly is the content of the Vulture Plan? Why is he keeping me in suspense and whetting my appetite? I really want to find the author right now and punch him in the stomach so that I can dig out all the treasures in his stomach!”

“Li Yao! Li Yao!”

Yu Xin was calling him.

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. Then he heard the lively laughter around him.

“You can’t be serious, right? It’s not easy for Boss to treat a guest, and you’re actually not giving him any face. He’s only drunk half the wine, and you’re already lowering your head to read a novel?”

Yu Xin shrieked and smashed a bottle of cold beer in front of Li Yao. “No, I’m going to drink!”

Blinking his eyes, Li Yao finally withdrew his attention from the novel and looked around.

It was a “flies’ restaurant” that could be found everywhere around the university town. The environment was not very good, but it was cheap and delicious. It was most popular among college students.

Since it was the graduation season, many brothers, sisters, and lovers had spent a few unforgettable years with each other. Soon, they would have to go their separate ways. It was inevitable that they would drink until they were drunk and cry before they left. Therefore, it was also the best season for business in the restaurant.

In the small shop, almost ten tables were packed to the brim. Many people were sitting in the open air, enjoying the breeze, drinking wine, and humming songs. Cheers and laughter were echoing nonstop. The place was quite lively.

Li Yao thought carefully for a moment. Alright, he was acting up again.

Today, it was indeed ‘Zhou Ping’, the big brother of the dormitory, who had invited the few of them to have a good gathering. This was because Zhou Ping had found a good job in an investment company. The base salary was high, the benefits were good, and the promotion channels were smooth. In the economic crisis, this year was the year when it was the most difficult to find a job.

However, Li Yao, Zhao Kai, and Yu Xin, who were also unemployed, were not jealous of Zhou Ping’s luck.

Usually, Li Yao, Zhao Kai, and Yu Xin would call each other by their names, but Zhou Ping was their boss.

Firstly, Zhou Ping was two years older than them. He had gone through a lot to get into a university. His skin was dark, thick, and mature. He looked like a middle-aged man in his thirties.

Secondly, in their mediocre second-rate institute, Zhou Ping was a rarely-seen ‘top student’. Over the past few years of his university career, he had spent almost all his time in classrooms and libraries. Before he had even graduated, he had already gotten quite a few very valuable certificates.

Thirdly, Zhou Ping took very good care of everyone. Although he didn’t talk much, he was very reliable and sincere. He never said no when he needed help. He indeed had the demeanor of a big brother—he had a younger brother and a younger sister at home. He was the big brother in the first place!

Fourthly, although Li Yao, Zhao Kai, and Yu Xin weren’t some rich second-generation heirs and were at the bottom level of the city or were youths of a small town, at the very least, their family didn’t have much of a burden. At most, they had to start from scratch. As for Zhou Ping’s family, it was much more difficult. In his early years, his father had been paralyzed in bed. It was said that his father had spent a lot of money on medical treatment and even owed a lot of money. His father had to rely on his mother to work all by himself. It was truly like breaking a copper coin into two halves to spend. Over the past two years, with his scholarship, things had only been slightly better. It was just “slightly better”.

Under such circumstances, when Zhou Ping found a good job, not only were Li Yao and the other two not jealous, they were actually happy for him from the bottom of their hearts.

According to Li Yao and the other two, there was no need for Zhou Ping to treat them to a meal. Thanks for the thought, brothers. Later, it would still be “AA”.

In the end, Zhou Ping’s stubbornness flared up. He said that today’s meal had to be treated by the boss—he had received a lot of care from his brothers. He couldn’t just freeload off his brothers for four years in university, could he? He also had a little scholarship. Whoever wanted to go dutch was not giving him face.

The four of them ordered a table of delicious dishes. Zhou Ping also generously ordered a crate of beer, but in the end, none of them knew how to drink. Half a crate of beer was not finished yet. One by one, their faces were flushed red, and just as they were about to slip out of the table, Li Yao picked up his cell phone again. After reading two chapters on his cell phone, he forgot that he was still eating and drinking.

“That’s not right. Boss is so absent-minded when he’s treating you to dinner.”

Zhao Kai was also a bit drunk. He held his belly and tapped his chopsticks as he said, “I’m starting to regret recommending you to read . No matter how good it is, it’s just an online novel. It’s a pastime when you don’t have anything to do. You’ve been engrossed in it for several days and nights. It’s a bit scary to watch.”

“Yes, Li Yao. Is it really that good?”

Yu Xin continued, “Look at your scary appearance. I turned around and flipped through two pages, but I still couldn’t find anything good about it. It’s just a common, common trick. Do you have to make it look like… you were possessed?”

“Possessed?”

Li Yao put down his phone and touched his cheek. “What do you mean?”

“You don’t know what you look like when you’re reading.”

Zhao Kai took out his phone and began to act. “One moment, I’m gritting my teeth. The next moment, I’m scowling with anger. The next moment, I’m wearing an evil smile and grinning hideously. I’m just like a weasel that’s preparing to steal a chicken. There’s no need to mention how shameless and sleazy I look. We wanted to ask you to come with us to yesterday’s job fair. We called you several times, but you ignored us. When I stabbed you, the look in your eyes was really… as fierce as an awl. What’s going on with you? I’m also chasing after ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’. It can’t be as exaggerated as yours, right?”

Chapter 3172: Childhood Dream

“Is that so?”

Li Yao vaguely recalled that it did seem to be the case.
