He had heard somebody calling him and stabbing him, but he had been watching the ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ versus ‘Iron Plateau’s Xiong Yan Xibei’ during the great war on Iron Plateau. It had been a thrilling moment, and he had been filled with brutality and brutality.

Then, Li Yao slapped his head. “Damn. Yesterday’s job fair. Oh my!”

“You can’t blame us for that.”

Yu Xin said, “We really wanted to call you. We wouldn’t have joked with you about such a big thing. But you didn’t know that there was brutality in your eyes, cunningness in your brutality, and shamelessness in your cunningness. In short, Zhao Kai and I were too scared to call you again.

“I say, Li Yao, are you alright? It doesn’t matter if you don’t go to a job fair or not. In any case, we went to take a look. Those who came were all pheasant companies, but your mental state is clearly problematic!”

“Exactly.”

Zhao Kai said, “If you don’t believe me, you can ask Boss. He heard you screaming the night before yesterday. You really have a problem. Do you want us to accompany you to the hospital?”

“Ghost screams?”

Li Yao looked at Zhou Ping. “Boss, what’s my name?”

“They are not lying to you.”

Zhou Ping smiled and said, “Two nights ago, when I came back from the library, it was already very late. The two of them were both lying on the bed with headphones on. I don’t know whether or not they were asleep, but as soon as I opened the door, I heard you scream, stretching out your arms and shouting—’Cultivators are here!’ I was so scared that I didn’t dare to move for a long time. The two of them also jumped up from the bed and almost fell off.

“The three of us were dazed for a long time. Then, we noticed that you were snoring again. Your hands that were hanging on the edge of the bed were cramping fast, and your fingers seemed to be cramping crazily, as if you were playing a piano that did not exist at all. By the way, do you know how to play the piano?”

“I don’t know how to play the piano!”

Li Yao scratched his head for a long time. “Probably a cramp in my hand!”

“I don’t think his hands are cramping. It’s his brain.”

Yu Xin cursed, “Degenerate!”

“What the heck!”

Li Yao also started laughing. He tried his best to forget all the inexplicable strangeness amidst his brothers’ laughter and curses. “The three of us are more or less the same. Let’s not talk about the pot calling the kettle black here, alright? I like to daydream. Zhao Kai likes to read webnovels. Yu Xin, don’t tell me that you haven’t been secretly reading comic books over the past few days? Sigh, if you really want to talk about depravity, it’s not up to the two of you. Our boss is an authentic top student, a soon-to-rise investment magnate—only he has the qualifications to say that the three of us have fallen!”

“Yes, yes.”

Yu Xin and Zhao Kai, these two fellows, were not ashamed. On the contrary, they felt proud. With a grin, they acknowledged Li Yao’s theory. The three of them raised their glasses towards Zhou Ping: “Boss, a toast to our future big shot in the investment world, the big shot in the financial world. In the future, us three degenerates will have to hug your thigh!”

“What are you talking about?”

Zhou Ping was embarrassed. His face was flushed red. He wasn’t someone who liked to show off and talk, but his brothers in university had indeed gotten along quite well with each other. Today, they had just settled the matter of work. He thought that his years of hard work had finally paid off, and his future days were getting better and better. He was indeed full of emotions and wanted to give an explanation. He drank a cup of beer in one gulp and wiped his face as he said, “Reading a few comic books and daydreaming is considered depraved? I never thought so! You don’t know this, but I’ve been living too hard over the past few years. It’s too hard… But there’s nothing I can do. If I had conditions like yours, I would also want to live a comfortable and relaxing life for a few years and watch the novels and cartoons that I like. I would be carefree and let my imagination run wild. Really, this is not depraved. This is called youth!”

“Really?”

Li Yao and the other two were all interested. Yu Xin slapped his thigh and said, “Boss, you like cartoons, too? I didn’t know that! We always thought that you were a saint who had no desires and only wanted to travel in the sea of knowledge! Come on, tell us what you like to watch. Chances are that we are the same kind of people!”

“No, no. Of course, I’m not watching it now. I watched it when I was little.”

Zhou Ping didn’t know what to say. He thought for a long time and burped. His eyes were red even before he opened his mouth. He said, “I don’t know what’s popular with kids nowadays, either. Anyway, when I was very young, I saw a TV station play a cartoon called ‘Space Fortress’. It was about a giant spaceship that contained the entire city and a war against the aliens.

“Yes, that’s a very old film.”

Yu Xin said, “Boss, how many years have you been doing this? I remember that you are only two to three years older than us. Why do you look like you are twelve to thirteen years older than us? Are you a post-80?”

Li Yao and the other two all laughed.

Zhou Ping started laughing himself.

“Our small town can’t be compared to your city. All kinds of information come in slowly. Anyway, it’s fine as long as you know that there is such a cartoon.”

Zhou Ping said, “Now, now that I’ve said it, don’t laugh!”

“Nonsense. Who dares to laugh at our most admired boss?”

Yu Xin said, “Boss, just say it. We’ve been together for so many years, and I’ve rarely heard you talk about your childhood and your family. Now that we’re about to go our separate ways, just say what you want to say. Whatever you’re happy or unhappy about is in the wine. Let’s get drunk and forget everything tomorrow. It’s a new day!”

“In fact, who doesn’t like watching cartoons, reading novels, playing games, and doing those very interesting, bizarre daydreams, right? I still remember that when I first saw the cartoon called ‘Space Fortress’, none of you knew—”

Stimulated by the alcohol, Zhou Ping finally opened his heart. “At that time, our family was boat runners. We drove the kind of ‘Tutututututututu’ diesel-powered barge all day long. We rarely went ashore, so naturally, we didn’t have much entertainment. When I was young, I thought that the whole world was like this—a few narrow waterways, a barge carrying sand, stones, or all kinds of goods, an unpleasant smell of diesel, and a ‘Tututututu’ sound. The only joy was to climb to the top of the cabin every night and look at the stars in the sky.

“It was not until I watched ‘Space Fortress’ that I realized that there was such an amazing world out there—a starship that could fit an entire city, a deformed fighter jet, a giant alien, and a dazzling space war. Really, I’m not good with words.

“It’s alright.”

Li Yao spoke on behalf of everyone. “We all understand. It’s like our souls have left our bodies and entered a new world.

“Yes. Soul out of body. That’s it!”

Zhou Ping continued, “I was hooked by the cartoon ‘Space Fortress’. I was completely hooked. When I finished watching it, it was gone. It was as if—as if the entire world had collapsed.

“I don’t feel good. My heart feels like it’s being scratched by a cat. Every night, when I sleep and dream, I dream of a lot of space fortresses, star wars, transformation planes, and giant robots. The scenes are very vivid, as if every scene and every character has a life of their own. They are completely different from the usual dreams.

“I feel—I feel that… you are not allowed to laugh when I say it. I feel that I lived in the universe in my previous life as the captain of a super-large warship!

Puchi!

Yu Xin still laughed out loud.

Zhou Ping glared at him.

Li Yao and Zhao Kai each knocked Yu Xin’s head with their chopsticks.

“I’m not laughing, Boss.”

Holding his head, Yu Xin grimaced and said, “I did drink too much. Please go on, Boss. I’m not laughing at you. Almost all the kids have had such dreams before. I did, too. Really. I dreamed that I was a certain someone in the endless universe in my previous life. Of course, I was not as awesome as the captain, but I did. Please go on.”

“I knew that nobody would believe it, not even myself. But when I was young, I truly believed that I was the captain of a cosmic warship in my previous life.

Zhou Ping narrowed his eyes, lost in his memories. “Every night, I pondered over the plot of ‘Space Fortress’. Then, I dreamed of a cosmic warship. I remember that there were a lot of details, from the structure of the warship to the distribution of personnel to the route in the sea of stars and the various problems that the captain would encounter during the voyage. It was as if a large logbook was burning inside my brain.

“I have an exercise book. It’s the most important treasure of my childhood. I’ve drawn a lot of sketches of cosmic warships, some of which are similar to ‘Space Fortress’, but most of which I came up with myself. It’s very beautiful and delicate. I can’t believe that a ten-year-old can draw something like that. There are also fighter jets and many… What are you called? Mechas and such.

“I can also steer a ship.

“I’m not lying to you. I seem to be a natural at sailing. The dashboard and the steering wheel of our barge are even simpler than toy blocks in my eyes. The river in front of our house is even more peaceful than a brook in my eyes. I’ve been helping my father take care of the affairs on the boat since I was eleven or twelve years old. I can tell the status of the diesel engine just by listening to it. I can tell which part of the boat is wrong with a touch. I seem to have melded with the entire boat. I can steer the boat all the way to the sky.

“I thought that I would be tied to the starship for the rest of my life. One day, I would sail the biggest, most awesome starship to explore the entire world.

“Once, when I saw an aircraft carrier on the news, I was immediately attracted by the magnificent object. I thought to myself, No. I firmly believe that my dream is to be the captain of an aircraft carrier. I was born for the dream. I can. I will!”

Veins were bulging on Zhou Ping’s forehead and fists. He punched the desk heavily, but nobody noticed that all the college students were immersed in their own emotions.

Li Yao and the other two were fascinated.

“What happened next?”

Yu Xin asked.

However, it was a silly question. Their university had nothing to do with the oceanic shipping industry. Their boss had never received professional training in the field. Besides, he had just found a job in an investment company. Naturally, his absurd dreams and hilarious ambitions were gone.

Chapter 3173: This Is Growth

As expected, Zhou Ping picked up a piece of food but didn’t eat it. He stared blankly for a long time before he put down his chopsticks and said, “Later, later it was nothing. Later, my dad’s health wasn’t very good. When he went to town to buy some things, he was hit by a motorcycle. At that time, there were no surveillance cameras. In the dark, there was no way to find the perpetrator. What could he do? He could only blame his bad luck. He was in the hospital for three months. He borrowed all the relatives and friends he could at home. At the very least, he managed to keep his life. But it was impossible for him to stand up. He could forget about doing the work on the ship.

“As it turned out, our family’s conditions were not bad. Not bad. Really. At the very least, we didn’t need to worry about food and drink. This won’t do. My father can’t move, and my mother can’t take care of such a large barge by herself. So, she sold the barge and returned to her hometown to open a grocery store. From then on, our family has never touched a barge again.”

“Oh…”

This was the first time Li Yao and the other two had heard Zhou Ping mention his father’s health. The three did not know how to comfort their eldest son.

“It’s alright. It’s all in the past. Isn’t life better now?”

Zhou Ping smiled and said, “The first few years were really difficult. Really, the feeling of not having money is too f*cking uncomfortable. At that time, I was around 13-14 years old. I thought that I might as well not go to school and go out to work to help make up for my family’s expenses. In the end, my mother went mad and beat me, insisting that I go to school. She said that this was the only way out for our family. Yes, the only way out.

“In fact, I’m not very smart. You should be able to tell that I’m really not a ‘top student’. Besides, I’ve never studied hard since I was a kid. I’m not interested in anything except the affairs on the starship. I’m not good at studying at all.

“However, every time I read a book and my head started to hurt, the image of my mother crying and beating me would appear in my mind. Sigh. When I thought of this, no matter how much my head hurt, there was nothing I could do.

“Later, there was no later. Later, I was admitted to our school. Although I wasted a few years more than others, I still had a solid foundation. Now, I have also found a job. Just now, I called my parents and my sister-in-law to tell them the good news. They have not been so happy for many years. I am also very happy to see them happy. Really, I am very happy!”

“Then you—”

Zhao Kai drank a bit too much and asked, “You don’t want to be the captain of an aircraft carrier?”

“That’s just the imagination of a child. How is it possible?”

Zhou Ping pursed his lips. “I don’t know how to say it, either. In any case, ever since I focused all my attention on my studies and had not touched a ship for many years, the… feeling, or rather, spirituality, slowly disappeared from my body.

“As time went by, I rarely had messy dreams about starships, nor did I think about the boring things such as planetary wars or space jumps. Sometimes, when I passed by the river, I felt that the noises coming from the starship were strange, too. A few years were as long as decades or even hundreds of years. It felt like a lifetime ago.

“Very normal.”

Yu Xin said, “Everybody used to feel that way—you’ve grown up! Right, did you ever think of applying to a shipping school?”

“No.”

Zhou Ping shook his head and said, “Even if you went to such a school, it’s impossible for you to become a captain the moment you graduate. You still have to start from the bottom. Even if your salary at sea is a little high, it’s very normal for you to not go home for a year and a half. With my dad like this, my mom is getting older. Her entire body is aching and she has all kinds of illnesses. I pushed her to go to the hospital, but she refused. It would be even worse if I was away from home all year round.

“Therefore, this is the dream of my childhood. Hehe. An aircraft carrier? How is that possible? Even if my father wasn’t hit by a motorbike, I wouldn’t have been able to become the captain of an aircraft carrier. That’s just the imagination of a child, isn’t it? I wouldn’t have been able to become the captain in the first place, would I?

“All in all, my current job is in the capital city. I can get anything I want, and I can always go home. If I work hard enough, I might be able to buy a suite in the capital city and bring my parents over. That is my biggest dream right now.

“Exactly!”

Li Yao, Yu Xin, and Zhao Kai all gave Zhou Ping a thumbs up. “That’s very ambitious of you, Boss. Buying a suite in the provincial capital is a hundred times more difficult than being the captain of an aircraft carrier or even a spaceship. You are indeed an investment magnate and a financial magnate!”

For a moment, all four of them laughed.

“Since Boss has said so much, I will tell you my childhood dream, too!”

Zhao Kai burped as he said, “The vivid dream that Boss spoke of, in fact, I’ve also had it before. Really, I’m not talking nonsense at the dinner table, but I’ve forgotten exactly what cartoon it was. It might not be a cartoon, but something else.”

“All in all, I had a lot of bizarre, colorful dreams when I was a kid. In the dreams, I seemed to be a… explorer who didn’t know anything about special forces. I didn’t even look like a human being. Instead, I had four arms and a tail. Then, I was running in all kinds of harsh and dangerous environments, including jungles, cliffs, volcanoes, and magma. Many ferocious alien beasts were chasing after me.

“I thought to myself, if people really had a past life, then in my past life, I was probably a strengthened super special forces soldier who went out to adventure, scout, and fight all day long. Haha. Isn’t it exciting?

“Then, because of the weird dreams, I’ve been very active since I was a little kid. I couldn’t sit down for even one second. My parents and teachers all said that I had needles on my ass that would prick me whenever I sat down.

“I like to go to gym class, or go running on the street by myself after school, climbing trees, climbing buildings, and so on. One time, even my parents didn’t know about this. One time, I fell from a tree that was more than ten meters tall. It was really more than ten meters. It was at least three to four stories high. And guess what? I seemed to have awakened some kind of strength in midair. All of a sudden, I saw all the bones and muscles in my body. Like a cat, I adjusted my posture in midair and landed lightly. Other than spraining my ankle, I was completely fine. Really!”

Li Yao and the other two exclaimed.

However, speaking of which, Zhao Kai’s physical fitness was really good. Usually, he would be holed up in his dormitory reading novels. His physical education results were very outstanding. Moreover, his arms and legs were long, but his torso was not clumsy at all. He was like a giant spider.

“If I had been born twenty years later, I would’ve known that I should’ve been a parkourist or a rock climber or an explorer in my life.

Zhao Kai sighed and said, “At that time, we didn’t know about these professions. We only knew about the gymnasts.”

“Being a gymnast is not bad.”

Yu Xin said, “You are so talented in sports. Why don’t you try it?”

“On the other hand, a gym coach has come to our elementary school to pick good seedlings. He has also taken a liking to me.”

Zhao Kai opened his hands and said, “Do you think my parents will agree? How can a bowl of rice like gymnastics be so delicious? Of course, it’s glorious to win the championship. If you don’t win the championship, you’ll be injured. If you practice until you’re neither good nor bad, what are you going to do in the future? So, just study honestly. Later on, the number of books you have to study is increasing. Our school’s PE class has gone out of tune. After school, we don’t have time to climb trees and go running on the streets. It’s just as Boss said, those strange dreams of adventuring on foreign planets are getting fewer and fewer. Naturally, the ‘spiritual’ is gone!”

“That’s true. Even sports aren’t easy.”

Yu Xin thought for a moment and asked, “Do you like gymnastics, rock climbing, or parkour?”

Zhao Kai raised his head and drank half a bottle of beer. He used a wet towel to wipe his eyes for a long time before saying, “Is it important?”

“That’s true. Hahahaha. Although childhood dreams are interesting, one has to grow up and face reality!”

Yu Xin smiled and said, “I had dreams when I was a kid, too. I often dreamed of a lot of colorful paintings. Some of the paintings were even the size of an entire planet. I thought that I had artistic cells. In my previous life, I was a world-renowned, no, a world-renowned artist. My paintings even had all kinds of… ‘ability’. Why do I feel so ashamed to say it out loud?

“All in all, it was not until I grew up that I realized that every kid thought that they were a unique existence when they were little. They thought that they had a special mission when they came to this world and that they had countless messy, colorful dreams. It was not until they grew up that they realized that there was nothing special about themselves at all and that they were merely daydreaming. That was when they truly matured!

“Yes, everyone is the same. Everyone is the same.”

Zhao Kai muttered, “Hey, weren’t we talking about Li Yao reading novels in the beginning? Why are we talking about some bullsh*t ‘dream’ and ‘growth’ now? In short, you should understand what we’re trying to say, right? Li Yao, everyone has experienced your state before, but you have to get rid of it. You must not be addicted to these nonsense web novels!”

“Yes, Li Yao. Everybody cares about you.”

Zhou Ping sincerely said, “If you have anything to say, just say it to us brothers. If you feel uncomfortable, we will accompany you to watch. Don’t always be alone in the world of fiction, unable to extricate yourself. Novels are all false, made up, and meaningless. The real life in front of you is real, right?”

“Correct.”

Li Yao nodded hard. He thought so, too. “I understand what you mean, brothers. There has been something wrong with me recently. But today, after drinking and talking, I feel that I’m fine now. Really. I know what’s good for me. Thank you for your enlightenment. I really can’t daydream anymore.”

“We are all brothers. Don’t talk nonsense. It is not as serious as you make it out to be!”

Yu Xin smiled and said, “Speaking of which, nowadays, everyone’s childhood dream is quite colorful. They are going to be captains, artists, athletes, and scientists when they grow up. When they grow up, everyone will only have one dream—to make money!

“Yeah, how boring.”

Zhao Kai said, “Why don’t you help me realize my dream? I can make money, and you can be a gymnast, alright?”

“Bullsh*t.”

Yu Xin said, “Why can’t I make money? Why don’t you help me realize my dream of being an artist and become the captain of Boss’s carrier?”

“That’s why money comes first. Money comes first. Long live moneymaking!”

Zhao Kai was completely drunk. He leaned back in his chair and started dancing with joy. “What a bullsh*t dream! Go to hell!”

That night, the four people of Dorm 610 were all drunk.

Anyway, just like all the college students who had gone on a farewell dinner, they hugged each other and said a lot of things. They cried and laughed a lot of times. They talked about money, their dreams, their women, their careers, their ambitions, and their love. They made countless unrealistic promises and made countless heroic words that they would forget tomorrow morning.

In the end, the four of them supported each other and staggered out.

Li Yao was somewhat awakened by the breeze at the entrance of the restaurant. A few colorful bubbles appeared in the depths of his mind.

“Boss, you can go now.”

Li Yao said to Zhou Ping, who was still sober, “I want some fresh air to refresh myself.”

In fact, Zhou Ping was not interested in talking. He waved at Li Yao and left with Yu Xin and Zhao Kai.

“Boss!”

Li Yao suddenly shouted, “Where’s your homework?”

Zhou Ping stopped.

“What workbook?”

He turned back suspiciously.

“The workbook that you said was full of cosmic warships, your childhood workbook.”

Li Yao didn’t know why he suddenly asked such a question.

“I don’t know.”

Zhou Ping’s face was indifferent. He stared at Li Yao and thought for a long time before he remembered what Li Yao had asked. “I probably fell into the water when I sold the barge, or I burnt it myself. Who knows how long it took?”

“Then, do you remember how to paint a starship?”

Li Yao replied, “The starship that appeared in your dream.”

“It’s been a long time since I had such a dream.

Zhou Ping thought for a long time but could not remember the last time he had such a dream. He could only say, “It’s been a long, long time.”

He waved his hands hard. There was no telling whether he was driving away Li Yao or something that popped up from the depths of his soul. He walked into the black and white shadows illuminated by the street lamps.

Chapter 3174: Vicious Beast In The Heart

Li Yao waved his hands, savouring the feeling of his hands turning into a cluster of grey mist while he pondered over his own thoughts.

With the breeze, he was no longer so drunk. The words of Boss, Zhao Kai, and Yu Xin echoed in his ears again.

It seemed that there was more than one person who was as confused as he was. Maybe, most people in the world were wandering between their dreams and reality, hoping for more.

Besides, he had always wanted to be the captain of an aircraft carrier or an ocean liner, and he had always wanted to be a gymnast or an artist. Those were all his ‘dreams’. He had only read a few novels and had nightmares about tyrannosaurs, and he had been living in confusion all day long. What kind of dream was that?

Li Yao didn’t know where he had heard the saying, “Dreams are just excuses to avoid reality.” But on second thought, it did make sense. At the very least, it was very suitable for him.

Maybe, it was because he was avoiding growing up, society, work, and a difficult and boring life that he was trapped in the world of fiction and fantasy.

Fake. Fake. Those were all fakes!

Li Yao burped loudly. He felt that his throat was on fire.

He felt like vomiting. He wanted to spit out the confusion and the weird dream that had been haunting him for the past few days and stop bothering him.

This area used to be the outskirts of Jiangnan City. Ever since some of the city’s universities had moved here, forming a university town, the surrounding area had spontaneously formed quite a lively neighborhood. A myriad of lights illuminated the city, making it quite lively.

On the other side of the broad road, a new development zone was being built. There was also a ten-lane highway leading to the downtown area. From time to time, the roaring of engines could be heard. The sports cars that were rushing past reminded Li Yao of what was real and what was not.

“Beat, beat, beat you to death!”

“I won, oh oh oh oh!”

/ .

A few children were playing by the road.

They were wearing the masks of monsters, superheroes, and robots, and they were holding guns that were emitting light. They were playing the ancient war games that they would never get tired of. Laughter was echoing nonstop.

One of the children was retreating while playing and accidentally bumped into Li Yao. He exclaimed, took off his mask, and stuck out his tongue at Li Yao.

A few women’s curses came from the restaurants, grocery stores, and stores not far away. The children were all shocked and ran to their homes.

The restaurants and small shops here were mostly managed by the residents nearby. They were both shops and their own homes. After school, their children would feel that the weather was too hot, so they would move their tables and chairs outside to let the wind blow while they did their homework. In fact, it was just convenient for them to play with their little friends. Li Yao vaguely remembered that it was the same when he was a child.

His gaze fell upon the open textbook of a child. He could not help but laugh.

The textbook had depicted an ancient poet or politician, but the child had smeared it with a ballpoint pen until it was beyond recognition and turned it into a one-eyed dragon warrior wearing a helmet and armor—not just any armor, but also a crystal cannon on his shoulder. His body was glittering, and flames of war were surging on his back. He was somewhat similar to the crystal suit in Li Yao’s dream, like a space pirate who had dominated the sea of stars.

Li Yao recalled that he seemed to have done similar things when he was a child. All the ancient celebrities in the textbooks were never let off. They were smeared all over by him, making him look neither human nor ghost.

Presumably, Yu Xin, Zhao Kai, and even Zhou Ping were the same.

It was an urge that could not be described with pen and ink. In his boredom, he would smear the ink subconsciously. Often, the textbook would turn into a picture book before he realized it.

But there was no telling whether or not the children were dreaming of… the dreams related to their past life, the sea of stars, the war against the universe, and the billions of splendid worlds.

There was no telling what the dreams of these children were at this moment. To be a scientist, to be an artist, to be an adventurer, to be the captain of an aircraft carrier, or, even more exaggerated, to be a super hero who shook the universe?

There was no telling whether or not, when these children grew to the age of Li Yao, Yu Xin, Zhao Kai, and Zhou Ping, would they still remember the joy of playing the hero today, the heroism of being a scientist and an adventurer, and the courage to realize their dreams without caring about anything?

Li Yao sighed and suddenly felt dispirited.

“It’s useless. It’s impossible to win.”

He mumbled to himself, “Face reality and give up. It is too strong. Too strong. Nobody can defeat it. Give up!”

Li Yao did not know what he was talking about, either. Maybe the drinkers were just talking nonsense.

Holding the railing, he staggered forward and took out his phone.

His finger lightly touched the icon of the reading software. He hesitated for a moment before deleting the and the entire software.

Useless. Give up. Nobody can defeat it.

Dreams were all false. Reality was real. Everyone had to live in reality and could never escape, right?

Human beings were just ants living on Earth. They would never be able to fly into the sea of stars, would they?

Pursuing an illusionary dream was useless except for hurting oneself and the people around them, right?

Yes.

Yes!

‘Yes?’

But…

Why did he still feel a little unwilling? He always felt that he was missing something, something very, very important that he had sworn to defend and pursue with all his strength?

His internal organs and brain were empty, as if something had withered. He was no longer his real self; he was just a walking corpse with his own body.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Right at this moment, the sound of a thunder-like engine came from behind Li Yao. It was obvious that it was a super powerful luxury sports car.

In front of him, a child was chasing a bouncing ball to the road.

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently. The alcohol flowing inside his veins was all burning. The immense energy was flooding into every brain cell, turning them into supercomputers with amazing computational ability!

Even if he didn’t need to turn his head, he could hear over a hundred different engine sounds in an instant—it was the sound of all the cars driving on the street within a thousand meters.

He could even analyze the brand of each and every car, as well as their performance parameters and status, as well as their position in the previous second, the next second, and the next minute.

The chaos caused by the bouncing ball, the routes of the children, and even the trajectories of the falling leaves in the breeze were all within his control.

Li Yao was not thinking at all. Everything was as natural as breathing and heartbeat.

When he exerted strength in his legs and dashed out like an arrow released from a bow, he did not feel drunk at all. He only felt that every muscle and every bone in his body had turned into a component on a precise machine. It was seamless and seamless. There was nothing soul-stirring about it. It was definitely achievable.

He even lowered his head on purpose, paying attention to the angle of his entire face. At first, he didn’t realize why he was doing this, but very soon—

He was dodging the surveillance cameras near the street lights and traffic lights.

He seemed to know how many surveillance cameras there were in the area. Even when he was the most drunk, he still tried to hide his face from the cameras.

His figure was ordinary, his face was ordinary, and there was nothing special about the way he dressed. If one looked at the university campus, they would be able to find 300-500 college boys dressed like him within a minute. This was not intentional, but a natural habit.

BOOM!

When the powerful luxury sports car whizzed past him, Li Yao just happened to push the child to the ground. Fortunately,

The child was not injured, but his face was pale because of fear. He was speechless for a long time.

Li Yao, on the other hand, was scratched by the rushing air currents and fell to the ground unsteadily. His elbow was scratched hard, and he was bleeding.

The accident was so sudden that few people noticed it.

The driver of the sports car, on the other hand, should have sensed something. Although it was wrong for the child to suddenly run out, the speed limit was still higher than 50%.

However, the racing car, which was as red as fire, only slowed down for a moment. Seeing that the two of them were fine, it roared again and drove away as if nobody was watching.

The on-ramp of the overpass was not far ahead. It was a smooth road dozens of kilometers from the bridge. Since it had just been repaired, there were not many vehicles at night. Therefore, it had become a racing field for many people.

The fire in his heart suddenly exploded.

There was no telling whether he was angry or excited. Maybe it was both, but the inexplicable excitement was even more intense. The engines of the sports cars that were rushing past him were roaring like thunder and explosions. They bombarded his heart and woke up the existence that had been sleeping for a long time.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Sharp brilliance beamed out of Li Yao’s eyes as he read all the information nearby.

Within a one kilometer radius, there were a total of 47 surveillance cameras installed on street lamps and traffic lights. Other than them, there were also 132 merchants who might have installed their own personal surveillance cameras.

Over the past one minute, a total of 166 vehicles had entered the on-ramp one after another, of which 19 had been modified sports cars. It was obvious what they wanted to do by appearing in this place at this time.

The noodle restaurant 370 meters ahead of them was run by a young couple from the Shanbei Province. Most of the taxi drivers in Jiang Nan City were from the Shanbei Province. Therefore, every night, many fellow villagers would gather here. Naturally, there were always a few taxi drivers parked by the road.

Then, there was only one question left.

Li Yao looked at the child in his arms.

The little fellow was only six or seven years old. He had still lost his soul, so it was naturally impossible for him to remember his appearance.

Li Yao looked at the robot mask hanging around the little guy’s neck and laughed silently.

Chapter 3175: Uncontrollable Power

Li Yao was dreaming again.

He dreamed that he was driving a burning tank on a road that was full of thorns under the dark night sky and the roaring wind. Not only did he overtake the vehicles of his competitors one after another, he also forced them to crash into each other and roar in the darkness.

Sometimes, it was not a tank that he was riding on, but an iron demon that was hundreds of times larger than a tank. He was like an invincible expert who was roaming among the stardust and laughing in the galaxy. All kinds of weird-shaped, ferocious enemies had fallen because of his roars. Even the shining stars had to restrain their furious brilliance in front of him.

The valiancy Valkyrie riding on the tyrannosaurus re-emerged and overlapped with the image of ‘Ding Lingdang’ in the novel ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’, inviting him to ‘wake up’.

Just when he was about to agree, his opponent seemed to have been greatly affected and turned into a cluster of smoke, breaking into pieces and fleeing into the void.

Li Yao woke up and found himself lying on the bed of his dormitory. His limbs were sore, his internal organs were empty, and he had a splitting headache.

“Wu…”

He had never felt such pain in his head before, as if a burning nail had grown out of the center of his eyebrow.

He had never been so hungry before. The hunger seemed to be swallowing his own internal organs.

Holding back the pain and hunger for a long time, he recalled that he and his roommate were drunk. Then, his memory was fixed on a few kids who were fooling around in toy masks. Later… he seemed to have lost his way. He must’ve called a taxi back to school. The driver of the taxi was driving so fast that goosebumps were all over his body.

He took out his phone and looked at the time. It was already the afternoon of the next day.

“I really can’t drink like this in the future.”

Li Yao thought to himself. For a moment, he did not dare to get out of bed for fear that he would be dizzy and fall off the ladder.

He heard what Yu Xin and Zhao Kai were talking about. They were beaming with joy and gloating.

“Li Yao, are you awake? That’s perfect. You were the last to come back yesterday. I have something to ask you!”

Hearing the noises on Li Yao’s bed, Yu Xin shouted, “Did you hear anything on the overpass when you came back?”

“What’s going on?”

Li Yao didn’t know what was going on, but the name of the bridge brought back some of his broken memories.

“God of Racing!”

Yu Xin shouted in an exaggerated manner, “The mysterious God of Racing has actually used a taxi to take down more than ten sports cars. This is truly inconceivable! Today, the entire school is discussing this matter. We thought that you would at least have heard some sound when you came back and would have some firsthand gossip!”

“Mysterious… Racing God?”

Li Yao’s eyes bulged. He vaguely remembered that there was indeed such a thing. What had he done when he was, uh, drunk?

“You can check it out on the campus network.”

Zhao Kai also laughed and said, “These guys run like lightning on the highway all day long. I don’t know how many people have woken up from their dreams. This time, they probably met a professional racer. They really deserve it!”

“Even a professional racing driver wouldn’t be this crazy, right?”

Yu Xin stuck out his tongue and said, “I’ve never heard of a taxi being able to compete with a modified sports car… How professional is this?”

Li Yao calmed himself down and opened the campus network of his school.

Since the school was very close to the highway, they were not the only dormitories that were disturbed by the roaring engine in the middle of the night. Therefore, many posts were discussing the matter.

As it turned out, when the group of bikers from last night were running as fast as lightning, a taxi appeared out of nowhere. Yes, it was an ordinary taxi that had not undergone any special modifications. The taxi squeezed into the group of bikers and sprinted among the dozen or so priceless, highly-modified sports cars. Not only did the taxi suppress all the sports cars and take the lead, it was also intentionally or unintentionally interfering with the route of the sports cars. As a result, seven to eight sports cars were continuously rear-ended. Although there were no major casualties, the sports cars worth tens of millions of dollars had turned into scrap metal. Many drivers had broken bones and were screaming. It was quite a thrilling and satisfying scene.

In the first place, the students and residents of this area loathed the street racers who disturbed the residents in the middle of the night. They had reported them many times. However, those who had the ability to drive millions of luxury cars and chase after each other were not powerless civilians. Before the big accident, they had turned a deaf ear to such reports. Now that such a serious accident had happened, these people were asking for trouble. It was unlikely that they would be able to become a racetrack near General Gao openly. At least, the students and residents would have some peace and security. They would not be stingy with a magical urban legend.

Most of the urban legends were just rumors. But the soul-stirring racing match last night on the high street of the college town had been witnessed by a lot of videos. Although most of the videos had been shot by the dashcam and were barely legible, one could still see that the rumors and the rapid brightness were tearing apart the darkness like glittering wolves and leopards.

Li Yao opened a random video clip. The deafening roar of the engine immediately triggered his memories.

Li Yao’s eyes were bloodshot, and he was sweating hard.

“It is said that the mysterious God of Racing was not driving his own car but a random taxi that was not locked.

Yu Xin was still talking. “The weirdest thing is that, although there are a lot of surveillance cameras on the side of the road, none of them captured how he stole the car. The taxi that was stolen by him was found under the highway later, too. In order to avoid quarrels with the passengers, the taxi nowadays has surveillance cameras inside, too. But the driver’s face was never captured, only a funny mask. How weird!”

“Maybe he was not a professional racer at all.

Zhao Kai thought for a moment and said, “Judging from his methods, he seems to be one of those mysterious special forces soldiers.”

“Mysterious special forces? I’m in the ‘Dragon Group’!”

Yu Xin scolded jokingly, “Your imagination is quite rich. You’ve read too many web novels!”

Li Yao could not bear to listen any longer. Jumping off the bed, he first went to the toilet to retch for a while before ruthlessly washing his face. Ignoring the shouts of his two roommates, he clutched his stomach and ran out.

He had never been so anxious, nor had he ever been so hungry. He felt that every drop of energy in every cell of his body had been drained last night. He needed to eat. He needed to eat a lot of things!

…

“Fellow student, your noodles.”

The auntie at the window of the cafeteria pushed out a bowl of noodles and stared at Li Yao with a very strange look, causing Li Yao to break out in cold sweat.

No matter who it was, after eating three bowls of “super beef noodles with beef and two sunny-side-up eggs” in one go, it was likely that the cafeteria auntie would look at them like this.

And before he ate three bowls of Super Beef Noodles, Li Yao had already run through all three cafeterias on campus. On average, he changed four windows in each cafeteria and ate food that an ordinary person might not be able to eat for half a month.

But right now, his stomach was not as hungry as before. It was no longer growling. He was still far from being full, not to mention bulging and burping.

Li Yao was not someone who liked to copy the ostrich. He thought that he was safe just because his head was buried in the sand.

He thought that the problem did not exist when he covered his eyes with his hands. Such a self-deception was not going to solve anything—he was very clear about that.

Besides, no matter how much he tried to fool himself and explain himself with excuses such as ‘illusions’, ‘nervous breakdown’, or ‘drunk’, there was no way to explain how he could eat hundreds of buns, dozens of bowls of noodles, and countless high-calorie foods.

Although he went through three cafeterias and a dozen or so windows, the way he ate was enough to attract everyone’s attention. Thankfully, it was already past dinnertime. Otherwise, he would definitely be surrounded by people.

“That’s impossible. Such a way of eating has long surpassed the limits of a glutton. He will definitely be stuffed to death!”

“But I didn’t feel uncomfortable at all. On the contrary, I felt that there seemed to be a super-compressed crystal reactor inside my stomach that turned all the food into pure energy and nourished my long-dried cells and limbs. I could even feel the growth of my muscle fibers and nerve endings. What is going on?

“Last night, the so-called God of Racing. Was it me?

“How is that possible? Although I have a driver’s license, I went to take the exam together with Yu Xin, Zhao Kai, and the others not long ago. I only passed the three tests after stumbling and stumbling. After I got my driver’s license, I didn’t even touch the steering wheel once. It’s the standard ‘original driver’. If you really want me to drive, it would be good enough if I could walk in a straight line. Moreover, I drank alcohol, so I was dizzy. I didn’t know my own name. I couldn’t even walk steadily, so how could I drive?”

“Hehe. Stop lying to yourself. How can it be possible? Haven’t you seen enough weird things over the past two days? Compared to eating hundreds of buns in a row, racing is nothing. It is much more relaxing and refreshing than eating buns!”

Li Yao held his head and moaned in pain.

A peaceful life was like the surface of a frozen lake. Once it cracked, it meant that a great change was coming. Maybe it was a storm, or maybe it was a thunderstorm. In short, it was impossible to return to the past.

Not far away from him, the TV in the cafeteria was playing the latest international news. “Tragedy is unfolding in the biggest ‘T3’ game exposition in the United States. At least three armed gunmen have broken into the hall and opened fire. Several players, spectators, and visitors have been assaulted, including the production team of the popular game ‘World of Stars’. At least a hundred people have been killed or injured… Please watch the detailed report later.”

Chapter 3176: Death Of The Observer

The breaking news caught Li Yao’s attention.

It was not that the shooting itself was strange. In the hegemonic country on the other side of the ocean, guns were everywhere, extremists were everywhere, and there were ambiguous interests between the leadership and the arms dealers. The ‘Gun Control Act’ was often aborted, and similar shooting incidents happened from time to time. This year alone, Li Yao had heard of at least three to five shooting incidents with heavy casualties. There was even a concert in which there were more than five hundred casualties. It was an out-and-out massacre.

In the beginning, there were still people who considered the shooting case to be a negotiation. They were in high spirits, and their saliva was flying everywhere. As similar incidents occurred more and more frequently, more and more people stopped caring about it. To this day, however many massacres there were, it was still not big news.

Li Yao was only concerned about the location of the shooting.

Although he was not very interested in games, he knew that ‘T3 Game Exhibition’ was one of the three traditional games exhibitions in the world. It had everything from console games to online games to cutting-edge gaming platforms and technologies. Every year, it would attract hundreds of thousands to millions of tourists and fans of games to participate. Of course, first-rate video game producers and distributors would also take part in the exhibitions. Countless important works would be released in this place. It would even attract a large number of fans of animations and fantasy entertainment to play their game characters, enjoy themselves, and enjoy themselves. The lively scene was ten times grander than the Sky Horse Lake International Animation Festival that Li Yao and Yu Xin went to a few days ago.

It was not hard to guess that the security measures of the exhibition hall, which was packed with people, must be very tight. However, as long as the gunmen had a way to bring the deadly fully-automatic guns into the exhibition hall and start slaughtering, the damage they could cause would certainly be very serious.

Watching the TV, Li Yao saw shaking pictures on the news.

The blood-drenched crowd was still in a state of shock. The special police, armed to the teeth with loaded guns, and the shrieking alarms were mixed with what sounded like gunshots. It was like the end of the world.

“According to the latest news, John Garriott, the ace producer of the ‘Grey Island Game Producer Company’, known as the ‘Father of the Star Ring’, was also assaulted in the shooting incident. The status of his injuries is unclear…”

The news reported.

Li Yao was dazed for a moment.

He had never heard the name ‘John Garriott’ before, but he had played the game ‘World of Stars’ before, and he had heard a thing or two about ‘Father of the Stars’.

‘World of Stars’ was one of the most successful and influential games in the industry over the past ten years.

/ .

It was no longer a simple game but one that lived up to its name. With the joint efforts of John Galiot and countless other game players, it was built into a world that was close to reality and became a memory of a generation of youth.

In the beginning, ‘Battle of the Rising Star’ was just a shooting game, but it was well-received because of the real-life rendering of the world outside and the vivid depictions of the alien species. Later, ‘Star Ring: The Darkest Hour’, ‘Star Ring: The Supreme Throne’, ‘Star Ring: Children of Liberty’, ‘Star Ring: Brotherhood’, and a series of other games, including multiple platforms and games of various genres, were released. Almost every sequel or expansion was very successful. A lot of novels, cartoons, and doujinshi were born out of it, too. Last year, the first movie adaptation of the game was released. Although it was mediocre in quality, it earned a lot of box office sales with the help of more than ten years of popularity.

Last year, Li Yao, Zhao Kai, and Yu Xin had gone to watch the movie adaptation named ‘Star Ring: Origin’. They had played a few games about the ‘World of Star Ring’ before, so they knew a thing or two about it.

It was said that, in this year’s ‘T3 Game Show’, ‘Father of the Rings’ John Galiot was going to reveal more details about ‘Star Ring World’s’ latest heavyweight work, ‘Star Ring: Final War’. The completion rate of the entire game had exceeded 70%.

Not only had he and the “Grey Island Game Producer Company” worked hard for ten years to produce this masterpiece, but it had also already cost several hundred million to produce. It exceeded the cost of Hollywood’s heavyweight special effects blockbusters and was no less than a gamble. It was also the undisputed ace masterpiece of this year’s “T3 Game Exhibition”. Although it was not 100% completed, it had already attracted countless “Star Ring fans” to come and “accomplicate their dreams”.

No wonder the gunman chose to take action near the booth of the ‘World of Stars’—this would indeed cause the most damage.

‘World of Stars’ had a lot of forums in the country, too. Li Yao thought for a moment and opened one of them on his phone. As he expected, all the posts on the homepage were discussing the bloody shooting that had just taken place in ‘T3 Game Show’.

Naturally, the information on the forum was much more abundant than on the news.

Many fans of ‘Star Ring’ were on the other side of the ocean, participating in the series of experience activities in the booth of ‘World of Stars’, and interacting with the game producers such as ‘Father of Stars’.

They had sent live posts about the live stream with the intention of showing off and sharing. They did not expect that they would encounter such a tragedy.

Through the introduction of some posts, Li Yao had a deeper understanding about John Garriott, the ‘Father of the Rings’, as well as the entire world of the rings.

Ever since the entertainment form of video games was invented, it had been closely related to the concepts of science fiction, outer space, and alien creatures. From the earliest ‘space intruders’ to the later ‘Soul Douluos’ and ‘demonic beasts’, everything had been associated with science fiction, fantasy, and magic.

Before the world of space rings, there had been countless games describing space wars, alien species, bizarre-shaped aliens, and even the ultimate mysteries of the universe.

But the ‘World of Stars’ series was based on the detailed descriptions of the extraterrestrial species, the solid deduction of the extraterrestrial history, and the soul-stirring and reasonable plot… It was a bloody road through countless similar works.

It was all thanks to the gold medal producer, John Garriott, who had painted a splendid, bloody new world for the public with his extraordinary imagination and solid knowledge in physics, biology, and astronomy. He was the well-deserved soul of the world of the rings.

Many game critics even said that they suspected that John Garriott was an alien pretending to be a human being. If there were not such a ‘star ring’ in the depths of the sea of stars where tens of thousands of species coexisted, and he was one of them, how could he have painted such a world?

Even John Garriott said that he once had countless bizarre dreams in which a circular city larger than the entire solar system appeared in which countless alien species were living. He was enlightened by the dreams and created such a game—no, a world!

“I am like a mirror.

John Garriott once said, “Or rather, an ‘observer’ who did not know himself reflected all the information that the universe sent to my brain.

Li Yao sighed.

After all, he had played other people’s games and watched remakes before. It was truly regrettable that such a talented game producer had met with misfortune.

After refreshing the webpage, a new thread popped up. If Li Yao was not mistaken, there were hundreds of replies after only three minutes.

It was a video thread. The title of the thread was ‘Straightforward, ‘The Father of the Ring of Stars was shot in the Moment’!

Li Yao’s curiosity was aroused. When he clicked on the video, he found that it was a short video shot by a fan of the Star Ring on the spot. The camera was aimed at the booth of the World of Stars, which was splendid and full of futuristic and sci-fi feelings. In the middle of the booth was a middle-aged man wearing a promotional t-shirt and jeans. He looked casual, but his eyes were extremely deep. Naturally, he was John Garriott, the father of the Star Ring.

Li Yao’s English was not very good. Besides, there was too much noise in the hall. Nobody could hear what the father of the space ring was talking about clearly.

Thankfully, someone had already thoughtfully translated the video into Chinese. Even if there were some mistakes, one could still make out the general meaning.

“I have a dream that I’ve been having for twenty years and has become part of my life.

John Garriott pointed at his temples and said, “Some people say that I’m an alien who has crawled into the skin of an earthling. I have the same feeling myself. In the deepest part of my brain, there is always a voice that is screaming, roaring, and urging me to tell you everything I know and share my dream with you. It is not just for me but for thousands of people to dream together.

“Therefore, ‘World of Stars’ was created.

“The dream that lasted for twenty years gave birth to countless works about ‘star rings’. But instead of dimming and dispersing, it became clearer, clearer, and larger. I even felt that a ‘star ring’ larger than the solar system would jump out of my brain one day!

“The ‘Battle of Terminus’ is such a great work made of real dreams. It will lead us to the ultimate mysteries of the rings and even the universe. I promise you—”

BAM! BAM BAM BAM BAM!

When the video reached this point, gunshots suddenly rang out behind the cameraman.

The crowd was suddenly in chaos. The cameras were shaking crazily, too. In the moment of the shaking, they saw that John Galiot’s chest was bleeding, and he fell backwards in shock.

The video ended there.

Li Yao was soaked in cold sweat.

For some reason, he felt that the bullets did not just hit John Garriott’s chest but also his heart.

With trembling hands, he refreshed the webpage again. A new thread with a bold title popped up in front of his eyes.

The latest news.

John Garriott, the ‘Father of the Star Ring’, was declared dead in the local hospital because of the serious gunshot wounds!

Chapter 3177: Broken?

BOOM!

The sound of thunder in the summer afternoon broke the stifling heat in the depths of everyone’s heart. Large clusters of dark clouds condensed in the sky. Just a moment ago, the sky was clear and boundless, and the sun was shining brilliantly. But at this moment, the heavens and the earth suddenly turned dark. It was as if a faint black veil covered everyone’s eyes.

Was it his imagination, or was it because of the brightness? Li Yao suddenly felt that the color of the entire world was a bit dimmer than a moment ago—it was insignificant, almost indistinguishable.

This kind of feeling had also appeared once in the Comic Con that day. It was when he heard the news of the death of a famous fantasy novelist and cartoonist. However, it was only for a fleeting moment, and in the blink of an eye, he could no longer feel it—a person’s habitual ability, or rather, self-hypnosis ability, was very strong in the first place.

Holding his phone, Li Yao was dazed for a long time.

Dozens of posts were refreshed on the forum. Many fans of ‘Star Ring’ confirmed the death of ‘Father of the Star Ring’ through different channels. Some of them even commented in a corny tone, ‘A generation of youth has passed.’

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao stood up and walked to the window.

Lightning flashed and thunder rumbled outside the window, as if a ferocious beast from outer space was roaring. In the blink of an eye, torrential rain poured down like a waterfall. Floods raged between heaven and earth, transforming the world into a boundless swamp.

Li Yao could not go back without an umbrella.

He did not want to go back, either.

What had happened yesterday, what had happened today, what had happened in the Comic Con a few days ago, and maybe what had happened in the dream a long time ago… Little by little, they turned into a raging meteoroid shower and bombarded the place that had been sealed in the deepest part of his heart, leaving an irreparable gap.

“Do you want to be muddleheaded and mediocre for the rest of your life and enter the next reincarnation in a fuzzy and lackluster way, or do you want to follow your dream and find your true self—even if it means paying the price of destruction, even if it is only for one second?”

Deep inside his brain, a voice seemed to be asking him.

Countless thoughts spurted out of his brain like bubbles. Li Yao felt that he was becoming more and more ‘smart’ and ‘cleaner’ to a certain extent. He could think of many things that he had never thought of before, and he could face the weirdness that ordinary people could never understand calmly.

He sat back in front of his beef noodles.

At first, he wanted to find a notebook to record the weirdness and questions he had encountered recently.

However, his instincts rejected the idea instantly. There was no need to leave any notes—even his ‘dream notebook’ had better be burnt before the ashes were flushed into the sewer.

In that case, he would just meditate and record in his mind. After all, his memory was getting better and better.

Firstly, regardless of whether or not there was a scientific and reasonable explanation for everything, at the very least, eating over a hundred buns and a dozen bowls of noodles during a meal was absolutely abnormal. It was beyond all the known laws of physics. From this, it was inferred that the so-called “superpowers” were not illusions. They existed in this world, and he himself possessed… Of course, he could not say that he had the ability of a “big eater”, but rather, he had the ability to “quickly transform matter into energy and store it in an inconceivable way”. Moreover, his own body seemed to be being transformed efficiently by energy, becoming stronger and more agile. This point had yet to be verified, so he put it aside for now.

Secondly, was the ‘Mysterious Racer God’ who was racing on the elevated ring last night really him?

Logically speaking, it was impossible for him to do such a thing. But if there were really ‘superpowers’ in the world, it would not be surprising at all.

Considering that his memory of racing was quite clear, and that he was extremely hungry when he woke up today, it was evident that he had consumed a lot of energy. It would be terrible if he assumed that he was really the God of Racing.

Although Yu Xin, Zhao Kai, and the posts on the campus network all stated that the mysterious Racer God did not leave any traces that could prove his identity, Li Yao knew that it was impossible for him to hide himself 100% of the time. Wherever he went, he would definitely leave traces behind. Anyone’s actions would be exchanged with the surrounding environment. Even the smallest exchange of materials could be found out. The key was how much energy, resources, price, and time it would take to investigate.

So, would he be found out?

Li Yao estimated that it was unlikely on the official level, because there were no major casualties.

However, what followed was ‘third’.

Thirdly, let’s say that ‘superpowers’ did exist in the world, and he was not necessarily the first superpowered person. Then, would the superpowered people be organized? Was a superpowered person ‘kind’ or ‘malicious’ to other superpowered people? What was the attitude of the superpowered organizations toward the new superpowered people? Were they recruited as members, or were they going to be experimented on and dissected?

Would the super-powered organizations be interested in the videos of the high-rising rings and investigate further?

Li Yao disliked neither the members nor the subjects.

He was free and did not want to be controlled by any form or force.

Besides, he had a vague feeling that there was a scheme that was even more terrifying than ‘superpowers’ and ‘superpower organizations’. He knew what the scheme was, but he had forgotten it later.

He had to find the answer without too much interference.

Fourthly, from the looks of it, no matter how many strange dreams he had, the mutations that had happened to him over the past few days were all triggered by the novel ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’.

Why was he so familiar with the scenes and characters in 40,000 Years of Cultivation? Li Yao was now certain that he had never read the novel before. Definitely not.

Who was ‘Master Lie Niu’, the author of the novel? What did he know? What was his purpose in writing the novel?

It seemed that these questions could only be found in the world of fiction.

Fifth, it was a question mark.

Was it his intuition or his imagination? It seemed that every time… the creator of a fantasy world, be it a novel, a comic author, or a game producer, unfortunately passed away, the entire world would suddenly become a bit lackluster.

A master of fantasy novels, a mangaka who was used to procrastinating, and a gold-medal game producer. Right, maybe there should be a web novelist, too. They should be four completely unrelated creators. Was their deaths within a week a coincidence, or was there a deeper reason?

Was it possible that more similar creators would be killed in the near future, or that they were just the more successful and well-known creators in the circle? Their deaths would be the news, and outside of the public’s attention, countless unknown creators would be killed a long time ago, and nobody would care about them?

Li Yao thought everything through with half a bowl of noodles. He was quite surprised himself.

His thoughts were clearer and more agile. Besides, now that he was faced with such a complicated problem, not only was he no longer scared or hesitant, he was also more and more excited.

In a month’s time, if he still didn’t find anything in a month’s time, it wouldn’t be too late for him to find a job and return to the right path of life.

Li Yao thought to himself, Come to think of it, what exactly is the ‘right track of life’?

The summer afternoon storm came and went quickly.

Outside the window, the torrential rain turned into a light drizzle. Sunlight tore through the dark clouds, and a rarely-seen rainbow appeared.

Li Yao drank the boiling hot noodles and strode into the drizzle and sunlight.

…

Li Yao once again fell into the world of .

However, this time, he had come prepared, not just to read novels for fun, but also to do an experiment on himself.

He chose to use the computer to read the webpage of the novel. Then, he placed his phone to the side to take a picture of himself reading the novel. His left hand tightly clutched a sharp fountain pen. When he felt that he was about to be completely lost, he ruthlessly stabbed his thigh to regain consciousness.

Fortunately, he was about to enter June. Most of the students of the graduating class had found their next home. Yu Xin, Zhao Kai, and the others were all out all day. They hardly returned to school unless there was something important. He was the only one left in the dormitory. It was quiet and comfortable, and he was not afraid of being disturbed.

Li Yao was self-taught. He bought a lot of high-energy food such as compressed biscuits and chocolate from the supermarket, locked the door, and installed a simple ‘alarm device’ with a basin and a stool. He then closed the curtains tightly.

He read each chapter carefully and recorded his psychological and physiological reactions in his mind.

“It’s really strange. It’s not an illusion. My body has indeed become stronger. I can do a hundred push-ups in one breath.”

“Just a moment ago, he continuously raised his leg and jumped for 15 minutes without even breaking a sweat. If he was outside in the playground, he would definitely be able to run with an astonishing result… What kind of terrifying physical strength is this!?”

“My ability to draw a map of buildings and streets has also increased. I seem to be able to draw all the streets that I have walked on in my life.

“I understand it now. There’s a pattern here… It seems that the more chapters I read in , the stronger the ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ in the novel becomes. In reality, I am also known as ‘Earth’s Li Yao’, and my strength is getting stronger and stronger. Of course, my strength is no match for the characters in the novel, but the speed of my improvement is the same. What is going on?

“I understand why Zhao Kai said that he wanted to skip the first 400-500 chapters. It’s true that the style of the chapters before and after is extremely different. It’s not that the writing style and the depiction of characters have improved by much, but rather, the things that the author wants to write are completely different. They are simply two novels with the same name. They are written by two completely different people… Has the author been changed? Or has the studio been possessed by the author? Or… does the author himself not realize this?”

Another three days passed. Not only was Li Yao’s mystery not solved, it was getting bigger and bigger.

However, he had a vague feeling—as long as he could finish reading the novel , all his questions would be answered. He would clearly understand who he was.

However…

The more he read, the more excited he became, and the more he was surprised by the books and himself. The story came to an abrupt end.

“That’s all?”

Li Yao stared at the in a daze.

This was the end of the fourth volume. The protagonist, ‘Cultivator Li Yao’, had just finished his adventure in the Ancient Saint World and was preparing to return with a bunch of ancient saint-level experts for the ultimate confrontation between the federation and the imperium. It was the most exciting moment of all, and the result was gone?

Looking at the author’s last update, it was seven days ago.

This was not good. It was very rare for a web novel to not have a chapter updated every two to three days. It was a different matter if it was a finely-carved, gem-like masterpiece, but followed the path of quantity, quantity, and satiety. The author had to update at least two chapters a day. Under these circumstances, if the author did not update for a week, it was very likely that he would… stop updating?

Chapter 3178: Looking For Master Snail

Li Yao’s Adam’s apple seemed to have been punched heavily. Then, a ton of cement was poured into his chest from his throat, making him unable to breathe.

An inexplicable fury rose up in his heart, making him want to swallow the entire computer.

Did he stop updating? He actually stopped updating! How could he stop updating? Wasn’t this author too irresponsible?

Who would win the war between the federation and the imperium? What would the so-called ‘Alliance of Holy Accord’, which was ten thousand times more evil than the imperium, look like? What was the truth behind the Pangu Civilization? More importantly, what was the damn ‘Vulture Plan’ up to? Would the ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ find Earth and return to it?

In the beginning, the author had dug so many pits and painted a splendid, splendid universe, whetting one’s appetite. But in the end, the author had cut off the chapters irresponsibly and abandoned the pits.

How could it be?

Li Yao could not wait to crawl into the computer and find ‘Master Snail’ through the wireless network. Then, he would hold the guy’s collar and force him to confess everything.

Li Yao’s eyes were bloodshot. He gnashed his teeth, not knowing why he was so frustrated and angry.

He opened the comments section and saw that there were many people who were equally furious as him.

After seven days of no updates, the comment section was in chaos.

Li Yao read the post in order of the time it was posted. In the beginning, some people were concerned about the author and asked him to explain to them. But as the author disappeared, more and more readers couldn’t hold back their anger and started cursing in the posts.

“In the beginning, the book had 400-500 chapters. It was quite smooth and easy to read. What was the author writing at the end of the 500 chapters? Was he writing a novel or was he writing a thesis? It was really too ugly! I pinched my nose and read the latest chapter. I thought that I would be able to restore the top level. I didn’t expect that the author would actually stop updating? Damn eunuch!”

“I’ve never seen such a f*cking f*cking f*cking f*cking f*cking f*cking novel. Every once in a while, the two of them would sit there and converse like a broken toilet bowl. “Hua hua hua hua hua hua” 3-5 chapters in a row, 10 or so chapters. When it came to the latest chapter, the ‘Ancient Saint Chapter’, it was even more excessive. The f*cking Ancient Saint 12 powerhouses! The 12 of them chattered for several dozen chapters in a crooked meeting. There’s no end to it, right!? When I saw this, I knew that the author’s talent had run dry. He couldn’t continue writing. Sure enough, he became a eunuch, right? He abandoned the f*cking f*ck!”

“In fact, I knew a long time ago that this author was going to be castrated. At first, he could still be considered to be doing interstellar training. But when he wanted to level up his monster fighting essay, he insisted on bringing a lot of personal goods with him. He made the scene bigger and bigger. He talked more and more sense. He reversed the plot time and time again, dragging the plot to the extreme. His grades are getting worse and worse, right? In short, with the author’s skills, it’s simply impossible for him to handle such a big theme. In short, he just kept digging pits and then used bigger pits to cover up the small pits in the beginning. In the end, when he really couldn’t fill them up, he simply castrated. Sigh. It’s a pity that I wasted so much time and money!”

Of course, there were also some diehard readers who were trying their best to defend the author. They said that they had communicated with the author in the WeChat group a few days ago and the author had only taken a few days of rest because something had happened to him and he had broken his hand. He would continue to update after he recovered.

In the thread below, there were immediately people who ruthlessly rebutted, “Come on, I can only fool new readers like you. In fact, this author’s background has long been exposed by us. This guy is now wearing an alias named ‘Master Crouching Bull’. He is still pretending to be some pink and tender newbie, but in fact, he is an old turtle whose skirt reaches the ground. His original name is ‘Zhang Daniu’. However, he is always a eunuch and has a rotten tail. He can only change his alias time and time again to deceive innocent new readers. Sigh, I didn’t expect that I would be fooled this time. Damn it!”

Li Yao browsed through a lot of reviews and comments. Many indignant readers were shouting that they were going to send razor blades to the author. Some of them were planning to beat the author up, etc.

But Li Yao was still brooding over the previous message.

Something had happened to the author. He was injured?

Li Yao recalled that had stopped updating seven days ago. In other words, if the poster was not lying, the author should have been injured seven days ago.

It happened to be the time when the author of the online novel was smashed to death by the headlights during the Pegasus Lake International Anime Festival, when Gilson, the master of fantasy novels, and the mangaka Takaya Ebo unfortunately passed away.

Was it a coincidence?

So many coincidences.

“This won’t do. I must find ‘Master Snail’. It will be best if I can ask him face to face what exactly is going on with his novel.”

Li Yao mumbled to himself. Then he hesitated, not sure whether or not he was too obsessed.

However, when his eyes fell on the dumbbell in the corner, all his hesitation was gone.

The pair of dumbbells had been bought by four people in the dormitory together for the purpose of exercising. They were very solid and big. All the barbells added together weighed more than twenty kilograms.

However, Li Yao was not talking about the dumbbell, but the grip made of steel. On the grip were two deep handprints, which were the traces of his strength yesterday.

/ .

Even if he was the champion of the Hercules competition, could he leave his fingerprint on the grip?

Li Yao walked over and picked up a five kilogram barbell. He held it in his hands and exerted his strength. The barbell was torn open like a sugar cake.

“Clang!”

Li Yao threw the torn dumbbell to the side and looked at his terrifying hands. His heart was in turmoil and could not calm down for a long time.

Admit it. It was not a power achievable by human beings. With such power, he would never be able to live a normal and peaceful life.

Of course, if he ignored the matter and buried his head in the sand, deceiving himself, such strength and dreams would gradually wither and fade away.

However, what kind of person was he exactly?

Li Yao pulled himself together and searched for information about ‘Master Snail’.

In the information era where the network was highly developed, it was difficult to completely hide a person’s information. Master Snail had his own social media account. By searching for words and micro-message numbers, he could gather clues about him.

First of all, according to the search entry, Master Snail’s real name was ‘Zhang Daniu’. He was a resident of Lishan County under the jurisdiction of Jiangnan City and lived there all year round.

“In the same city as me? That’s easy!”

According to Li Yao’s research, Lishan County was the most remote county in the mountainous region of Jiangnan City. However, with the current state of transportation, even if the author were to hide in his hometown to recuperate, it would not take a day before he was found.

Then, following the name of ‘Zhang Daniu’, he found some of the novels written by the author in the past. Well, they were all in the same style as the first 500 chapters of ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’, which did not move him at all. This made him even more certain that the 500 chapters of ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’ did not seem to be written by the same person.

Then, Li Yao entered the ‘Old Bull’s Meeting Room’, a group of ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’, and asked the readers about the author, pretending that he knew nothing.

The smell of gunpowder in the book group was slightly less intense than in the book review section, but it was only ‘a little’. Many people were pessimistic and thought that the book was really wasted. Some of them were even cursing and venting their dissatisfaction.

However, Li Yao asked patiently and got a few useful information.

Firstly, three to five days ago, the author had appeared in the group and explained to everyone that he was really injured. However, some of the readers did not believe it and had an argument with the author. In the end, the author said, “I’m a f*cking eunuch. I’ll be a good man if I change my alias.” From then on, he never went online again.

Alright. At the very least, it meant that the author was not in an accident at that time. Well, not a serious accident.

Secondly, there was the author’s WeChat number in the group. Many readers proved that it was indeed Master Snail himself.

However, many readers had sent messages to the author, and none of them had received a reply. It seemed that it was impossible to contact the author through this method.

Li Yao opened Master Lie Niu’s WeChat and sent him a message. “Hello, Master Lie Niu. I’m a loyal fan. Can we talk?”

As he expected, there was no reply at night.

Li Yao was impatient from waiting. He simply contacted Zhao Kai. “Zhao Kai, do you know that has stopped updating?”

“I do.”

Zhao Kai replied very quickly, “It’s just a web novel. It’s normal to break off. Just read another one. What’s wrong? You’re not still reading a novel in your dormitory, are you?”

“No. I was just asking.”

Li Yao thought for a moment. “You’ve read a lot of web novels. Do you know how to reach out to the author and ask him to talk to me?”

“Crazy!”

Zhao Kai seemed to be amused. “You’re not a girl, so why would the author want to chat with you? Hey hey hey, Li Yao, what exactly do you want to do? Are you really staying in the dormitory and not going out?”

“A girl?”

Li Yao was dazed. He tilted his head and thought for a moment. Then he put on a smile that he didn’t notice himself and said, “I have something to tell you right now!”

He closed the chat window with Zhao Kai and thought for a long time.

Very good. He remembered that this damn pervert, Yu Xin, had trained with a female character’s alternate account when he was playing games last year. In order to fool others, he had specially applied for a micro-message number that looked like a girl’s. There were all kinds of photos, moods, patterns, and everything.

Therefore, Li Yao opened Yu Xin’s profile picture.

After much persuasion, at the risk of being mistaken for a fellow Cultivator by his roommate, he finally got the female version of his WeChat account. After careful modification, he changed his name to ‘Rabbit in the Sea of Stars’ and signed it with the words ‘Love when you want and leave when you want’ before he added a few fresh, slightly naughty, and slightly sexy pictures.

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao applied to join the ‘Old Cow Meeting Room’ again under the name of ‘Little Rabbit in the Star Ocean’ and opened Master Lie Niu’s profile picture.

“Hello, Master!”

“I’m a second-year student of Jiangnan Normal University. I’ve liked reading science fiction since childhood, and I especially like your works. Can I talk to you?”

Chapter 3179: Bait!

After sending the message, Li Yao closed the chat window and returned to the book group and the reviews section to collect more information.

Come to think of it, Yu Xin’s account was not one that he used often after all. His disguise just now was a bit hasty, too, and he had left a lot of loopholes. It shouldn’t be difficult for someone to discover that the ‘little rabbit in the sea of stars’ was an imaginary existence.
