If Master Snail was a gentleman with a heart as still as water, or if he had some basic wisdom, it was hard to say whether or not he would be fooled.

Therefore, he had better think of more solutions.

Li Yao once again beat around the bush in the book group chat. In the end, those who remained here were all loyal readers of 40,000 Years of Cultivation. Other than expressing regret and indignation at the author’s abrupt loss of updates, everyone else had a lot of insight into the novel. Li Yao’s words drew out the feelings of many readers who were reading the book. Quite a few people gave a lot of insightful comments.

It was a pity that, although many readers had high praises for this novel, and there was no lack of diehard fans like Zhao Kai who were addicted to it, there was not a second reader like Li Yao who had been triggered by into a dream that was both illusory and real, let alone having a strange superpower.

In the eyes of most readers, this was just a passable web novel. Even if he really stopped updating and became a eunuch, it didn’t seem to be a big deal. All he needed to do was curse a few times. No one had ever felt that Li Yao… was like being bitten by thousands of ants. It was an unbearable feeling.

Li Yao memorized the comments of the readers and some of the highlights of the reviews and comments section, especially the replies of the authors. He tried to figure out the author’s preferences.

Then, he found a few comprehensive online novel forums and searched for posts related to . Whether they were recommended or criticized, the opposing parties’ opinions were quickly swept through. If he could really get in touch with Master Snail, how he could attract the other party to communicate face to face would depend on his methods.

Communication through the network was partly because it was unsafe and other people could steal the information at any time, and partly because the initiative was not in their own hands and they could go offline at any moment.

40,000 Years of Cultivation was his only clue. If this clue was cut off, it would be extremely difficult to find another clue.

Therefore, Li Yao had to suit his taste.

Thankfully, after a few days of ‘training’, his eyesight and brain power had both been significantly improved. His reading speed was three to five times faster than before, and he could deal with both the computer and the screen at the same time. That was why he was able to browse through hundreds of thousands of words of information in only one hour.

The notification panel above the phone was still empty. There was no response.

“Alright. It seems that the guy didn’t see my message at all. Or he simply laughed it off when he saw it. He even saw through my prank immediately.

Li Yao thought to himself, It does make sense now that I think about it. Judging from the plots of the battle of wits and bravery in and the magnificent depiction of the universe, the author should be a person with a high sense of humor and a very quick mind. Such a clumsy…

Beep beep beep!

Master Snail’s profile picture popped up in the notification bar at the top of the phone. The other party sent a very reserved smile.

“…”

Li Yao scratched his hair. Alright. Since he was hooked, he might as well chat.

“A real person?”

Li Yao said, “Are you really ‘Master Snail’? I’m sorry. I’m a bit excited. I really like your books!”

Li Yao bought a cute, playful, and girlish ‘Pink Bunny’ emoji for the account. At the end of the sentence, a pink bunny with starry eyes was attached with an adoring emoji.

The other party was silent for a long time. It was so long that Li Yao thought that the other party had seen through his trick and went offline. Finally, Master Snail said, “Hello, hello. I didn’t know that there was a female reader in .”

“What are you talking about?”

Li Yao sent another pink bunny pouting emoji that seemed to be angry, “Who says we girls can’t watch sci-fi? Can’t we only watch those lovey-dovey and lovey-dovey things? Can’t we imagine an impassioned and soul-stirring story happening in the sea of stars? Let me tell you, there are many female students in our department who like to watch your . I alone have recommended it to three to four sisters. We are all chasing after it and often discuss the plot after lights out in the middle of the night!”

“Is—is that so?”

Master Snail seemed to be at a loss. “Really?”

“Of course it is!”

Li Yao once again sent a pink rabbit emoji with its hands on its hips, nodding its head heavily. “Although we usually read more novels in the girls’ channel, it doesn’t mean that we will reject the real good books in the boys’ channel! It’s just that the web novels written by ordinary boys are all about fighting and killing. They are selfish and have a lot of harems. They simply treat us girls as machines without feelings and will. We are at the mercy of the male protagonist… Of course, we don’t like reading such things!”

“However, your novel is different. Although at the beginning, it was still an old-fashioned way of leveling up and fighting monsters, your values are very upright. The protagonist is not a scumbag who has no moral bottom line. Moreover, he is very serious when it comes to relationships. After writing millions of words, he still only loves his wife. He is simply a clear stream in online novels!”

“As the saying goes, a book is just like a person. I believe that a real person must be an upright person in life. A noble person who has broken away from the vulgar interests. Most importantly, he is very loyal to his feelings and knows how to respect and love women, right?”

“Yes, of course!”

Master Snail said, “You are too courteous. Your evaluation is too high. I don’t deserve it. I don’t deserve it. Hahahaha!”

“You are indeed modest. As I expected, you are as low-key, easygoing, and elegant as Master Lie Niu.

Li Yao probed, “Right, can I call you ‘real person’, or should I call you… ‘Teacher Zhang’?”

“No, no. I wouldn’t dare call myself a ‘teacher’.”

Master Snail said, “My pen name is ‘Master Snail’, and my real name is ‘Zhang Daniu’. Everybody calls me ‘Old Bull’ in the book group. But in fact, I’m not old. We are all friends. Why don’t you call me ‘Big Bull’!”

“That won’t do. Your novel has indeed given me a lot of inspiration. I have been deeply influenced by both in life and in work. Of course, you are my ‘teacher’.”

Li Yao confirmed that the other party’s name was really Zhang Daniu, “How about I call you ‘Teacher Niu’? You can call me ‘Little Rabbit’. Oh right, Teacher Niu, why did you suddenly stop writing such a good novel? I saw in the group chat that you were injured. Is it true?”

“Cough. Don’t mention it.”

Master Lie Niu—Zhang Daniu sent a frustrated emoticon over. He was no longer as reserved as before. “Don’t listen to the comments section and some people in the book group. It’s true that, oh my, how should I put it? The endings of some of my previous books are not perfect, but a bad ending is not equal to a eunuch. How can these people insult my innocence out of thin air?”

“As for this time, I was really injured. I slipped in the bathroom and fell heavily on my right hand. The bone in my right hand is broken, and it’s swollen to the point that it’s even thicker than my thigh. The moment I touched it, I felt an excruciating pain. The doctor said that I need to rest for a while. The swelling has only just subsided today. How can I have the strength to write?”

“Is that so?”

Li Yao pondered for a moment and did not think that the guy was lying. “Then, will you continue to write and paint the marvelous world of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’ for us after your wounds are healed? I really want to know what happened later and what the so-called ‘Vulture Plan’ is!”

“Well…”

Zhang Daniu hesitated for a moment before he laughed and said, “I will definitely write it when I have the chance. Really. Thank you for your support. Let’s see how it goes. I will try my best.”

“Is that so?”

Li Yao found a pair of round eyes, drooping rabbit ears, and the cutest and most “cute” emoticon from the emoticon pack and sent it over. “Please, please, Teacher Niu. Can you spoil the rest of the story in advance? Just a little bit, just a little bit. Sob sob sob, I can’t wait!”

“Well, spoilers are not mysterious at all!”

Zhang Daniu was still very persistent on this point. “However, I can tell you in advance that the rest of the story is very grand, brilliant, soul-stirring, and bizarre. It’s true. I’ve thought through all the final secrets of the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance, as well as the Pangu civilization and even the entire universe. Of course, the ‘Vulture Plan’ and the ‘Origin of Earth’ are necessary, too. I’ve written the outline. I guarantee that nobody will guess what I’m going to write!”

The outline had been written. The Vulture Plan, the mission of Earth, and the ultimate secrets of the universe were all inside!

Li Yao’s heart was beating fast. He was getting closer and closer to the truth. Chances were that, as long as he got the outline of the author, his superpowers would be maximized.

“If you say so—”

Li Yao sent a few smiley faces. “I’m more interested in what happened next!”

“Cough cough.”

Zhang Daniu said, “There’s still a lot of stuff that needs to be sorted out. I can’t explain it in such a short time!”

“Then… why don’t we talk face to face?”

Li Yao said, “Are you in Jiangnan City, too? Why don’t we have a chat? I have a lot of science fiction questions that I would like to ask you in person!”

“Huh?”

Zhang Daniu said, “Hey hey hey?”

“Please don’t blame me for my presumptuousness. This is sort of ‘privacy’. In fact, I came to you with a mission.”

Li Yao sent an emoji of a rabbit sticking out its tongue. “There’s a science fiction association in our department. All the sisters in the association like to read your novels very much. We’ve been preparing to share the book with the theme of 40,000 Years of Cultivation. Then, all the sisters said… I heard that Master Snail is in Jiangnan City. I wonder if he could be invited as a guest of the reading club. If he could come, everyone would be so happy! So, I shamelessly asked you to come. You don’t mind, do you?”

“Of course not. Of course not.”

Zhang Daniu said, “Sisters of the Science Fiction Association?”

“Exactly!”

Li Yao said, “The Teacher’s Institute has always been a bit disproportionate between men and women. Our department is even more miserable. We are all girls with long hair and swaying skirts. There are not many boys. The development of our Science Fiction Association is quite difficult. Teacher Niu, please guide us!”

Chapter 3180: 7 Pm, Wait For Me!

“Invite, invite me to be a guest? Oh my, this, oh my, invite me to be a guest?”

Zhang Daniu’s tongue was a little tongue-tied. “Is this… also considered science fiction?”

“How funny. How is yours not science fiction?”

Li Yao sent another smiling emoji. “Didn’t you post it in the ‘science fiction section’ on the web novel website?”

“That’s true, but at that time, wasn’t it because the competition in the fantasy column was particularly fierce and there were too many gods that I had to avoid the limelight!?”

Zhang Daniu said in embarrassment, “Don’t tell me you don’t think that mine is… pseudo-science fiction or something? I’ve heard that you university students are all particular about this. You like to watch the more orthodox, mainstream science fiction. You don’t like to watch our kind of fighting pseudo-science fiction.”

This guy had some self-awareness.

Li Yao thought to himself.

But there had to be a reasonable explanation for the question. Otherwise, it would be very difficult to fool the guy out.

His pupils constricted slightly, and his computational ability soared to the maximum. The weird stories that he had just found on the Nexus were all broken into pieces and condensed into a flawless speech.

“What are you talking about? The sci-fi fans in our university are not as shallow as you think. There are so many ridiculous stereotypes. What orthodox sci-fi and pseudo-scientific? I don’t agree with such a method!”

Li Yao said solemnly, “In my opinion, the reason why our country’s sci-fi industry can’t be developed and why it’s been struggling for so many years is because there are too many people and fans in the industry who don’t focus their attention on the content and ideology of the work. They don’t want to promote it together and improve it together. Instead, they work together to condemn other people and themselves. Are these people really passionate about sci-fi? No. They are merely using something that even they don’t understand to generate a ridiculous sense of superiority and pitiful vanity.

“The temple is small, but the wind is strong. The pond is shallow, and there are many turtles. The science fiction field itself is a very small circle. The science fiction industry of our country has been lagging behind the developed countries for a long time. It really can’t be any smaller. As fans and people in the circle, if we don’t think of doing everything we can to make this ‘small pond’ bigger, to turn it into a big pond, a big lake, and even an ocean, and then introduce all kinds of strange and bizarre species to form a colorful ecological circle, we will start biting each other when we are still a small pond. We want to drive other people out of this pond and call ourselves kings behind closed doors… Can such a pond give birth to a flood dragon other than turtles?

“You don’t need to undervalue yourself. Really. I’ve seen some of the posts on the net regarding orthodox sci-fi and pseudo-scientific science fiction. It’s pure nonsense. It makes people think of the struggle for power and profit in the Little Court of South Brightness. What kind of situation is this? Who’s orthodox and who’s not? What’s the point?”

“When it comes to your , although many people say that it is a fantasy in the skin of science fiction, I feel that it is the exact opposite. In fact, you are still a science fiction in the skin of fantasy. You are full of thoughts about human nature, history, and the universe. The brilliance of compassionate humanitarianism and ultimate care is shining everywhere. It is not hard to say that your thoughts are profound. Moreover, you can quietly integrate these profound thoughts into the soul-stirring, perilous plots one by one. Reading them is gratifying, and after reading them, the aftertaste is long and drawn out, making people unable to help but clap the table and exclaim in admiration. I really don’t know how your brain grew. It’s too awesome!”

/ .

There was no response from the other side for a long time. They seemed to be intimidated by Li Yao’s words.

“Crap!”

Li Yao was somewhat annoyed. “Such cheesy flattery is too much. Anyone can hear the sarcasm in it and know that I’m talking nonsense. Did the guy go offline because he was angry?”

Just as he was about to reply with a few words of compensation, a message came from the other side.

“It—it’s true!”

Zhang Daniu seemed to be so excited that he was about to cry. “I didn’t expect, I didn’t expect you university students to understand me so well. Aiya, aya, aya. You’ve practically spoken to my heart!”

“Huh?”

Li Yao was dazed for a long time. He mumbled to himself, “…He believed it? No way. He actually believed such shameless flattery?”

Although it was somewhat unbelievable, there was no turning back now. Now that he had come this far, he could only continue with this train of thought.

“It’s true, Master Niu. I meant what I said.”

Li Yao spat on the screen of his mobile phone. He tried his best to hold back his disgust as he typed, “Let’s take a step back. Let’s take a step back and take a step back. Let’s say that there are still some omissions in your work. With such a long length and such a fast updating speed, it’s only natural that there are some omissions. But no matter what, you’ve always been down-to-earth. One step after another, you’ve done your best to contribute to our country’s sci-fi industry and make your own contributions. As for those people who shout ‘authentic sci-fi’ all day and mock ‘pseudo sci-fi’, what have they done other than ridicule?”

“Not really. I didn’t contribute anything. I only did what I could.

Zhang Daniu said somewhat fearfully, “Then, don’t tell me that the students of your Science Fiction Association don’t find my too long-winded and… a little boring?”

This was a big problem.

Li Yao thought for a long time but failed to come up with a response. It was indeed lame. How the f*ck was he supposed to flatter it?

Suddenly, he had a flash of inspiration. His fingers were like flowing water as he sent out lines of information:

“I remember that in one of Mr. Feng Zikai’s essays, he once said—in his hometown, the water-grilled rice cake is a very nutritious thing, but the rice cake made from glutinous rice is too solid. Even if it is cut into pieces the size of fingers and cooked, it is still difficult for the elderly and the children to digest. Eating it can easily hurt their stomachs. They can only eat 3-5 slices at most before putting it down.

“However, if the rice cake is sealed with a ‘explosive rice cake’ and processed at a high temperature and high pressure, the rice cake as thick as a finger will explode into the size of an arm and turn into food.

“A bento the size of an arm has the same nutritional value as a rice cake the size of a finger, but it is more likely to be absorbed by the human body. It is an appetizing fruit for the young and old. Even many people who don’t like rice cake in the first place like to hold a few bento rice cakes and taste the slightly sweet flavor… Don’t you understand the logic?

“The so-called orthodox science fiction and hardcore science fiction are, in fact, the rice cake. Of course, its nutritional value is very high, but it requires a certain degree of scientific literacy and aesthetic interest in order to enjoy it. The threshold is too high. Many people will be scared away after seeing it.

“As for your , I can’t go against my conscience and say that it doesn’t have the slightest bit of water. However, it just so happens that this water changed it from a ‘annual cake’ to a ‘bumpy rice cake’, allowing more readers to accept it. It even caused readers who were originally completely uninterested in science fiction to gradually fall in love with the sea of stars. They fell in love with the universe. They fell in love with thinking about the past, present, and future. From then on, they stepped into the temple of science fiction and slowly came in contact with more science fiction works… Just think about it. You have so many readers. Even if only one tenth of them were influenced by you and became true fans of science fiction, you would still have made a great contribution to our country’s science fiction industry!”

“Ouch! Ouch!”

Zhang Daniu said, “Even I didn’t know that my ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’ had this much significance?”

“Of course you don’t. I can tell from your words that you are too humble.

Li Yao said, “It’s alright for you to be humble, but we, your loyal readers, will not allow you to suffer the slightest grievance. How about it, Master Niu? Come and be our guest and guide us. The students really want to meet the legendary Master Niu!”

The other side was silent for a while.

Li Yao’s heart was in his throat again.

“In fact, I’ve been quite busy recently. As you know, many seminars and seminars are asking me to join them. There are also reception parties for some publishing houses, charity events, and charity events. Of course, I have to think about the important questions about humanity and the future of our civilization.

Zhang Daniu’s words almost caused Li Yao’s throat to burn. He could not wait to reach his hand into his phone and ruthlessly pull this fellow over. Fortunately, this fellow’s next sentence was, “However, since our fellow students are so enthusiastic, how can I say this? For the sake of our country’s science fiction career, I have a successor, right? No matter how busy I am, I will definitely make time to support my fellow students! Tell me, what time should I directly come to our Normal University?”

Li Yao took a long breath in relief and wiped off the sweat on his forehead.

“Not at all. I’m here to see you first. How about dinner?”

Li Yao said, “Do you live in the downtown area or somewhere else? You’d better find a place that is closer to your house. Don’t waste Teacher Niu’s precious time!”

“Alright, how about tomorrow night?”

Zhang Daniu said, “You are a student and don’t have any income yet, so you don’t need to treat me. Let me treat you. Do you know of the hidden crane cuisine in the city? Let’s meet at seven o’clock tonight, alright?”

Li Yao knew that it was a newly opened high-end restaurant with an elegant and famous environment.

This guy was quite generous!

“Alright, Master Niu.”

Li Yao sent another emoji of a rabbit with starry eyes. “Seven o’clock tomorrow night. Hidden Crane cooks. See you there!”

After ending the conversation, Li Yao went to the toilet and vomited for five minutes before deleting all the conversation logs.

It was too embarrassing. Nobody was allowed to know about it. Whoever did would be executed!

As for tomorrow, it was impossible for Li Yao to play the role of the ‘rabbit in the sea of stars’ anymore. After all, he had locked onto the author and there was no way that he could run away!

Li Yao looked at himself in the mirror and grinned hideously.

He roared and exerted his strength. Muscles that looked like bombs bulged out of his body and tore his short-sleeved shirt apart. Veins and veins ran through his body like boas under his bronze skin. Scorching air was spurting out of his pores. He had torn the dumbbell into two halves!

“Zhang Daniu…”

Looking at the super muscular man in the mirror who had almost doubled in size, Li Yao grinned hideously. “Wait for me at seven tomorrow night. I’m coming!”

Chapter 3181: Meeting The Author

The middle district was the oldest district of Jiangnan City.

Although it had fallen behind the high-rise development zone in the recent city-building campaign, it was still quite popular because of the beautiful scenery and the favorable location.

It was especially so for the few commercial walking streets that had all kinds of distinctive colors. Every time the lanterns were lit, crowds of people would come and go. It was quite lively.

The ‘Hermit Crane Cuisine’ was located in a prime location on a pedestrian street named ‘Pingyang Road’. It had a lavish entrance and was a forest of man-made bamboos. In the downtown area, it forcefully created an atmosphere of tranquility amidst the hustle and bustle. It did not have the slightest intention of hiding.

Opposite the Hidden Crane Restaurant was the top department store of Jiangnan City, which had a history of 30-40 years. When it was built, it was the tallest building in the entire city. It had flourished for a time, but naturally, it had long declined. No matter how it was renovated and renovated, it could not change the overly cramped layout. However, it was more than enough to act as a high point to control a radius of 300-500 meters.

In the men’s restroom on the fifth floor of the first department store.

From here, he could overlook the entire Ping Yang Road and clearly see the passersby and tourists coming and going.

6:30 p.m.

Li Yao had been standing there for half an hour, observing the stores and people nearby with the telescope. Not only did he have to meet the author of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’, he also had to determine whether the author lived nearby or far away. It would be best if he could follow the clues and lock onto the author’s nest.

As time went by, his eyes became sharper and sharper. He could lock onto the different features of hundreds of passers-by and analyze their trajectories clearly.

“Master Niu, are you here?”

After scanning the environment and the passers-by, Li Yao sent a message in the name of ‘rabbit in the sea of stars’.

“I’m here. I just got here. I’m wearing blue, and there’s a ‘Grandma Fried Chicken’ next to me. Are you here, too?”

The other side replied.

/ .

Li Yao aimed the telescope at a milk tea and fried chicken shop twenty to thirty meters away from the Hidden Crane Restaurant.

Other than a few high school girls who were buying milk tea, there was a skinny man wearing a wrinkled blue suit.

“Is it him?”

Li Yao observed carefully. The guy seemed to have just shaved his head, but the depression on his face could not be concealed no matter how hard he tried. Maybe because of the years of desk work, his eyes were somewhat green, his cheeks were sunken, and his back was slightly hunched. The cheap suit he had bought was a size larger, too. It looked like a plastic bag on his body. A gust of wind could blow him away easily.

Of course, even if the suit was well-fitting, it still looked quite awkward. Today was the hottest day since the summer. The highest temperature was 35 degrees. Other than real estate agents and insurers, who would wear such a serious suit?

While Li Yao was staring at this guy, this guy was using the dressing mirror of a nearby clothing store to tidy up his appearance. He sprayed something like a breath of air freshener into his mouth. At the same time, he used the reflection of the mirror to secretly glance at the high school girls who were drinking milk tea.

“There seems to be no one else nearby.”

Li Yao scratched his head. He had thought that he would be able to write characters such as Yan Xibei, Xiao Xuance, Jin Tuyi, Lu Zui, and Boss Bai. The author could be down and out, he could be down and out, he could sigh, but there had to be a hint of ambition in him.

But it seemed that there were none.

After carefully scanning the surroundings and making sure that no one was following the author, Li Yao sent a message saying that he would be there soon and got off the elevator.

He squeezed back into the crowd, but he wasn’t in a hurry to greet them. The few female high school students were still drinking milk tea. From time to time, they would burst into a burst of youthful laughter. Zhang Daniu had also been dawdling next to them. His eyes were constantly darting around.

Li Yao took out a toothpick crossbow from his pocket. It was a toy that kids often played with. With the cover of the crowd, he threw a toothpick at Zhang Daniu.

The two parties were seven to eight meters away from each other. When the toothpick lightly flew over, it no longer had any strength. Zhang Daniu was completely oblivious. It was not until the toothpick pierced into his back that he let out a cry and turned around to rub his back. He simply didn’t realize what had shot him.

“So, he doesn’t have any ‘superpowers’ and is just an ordinary person with dull senses?”

Confirming this point, Li Yao threw the toothpick crossbow into a trash can on the side of the road. Only then did he go forward and heavily pat the other party’s shoulder. With a beaming smile, he said, “Master Niu, hello!”

Zhang Daniu jerked his head back and discovered a young man whom he had never seen before. The excitement on his face turned into surprise. “You are…”

“I’m Rabbit’s senior brother. I’m from the Jiangnan Normal University. I’m truly sorry. It’s truly unfortunate. Last night, a thief broke into our school’s female dormitory. The few girls were scared quite badly. They were shouting and shouting in the middle of the night. Today, the school’s leaders flew into a rage. The dormitory is strictly guarded. They say that it’s best if the girls don’t come out at night and run around!”

Li Yao grinned. “So, there’s nothing we can do. I’m the only male member of our Science Fiction Association. Rabbit and the rest of them asked me to be their representative to express their sincerest apologies to Teacher Niu. The few girls all said that there would be plenty of time in the future and that they would find a day off in a few days. During the day, they would come and talk to Teacher Niu about literature, science fiction, and their dreams. You don’t mind, do you?”

“Ah—”

Li Yao saw Zhang Daniu’s face, which had just been shaved clean and still had a few cuts on it, collapse bit by bit. Like an ice block under the scorching sun, it quickly lost its shape. “No, no. Of course not. Hahahaha. Girls should be careful when they go out at night. There are too many bad people in society nowadays…”

“It’s great that you can understand. Rabbit and the others were worried that you would be angry and not go to our school as a guest, so I told them, how could Teacher Niu, who could write ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’, be such a petty person?”

Li Yao smiled happily. “It’s the same today. I have a lot of questions that I want to discuss with Master Niu, too. Besides, I know everything about Rabbit and the rest of them. You can ask me whatever you want to know!”

“I—I don’t want to know anything!”

Zhang Daniu said, “The main thing I want to know about is the development status of your sci-fi association. I’m concerned about the current university students’ views on sci-fi, their dreams, and their future. What else can I know?”

“Yes, you are right. Let’s talk about that.”

Li Yao slapped his head. “I almost forgot to introduce myself. Do you think it’s a coincidence? My name happens to be ‘Li Yao’. I share the same name as the male protagonist in your book. I especially like to read . Really. I even feel like I’m the Li Yao in the book. I feel… connected to Teacher Niu. I feel like I’m his soul mate!”

“En en.”

Zhang Daniu seemed to have lost his desire to chat. He replied to Li Yao half-heartedly.

“Don’t just stand there. It’s too hot.”

Li Yao pulled Zhang Daniu. “Let’s eat first. Shall we talk while we eat?”

“…Sure.”

Zhang Daniu replied lethargically. Then, he suddenly came back to himself. “Where are you going?”

“Crane cuisine!”

Li Yao said, “Rabbit said that you asked her to cook in He Yin, didn’t she?”

“…Ah, y-yes, that’s right. Wait at the entrance of Crane Concealing Kitchen. It’s easier to find it here.”

Zhang Daniu glanced at the low-key and extravagant Hidden Crane Restaurant. The corners of his eyes twitched a few times as he said, “However, I’ve been having a stomachache for the past two days. It’s not suitable to eat these raw and cold things. Those raw fish fillets all have parasites. Why don’t I treat you to a casserole made of bones? It’s a century-old restaurant nearby. The bones are rotten. It’s much better than raw fish fillets.”

The two of them walked for more than ten minutes before they left the pedestrian street and arrived at an old-fashioned residential area that was full of life.

Li Yao didn’t care what he ate as long as he could confirm that Zhang Daniu lived nearby.

While he was walking, he carefully observed that Zhang Daniu was holding his right hand and avoiding passers-by. His right hand was slightly swollen, indicating that he was indeed wounded.

Zhang Daniu seemed to have eaten a bellyful of dust. His face had always been gloomy, but when the steaming, mountain-like bones were brought to the table, he seemed to have accepted his fate. A dry smile appeared on his face again.

“A thief has entered the female dormitory. Rabbit and the rest must be scared out of their wits, right?”

Zhang Daniu laughed. “Rabbit… Her boyfriend must be worried sick, right?”

“Rabbits don’t have boyfriends.”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He replied immediately, “My junior sister is a girl who pursues the soul and has a great sense of aesthetics. She is a maverick who always thinks about the universe, the future, and civilization. Ordinary people are nothing in her eyes.”

“Oh…”

Zhang Daniu’s smile lit up.

“What about you?”

Li Yao took the opportunity to inquire about Zhang Daniu’s situation. “Master Niu, you are here for a meal. Don’t you need to report to your wife?”

“No, no. In fact, I’m single, too. I’ve been wandering alone for more than thirty years. How can I have a ‘master’s wife’?”

Zhang Daniu smiled and said, “Also, fellow student Li Yao, let me give you a suggestion. We are all fans of science fiction. We are the same kind of people. Don’t call me ‘Teacher Niu’. ‘Teacher Niu’ is too short. It sounds like the difference in seniority, but in fact, we are about the same age. You are all in your twenties this year, right? I am also in my thirties. My mentality is especially young. We can be considered to be the same age. Don’t call me ‘Teacher Niu’. Just call me ‘Brother Niu’!”

“…Brother Niu.”

Li Yao took a deep breath and tried to control his facial expression. He looked at Zhang Daniu’s right hand and said, “Brother Niu, do you want to drink some wine? With the fresh blood, your wounds will heal faster!”

“Alright, let’s drink a little then!”

Zhang Daniu waved his hand. “That’s right. Don’t treat me as a teacher. Just treat me as a friend. Between friends, what can’t we talk about and what can’t we help with?”

“That’s right, Brother Niu. After reading , I knew that you must be a very straightforward and confident person. Indeed, it’s better to meet you in person than to hear your name. Come, let me propose a toast to you first!”

Li Yao was planning to get Zhang Daniu drunk so that he could ask for some crucial information. After all, his body had been greatly strengthened. Ordinary alcohol was simply not enough to numb his nerves. One glass to the left, one glass to the right. After three rounds of alcohol, five dishes had passed. Under Li Yao’s meticulous plan, Zhang Daniu had already drunk so much that his face was flushed red, his legs were shaking, and even his tongue was a little loose.

“Brother Niu, can you tell me the details of your journey in creating ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivator’?”

Li Yao took the opportunity and said, “This is also a task that Rabbit asked me to do. Everybody wants to know how you thought of writing such a work in the first place.”

Chapter 3182: - Dream Book

“Actually, it’s nothing.”

Zhang Daniu chugged down a glass of cold beer and burped loudly. He waved a bone in his hand and said, “You know, I’ve written a few things in the past, but is completely different. I don’t know when it began, but some fragmented, mottled dreams would always appear in my mind. They were like countless shining lanterns floating, circling, and dancing in the depths of my mind. Each lantern contained a planet. Each and every one of them contained countless stories of countless people on a myriad of different types of planets.

“I didn’t have to do anything at all. As long as I put my hands on the keyboard, the wonderful stories that happened on the planets in space would flow out on their own as if they were alive and beyond my control.

“Another… dream?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. Pondering for a moment, he asked, “Then, did anything weird happen to Brother Niu during his creation? Or was there anything wrong with his body?”

“Strange? Weird?”

Zhang Daniu blinked his eyes. “I don’t think so. Just some tendinitis and lumbar strain. What do you mean?”

“Nothing. Nothing.”

Li Yao smiled and pretended to pour wine for Zhang Daniu, only to ‘accidentally’ knock his cup to the ground.

…If the other party really possessed superhuman reaction speed, he should have reflexively caught the wine cup, right?

But Zhang Daniu was truly slow to react. The wine glass fell to the ground, splashing beer foam all over his body.

“I’m sorry. I’m really sorry, Brother Niu.”

Li Yao hurriedly wiped the paper and asked, “Right, did someone mention to Brother Niu that the 400-500 chapters of are written in completely different styles? It simply seems as if they were written by two different authors, giving people a very intense feeling of tearing… I feel that from a business point of view, this kind of writing style is very damaging to one’s grades. Readers who like the style in the beginning will inevitably become impatient towards the end, while readers who like the style in the end often can’t even persist for 400-500 chapters. What did Brother Niu consider in the beginning to write in this way?”

“Don’t—don’t mention it!”

Zhang Daniu did not care about the wine glass falling to the ground at all. He slapped his thigh and said, “You won’t believe it even if I tell you. I—I don’t know what’s going on, either!

“In fact, this novel has always been written in accordance with the previous 400-500 chapters. It’s written very smoothly, and it doesn’t require much effort. There are still quite a few readers who support it, isn’t it pretty good? I originally thought that I would just keep on writing like this. In any case, I’ll just kill monsters, level up, slap faces, and while I’m at it, I’ll also pick up girls. I’m planning to find a new girl for Li Yao every time I change a map. Every time I change a map, I’ll find a new girl for Li Yao, and I’ll change another map. Hey, guess what. Two girls. Alien sisters. I guarantee the readers will cheer for them!”

“Besides, think about it. How many face-slapping, revenge, and messy plots can a girl trigger? You can find three to five, ten girls, and a few million words will be written out. Isn’t that relaxing?

“Uh…”

Li Yao felt a bit uncomfortable hearing that, but he still nodded. “That’s right.”

“Yes. I had it all planned out. The Speaker of Parliament of the federation, the empress of the imperium, the leader of the Holy Alliance is a crystal processor in the shape of a human being—it can also be a metal-featured girl. There are also beauties of the demon race, the demon race, the deities, the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and dozens of other species who are competing with Li Yao in the magnificent sea of stars for the ‘struggle over the great daos’. Just tell me if your imagination is rich or not!”

Zhang Daniu’s mouth was filled with the smell of alcohol. “In the end, when I was writing 400-500 chapters, for some reason, I caught a cold and had a high fever. It was truly the worst fever I’ve ever had in my life. It was 389 degrees for several days. No matter how I took injections or medication, it was useless. My entire body was dizzy, and I felt as if my soul had left my body. I was simply unable to tell whether I was asleep or awake, whether I was in reality or in a dream.”

“Oh?”

Li Yao’s eyes were shining so brightly that his hands were about to dig a piece of wood out of the desk. He tried to hold back his shock and said, “There’s actually such a thing? He’s actually so seriously ill! But I’ve seen Brother Niu’s updated record. It seems that he insists on updating at least two times a day. He has never stopped updating before… Do you have a draft?”

“Where did the drafts come from? They were written and sent on the spot.”

Confusion was written all over Zhang Daniu’s face, too. “To tell you the truth, I was really muddle-headed during those few days. Even I thought that I was definitely going to stop updating. I didn’t have the slightest feeling of touching the keyboard. I only felt that, indistinctly, indistinctly, it was as if a beam of light fell from the sky and shot straight into my brain. There was also a voice that sounded like a fly. “Weng weng weng weng, weng weng weng weng weng.” It rang chaotically inside my head, almost driving me crazy.

“I was in this muddle-headed state for three to five days, or maybe five to seven days, before I gradually woke up. I originally thought that I was doomed and would definitely be cursed to death by the readers. In the end, when I opened the manuscript and the website to take a look, I discovered that it was truly strange. As it turned out, I had been updating every day, and not a single day had passed!”

“So… amazing?”

Li Yao was deep in thought. “You mean to say that, even though you were unconscious, you still persisted in writing—and the things you wrote were very logical, so much so that the plot could be connected?”

“That’s right. There were even more chapters over the past few days. Three to four chapters a day. Four to five chapters a day!”

Zhang Daniu gnawed on the bones until his mouth was dripping with oil. Although he was drunk, he could not hide the triumphant look on his face. “At first, I also felt that it was a little strange. How come I didn’t know what I wrote? Later, I pondered for a few days and finally figured it out. As the saying goes, ‘what you think in the day, you dream in the night’. I guess it’s because I’m too passionate about the sci-fi industry, too passionate about literary creation, too passionate about the universe, the starry sky, and the future. Therefore, a lot of novel plots and character descriptions have been stored in my subconsciousness. Writing has become an instinct. It’s like breathing and heartbeat. I don’t need to use my brain at all. Hahahaha!”

“It’s not that I’m boasting, but there are precedents… I remember there was a scientist. I don’t know which country he was from, but he was the one who discovered the periodic table of the elements. It was said that he racked his brain during the day, but no matter how hard he tried, he couldn’t come up with anything about the periodic table of the elements. In the end, he had a dream at night. Hey, he had a flash of inspiration, and he had a dream all of a sudden!”

“Of course, I can’t be compared to other scientists, but that’s what I’m talking about. Dreams are all human subconsciousness, aren’t they?

“Is it… right?”

Li Yao said, “Your experience is like a ‘heavenly gift’. It is too legendary. Can you tell me more about it? Where did you write when you were in a coma because of a high fever?”

“Let me see. It seems to be Huangpu Shiyi, the first appearance of the Immortal Cultivators of the Temple of Immortals.”

Zhang Daniu thought hard for a long time. “Do you still remember? In the plot, a Refiners’ Meeting of the Flying Star Sector came to an abrupt end. It ended so suddenly that I felt that I was being torn apart. It was during those few days that I started having a fever. It was not said that I would be completely cured within a week. I was tortured for two to three months on and off. My brain did not feel right. It was not until I reached Iron Plateau, Yan Xibei, the Blood Stripe Virus, and the ‘apocalyptic strike’ that my body was fully recovered.

“No wonder.”

Li Yao nodded and said, “At first, I was wondering why the Temple of Immortals and the Immortal Cultivators showed up out of nowhere and ended everything with an explosion when I could tell that you were trying to describe how the protagonist secured the championship in detail in the Refiners’ Congress of the Flying Star Sector. The whole story felt weird. As it turned out, you had such a serious fever. That explains a lot.

“Besides, starting from the Temple of Immortals, to Iron Plateau, to Yan Xibei, and to the strike of the apocalypse, it was almost the turning point of the entire novel. Since then, the pattern and the plot have changed. It seems that you have… awakened something during your fever and coma.

“Awakening? You don’t say. That’s a good word!”

Zhang Daniu slapped the table and said, “After I completely woke up, it’s not that I didn’t think of taking back the plot and continuing according to my original plan—dozens of girls. But on one hand, I’ve already gone too far in this plot line. On the other hand, how can I put it? It’s like something in my brain has awakened. My hands have been controlled. Before I could react, I’ve manipulated the characters in the novel to spurt out dozens of chapters of dialogue. I’ve created a lot of mysteries of the universe, discussions of civilizations, and so on. Many readers who don’t know the truth say that I’m writing too slowly, but they don’t know that I don’t want to. I’m also very troubled!”

Are my hands… out of control?

Li Yao felt more and more that he had made the right choice to fool the author. The clues provided by the author while he was drunk were too valuable.

However, that was not enough to answer all his questions.

“Brother Niu, if I may ask.”

Li Yao continued to bring the beer mixed with white wine to Zhang Daniu’s mouth. “Are you going to continue writing the story of 40,000 Years of Cultivation? Many readers are looking forward to it. Really!”

Although Zhang Daniu was drunk, the question still made him clearly embarrassed.

“Well, how should I put it? Everybody is talking so happily. I don’t want to lie to you.”

Zhang Daniu heaved a long sigh. He looked at the bone in his hand and said, “It’s hard to explain in a long time!”

“What?”

Li Yao’s heart was beating fast, “Don’t tell me Brother Niu is really going to give up? Don’t get me wrong, I don’t mean anything else. I really like this novel and want to see the rest of the story. Aren’t you writing quite well? Even if you suffered a minor injury, I can see that your hand has almost recovered. It shouldn’t affect your typing, right? If you really can’t, there’s also the voice input method. It would be a pity if you really give up!”

Chapter 3183: Sneaking Into The Night With The Wind

“What’s the use of pity? If it doesn’t make money, even a god can’t do anything about it!”

Zhang Daniu was even drunker. His eyes bulged as he tapped the clay pot with a bone. He spoke incoherently, “Speaking of which, being an online writer is really the most bitter profession in the world. It can’t even be called a profession at all. It’s just that… In the early years, it was said that those who sold their art under the overpass were of the lower class. It was lively. There were many who applauded and shouted. There were few who had the support of real money. When the Emperor passed away, he didn’t even let you sell it. Who knows, he might have violated some taboo. This thing… how can it be justified?”

“As you said, the feeling of being torn apart before and after is especially serious. I like to watch the front, not the back. I like to watch the back, and I don’t like to watch the front. This saying is completely correct. That’s why, ever since the 400-500 chapters changed styles, my grades have been sliding down. At the very least, my family wouldn’t be hungry if I ate to my heart’s content, and I could also pay rent. Recently, my grades have been getting worse and worse.

“I also know that I can’t write like this. Readers read books to relax, to have fun, and to have fun. Who would be willing to spend money to watch you sit there and discuss the justice of dozens of chapters? Society, the future, and so on, right? So if I continue writing like this, I will definitely die. I’ve tried to recycle it several times, but I can’t control myself!”

“Well…”

Li Yao didn’t expect it to be like this. He nodded his head and said, “Even a penny can’t kill a hero!”

“Right?”

Zhang Daniu said, “So, it’s not just a matter of these two days. Ever since my style has changed and my grades have plummeted, I’ve been thinking of finding a suitable and decent way to end this book. For example, let me think about it. After the protagonist finishes fighting a boss, he will suddenly wake up with a jolt and realize that everything he’s been doing is a dream. Then, he will forget everything that happened in the dream and focus on his studies every day. What do you think of this idea?”

“…”

Li Yao said, “I think you will be killed by the readers.”

“Exactly. That’s why I’ve been wondering how I should clean up the mess, too!”

Zhang Daniu said, “Anyways, it doesn’t matter how this book ends. I’m already thinking about the next book. I’m absolutely not going to make this book’s mistake. I’m going to train and level up. I’m not going to let go of the flag of face slapping and girls chasing. I’m going to walk on the path of fantasy, and I’m going to walk on the path of interstellar cultivation. In short, I can’t continue writing this damn science fiction.”

“Why?”

Li Yao was dazed. “Isn’t science fiction good?”

“Not good. Of course not good.”

Zhang Daniu shook his head and said, “You know it, right? The most important thing about webnovels is desire. Whether it is cultivation, face-slapping, picking up girls, or the courtship of female fans, it doesn’t matter what kind of skin you’re wearing. It doesn’t matter what kind of theme you’re talking about. The bottom line is that you have to satisfy the unspeakable desires in the depths of human nature. Whoever can precisely control the desires of the readers and scratch that itching meat will win!”

“But science fiction… Oops. How do you say science fiction? If it is really written well, it must be anti-humanity, anti-desire, or rather, it must surpass human nature and desire. The further it is from human nature and desire, the more brilliant it will be. You can’t set two bizarre alien species to fall in love on an incredible planet. What kind of thing is that?

“Do you understand now? To write online novels, you need to make money. You need to set your sights on desire and ruthlessly attack it. You need to satisfy what the readers care about the most and what they are most familiar with. But to write a good science fiction novel, you need to be anti-human, anti-desire, and anti-superhuman. You need to use things that the readers are completely unfamiliar with and have never thought of to create a sense of surprise. These two natural contradictions are irreconcilable. The conclusion is that only a fool would write science fiction online!”

“Wait—”

Li Yao said, “Brother Niu, don’t you love science fiction and want to contribute to our country’s science fiction industry?”

Zhang Daniu was stunned for a moment. He chuckled and said, “That may be the case, but haven’t I not paid the rent for the next quarter? I’d better love the evil money and myself first. When the day comes when I can make a name for myself, when I’m rich, when I have a nice car, and when I have five beautiful girls, I’ll come back and contribute to the science fiction industry, alright?”

Li Yao had nothing to say.

“Besides, how should I put it?

Zhang Daniu continued, “Now that you’ve asked this, I have nothing to hide. This is probably God’s will—even God doesn’t want me to continue writing this book.”

“Oh?”

Li Yao’s eyes turned sharp again. “How so?”

“That’s right. The past two months have not been smooth at all. I feel that I can’t even drink cold water.”

Zhang Daniu gestured and said, “It’s not that it’s been a month or two. It’s just that things haven’t been going well ever since changed its style. When I was shopping in the neighborhood, a flower pot almost fell off and hit my head. When I went to walk around in the garden, I ended up being f*cking bitten by a dog. When I was sleeping at home, the gas valve broke down for some reason. Thankfully, there was no gas poisoning. Just last month, my computer broke down three times for no reason. Every time it was repaired, it broke down. Every time it was repaired, it broke down. The repair shop said that they had never encountered such a thing before… Can you imagine how determined and persistent I was to maintain a normal update under such circumstances?”

“Is there such a weird thing?”

Li Yao secretly clenched his fists under the table and said, “Then, then has Brother Niu been involved in any dangerous accidents recently, such as… being hit by a car?”

“Not really.”

Zhang Daniu shook his head and said, “I was already angry enough when I encountered so many annoying things. I didn’t go anywhere at home and the car didn’t hit me. Anyway, I was in a trance for quite a while. That day, when I went to the toilet, I finally couldn’t bear it any longer and wanted to give up. However, after writing for such a long time, I have feelings for it. So, I decided to throw a coin to decide whether to continue writing or to become a eunuch. If I was facing the front, I would become a eunuch. If I was facing the back, I would rot. If the coin was standing up, I would continue writing. As a result, just as I threw the coin into the air, my foot slipped and I dropped my hand. Tell me, if this isn’t the work of the heavens, then what is it?”

“At that time, I was in so much pain that tears were rolling down my face. I was really disheartened and gave up on myself. So, I simply cursed, ‘What stupid book is this? I’m not writing any more!’

“In the end, it was very strange. I seemed to have gotten rid of something. A cool breeze disappeared from my body. Although my hands still hurt in the next few days, the weird things never happened again. I ate, slept, and felt refreshed. Except for the fact that I had no money, everything else was good.

“So, now you’re asking me whether or not to write ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’. I can’t lie to you about that. I really don’t know!”

Zhang Daniu was already dead drunk. He kept on slipping under the table.

Otherwise, he wouldn’t have told Li Yao the truth.

“However, yesterday, I heard from your junior sister, Little Rabbit, that many people still like to read this book and are silently supporting Brother Niu. How should I put it? I feel sorry for everyone.”

Zhang Daniu slumped in his chair. “In fact, it’s not that bad. Today, it was you who came. If it was really your little junior sister, I—I really don’t know how I would face her. She was about to give up. How could I have the face to go to your place to be a guest!? Then, do you really have many university students who like to read my books?”

“Yes.”

Li Yao nodded. “They are all college students with long hair and swaying skirts.”

“How about…”

Zhang Daniu’s eyes were somewhat sluggish. “For the sake of these enthusiastic readers who support me, I will be a fool again and continue writing?”

“Well—”

If it were before a meal, Li Yao would definitely not hesitate to coax the author to continue writing.

But as it turned out, if the accident continued, there was no telling what would happen next.

Li Yao felt extremely uncomfortable. He would not allow himself to push the author into the fire pit.

“Well, it’s still up to you, Brother Niu. I will respect your choice no matter what.”

Li Yao said, “However, you’ve written the outline, including the Vulture Plan and the secrets of Earth, haven’t you, Brother Niu?”

“…Yes, yes!”

Zhang Daniu was already so drunk that he didn’t know whether to nod or shake his head, “I—I have everything. However, these are all my secrets. Even if this novel is of no use to me, I can still continue to use the next novel. Come, let’s not talk about these unhappy things. Continue drinking, drink!”

“Brother Niu, you’re drunk.”

Li Yao said, “Let me take you home.”

Zhang Daniu struggled for a while. Naturally, he was no match for the stocky Li Yao.

The house he rented was in the old neighborhood next to the casserole bone kiln. It took him three tries to find the right door.

When he entered the door, he was so drunk that he couldn’t even answer the keyhole.

Li Yao took the opportunity to observe the layout of the room.

It was a typical old house decorated in the style of the last century. One room and one living room were packed with yellowing books and newspapers. It was perfect for one person.

There was barely any furniture or appliances in the room. On the old, mottled desk was a battered laptop, which was Li Yao’s target.

Over the course of the meal, Li Yao basically understood Zhang Daniu’s personality. This was a rather casual fellow. It was unlikely that he would place the outline of a novel in an encrypted USB drive or a safe.

However, Li Yao did not steal the computer directly.

“Brother Niu, I’m leaving. Let’s contact each other via WeChat. I’ll see you when I’m free!”

Li Yao noticed that the ventilation window of the toilet was not locked, which was enough.

After making sure that the other party had heard his words, he closed the door loudly from the outside, went downstairs, and walked out of the neighborhood with his hands in his pockets.

Although it was an old neighborhood, there were still some surveillance cameras on the side of the road. When he helped Zhang Daniu back just now, he had already been captured by the surveillance cameras, so he had to take another video of him leaving the neighborhood.

Chapter 3184: Writing Heroes

Zhang Daniu’s balcony window was right above him. He was only one step away from the desk where he was working. Within five seconds, he would take away the computer quietly, find a place to copy all the files inside, and put the computer back quietly. Nobody would notice anything wrong.

Then, Li Yao would be able to search through the files slowly and find the outline of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’. He would be able to figure out the truth of the Vulture Plan and the secrets behind ‘Earth’. Chances were that he would be able to unlock even more powerful ‘superpowers’ or, according to the book, ‘superpowers’!

However, when Li Yao was about to leap forward, the muscles all over his body suddenly tightened.

No.

His pupils suddenly constricted to the size of two needle tips, radiating with vigilance.

He heard a heartbeat.

An unusually rapid heartbeat, more than 200 beats per minute, came from Zhang Daniu’s bedroom. It sounded both like an intense drumming and like a racing engine in the dead of night.

It was impossible.

Although alcohol could stimulate the heartbeat and blood circulation of the human body, it was impossible to maintain a heartbeat of 200 beats per minute for a long time—this was a phenomenon that could only occur when one was overloaded with intense exercise!

Li Yao was very sure that when he left, Zhang Daniu was already fast asleep on the bed. His heart was beating fast, but it was still within the normal range. There was no way that such a weird phenomenon could happen.

Besides, other than his heartbeat, Li Yao seemed to hear squeaking noises coming from the top of his heart, which should be Zhang Daniu’s head, as if the author’s head was full of complicated gears that were spinning at the highest speed because of mysterious forces!

“Is he drunk?”

Li Yao’s heart tightened. It would be terrible if an innocent victim was poisoned because of him.

“Ding! Ding! Ding!”

Right then, Li Yao heard weird noises inside the room, as if coins were falling to the ground one after another.

“Is he awake?”

Li Yao could not hold back his curiosity any longer. He stuck his head out of the window and peered inside.

Zhang Daniu was indeed awake. He was sitting on the edge of the bed and looking at the ground.

But he didn’t look like he was awake. His eyes were deeply hollowed, as if they had been hollowed all the way to his brain. His nostrils were expanding exaggeratedly, and he was breathing fast. There was a mysterious smile on his face, as if he had fallen into an invisible devil’s den. He was mumbling something that nobody knew.

There was an open piggy bank on the bedside table. He was holding a handful of coins in his hands. Twisting the coins with his fingers, he flicked them up high before they fell to the ground one by one. Then he lowered his head and looked at the coins.

Li Yao stared at his lips for a long time and finally figured out that he was mumbling, “Why can’t I stand up? How weird!”

Bullshit.

Li Yao thought that they were either flowers or words. How could they stand up?

Very soon, all the coins were gone.

Zhang Daniu’s hands were empty. He stared blankly for a long time before he suddenly picked up a coin, placed it on the bedside table, and carefully stood up.

Looking at the standing coin, he finally let out a strange, satisfied laugh. Then, he staggered to the desk and turned on the computer with trembling hands.

He tilted his head and stared at the computer for a long time. The corners of his eyes and his mouth were cramping crazily. His appearance was extremely appalling.

“What is he doing exactly?”

Li Yao was confused. “Is he drunk or not? Is he asleep or sober, or is he… sleepwalking?”

That’s right. A bolt of lightning flashed through Li Yao’s mind. The other party was indeed sleepwalking. Everything was an unconscious reflex.

“Squeak—”

Hardly had the thought occurred to him when Li Yao ‘heard’ an extremely high-pitched shriek coming from Zhang Daniu’s brain. It sounded like infrasonic or ultrasonic waves. Normal people would not be able to hear it, but it would cause intense stimulation to the brain nerves. Li Yao was so scared that he almost fell off the balcony on the fifth floor.

Then, Zhang Daniu’s hand moved.

He did not look like someone who was drunk or sleepwalking at all. He was simply an extremely efficient typewriter. At the beginning, the sound of typing was still “Dada Dada”, but very soon, it turned into “Sha Sha Sha Sha”. After 3-5 minutes of “warm-up”, the sounds of the dozen or so characters typing mixed together and turned into continuous, continuous noise.

His fingers did not look like the fingers of a human being at all. They were more like bullets from a machine gun that were savaging the poor keyboard.

In the dark night, the dim lights, the weird smile, and the weird expression of the author, who was madly typing on the computer while sleepwalking, and the gecko-like superpowered person hanging outside the window… Even Li Yao himself didn’t know what was going on with this scene that looked like the opening scene of a horror movie.

And the most terrifying thing was…

Li Yao’s eyes were sharp. From his angle, he was able to see Zhang Daniu’s computer screen. He discovered that the author was not typing randomly on the keyboard, but had typed out a paragraph that was coherent, logical, and meaningful. He also noticed keywords such as ‘Li Yao, Ancient Saint World, Dragon Snake Space Zone’.

It was the latest chapter of ‘40,000 Years of Cultivation’. ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ had finally returned to the federation with the 11th Saint Cultivator, preparing for the final battle between the federation and the Black Wind Fleet!

Li Yao almost exclaimed.

How could it be?

It was not because the author could not type when he was drunk or sleepwalking, but because his typing speed was too fast.

It must be noted that a national-certified typist could process 200-300 words of text input task every minute. If it was a professionally trained, experienced senior typist, it was possible to process 300-500 words at most. If converted to an hour, it would be 10,000 to 15,000 words.

But that was something that could only be done once in a while when one was familiar with the script. It could only be done once in a while with the help of some speed-recording skills. It could not last long.

On the other hand, for an author to think of words, plots, and depictions in their mind, they had to be coherent and logical. At the very least, there wouldn’t be any major contradictions. Naturally, it was impossible for their writing speed to be so fast. Even by the standards of web novelists, writing at a speed of 2,000 words per hour was already their limit. Of course, some people said that they had written 6,000 or 8,000 words per hour, but that was a very rare phenomenon.

On the other hand, Zhang Daniu’s ‘hand speed’ was apparently higher than 6,000 to 8,000 words per hour. It was even higher than the input rate of more than 10,000 words per hour.

Li Yao didn’t know how fast he was. He only felt that his hands were like those in novels where they turned into grey mist. Even the words on the screen couldn’t keep up with his speed, leaving blurred shadows behind.

Moreover, Li Yao noticed that his eyes were fixed on the screen from the beginning to the end. He did not pay attention to the keyboard, nor did he look at any electronic or notebook drafts or outlines.

So, he had written more than ten thousand words in less than half an hour without a draft?

Could a normal human being really do such a thing?

Li Yao suspected that he was seeing things.

However, it was not very strange when he recalled that he had ripped a dumbbell into two halves with his bulging muscles in the dormitory yesterday.

This was a real world of superpowers.

Could it be that Zhang Daniu is the same as me and is actually a superpowered person? It’s just that his superpower is that he can write a novel at an incomparably fast speed without his consciousness being clear. He’s like… a “Writing Man”!?

“Alright, there are all kinds of strange things in the world. Since can trigger the readers’ superpowers, what’s so strange about triggering the author’s own superpowers?”

Li Yao mumbled to himself.

He had planned to steal Zhang Daniu’s computer, only to discover such a weird phenomenon by accident. There was no hurry.

Firstly, Zhang Daniu had been sitting in front of the computer the whole time as if he had gone mad. Li Yao was not sure whether or not he would turn into a terrifying bomb if he took away his computer.

Secondly, Li Yao knew that people who were sleepwalking could not be scared. If they were woken up suddenly, their soul might be hurt.

Li Yao could only patiently curl up outside the window and wait for the author to finish writing.

But after waiting for two hours, Zhang Daniu’s speed still showed no signs of slowing down. The laptop was almost smoking because of his attacks.

This did explain one question—Zhang Daniu had once said that his own computer often broke down. He had sent it to be repaired several times, and every time, it would break down.

Bullsh*t. If the keyboard was savaged at this intensity, perhaps other than typing crazily, there would also be subsonic waves, ultrasonic waves, and other strange interference waves. How could the computer not break down?

Thankfully, it was in the middle of the night and no one was passing by the neighborhood. Otherwise, Li Yao would definitely have been discovered after hanging outside the window for so long.

Even so, his arms were sore, his stomach was empty, and his internal organs were all jumbled up.

Not good!

When the first star appeared in front of his eyes, Li Yao felt that he was running out of strength.

Even those with superpowers had to abide by the law of conservation of energy. His body had undergone tremendous changes over the past few days. The strengthening of every cell was at the cost of absorbing tremendous nutrition. Right now, his appetite was more than ten times that of a normal person.

Last night, when he was having dinner with the author, although he had ordered a big pot of meat and bones that could satisfy two or three muscular men, it was far from enough for Li Yao’s bottomless stomach.

At that time, there were too many people and too many eyes. It was impossible for Li Yao to order 30-50 pots of meat and bones in one breath. Later, he thought that he could finish the mission in five minutes, but he didn’t expect that he would need to replenish his nutrition in advance. At this moment, his stomach was rumbling like thunder. He was hungry in an instant.

Chapter 3185: Dream Traveler

“According to the novels, things such as ‘superpowers’ or ‘techniques’ are double-edged swords. The strengthening of muscles and brains, the enhancement of computational ability, and the release of intense brainwaves all require the support of endless energy. The cells of the supernaturals who have been ‘reborn’ are all ferocious monsters who will devour their masters’ bodies without sufficient nutrition.

Li Yao thought to himself, I feel like I’m burning my life force right now.

In any case, judging from the author’s appearance, he wouldn’t be able to stop for a while. It was impossible for Li Yao to hang on the outer wall all the time. Although it was late at night, there would always be residents coming home from the night shift. It would be very awkward if he was discovered.

Li Yao evaluated his combat ability.

Although he could tear the dumbbells with his bare hands when he was at the peak of his physical strength, he did not feel that he could fight against a strong police station when he was hungry.

Why don’t you go out and replenish your energy first?

He tiptoed back the way he had come and walked far away from the neighborhood before he returned to the main road. He found a 24-hour convenience store and bought five boxes of chocolate peanut candies and more than ten cans of functional drinks. Then, he found a quiet and uninhabited place in a poor street and stuffed the chocolate peanut candies and drinks down his throat. Only then did he feel a little better.

Looking at the overdraft limit on his phone, Li Yao secretly sighed.

He hoped that all the mysteries could be solved as soon as possible. At the very least, someone could tell him what was going on. Otherwise, just absorbing so many high-nutrition substances every day would be too much for his wallet.

It was around two o’clock in the middle of the night. Li Yao was not in a hurry to steal the outline. He planned to wait until it was the darkest before dawn, which was also the time when people were the most tired. It seemed that the author was not made of iron and needed to sleep, right?

Tonight, he was not planning to return to school. While there was no one around, he would pay attention to the news of the T3 Game Show.

For some reason, Li Yao felt that the shooting was quite weird and had a lot to do with the death of the famous novelist and the mangaka.

When he logged into the game forum again, the news about the shooting gradually spread.

There were four shooters in total. Three of them had been killed in the crossfire with the police, and one of them had been captured because of an injury. It was quite unexpected. According to the latest investigation, the four shooters were all white men with clean backgrounds, normal jobs, and happy families. They had nothing to do with extremist organizations and did not look like crazy terrorists at all.

However, when the authorities searched their comments on social media carefully, they discovered that they had joined an organization named ‘Apocalypse’. They believed that ‘Apocalypse Arrives 2018’ was probably a replica of ‘2012, Armageddon’. It was none other than the apocalypse and the final trial at the end of 2018. A catastrophe that was about to restart the entire world was inevitable, and only by helping the heavens harvest the souls of the addicts would they have the opportunity to be redeemed and live a brighter and happier life in the post-apocalyptic world.

It was unclear whether the unknown ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ existed or was merely a disguise for the more famous extremist organizations.

However, dozens of bloody records were added to the list of victims of the T3 game exhibitions.

In the production team of the game ‘Star Ring—Final Battle’, other than ‘Father of Star Ring’ Garriott, there were also two important producers who declared their death in the hospital. They were a script writer and a scenario architect. They were both Garriott’s most trusted assistants and friends who had followed him for more than ten years.

/ .

Considering that the progress of ‘Star Ring: The Final Battle’ was only around 70%, the departure of the three heavyweight producers meant that the game would be out of stock indefinitely. Even if the game was completed one day, the final version would certainly be very different from what the ‘Father of Star Ring’ had envisioned.

It was a pity that nobody knew what the ‘final battle’ that the ‘Father of the Ring’ envisioned in the beginning would be like.

Li Yao found a mobile restaurant that was open late at night and ordered a large bowl of fried noodles with eggs, sausages, and slag. He quickly refreshed the webpage while he ate.

He browsed through all the major gaming forums, animation forums, and fantasy literature forums, hoping to find news of new producers, cartoonists, or novelists suffering accidents.

So far, there had been no news as sensational as the shooting at the T3 Game Exposition, but Li Yao had found a very interesting post by searching the keywords.

It was a post posted on a large comprehensive Q&A website. In fact, it was a response to a question. The question was, “How to comment on the departure of ‘Drunk Stardust’?

Li Yao didn’t know anyone named ‘Drunk Star Dust’. He searched the relevant articles and found out that it was an unknown web novelist who had left the world quietly in the middle of the night due to a heart attack.

Presumably, this question was raised by a loyal reader of ‘Drunk Star Dust’.

It was a pity that the web novelist was not very famous. Except for a few readers, he didn’t receive many responses. The answers were mostly about the condolences of the author and the appreciation of his work. They were not of much help to Li Yao.

But one of the answers caught Li Yao’s attention.

“I have never seen the work of ‘Drunk Stardust’, but I am not surprised to hear of his death. Do you think ‘Drunk Stardust’ is just the first?

“Let me tell you—I’m a crazy fan of fantasy novels, comic books, and games. Over the past three years, I’ve collected more than a hundred accidental or fatal accidents among the domestic and foreign producers of fiction, comic books, and fantasy entertainment. Here’s the list!”

The answerer released a list of names of the creators in the field, followed by their age and cause of death.

With a quick glance, Li Yao estimated that the ratio of patients both domestically and internationally was about 50%. The ratio of patients to accidents was also 50%. However, more than half of the patients were under fifty. It was indeed a bit shocking.

However, most of the creators of the fantasy works were not very famous. Some of them, such as ‘Drunk Star Dust’, were known by only a few people in their respective fields. Accidents were happening to almost ten billion people in the world every day. Nobody had ever linked their deaths together before.

“You may think that I’m crazy, but please believe me. I’m not crazy. I’m absolutely clear-headed. The voice inside my head will not lie to me.

The answerer continued, “The deaths of so many writers, cartoonists, and producers are definitely not due to accidents or diseases, but because some mysterious force is manipulating everything and directing a tragic death.

“I don’t know what this force is, nor do I know what its purpose is, but I believe that this is just the beginning. The reason is simple. The creators who have passed away over the past three years do not have enough fame and influence. This is just a few drops of cold rain before a storm.

“Soon, very soon, even more important creators, some masters who created bizarre fantasy worlds for us, will leave. Believe me, no matter how normal they seem, their deaths are accidents, schemes, and part of an enormous scheme!

The answer had dozens of comments, which was quite popular under the question.

However, most of them expressed their objection and even cursed the answerers.

Many people were cursing the responders for playing to the gallery and using the deceased to attract attention. Whether it was the writer, the cartoonist, or the producer, they had been working day and night for a long time. The mental pressure was high, and the odds of sudden illness and chronic disease were much higher than those of normal professions. What could the deaths of more than a hundred people worldwide say about three years?

The conclusion that ‘a Master will pass away very soon’ was even more shameless. Since he was known as a Master, it was only natural that he earned his fame and fortune. He was too old to be surprised by the death of a Master in his sixties.

In short, no one believed the speaker’s words. Most people were cursing him.

Li Yao, on the other hand, became interested in the answer because he discovered that the time of the answer was exactly eight days ago, which was also the day of the Pegasus Lake International Animation Festival.

While the respondent was writing the message, in the west, Gilson, the author of ‘Blood and Sand’, a famous master of fantasy literature, was holding his chest and falling to the ground in pain. In the east, the famous cartoonist Gao Wuyi, who had painted ‘Strong Colonial Hunters’, was humming a cheerful tune and walking on the bloody road to hell.

Was this a coincidence?

Li Yao paid special attention to the answerer’s account. The answerer had named himself ‘Dream Traveler’.

Clicking on his name, one would be able to see all the answers he had left on this Q&A website. There were quite a few pages.

It was his last reply.

The penultimate rule, on the other hand, was a response to another question, ‘What happened to you?’

In this reply, Dream Traveler wrote, “When I was in the third year of primary school, I went swimming in the reservoir next to my grandma’s house and drowned for at least half an hour. From then on, I became a Dream Traveler.

“I know that no one will believe it. Anyway, ‘Do You Know’ has long become a temple for braggarts and posers, but I don’t care whether you believe it or not. I’m just telling my story.

“I have a feeling that ‘they’ will arrive soon. If I don’t talk about it now, I might not have a chance to open my mouth in a few days just like those people.

Chapter 3186: Past Life Or Parallel World?

“Who are ‘they’?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment.

It was not hard to tell that Traveler Meng did not receive a good education. His words were somewhat incoherent, but the straightforward way he wrote attracted Li Yao’s attention immediately.

“When I fell into the reservoir, I struggled hard and choked on a few mouthfuls of water. My body was cold, but I felt that my stomach was on fire. It was extremely uncomfortable.

Dream Traveler wrote, “But very soon, I sank into the depths of darkness. I felt that I was a mosquito that had been embedded in amber for countless years.

“Some people say that, when a person is on the verge of death, their brain cells will go through their last vigorous activity, allowing the memories of a lifetime to flash in front of their eyes like a racing lantern. I was in the same situation at that time. However, what flashed in front of my eyes, other than myself in this life, were my past life and even my past life.

“At that time, I was just a primary school student. I had always lived in the small town of my hometown. At most, I had seen the colorful and bizarre scenes of the big cities from the fuzzy black-and-white televisions. However, when I was entangled in my dying memories, I ‘saw’ myself turn into a well-dressed white-collar worker who was working in the big city’s CBD. In the office, I racked my brains, schemed against each other, and sharpened my head as I climbed upwards—it must be noted that when I was in the third year of primary school, even the capital city and the national metropolises did not have a CBD. And in the white-collar life that I ‘recalled’, there were many details of office politics that a primary school student could never imagine.

“While I was hesitating and confused, the memory suddenly broke apart. I seemed to have become a different person. I was no longer a white-collar elite in the big city of CBD, but a mechanical worker in a certain western resource city. The new ‘memories’ are even weirder. I remember clearly many details about the manufacturing and operation of the machines, as well as the natural style of the western city, including the taste and cooking methods of the local specialties. They were all very unpopular. In the era when transportation was inconvenient and information was scarce, a primary school student living in a third-tier town in the southeast had absolutely no access to relevant information.

“I don’t know if I wrote it clearly enough. Can you understand what I mean?
