“That is to say, a third-grade elementary school student, a child no older than ten years old, fell into the reservoir. When he was seriously drowning and on the verge of death, he should have been able to recall the short ten years of his life. But for some reason, I broke through the boundary between ‘previous life’ and ‘this life’ and saw the scenes where I was a white collar of the CBD in my previous life and the scenes where I was a mechanical worker in my previous life. These scenes were incomparably clear, and the delicacies in the scenes were all delicious and flavorful. To this day, I still can’t forget everything that I ‘saw’ at that time, as well as the scent that I ‘smelled’.

“When I woke up and my soul returned to the body of a ten-year-old elementary school student, I found myself lying in bed at home with my mother, who could not cry even if she wanted to. It was not until later that I learned that it had been more than half an hour since I was rescued from the reservoir. My face was blue, and my hands and feet were pale. I had lost my breath a long time ago. It took me more than half a month to save my life, and I didn’t wake up until an entire month later.

“But this is not the end.

“Since then, although my body has recovered, my mind has been in a trance. I can often see things that ordinary people can’t see and hear sounds that ordinary people can’t hear. In the dead of night, when I enter the dream, I often return to the two dreams that I’ve had hundreds of times. Or rather, I return to the memories of my previous life and my previous life.

/ .

“As I grew older and my brain gradually matured, the past life and the previous life in the dream became clearer and clearer. I even had a feeling that I was very clear that I was dreaming, and the world in the dream was the world of my previous life and the previous life. This was a ‘clear dream’, and I was a traveler traveling through the dream—the memories of my previous life.

“When I was sixteen, my third dream, which was the memory of my previous life, was awakened.

“It seemed to be a very dark and miserable world. Wars, famine, and chaos were everywhere. Like most people, I bathed in smoke and cannon fire in the army, and after I was wounded and maimed in the battlefield, I returned to the military factories to provide weapons and ammunition for the frontline. Just like that, I lived a bleak life. In the end, a million super-large bombs blossomed in the sky of the planet, and a light that was even more intense than the solar storm cleansed the entire world.

“When my adolescence came to an end, when I was 18 or 19 years old and in my early twenties, I had a total of seven dreams of my previous life. Other than that, there were also some bizarre, mottled dreams that seemed to be wandering in the sea of stars. They were very sci-fi and futuristic. But these strange dreams were not as clear as the memories of my previous life. It was hard to say what they actually meant.

“In short, the memories of my seven lives have something in common:

“Firstly, they all took place on Earth. Although the general trend and the details are different every time, such as the names of countries and cities, the social outlook, and the international political situation, they are basically limited to the 1970s and 1980s to the 1920s and 1930s. The basic language, technology, and civilization are very similar, too.

“Secondly, no matter how hard I tried or how decadent I became in every life, I was unable to change the course of the entire world. In the 21st century, the world went from prosperity to decline, from stability to collapse, from peace to war. Hurricanes, earthquakes, tsunamis, ozone hollows, glaciers melting, drastic changes in solar activity, mutated plagues, extremism, fanatical politicians wielding nuclear weapons… All of these pushed the entire world to the brink of destruction.

“Thirdly, I don’t know whether it was a coincidence or not, but I remember clearly in every memory of my life that the collapse of the world began after the accidental death of a batch of science fiction, fantasy, and fantasy novelists, cartoonists, movie producers, and game producers. At first, it was the ‘creators’ who used their imagination to create an absurd illusory world. Then, it was the astrophysicists who were more imaginative but not easily understood by the public. Then, it was the scientists in various fields and the cutting-edge scholars who represented the limits of human intelligence… They were like dominoes that had been pushed down. No force could stop their complete collapse.

“If that’s the case, the scenes in my dream cannot be described as ‘past life’.

“Because the memories of my previous life should be one-sided, one-dimensional, and not parallel—my ‘this life’ was born in the 1970s and 1980s, so my previous life should have been born in the early 20th century, and my previous life should have been born in the late 19th century. This is the most reasonable.

“Why do I see seven different ‘me’ in my dreams, appearing on seven Earths that are subtly different but have equally lackluster endings—around the 21st century?

“Since I was ten years old, the question has baffled me for the first half of my life. I tried to find the answer in countless science fiction, philosophy, physics, and absurd books, but to no avail. I can only say that what happened to me is more like a ‘parallel world’ mentioned in some science fiction novels and movies, or even a concept of ‘reincarnation’.

“That is to say, there is not just one Earth, but at least seven—no, eight Earths. The eight Earths are subtly different, but their development paths are generally the same. The Earths that were continuously reincarnating between the 1970s and 1980s to the 1920s and 1930s are like a self-recycling computer game. Every time an Earth is on the verge of destruction in the 1920s and 1930s, it will be ‘rebooted’ back to the 1970s and 1980s, and everyone living on the Earth will have their memories of their previous life erased and enter a new cycle in a brand new posture. As for me, perhaps because the memories of my previous life were not completely erased, and also because I woke up from the extreme near-death experience of drowning, I actually saw the scene of the previous cycle and the previous cycle.”

“I don’t know why this is happening. What is the significance of such a ‘reincarnation’? I only know that it is not far away from the end of our cycle, because the creators of the fantasy worlds in our world are gradually dying, too. This is the horn of doom.

“Yes, you can say that it was all my nonsense and the imagination of a neurasthenic. But before you mock and attack me, take five minutes to think about it and look at the other answers below the question. I’m not the only one who has encountered such a weird ‘supernatural event’. Thousands of other people have experienced weird things that science cannot explain.

“Even you, who are reading the threads, don’t have similar experiences?

“Tell me, have you ever been to a completely strange place, only to vaguely remember the nearby streets and sceneries as if you had been here in your ‘past life’?

“Did you do something or say something, only to feel that the same thing had been done or said somewhere else?

“Have you ever seen a stranger who suddenly gave you a very familiar feeling, as if you used to have a very close relationship, but in fact, you never had any interactions with him, unless it was… in your previous life?

“Do you ever feel that you’ve lost countless important days in your life? Do you feel that many of the memories that you had at that time are mottled and hazy? Do you feel that you’ve forgotten the most important things in your life?

Chapter 3187: Earth'S Bug

“What the guy said… makes sense!”

After hearing ‘Dream Traveler’s’ words, Li Yao suddenly remembered that he had had a lot of weird dreams ever since he was a little kid—not just the T-rex who had put on lipstick, the yin-yang man who had a bright silver gun under his crotch, the pregnant woman who had given birth to two computers, and so on. In some of the other dreams, he seemed to be a middle school student who was sitting in a classroom taking a test. The question of the test was very simple, which was to write the full name of the words ‘England, Europe’, and so on. But every time, he would answer wrong, because the full name of ‘England, Europe’ was different every time.

Is it possible that there were almost ten different versions of Earth in my previous life?

Li Yao mumbled to himself, “But, why were the Dream Traveler’s previous life, past life, and past life in different professions while I was a middle school student in the dream?”

Li Yao continued reading patiently.

That was the end of the answer. But after two days, Dream Traveler changed the answer and added a few more lines, which seemed to be his response to the netizens’ comments.

“You all say that this kind of phenomenon is not strange. There has long been a reasonable explanation in science. It is called ‘feelings’. It is a small defect in the evolution of the human brain. When short-term memories are transcribed into long-term memories, there will be interference and confusion. Not bad. A perfect explanation.

“Including the question below—’What kind of supernatural events have you experienced?’, you’ve offered plausible explanations for all the answers. They are either coincidences, hallucinations, chronic memory confusion, or even mass hysteria. In short, you just don’t want to believe, or rather, don’t dare to believe, the existence of supernatural events. You can only bury your head in the sand named science and deceive yourselves.

/ .

“But has it ever occurred to you that the truth of the ‘feelings’ is not like that? What if the ‘previous life’, the ‘parallel world’, or the ‘reincarnation space’ really exists? What if there is a science on the scale of the universe that can completely cover the science on the scale of Earth?

“Humans are creatures who like to fool themselves.

“Even if countless people in this thread say with certainty that they have indeed encountered a supernatural event, you readers will not take it to heart at all. You will only blindly believe in the so-called ‘science’ and laugh at these ‘strange rumors’.

“True.”

“Whether they are awakened or not, whether they open their eyes or not, whether they can find the truth or not, destruction is inevitable.

When Dream Traveler wrote to this point, his pen was brimming with resentment.

The more Li Yao observed, the more serious he became. Thinking back to the weird things that had happened to him, he was even more suspicious about the authenticity of the world.

Then, it seemed that other readers expressed serious doubts about what Dream Traveler said, too. They were probably asking him, “If we are really living in the space of reincarnation, is everything, including ourselves, false?”

“I think, so I am.”

Dream Traveler replied, “We are human beings who are the spirits of all things. We are not pigs who are driven by our natural instincts. ‘Thinking’ is the only sign that we define ourselves and distinguish ourselves from everything else in the world. Even if we do live in an illusionary building in the sky, even if the earth below our feet is a beach that can be swallowed by tides at any time, and our civilization is a tower made of sand on the beach that is constantly collapsing and reshaping, our thoughts are real, and our lives are real, too.

“The tower can collapse, but even the surging tides tens of thousands of meters high cannot erase the fact that it once existed.

“On the other hand, even if I am an out-and-out lunatic and everything I say is nonsense, even if there is no such thing as reincarnation space or circulation of the Earth, even if the earth below our feet, the air we breathe, and the sun, moon, and stars in the sky are all real and the cells that make up us are also real, what if we give up our thoughts and dreams and become blindly obedient lambs, silent bricks, and ignorant pigs? What if we become a cold component on the social machine and are merely satisfied with the stimulation of our natural instincts? Are we really ‘alive’ then?

“Life or death, reality or illusion, it will always be a problem.

So much for the answer.

But Dream Traveler also left a few links that pointed to a few similar questions, including ‘Tell me about your weirdest dream’, ‘What have you experienced that made you doubt that the world is a fake or a bug’, ‘Have you ever come across any cases where ‘the gods are watching’, ‘What does the Yin-Yang Eye feel like’, ‘What does depression or schizophrenia feel like’, etc.

Li Yao flipped through the answers one by one.

As expected of the nation’s largest website for bragging and posturing, ‘Do You Know’ had a vast majority of thrilling and thrilling answers that were full of twists and turns. Its imagination was rich to the extreme. With just a little bit of editing, it was able to produce a series of very wonderful short stories. Li Yao couldn’t help but read them with great interest.

Under the question ‘What kind of supernatural events have you experienced before?’, many respondents had experienced telepathy with their deceased family members and even saw their deceased family members directly.

Some of them even swore to the heavens that there were really deities, eminent monks, and masters in their hometown who could communicate with spirits.

And below the question “What have you experienced to make you doubt whether this world is false or if there is a bug?”, the answers became all sorts of strange and bizarre, making one’s blood run cold when carefully pondering.

One of the answers left a deep impression on Li Yao.

The person who answered the question said that he was a member of a chain beverage store and liked to drink a beverage named ‘Lemon’s Ice’. The ingredients of the mixed drink included salted lemon and another mint lemon-flavored carbonated water. The pleasure of drinking a cup of it on a hot summer day was very stimulating. He had drunk dozens of cups of it in the store.

But today, he was soaked in sweat because of the scorching sun. When he went to the store to buy lemon ice cream, he discovered that there were no air bubbles or carbonated acid at all. It was like cold herbal water and couldn’t taste worse.

The main point was… when the respondent expressed his doubts to the shop assistant, the shop assistant told him that the chain store’s “Lemon Cheerful Ice” had always been mixed with salted lemon, pure water, and special herb packages. It had never been added with any carbonated drinks or sodas, so it didn’t have the slightest bit of stimulation. It focused on pure, natural, and healthy concepts.

At first, the answerer thought that the shop assistant was new or that the shop assistant had adjusted the process of making the drinks. But when he questioned the shop assistant, who was an experienced shop assistant, to the agents and trainers behind the chain stores, the answer was the same. Since the establishment of the chain store, every store in every corner of the country had the same process of making lemon ice cream. No soda or soda was added.

It was scorching hot with a temperature of almost forty degrees.

But the one who answered felt that he had fallen into an ice cave. A bone-chilling coldness was popping up from the back of his head.

“I’m a regular customer of this chain store. Over the past two years, I’ve visited this place at least forty to fifty times. Of the three times I’ve visited, I’ve ordered ‘Lemon Caltrops’ twice.”

“I remember clearly the picture of the bubbles bouncing on the lemon slices. I can feel their burning of my throat and their hissing sounds. Such a refreshing feeling is the greatest pleasure in a summer afternoon. Am I wrong about that?

“But it’s impossible for the staff to lie to me, and it’s even more impossible for the trainers in the chain store’s headquarters to lie to me. It’s true that they never provided the lemon ice cream that I remember!

“Is this a bug in my memory or a bug in the entire world? To this day, I still don’t know what is going on.”

Li Yao was dazed for a long time by the reply. He took a deep breath and continued reading the comments.

Then, he saw the comment of ‘Dream Traveler’.

“Maybe, you remembered it right, and the staff did not lie to you.

Dream Traveler said to the responder, “It’s not that your memory is bad. It’s just that your memory is too good. For some unknown reason, or rather… there is a bug in this world that has restored part of your memory cells to the state of ‘the previous life’ or ‘the previous version’.

“In your previous life, ‘Lemon Caltrops’ was indeed made with soda or soda. Maybe you liked the drink so much that it left a deep impression on your memory cells and lasted until ‘this life’. However, in this life, the Earth has undergone subtle changes in many details. For you, ‘Lemon Caltrops’ is a different approach.

“There’s no need to panic. BUGs often appear on our Earth. Look, 170,000 people have paid attention to this question. There are a total of 30,000 answers. Even if 90% of them are bullsh*t and only 10% are true, it also means that we have already observed 3,000 Earth’s BUGs, right?”

“My name is ‘Dream Traveler’. This is my personal page and my contact information. I often run into similar situations, too, and I’ve learned a thing or two about them. We can talk more about them if we have the opportunity!”

It was a new clue.

Li Yao entered Dream Traveler’s profile. The content was similar to what he had left on the Q&A website, except that it was more detailed.

However, his personal number was left on the bottom right corner of his profile page, too. He also welcomed calls from friends all over the country who were equally confused or had discovered a ‘earth bug’.

Li Yao stared at the number for a long time, hesitating whether or not he should call it.

—

The question “What kind of things have you experienced that made you doubt whether this world is false or if there is a bug” was a real question on the nation’s largest question-and-answer and posturing website. The person who had mixed the flavor of the lemon drink was also a real person. There was no way to release a link here, but if everyone was interested, they could search for it themselves. So far, there were indeed 30,000 answers, or in other words, 30,000 “earth bugs” had been discovered.

Chapter 3188: Unexplicable

Right now, it was the darkest time before dawn. Blurry mist was spreading between the sky and the earth. The entire world seemed to have been swallowed by a monster and fell into its stinky stomach. Only the lone lamp in the supper stall was resisting the intense darkness.

Dream Traveler should be sleeping. Li Yao was not sure whether or not calling him so early would make him unhappy.

However, on second thought, judging from the emotional analysis between the lines of Dream Traveler’s words, he must’ve been lonely and desperate to communicate with friends who had similar experiences.

Li Yao could not wait any longer. He made up his mind and dialed the number.

The gloomy and gloomy ringtone echoed for half a minute, but no one picked up the call. Maybe the guy had set his phone to silent mode before he went to bed.

Li Yao waited for more than ten minutes. When he was about to call again, the light above the supper stall turned off.

The sky in the east was vaguely dyed in a hideous color of blood, while weird stars were blinking in the west. Below Li Yao’s feet, the earth was shaking as if it had turned from a solid into a broken half-fluid. A mysterious and terrifying force was about to break out of the ground.

The owner and his wife as well as the customers around Li Yao were all shaking.

An earthquake?

Li Yao, the owner, and the other customers looked at each other in bewilderment, not knowing what was going on.

Jiangnan City was located in the eastern coastal region, not in the plate tectonic belt. In history, serious earthquakes were rarely seen, and in the last thirty to fifty years, not even the shadow of an earthquake had been seen.

Therefore, it was not until the shaking of the earth became more and more serious, when the pots and pans of the supper stalls collided with each other, that everybody suddenly came back to themselves.

The owner and his wife shouted and pushed the food stall to the center of the road, avoiding the skyscrapers on both sides of the road. Other than that, there seemed to be nothing else that they could do. They all lowered their heads and watched the hundreds of windows shake at the same time.

Very soon, lights were turned on in the windows of almost all the skyscrapers. Cries of chaos were echoing nonstop. Some people were leaning their heads out of the windows, half-dressed and half-asleep. Thankfully, the intensity of the earthquake was not too high, and the people’s heads were still clear. No one dared to do anything stupid like jumping down from a three- to five-story building. Quite a few people ran down the stairs, panting. They gathered in the middle of the road and in the square, looking at the clear sky in confusion.

The earthquake subsided for three to five minutes.

No, to be more precise, it was not an earthquake, but an earthquake. The epicenter of the earthquake should be very far away from Jiangnan City. Such an earthquake could not cause any substantial damage.

Since it was already dawn, the people who had gone through a false alarm were not willing to go back to sleep just like that. They gathered in twos and threes to discuss and guess where the serious earthquake had taken place. There were also some hot-blooded young men who were grinning as they stared at the girls who were thrown out in a hurry without even wearing shoes. There was not the slightest bit of tension in the air.

But for some reason, Li Yao felt cold fear.

The prophecy of Dream Traveler was coming true one by one. At first, it was the death of the creator of the fantasy world. Then, it was the earthquake and other natural disasters.

Li Yao stared at the news page on his phone and refreshed it time and time again. Sure enough, ten minutes later, a new message popped up.

“In the early hours of this morning, a 9.2-magnitude earthquake took place in the Western Pacific Ocean. The epicenter of the earthquake was very close to the Edo Bay and has already caused serious chaos in the eastern part of the islands. According to the tsunami warning issued by the Fusang Meteorological Department, there are still tsunamis at least fifteen meters high that are attacking the islands one after another. Sources have pointed out that the earthquake may be the greatest crisis that Fusang has experienced since the Second World War. It is even possible that this is just a precursor of a series of strong earthquakes!”

Since it had just happened, the news was very brief.

But Li Yao knew that the shorter the news was, the bigger it was.

It was obvious what had happened. The Fusang Islands were a series of islands located in the middle of the Pacific Ocean, but the city of Jiangnan was standing on a solid continental shelf. The earthquake that took place in the east of the islands had even affected the city of Jiangnan, which was thousands of kilometers away. Was such an earthquake really only 9.2?

The news websites had their own procedures. It was impossible for them to respond to an earthquake so fast. However, the short videos sent back by the citizens far away in Fusang were already spreading out on social media. Through the shaking videos, one could see that the super metropolis of the planet was soaked in flames and smoke. Earsplitting alarms were ringing louder and louder, as if they were back to the bombing of World War II. There were also rumors that more than half of the nuclear power plants on the east side of the island had suffered a serious nuclear accident. The radiation index was even higher than that of Chernobyl. The situation had gone out of control.

Li Yao cursed silently. He had a feeling that everything had been peaceful a moment ago, but in the blink of an eye, a storm had broken out.

Not good!

He suddenly felt a chill run down his spine as he ran desperately towards Zhang Daniu’s neighborhood.

“If—if what Dream Traveler said is true, won’t Zhang Daniu be in great danger?”

Li Yao had no time to hide himself. He dashed through the alleys like an arrow released from a bow. “Although the earthquake in the Pacific Ocean couldn’t cause much damage to Jiangnan City, the aftershock of the earthquake should be enough to kill one or two people!”

Li Yao ran into the old neighborhood.

At this time, quite a few people had already gathered on the road of the neighborhood. The elderly were light sleepers, and once they were woken up, it would be very difficult for them to fall asleep again. They simply gathered together to discuss what had happened just now, preparing to do some early tempering.

Naturally, Li Yao was no longer able to use the gecko-climbing technique in front of everyone. He could only obediently walk up the corridor to the fifth floor. Time was of the essence. The door was locked. Li Yao simply let out a low roar and kicked open the door of Zhang Daniu’s house.

Thankfully!

Zhang Daniu was still madly typing on the keyboard, letting out a series of dense sounds of impact. The earthquake a moment ago didn’t have the slightest bit of effect on him. His pair of eyes turned from deep-set to bulging, completely dyed red.

As he expected!

The bookshelf on his left was piled up with thick, heavy hardcover books and encyclopedic dictionaries that had been tilted by the earthquake. Along with the author’s exaggerated tapping movements, the angle of the tilting became more and more obvious. It was like a crumbling cliff that could collapse upon him at any moment.

Many of the hardcover books and encyclopedias were printed on copper paper. They were extremely solid and looked no different than bricks. If somebody was hit in the head, there was no telling what would happen.

“Brother Niu!”

Li Yao roared and lunged forward.

The moment he lunged over, the bookshelf, together with the encyclopedia and the hardcover books on it, indeed collapsed. The sharp edges and corners of the book went straight for the author’s temples.

“Huh?”

Zhang Daniu suddenly shivered. He was woken up by Li Yao’s shout and turned around in a trance.

“Let’s go!”

Li Yao had no time to block or hug the author. He simply jumped up and kicked the author into a corner, while he bore the consequences of the collapse of the bookshelf in the author’s place.

“Ah!”

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu screamed at the same time.

When Li Yao finally crawled out of the messy pile of books, the first thing he did was not to check his own injuries but to look at Zhang Daniu in the corner.

Thankfully, he had restrained his strength in time. Other than a black eye and a swollen face, it seemed that his brain was not hurt at all.

Zhang Daniu finally woke up.

But he was even more confused than when he was sleepwalking. He stared at Li Yao in bewilderment for a long time, and his face gradually changed from confusion to pain. He rubbed his hands, his head, and his ass hard.

“What—what is going on?”

Zhang Daniu held his head and grimaced. “My head hurts. I remember that I was drunk…”

“That’s right.”

Li Yao crawled out of the pile of books and stood in front of the author. He nodded and said, “Brother Niu, it’s quite normal for you to have a headache after a hangover.”

“Then why is my hand also in excruciating pain?”

Looking at his fingers that were as swollen as carrots, Zhang Daniu discovered that his fingernails were somewhat cracked. He asked in disbelief, “What—what did I do exactly?”

“You may not believe it.

Li Yao replied, “You’ve been writing in your sleep.”

“Is that so? Did I sleepwalk again?”

It seemed that this was not Zhang Daniu’s first time doing “writing in a dream”. He was not suspicious of this. Instead, he touched his ass and said, “Ah, my entire body hurts. It hurts so much. Especially my ass. My ass hurts a lot, too. That’s not right. When I get drunk, I can even get up my ass!”

“Uh…”

Li Yao thought for a moment. “Brother Niu, you’ve been writing in your sleep since eleven o’clock in the morning. You’ve been glued to the chair for five to six hours without moving at all. Your blood circulation is not smooth. It’s perfectly normal for you to have backache, including hip discomfort.”

“Is that so?”

Zhang Daniu scratched his ass and agreed with the theory for now. But after staring at Li Yao for a while, he suddenly remembered something else.

“You are… Oh, you are fellow student Li Yao, the senior brother of the ‘little rabbit in the sea of stars’. We drank together last night. But, hey, but as I recall, didn’t you leave last night? Let me think. You sent me home and you left. You even closed the door very hard. I have an impression that you must have left!”

Zhang Daniu reached for his phone. “What time is it? Why are you here?”

“It’s 5:30 in the morning. Brother Niu, you don’t know, but something big has happened—an earthquake!”

Li Yao said, “A 9.2-magnitude earthquake just took place in the Western Pacific Ocean. Even our city was affected by it. I was worried that something might have happened to you, so I came to check on you as soon as possible. Now, I’m in time!”

“What?”

Zhang Daniu’s eyes were wide open. He was suddenly wide awake. “Because of the earthquake in the Pacific Ocean, you came all the way from school to warn me at five o’clock in the morning… How—how did you get in?”

“Well—”

Li Yao did not know how to respond.

The two looked at each other in bewilderment. Suddenly, the muscles all over Zhang Daniu’s body tightened, and his face turned pale.

“Wait, you did go back to school last night, didn’t you? Even if you didn’t go back to school, you just dropped me off at home and left, right?”

Zhang Daniu asked in a trembling voice.

“…That’s right.”

What else could Li Yao say?

“Then, now that you’ve sent me home, if you leave—”

Zhang Daniu asked, “How did you know that I would wake up in the middle of the night to write a novel?”

Chapter 3189: Honest

Li Yao was stumped by the question. He scratched his head for a long time, not knowing how to answer it.

Cold sweat was rolling down Li Yao’s face and back. He didn’t know how to respond at all. “Well, Brother Niu, no, Teacher Niu, we are the graduating class. All the students have gone out for their internships. None of them are in school.”

The air was frozen for a moment.

The atmosphere became subtle.

Every word that Li Yao said was like an intense electric current that was flowing between Zhang Daniu’s facial muscles and nerves.

The author’s expression turned angry at first. It was an instinctive reaction of being deceived. Every pore on his body opened up as if it was a conditioned reflex, as if he was about to spurt out steam.

Then, his intense anger turned into deep disappointment. He felt so wronged that he was about to burst into tears.

But in the end, when he sorted everything out, he discovered an even more terrifying truth that made him shiver uncontrollably.

“You, you, you, you are a pervert!”

Zhang Daniu was so scared that his voice turned hoarse. He didn’t know whether he should cover his chest or his ass, “It’s already dawn. The next-door neighbor should be at work. Li Yao glanced at the door that was still open. His head was dizzy, too. I just want to know the truth of the Vulture Plan and the secrets behind Earth. Really, I’m a loyal fan. Didn’t you say that the end is coming? I might not be able to fill the two holes easily. “Watch your ass!”

Zhang Dapao groped among the books and found a penknife, which gave him great courage. He waved the penknife and shouted, “Get out! Get out! Now!”

“Nonsense. It’s a lie. Wouldn’t it show that I’m far-sighted and confident?

Zhang Daniu flew into a rage out of humiliation and spoke the truth, “I was originally planning to write 30-50 million words as long as the results of this book were good. Anyway, there’s enough time. I’ll write while I think about how to make up the ‘Vulture Plan’!”

Li Yao did not expect such a trick at all. “What—what if you haven’t figured it out in the end? It is the most important trap in the entire book!”

“If I can’t figure it out, I might as well become a eunuch or a cripple!”

Zhang Daniu replied confidently, “What’s the big deal about writing a web novel?”

Li Yao was completely frustrated.

He felt that his head was humming. A mouthful of sweet blood was stuck in his chest. He wanted to spit it on the author’s face.

“How—how could you do that?”

Li Yao was extremely angry and heartbroken. “Master Niu, I thought that you were a very ambitious author with integrity. How could you deceive the readers’ feelings?”

“And you f*cking lied to me about my feelings. In short, there is no outline, no secrets. Do you understand? If you do, then get the hell out!”

Zhang Daniu took out his phone and pretended to make a call. “You really think I don’t dare to call the police?”

“No, Master Niu. I’ve thought about it. It’s definitely not as simple as you said. Maybe even you don’t realize what the ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’ novel is all about. The ‘Vulture Plan’ definitely has a truth. Maybe, you are indeed farsighted and confident. But you don’t know it yet. You need someone to remind you.”

Li Yao was so anxious that he decided to take a gamble. “Truth be told, Master Niu, I was inspired by your novel recently. I found that I had undergone tremendous changes both physically and psychologically. I—I have awakened!”

Zhang Daniu’s face and voice both changed. “I’m not interested in your physiological and psychological changes. Get out!”

Li Yao took a deep breath and said, “I discovered that I was a—superhuman!”

Chapter 3190: Transmigrating

The air froze again.

The room was filled with an awkward and weird atmosphere.

Zhang Daniu’s gaze was as if he was looking at a mental patient. The paper cutter in his hand continued waving as he slowly moved towards the door.

“It’s true. You’ve written so many bizarre and unbelievable plots in your novels. I thought that your mind should be very open. Why can’t you accept that there are some bugs in your real life?”

Li Yao clenched his fists so hard that tears were about to fall. “Give me one minute. Just one minute. I can prove it!”

Looking at Li Yao’s cracking fists, Zhang Daniu did not dare to move. He asked cautiously, “How are you going to prove it? Try rubbing a fireball with your superpowers.”

“It’s too difficult to make a fireball. I haven’t awakened it yet.

Li Yao looked around and picked up a hardcover encyclopedia that had barely been opened and was emitting the fragrance of ink. “My current strength has already surpassed the limits of the human body. The day before yesterday, in the dormitory, I forcefully tore open a dumbbell wrapped in metal rubber. Although you don’t have a dumbbell here, this 1,000-page encyclopedia is made of very solid materials. It’s as thick as half a palm. If I can forcefully tear it from the middle, I will be able to tear it into two halves. This should be enough to prove that I am not lying, right?”

Zhang Daniu blinked his eyes and stared at the encyclopedia for a long time. He said hesitatingly, “You mean to tear it in the direction of the spine instead of tearing it into two halves along the spine?”

“Of course!”

Li Yao held the encyclopedia in his palm. His fingers were like ten burning iron chopsticks that were almost embedded into the dictionary. “If there are no questions, shall I begin?”

“You—you may begin.”

Zhang Daniu had no choice but to agree. However, his eyes were not on Li Yao, but on the door and the window.

“AHHHHHHH!”

Li Yao screamed four times in a row. He clenched his fists so hard that his fingers turned white. The muscles all over his body bulged, and the explosive force almost tore his T-shirt apart. He didn’t die until ten seconds later.

He looked at Zhang Daniu.

Zhang Daniu looked at him.

The two of them looked at the encyclopedia in his hand—it was still intact. Even the cover of the book was intact.

“I can explain that to you.”

Li Yao said somewhat embarrassedly, “My superpower has just awakened and is still in a very unstable stage. I need to meet two conditions in order to maximize my potential. The first is sufficient high-calorie food. Right now, my appetite is comparable to that of an entire basketball team. If I don’t eat enough, I simply won’t be able to exert my strength. The second is to read your novels. Really, it’s ridiculous to say it out loud. Every time I read your novels, I would feel my blood vessels expand and my blood boil. An inexplicable impulse would surge and surge in my body. I don’t know how to describe this impulse. In short, as I read, I was suddenly filled with strength!”

“Don’t doubt me. Don’t look at me like I’m a mental patient or a maniac. I’m speaking the truth. I—I still have evidence. Just seven days ago, I was just an ordinary college student with a thin and weak body. But now, in just one short week, just because I’ve read your novels, my muscles have continuously expanded, and my bones have also expanded by quite a bit. My entire body has undergone a complete transformation. I have undergone a heaven-shaking and earth-shaking transformation. I have turned into a stocky and strong man.

“Take a look if you don’t believe me. My muscles are strong, my skin is bronze, and my body is as smooth as a leopard’s. Look, Master Niu!”

“…”

/ .

Faced with Li Yao, who was making beautiful poses, Zhang Daniu took a deep breath and screamed, “Help! Help! Help!”

“Don’t shout!”

Li Yao panicked all of a sudden. He was even more depressed as he thought, “Why don’t you believe me?” He lunged forward, wanting to cover Zhang Daniu’s mouth, but the author suddenly ducked past him and stumbled to the door. Li Yao was both anxious and scared. He punched the wall hard. “I’m telling the truth!”

“Bang! Huala!”

The punch was as loud as thunder. His fist had punched a hole in the wall!

“Well…”

Staring at his own fist, Li Yao felt no pain at all.

“What?”

Zhang Daniu turned around and looked at the hole on the wall in disbelief.

Although Li Yao did not smash the load-bearing wall but the partition wall inside the room, the strength of his punch was enough to shock everyone for a long time.

Zhang Daniu swallowed hard. He hesitated for a long time and moved closer, one step after another. He looked at Li Yao’s fist and then at the hole on the wall. He mumbled, “The quality of the building is a problem right now. Did he punch through the wall just like that?”

“As I said, this is the strange strength that I just awakened after reading your novel over the past week.”

Li Yao said helplessly, “If you still don’t want to believe it, you can check your own laptop and see how many words you wrote last night. How many words do you think an author can write in a night under normal circumstances? Ten thousand, twenty thousand, thirty thousand at most. But what about you?”

Zhang Daniu tilted his head and thought for a while. He ran over to look for his own computer and looked at the latest paragraph for a long time. Then, he used the statistical software to calculate the number of words that he had just entered last night. After that, he let out an incomprehensible mutter from the depths of his throat. He sat down on the pile of books and held the computer in a daze.

“You noticed that something was wrong, too, didn’t you? Maybe you wrote something in the past, but it can’t be as crazy as writing thirty to fifty thousand words a night, can it?”

Li Yao said very sincerely, “You know very well that you were really drunk last night. You couldn’t even walk stably. You only vomited three times on the way back. How could you have regained your consciousness in just one to two hours and write so many new chapters? Even if it was just a running account, it was a running account with regulations and logic. Is it possible for someone who is almost intoxicated?”

Looking at his red and swollen fingers, Zhang Daniu narrowed his eyes and recalled everything that had happened last night. A minute later, he gave up and shook his head hard, sighing in confusion.

“If you insist that this is your unique writing method and you can only think about it in your dreams, then I have nothing to say.”

Li Yao said, “Otherwise, can’t we calm down and talk rationally?”

“…Alright.”

Holding the computer, Zhang Daniu stared at the hole that Li Yao had made and said, “What do you want to talk about? I really don’t know anything, except that I wrote a damn novel!”

“It’s hard for me to explain everything. The truth is still hidden in the fog. Therefore, we might as well tell you what we know and what we know. Maybe we can piece together the whole story, or at least one step closer to the answer.

Li Yao didn’t wait for Zhang Daniu’s rejection and told him everything.

He started from the strange dream that had been haunting him—the T-rex that had been smeared with lipstick. He talked about the accident of the Heavenly Horse Lake International Anime Festival and the accident that had happened to the creator of the famous fantasy world. Then, he watched under the recommendation of his fellow students. The strange dream became more and more vivid and real, and his body underwent various mutations.

Then, there was the shooting at T3, the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’, last night’s incident, the series of answers and speculations on the Q&A website of ‘Dream Traveler’, as well as the latest news about the super earthquake in the Western Pacific Ocean. Li Yao had just learned that Class 9.2 of the Richter Scale was already a terrifying natural disaster. It was the second strongest earthquake ever recorded by mankind. If the epicenter of the earthquake was close to land, it was entirely possible to destroy an entire city.

If such an earthquake was just a sign of a larger earthquake, there was no telling what kind of terrible destruction the Western Pacific would suffer when the real earthquake arrived.

Zhang Daniu seemed to be listening carefully. He browsed through the webpage that Li Yao opened and the answers of ‘Dream Traveler’ one by one. It took him half an hour before he understood everything. Then, he crossed his arms and pondered for five minutes before he asked, “Then, what’s your conclusion?”

“I don’t have a conclusion, but I do have a guess. A very crazy and absurd feeling. Over the past few days, the feeling has been lingering in my head, but I couldn’t figure it out at all. It was not until I read ‘Dream Traveler’s’ answer and the epiphany just now that I grasped the most crucial clue.

Li Yao stared at Zhang Daniu and said, one word after another, “I feel that I am Li Yao—not just this ‘Earth’s Li Yao’, but also the ‘Cultivator Li Yao’ that you wrote. I transmigrated to Earth from the magnificent, mysterious universe of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’!”

“…” Zhang Daniu.

“…” Li Yao.

“…” Zhang Daniu.

“Master Niu, if you have anything to say, just say it. If you have any opinions or suggestions about my theories, just give them to me. My ideas are still immature, immature, and in desperate need of your enlightenment.”

“I wouldn’t dare to give you pointers, but if you really want me to, then I will.”

Zhang Daniu pondered for a long time and said earnestly, “Well, Student Li Yao, it’s good that your imagination is abundant and you like reading. Yes, it’s good. If you have nothing to do, you can read a novel for entertainment. It’s not a problem at all!”

“However, although we are reading, we must be clear in our hearts that the novels are all false and that the authors are making up stories. I’m making up stories to feed my family, and you’re reading novels for fun. Isn’t that great?”

“Haha, I’m done laughing. We still have to study hard. We still have to work hard when we need to work hard. Really. Although there is Yan Ruyu and the House of Gold in the book, this book is not the other book. You understand what I mean, right?

“In short, it can be seen from the fact that you can train your body to be so well-built that you are a young man who is very determined and has self-control. As long as you can withdraw from the fantasy world and focus your willpower and self-control on your studies and work, I believe that you will definitely have a bright future. In short, is a lie. I made it up to make a living, and things like the are even more nonsense. You can’t have transmigrated from the , do you understand?”

Chapter 3191: Reasons

“Master Niu…”

Li Yao was disappointed. He sat on the ground, holding his head, and said in frustration, “You still don’t believe me after all this time!”

“Nonsense.”

Zhang Daniu said, “Although it’s true that I love to fantasize, and my mental fortitude is especially good, and I can accept things that are abnormal and beyond common sense, your story is too exaggerated and illogical. Not just me, any normal person would find it hard to believe!”

“What do you want me to believe in exactly? Do you want me to believe that I am a f*cking ‘God of Creation’? If you want to go crazy, then go crazy yourself. But really, don’t get involved in the so-called ‘Four Universities of Cultivation’. You will get me killed if you do this!”

According to the crosstalk, this is the old society’s underpass where the artists sell their art. They throw down the land, do their hard work, sell their gimmicks, and go down to the ninth class! Don’t be fooled by their glamorous appearance. You call yourself a teacher, but in fact, it’s just bullsh*t. There are many people watching the show, but there are few who have the support of real money. Even if you write your fingers off, you can barely make a living… Even so, you can still sell it every day. When it rains, it rains, or the emperor dies, they won’t let you sell it!”

“If I may be frank with you, your actions right now mean that you want to exterminate me and force the book ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’ to death! Think about it yourself. With your current mental state, if you go out on the street and do anything illegal, the reporters will interview you and report that you are addicted to webnovels and can’t free yourself from reading them. Then, you will post the title of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’ and label it as a ‘spiritual opium’. I will be finished. I will be completely ruined. I might even implicate countless colleagues… Do you understand?”

Li Yao lowered his head and did not say anything for a long time.

“Do you understand my earnest words, or do you not?”

Zhang Daniu said in distress, “It’s true. It’s really just a random story that I made up to exchange for two dollars. There’s absolutely not the slightest bit of educational significance or philosophical thinking in it, nor is there the slightest bit of malicious metaphor for the real world… If you can’t tell what’s true from what’s false when you read the book, and you still let your imagination run wild and your body and mind undergo drastic changes, then it’s really not my problem, but your own problem!”

“I—I understand.”

Li Yao finally raised his head. Two sharp eyes beamed out of his messy hair. “Thank you, Master Niu. I’ve figured everything out!”

“Is that so?”

Zhang Daniu was overjoyed. “You’ve really thought it through?”

“Yes, I have!”

Li Yao slapped his thigh and said, “That’s right. Of course, you are not ‘Marvelous Mark’ or ‘God of Creation’. You did not create the ‘Four Universities of Cultivation’!

“Yes, yes, yes, that’s all false. It’s better to believe that there are ghosts in the world than to believe the mouth of the storyteller. A writer can make up any kind of nonsense just to make some money!”

Zhang Daniu heaved a long sigh of relief and said with a smile, “I’m relieved that you’ve figured it out on your own. Oh, I’m so happy to see a young man who has lost his footing come to a halt!”

“Do you have ‘Romance of the Three Kingdoms’?”

Li Yao suddenly asked without rhyme or reason, “I think I saw it on the bookshelf just now.”

Without waiting for Zhang Daniu’s reply, he groped for a while in the pile of books and found a simplified version of the second volume of ‘Romance of the Three Kingdoms’. He looked at the name of the author on the spine of the book and fell into deep thought.

“You must’ve read the book many times. I believe that many plots and characters have left a deep impression on you.

While pondering, Li Yao said, “That’s true. How many people haven’t read ‘Romance of the Three Kingdoms’? Even if they haven’t read Luo Guanzhong’s original work, they’ve read his books, comic books, and TV series. They’ve played games about him and mentioned many famous people, including Cao Cao, Guan Yu, Zhuge Liang… The famous scenes, the three heroes fighting against Lu Bu, the three visits to the thatched cottage, the burning of Red Cliff… Most people’s first reaction was to think of Luo Guanzhong’s version.

“So, answer me a question, Master Niu. Do you think the Three Kingdoms were created by Luo Guanzhong?”

“Huh?”

Zhang Daniu was confused. “What do you mean?”

“Do you think that Luo Guanzhong is the ‘God of Creation’?”

Li Yao asked, “Was he the one who created the end of the Han Dynasty out of thin air, the struggle between heroes, and the soul-stirring battles?”

Zhang Daniu opened his mouth. It was obvious that he wanted to sneer, but he soon came back to himself and fell into deep thought.

“It seems that you’ve got it.

Li Yao said, “Of course, Luo Guanzhong is not ‘God of Creation’ or ‘Magical Mark Ma Liang’. Even though his ‘Romance of the Three Kingdoms’ is marvelous and classic, he did not create a real world out of thin air.

“On the contrary, a real chaotic world at the end of the Han Dynasty exists. The heroes and the world are real. The information of the history has been passed on from generation to generation with history books, folklore, and storytellers as the carriers. In the end, it was absorbed by Luo Guanzhong’s brain. After his integration, refinement, and refinement, the whole story was dissected and told to more people.

“Therefore, the real three kingdoms were in chaos before, followed by Luo Guanzhong’s ‘Romance of the Three Kingdoms’. The former is the cause, and the latter is the effect. Whether Luo Guanzhong writes ‘Romance of the Three Kingdoms’ or not, the three kingdoms are there, right?

“If someone who doesn’t know the first thing about history reads ‘Romance of the Three Kingdoms’ and leaves a deep impression, they will look back at the history books and discover the real three kingdoms. They will be shocked and think that Luo Guanzhong is a prophet or even a creator. Isn’t that absurd?”

Zhang Daniu thought for a long time and asked, “What are you trying to say?”

“What I’m trying to say is that maybe the logic of ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivators’ is the same. It’s not that you created the ‘Four Universes of Cultivators’, but that a brilliant, real ‘Four Universes of Cultivators’ does exist on the other side of the universe that we don’t know about.

Li Yao continued. “For some reason, the information about the ‘Four Universities of Cultivation’ broke through the barrier of Earth and flooded into your brain through a method that we can’t explain. You ‘observed’ it, or rather, ‘sensed’ it, and then you were able to depict what it looked like! Of course, the content of the picture is very similar to that of a painter who is drawing a model!

“Hehe. The more I think about it, the more it makes sense. It must be the case. If so, all the suspicious points will make sense!

“Why is it that the style of your novel before and after 500 chapters is vastly different, and the ideas and aesthetics contained in them are also vastly different? The reason is very simple. The first 500 chapters are about upgrading the face-slapping training methodology, which is the embodiment of your true level! And after the 500 chapters, it involves the discussion of the universe, the starry sky, the future, and society. Those very wonderful and imaginative things were not written by you at all, but written by the force that surged into your brain. With your true level, it is simply impossible to write such things. You are just its puppet! What do you think of my speculation?”

“I think…”

Zhang Daniu took a deep breath and pointed at the door. “Get out! Get out!”

“Don’t be like this, Teacher Niu. Didn’t you promise me that I would calm down and chat rationally? I feel that we have already grasped the edge of the truth!”

Li Yao hurried to say, “Besides, don’t you find the post of ‘Dream Traveler’ both weird and reasonable?”

“Bullsh*t.”

Zhang Daniu said, “I would rather believe that the ‘Four Universities of Cultivation’ is real than any post on ‘Do You Know It’? This ‘Dream Traveler’ is either a fellow writer or an out-and-out lunatic.

“Please don’t jump to conclusions. I think he is a thoughtful, confused, and frustrated man, just like me.

Li Yao took out his phone and dialed the number that he had just dialed. “Give me one last chance. Let me contact him. If I can’t reach him this time, I will—”

Beep! Beep! The number was dialed.

“It’s through!”

Li Yao’s eyes were wide open. He was so excited that his nose was sweating. He turned on the speaker and asked in a trembling voice, “Hello, is this ‘Dream Traveler’? I saw your answer on ‘Do You Know’ and felt that it was very interesting. I have a lot of similar feelings, too. I would very much like to have a deep conversation with you. Is it convenient for you?”

There was a long silence on the other end of the phone.

Zhang Daniu’s attention was also attracted. He stared at Li Yao’s phone curiously.

After more than ten seconds, a coarse female voice came over. “I’m not ‘Dream Traveler’. He doesn’t use this number anymore. Don’t call randomly in the future.”

“Well—”

Li Yao blinked. “So, you know ‘Traveler Dream’? Can you give me his latest contact information? I really need to talk to him about something important. Sorry to trouble you!”

There was silence on the other end of the phone for a while. Rustling noises came over, as if a girl was sobbing.

“I am his sister.”

The coarse female voice said, “Don’t call me again. My brother posted some crazy stuff online. He—he has schizophrenia and is being hospitalized. Don’t agitate him.”

Chapter 3192: The Mystery Of The Ark

Just as Li Yao was about to clarify, the other party had already hung up the phone. “Beep Beep Beep Beep” sounds came from the loudspeaker, making the atmosphere in the shabby room even more awkward.

Looking at the pitch-black mobile phone, Li Yao did not know what to say. He sighed and squatted on the ground, holding his head.

“Did you know? For a moment, I almost believed you. What a shame!”

Zhang Daniu was heartbroken. “I don’t know whether you are crazy, I am crazy, or the world is crazy. I actually believed you!”

Li Yao raised his head and opened his mouth, only to feel that his entire body was sore and feeble. He was unable to say anything. Even his own beliefs were beginning to waver.

“Do you have nothing to say?”

Zhang Daniu said, “If you have already calmed down and learned to face reality, Teacher Niu will treat you to breakfast before you return to school, right?”

Li Yao nodded helplessly.

…

Although it was only six o’clock in the morning, the breakfast stalls at the entrance of the neighborhood were already packed with people. The aroma of food filled the air, especially from an enormous frying pan, from which the sound of frying dumplings and fried buns could be heard. It was even more enticing.

Zhang Daniu sat down with 10 fried dumplings and 10 fried buns. He then commanded Li Yao to bring over their wontons, which were filled with chili sauce and vinegar. He happily drank a mouthful of the wonton soup and stuffed three fried buns into his mouth. Only then was he satisfied and beaming with smiles.

“Let me tell you, no matter how frustrating yesterday was, no matter how tired and frustrated one is, as long as one has a hot breakfast in the morning, all their worries will be gone!”

Zhang Daniu took a deep breath of the rich fragrance, “The real world is still quite beautiful. We still have to live our days down to earth. Don’t let your imagination run wild, alright?”

“…Yes.”

Li Yao picked up a fried dumpling and felt that it tasted like chewing wax, but he still forced himself to swallow it.

“Are you really alright?”

Zhang Daniu observed Li Yao’s expression and said, “Why don’t we call your family after dinner?”

“There’s no one at home.”

Li Yao picked up the bowl and covered his face. “My parents left me when I was very young. I was all by myself. My family was not hungry after I ate.”

“Oh, I see. I’m sorry. I didn’t know.”

Zhang Daniu scratched his face for a long time and said, “No wonder you’ve been dwelling in the world of fiction, hoping to find comfort in the imaginary four worlds of cultivation! Sigh. How should I put it? I’m not asking you to completely give up on… fantasy. You just need to pay attention to the difference between fantasy and reality. Don’t be too engrossed, understand?”

“How about this? Teacher Niu is not a completely unreasonable person. Although you are not a female university student with fluttering skirt and fluttering long hair, Teacher Niu still welcomes you to come over and exchange and discuss when you are free. As long as you don’t kick open the door in the dead of night, everything will be fine!”

Li Yao smiled. “Thank you, Master Niu.”

“There’s no need to thank me. If you really want to thank me, then stop talking nonsense like ‘cultivating the four universes’ all day long. Go back and prepare your resume. Find a stable job and live a normal life.”

Zhang Daniu said, “If you do this, you will be thanking me. Do you understand?”

“I understand.”

Li Yao said, but his eyes were still in a trance. He ate in silence for a while and could not help but ask, “What is the so-called ‘normal life’?”

“Just like that!”

While drinking the soup, Zhang Daniu said, “Find a job that is nine o’clock in the morning, nine o’clock in the evening, a job where you have less money and more work, a job where you have to endure the roars of your supervisor and the exploitation of your boss, a job where you have to work hard for a few years, earn the down payment for a house, shoulder a debt for a few decades, have your own home, find a wife who is in a similar situation to you, and after you give birth to a child, you should spend the extra time, energy, and money on the child. If you do, you should devote all your time and effort to the child. While you slowly become greasy, the child will also gradually grow up. When the child grows up, you will repeat this process. Isn’t this the life of a normal person?”

Li Yao frowned. “It doesn’t sound very appealing.”

“What else could it be?”

Zhang Daniu pursed his lips and said, “Don’t say that I’m not attractive. How many people want to live a peaceful life like this but can’t? Let’s face it. We are neither nobles nor heroes. Isn’t this the life of ordinary people?”

“I still think that I have superpowers. No. We both have superpowers.

Li Yao muttered in a low voice, “I am a ‘Cultivator Hero’. I have super strength, super speed, super vision and hearing, and super self-recovery ability. In the process of my continuous awakening, I also have the ability to move mountains and overturn seas and summon wind and rain. Shooting fireballs and electric arcs is nothing difficult for me. In short, I know all the techniques of the cultivators. And you are a ‘Writing Hero’. Your superpower is to write! The two of us should not live such a mediocre life. We should use our superpowers to fight against the evil forces that govern this world in the darkness!”

“What did you say?”

Zhang Daniu asked, “What is my superpower?”

“Writing.”

Li Yao replied, “I’m fast!”

“Then what?”

Zhang Daniu narrowed his eyes.

“Nope, just writing. This is a very powerful superpower!”

Li Yao spoke honestly.

“Do you think that you are speaking human language?”

Zhang Daniu said, “It’s just that Teacher Niu is a modest gentleman who has cultivated his body and mind for more than thirty years. I won’t lower myself to your level. If it were a second person, they would have long put the blame on you.”

“I was wrong, Master Niu.”

Li Yao held his stomach and said, “Hey, this pan-fried dumpling and pan-fried bun seem to be quite fragrant. Give me another twenty—give me twenty of each?”

Zhang Daniu glared at Li Yao. After a long time, he sighed and raised his hand. “Boss!”

In a moment, a mountain of fried buns and fried dumplings were placed in front of the two. Needless to say, Li Yao wolfed down the food. Zhang Daniu had been writing crazily last night and had consumed a lot of his energy. His appetite was much larger than usual, too.

A greasy TV was hanging in a corner of the restaurant. Usually, it would only be turned on at night. During the day, it was just a decoration. But today, it was turned on by a regular customer to watch the latest report about the earthquake in the Western Pacific Ocean.

It was said that the intensity of the earthquake had been revised to 9.3. It was the second strongest earthquake in history.

The epicenter of the earthquake was in the Edo Bay, only dozens of kilometers away from the largest metropolis in the world, which was also one of the top 10 metropolises in the world. At first, the devastating blast caused serious damage to the city, which was then followed by an overwhelming tsunami that turned half of the city into a swamp. Even the Fusang Islands, which were used to frequent earthquakes, were shivering under the deterrence of the catastrophe and entered a national emergency.

Because of the natural gas leakage caused by the earthquake, the entire city was filled with explosions, fireballs, and black smoke. It was a terrible battlefield.

Buildings dozens of floors high were as fragile as building blocks. The so-called civilization of mankind was nothing in front of nature.

To make matters worse, it was said that eight of the sixteen nuclear power plants on the seashore of the islands had been hit by earthquakes and tsunamis, with the potential of a nuclear leakage. That was the official version of the story. According to the sources on the network, several of the nuclear power plants had been seriously damaged. The intensity of the radiation was unprecedented!

“I didn’t expect the earthquake last night to be so serious. It is almost like the plot of ‘Island Drowning’.

Zhang Daniu grinded his teeth as he said, “Have you ever read <>, a particularly classic science fiction novel? If we go by the logic of you and the Dream Traveler, then the author of the novel must have experienced similar events in his previous life in order to write such a life-like novel, right? Hahahaha!”

Li Yao did not answer. He stared at the city that had been savaged in the news. The collapsed city seemed to be floating on a burning ocean. He felt that the world was coming to an end.

Right then, a black shadow suddenly flashed in the corner of the camera.

It seemed to be a man who had jumped down from the twenty to thirty floors that were gradually tilting. Then, he flew away from the camera!

“This is—”

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently. He could not believe his eyes.

“What happened to you?”

Zhang Daniu raised his head and saw nothing in the shaking camera.

“Nothing.”

Li Yao was not sure whether or not his eyes were deceiving him. “I think a fly flew past the TV.”

“This kind of small restaurant is like this. It’s dirty and won’t get sick after eating. What’s a fly scared of?”

Zhang Daniu pointed at Li Yao’s phone on the table. “Your phone is ringing.”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He opened the screen and found a message from an unknown number.

“Why are you looking for my brother? You’ve read all his posts online? Do you believe his crazy words?”

Right, this was Dream Traveler’s number. It was from Dream Traveler’s sister!

The fire of hope in Li Yao’s heart was burning brighter than ever. He held the phone with both hands and typed quickly, “Yes, miss. I don’t just believe it. I have experienced exactly the same situation as your brother. Maybe your brother is not crazy. Can you tell me more about him? At least tell me where he is receiving treatment!”

“Maybe he is not crazy. Maybe you are as crazy as he is.

The other party replied very quickly, “My brother is being treated in the Ark Psychological Research Center. Maybe you should go there and take a look.”

“An ark?”

Li Yao held his phone in a daze. He had never heard of such an institution to treat mental illnesses.

“What’s wrong?”

Seeing his ugly face, Zhang Daniu was curious.

Li Yao didn’t reply and simply pushed the phone over.

Zhang Daniu took the paper and glanced at it. He was not bothered at first, but soon, his face turned pale and weird, as if he had been hit by lightning.

“What’s wrong?”

It was Li Yao’s turn to ask, “Is there a problem, Master Niu?”

“I—I don’t think so. I don’t know. It must be a coincidence, right?”

Zhang Daniu stammered, “The Ark Psychological Research Center? I think it contacted me a few days ago.”

Chapter 3193: The Only Clue

“What?”

Li Yao almost spat out the scallion pancake in his mouth. He could not wait to grab Zhang Daniu’s neck and interrogate him. “What’s going on? Do you know this ‘Ark’? Tell me the details!”

“It’s—”

Zhang Daniu glanced at Li Yao’s phone again. At this moment, Dream Traveler’s sister sent him a new message. It was a link to a website. He clicked on it and his face turned even paler. “Yes, that’s right. It’s this ‘Ark’. A-a few days ago, didn’t we have a group chat where some authors often chatted and discussed ideas? Then, a stranger in the group came looking for me. He claimed to be a staff member of the ‘Ark Psychological Research Center’. He was doing a questionnaire on the lives and mental states of the self-employed in our country as well as free counselling. I filled in some of the basic information, including my contact information and address. Right, there’s also my neurotic and sleepwalking status. Of course, I didn’t say that I could write when I was sleepwalking. I’m not too sure about that myself.”

“How—how could you reveal such important information?”

Li Yao almost slapped the desk. “You don’t know who the guy is at all, whether he is a liar or not. How could you tell him such an important thing as your contact information and address?”

“At that time, I didn’t know, either. Even if he was a liar, how could he lie to me?”

Zhang Daniu explained, “The staff member was a girl who had just graduated from college. It was not easy for her to find a job. The questionnaire was included in her job evaluation. If she didn’t meet the requirements, she would be fired. I felt sorry for her and thought that there was no need for her to be so unreasonable. After all, she had to support the young woman’s legitimate job. Besides, she promised to send me small gifts and keep in touch!”

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao was rendered speechless.

“Is—is there really something wrong?”

Zhang Daniu said, “At that time, she also told me that their organization was not researching a very serious mental illness. They were only intervening in the psychological problems and sub-health states that are commonly seen in modern times. Freelancers like us are often under a lot of pressure or even criticized by society. Therefore, if there are any psychological deviations, we must not abstain from treatment and contact her as soon as possible. I think that it makes a lot of sense!”

“Alright, stop it. Let me think carefully.”

Li Yao closed his eyes and rubbed his nose.

“Yes. Have you figured it out?”

Half a minute later, Zhang Daniu asked cautiously.

“Yes.”

Li Yao suddenly opened his eyes. There was a sharp gleam in his eyes as he said solemnly, “Master Niu, I’m still a little hungry. Can we get another twenty buns and dumplings? No. Thirty. No. Why don’t we just take all of them?”

“…” Zhang Daniu.

“You see, I do have superpowers. How can I eat so much without superpowers?”

Zhang Daniu: “I believe you. I believe you have superpowers, alright? But Teacher Niu really doesn’t have any money. I’m not lying to you. I’m a dog if I lie to you!”

“That can’t be right. Last night, you promised that you would treat the ‘rabbit in the sea of stars’ to ‘hidden crane cuisine’. I’ve looked it up on the net. The most basic package there costs 400 to 500 credits per person. If the two of us were to order wine and add a few other dishes, we wouldn’t be able to finish it without 1,300 credits! The bone casserole we ate yesterday was 280 credits, which means that you still have at least 1,000 credits in your pocket. I’ve calculated that it’s enough for me to eat my fill and recover to my strongest state. At that time, I will really tear off the dumbbells for you to see!”

/ .

“…” Zhang Daniu.

Li Yao said, “Hey, Master Niu, don’t go yet. What do you mean by walking away? Running away is not going to solve the problem. We can be sure that the ‘Ark’ has its eyes on you! You can ask me to get lost, but can you really ask the staff of the ‘Ark’ to get lost? Are you even sure that the girl who did the questionnaire was really a girl and not a brawny man with a beard and hair?

“If you really don’t want to see me, I will leave right now. Really. But what if ten or so muscular men from the ‘Ark’ sneak into your house in the middle of the night? I won’t hear anything even if you shout for help.”

Zhang Daniu suddenly stopped. There were tears in his eyes when he turned around.

“Who the f*ck did I piss off?”

The author shouted in grief and indignation.

In the end, they didn’t buy fried buns and fried dumplings. Instead, they went to a nearby bun shop and bought a few hundred pieces of high-rise bread. There was no filling. It was pure flour. The hard bread was like lumps of stone.

Seeing the surprise in the lady boss’s eyes, Li Yao explained that they were from the construction site next door.

Energy-rich sports drinks were expensive, too. Zhang Daniu was reluctant to buy them, but with a gloomy face, he bought five bags of sugar and two bottles of mineral water from the supermarket and handed them over to Li Yao.

When he returned home, he watched Li Yao mix five bags of white sugar into the water and eat 30-50 high-rise bread with the water. Li Yao didn’t even burp once. Zhang Daniu sighed and realized that he was in big trouble.

“Now, do you believe me?”

Holding his half-full stomach, Li Yao said, “Dream Traveler predicted the tragic death of the creator of the fantasy and was sent to the ‘Ark’ for treatment. The ‘Ark’ was still active in your group of authors a while ago. If you think that it was a coincidence, your IQ will be too low for the series of schemes in ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’.

“Therefore, you are the one who should face reality. The reality is that you have been targeted. The enemy is very clear about your identity and address. It seems that they have unimaginable resources in their hands. Of course, you can run away, but where can you run to? How long can you run away? Will there be a series of accidents on your way to escape, like the creators of ‘Blood and Sand’, ‘Strong Colonization Hunters’, and ‘Star Ring World’?

“Do you see? Your only way out is to cooperate with me. We will take the initiative and find the truth together!”

Faced with Li Yao’s invitation, Zhang Daniu was silent for a long time. Then he said, “I’m wondering what the people of the ‘Ark’ will do to me if they find me.

“You have written similar organizations in your novels, right? What do you think they will do?”

Li Yao opened his hands and said, “The bottom line is three to five sessions of electric treatment, right?”

Zhang Daniu shuddered and stopped talking.

“It seems that you have understood your situation. Of course, my situation is no better than yours. Right now, we are in the same boat. If we don’t work together, we will really be screwed.”

Li Yao said, “I need your help. First of all, tell me more about the ‘Ark’ and the questionnaire at that time. Do you have a backup draft?”

Zhang Daniu turned on the computer and found the electronic version of the questionnaire.

Li Yao quickly glanced through them. They were all very common and irrelevant questions. It seemed that the purpose of the other party was to trick the author into giving him his contact information and address in order to pave the way for the next contact.

There was also a link at the end of the questionnaire. It was the other party’s official website, just like the link sent by ‘Dream Traveler’s’ sister.

Entering the official website, the first thing he saw was the abstract icon of a large ship with wings. It seemed to be the emblem of the organization.

Of course, Li Yao had heard the word ‘Ark’, which meant the last shelter before the world was destroyed. The appearance of an ark meant that an apocalypse was coming.

It was not until he observed more carefully that he realized that the ‘Ark’ was not just a center for psychological research and the treatment of mental illnesses. In fact, it was a foundation whose influence spread all over the world. The ‘Ark Foundation’ was supported by countless mysterious funds and financial groups and specialized in the study of life sciences, brain mysteries, genetics, neurology, and so on. Its purpose was to explore the mysteries of life and even the soul and to surpass the limits of human beings. At least, that was what the official website said.

“I’ve asked a few friends who are writing books. They’ve received similar questionnaires. Some of them have even investigated the ‘Ark Foundation’. There’s nothing wrong with it.”

Zhang Daniu said, “This is truly an international charity organization. They have a good cooperative relationship with many psychology associations, brain surgery hospitals, and neurological research organizations in many countries. They have branches in many developing countries and even war-torn countries. They pay attention to the mental and mental health of the local people. They have even unearthed many ‘genius children’ with extremely high IQ from war-torn countries. If it weren’t for the Ark Foundation, these genius children would definitely have been buried.”

“Is that so?”

Li Yao didn’t believe a single word.

There was no telling what was going on on the official website. He thought for a long time and had to summon his courage to call the sister of ‘Dream Traveler’.

He had a vague feeling that the attitude of the Dream Traveler’s sister was not that firm. If she really believed that her brother was a lunatic and Li Yao was a lunatic, she would not have taken the initiative to send a message and simply ignored it.

Since she had sent two messages and sent the official website of the ‘Ark Foundation’, was it possible that she was not sure, either?

This time, the call was picked up very quickly.

The fire of hope in Li Yao’s heart was burning brighter and brighter. He said in a hurry, “Hello, miss. I’ve read the link that you sent me just now. I’ve read it carefully, but I still believe that your brother is not crazy but has encountered something that cannot be explained by common sense. Can you give me a chance and your brother a chance so that we can have a good talk? Really, schizophrenia is a very serious mental disease. Once it is diagnosed, it is almost impossible to recover in a lifetime. Do you want your brother to be locked in a mental hospital for the rest of his life?”

There was silence on the other end of the line for a moment. Then, somebody took a deep breath and said, “What do you want to know exactly?”

Chapter 3194: Not A Dream

“I—I want to know your brother’s current status, especially his, the entire process of his illness!”

Li Yao was overjoyed. He held back his excitement and asked, “What’s your name, miss?”

“My surname is Dong.”

The man seemed to have made up his mind. He asked, “Where should I start? Our family is on the edge of a mountain in the central area. Although our family has been poor since childhood, my brother has shown his talent for studying since he was very young. He is not only the pride of our family but also the pride of our town. He was admitted to a famous university with the highest score in the city.

“After graduating from university, my brother found a very good job in the provincial capital and was highly valued by the leaders. In just a few years, he became the head of the department, bought a house in the provincial capital, found a girlfriend, and was about to get married. What I meant was that my brother’s career was on the rise, and his future and love were both perfect. There was nothing that could stimulate him.

“Moreover, he has always been sunny and cheerful since childhood. He likes reading and exercising. He doesn’t have any bad habits. He doesn’t eat, drink, gamble, or smoke. He doesn’t even smoke or drink. In our town, he is simply a weirdo.

“As for our family, although we were poor, we didn’t drag him down in any way. When the two of us were young, our environment was a bit poor, and we were short of money when we were studying. But ever since he went to college, he had scholarships to support himself. Over the past two years, Mom and Dad have rented orchards, and our living conditions have gradually improved. I graduated from college two years ago, and I have also found a stable job. In short, do you understand? Our family’s life is getting better day by day. There is simply nothing to worry about.

“But my brother is crazy. That was last year.

“In the beginning, he often called me to complain that someone was following him and that someone had planted a bug in his office. At that time, I thought that it was a business dispute or some internal strife in his office. I was just a college student who had just graduated. What could I do? I could only ask my brother’s colleagues in the company to take care of him. But my brother’s close colleagues in the company all said that my brother was the most valued middle-level officer in the company and that he was at the peak of his career. Who would follow and eavesdrop on him?

“After a month or two like this, my brother’s condition became more and more serious, and he gradually experienced hallucinations and auditory hallucinations. He called me and told me that he had found his stalker, who turned out to be his shadow! He said that there was a very mysterious force that could manipulate his shadow and even use it to interfere with and hurt him!

“It was only then that I realized that there was something wrong with my brother. I scrolled through my brother’s social media and his online comments and saw those… bizarre articles such as ‘Dream Traveler’. I then contacted my brother’s colleague. One of my brother’s best friends said that my brother had been using kitchen tinfoil as a hat to cover his entire head every day. He said that it would block his brainwaves from being sent out and prevent certain evil existences from locking onto his coordinates.

“The leader of my brother’s company thinks very highly of him and has been very kind to him. He didn’t fire him even though such a thing had happened.
