But one time, in a meeting to welcome a certain important partner, my brother pointed at the other party’s boss and shouted that he had seen that the other party was a brutal murderer in his previous life and ruined a great cooperation plan.

“After such a thing happened, naturally, I could not stay in the company any longer. I should have sent my brother to the provincial capital’s hospital for treatment. However, in our small town, someone in our family has been diagnosed with mental illness. It wouldn’t be nice if word got out. Besides, my parents and I still had a bit of a fantasy. We thought that my brother was under too much pressure because of work. Maybe he would be better off after resting at home for a while.

“Therefore, we brought our brother back to his hometown from the capital city and asked him to rest at home.

“In the beginning, my brother’s symptoms were indeed much better. He no longer had those messy, strange dreams, and he no longer pointed at other people’s noses and said that he had recovered from his previous life. We were all very happy and thought that my brother was about to recover.

“As it turned out, one day, my brother seemed to have read some news online about a playwright’s accidental death. His illness acted up again. He found a stainless steel frying pan at home and held it over his head. He kept mumbling words that no one could understand. His ten fingers twisted into a ball as he made some very strange gestures.

“I have to send him to the hospital now.”

“You said that my brother is not crazy. Please believe it. We, his family, hope that he is not crazy. But right now, he is covering his head in tinfoil and carrying a stainless steel pot. He is talking about his past life to everybody he meets, and he is scared of his own shadow. What is he if not crazy?”

Li Yao turned on the hands-free mode and listened to the conversation with Zhang Daniu. The two of them looked at each other for a long time and thought for a while. Then, Li Yao said cautiously, “Miss Dong, thank you for your frankness, but you can’t judge a book by its cover. Your brother said that he had the ability to travel through dreams and see his past life after he drowned in the reservoir in his third year of primary school. Have you ever heard of it before?”

“You are referring to his answer on ‘Do You Know?’

The stranger smiled bitterly. “Do you really believe what he said?”

“I don’t know.”

Li Yao said, “Therefore, I’m verifying it with you right now—a bold guess. Just be careful!”

“False.”

The stranger replied straightforwardly, “They are all fakes. He made them up.”

“Why are you so sure?”

Li Yao said, “Even if your brother’s mental state right now is really… unstable, you can’t say that what happened to him when he was a kid is definitely false!”

“Because my brother has never drowned before. Do you understand?”

The other party said, “I’m two years younger than my brother. When he was in the third grade of primary school, I happened to be in the first grade. I can already remember a lot of things. I never knew that my brother was in a drowning coma for half a month.

“Even if I’m too young and my memory is bad, my parents are not even sixty years old and their minds are very clear. It’s impossible for them to forget that their son drowned and was in a coma for half a month when he was young, right?

“Also, many of my brother’s primary school classmates are our next-door neighbors. They still live in town. After my brother’s illness flared up, I went to verify with these neighbors. None of my brother’s primary school classmates remember him drowning.

“The most crucial evidence is what my brother wrote in his answer. He said that he drowned because he went to the reservoir behind my grandmother’s house to play. However, my grandmother passed away before we were born. Our place is considered a mountainous area. It is very dry. There is no reservoir within a hundred kilometers!”

“Well—”

Li Yao was dumbfounded. “Is there such a thing?”

“Yes.”

The stranger replied, “Most of the people there are landlubbers. My brother is no exception. He doesn’t know how to swim at all. How can he drown himself in the nonexistent reservoir behind his grandmother’s house?”

Li Yao was rendered speechless. He was soaked in cold sweat.

“I’m sorry, Miss Dong.”

He stammered, “I—I don’t know. Your brother’s answers were too real and immersive. Wait. His answer was sent not long ago, and you said that he had been ill for a year. Does it mean that he wrote the answer in… a schizophrenic state, including many previous articles?”

“That’s right.”

The other party said, “At that time, his mental state was still alright. We were afraid to agitate him, so we didn’t dare to take away his phone. He held his phone and desperately wrote and posted. Many posts were posted at that time.

“I can’t blame you for that. It’s not right to say that there are no anomalies at all. Something very strange happened when my brother was hospitalized for the first time.

Li Yao raised his eyebrow. “What weird thing?”

“About your grandma’s house, the reservoir, the drowning, and the dream travelers.”

The other party said, “When I first saw his answer, I thought it was nonsense, too, because I was very clear that my brother had never drowned before. But one time, when I visited him, he told me in a low voice that drowning was real, not in this life, but in his previous life.

“He told me that he lived in a small town named ‘Lambang’ in Jiangdong Province in his previous life. There was a huge reservoir not far away from the town. He also told me a lot of local specialties, even snacks, as well as the living habits of the locals. He even spoke a few local dialects. The tones he uttered were completely different from the local dialects of our hometown, which gave me quite a scare.

“My brother asked me to go to the River East Province to find this place named ‘Liaojiang’. Although finding this place might not prove that he is not crazy, at least I can prove something!”

Li Yao was fascinated. He couldn’t help but ask, “You went?”

“I’m going.”

The other party said, “Maybe I shouldn’t go. I shouldn’t waste my time on a lunatic’s crazy talk. But he is my brother. The two of us have a very good relationship. He paid for most of my college tuition fees. As long as there is a glimmer of hope, I am willing to give it a try.”

“I went to Jiangdong Province, but I didn’t find a place named ‘Lambang’. However, according to the terrain that my brother described and the few dialects recorded, I found a county named ‘Lambang’.

“Do you know? The appearance of the place is exactly the same as what my brother described, including the reservoir in the southeast of the city, the lonely pagoda on the west side of the reservoir, and the dialect of the locals. I played my brother’s dialect for the locals, and they all said that he was a native of the area.

“My brother also mentioned a special local snack. It is to dry and grind the local specialty yellow flowers into powder before sprinkling it on the sour pulp noodles. It is sour and sweet and has a very unique taste. There are many places in the country where one can eat sour pulp noodles, but there is only one place where one can sprinkle yellow flower powder. Even in the towns 30-50 kilometers away, there is no such way of eating. I have never heard of such a way of eating in places further away.”

Chapter 3195: Sea Genius Concentration Camp

Li Yao felt goosebumps all over his back.

They looked at each other and noticed the coldness in each other’s eyes.

The narration was as creepy as a ghost story in the middle of the night. Li Yao seemed to see a picture where somebody was singing a folk song in the local dialect next to a reservoir in a remote town in the south, and a confused girl was staring at the reservoir with yellow flowers floating on the water…

Li Yao shook his head hard to calm himself down.

Of course, there were many “reasonable” and “scientific” explanations for this matter. For example, “Dream Traveler” had once gone on a business trip to this small southern town, “Chat Chuan”, or maybe he had gathered the local customs and terrain online.

But why?

Why did he scare his own sister with such a thing when he had been diagnosed with schizophrenia?

The whole thing simply didn’t make sense.

Besides, there was still the ‘Ark’.

Li Yao asked solemnly, “What’s with the Ark Foundation? You couldn’t have sent your brother to the Ark Psychotherapy Center in the first place, could you?”

“Of course not.”

The other party said, “In the beginning, we sent our brother to our local hospital. However, our hospital is a small place. The scale of the specialist hospital for mental illnesses is very small. The doctors’ level may not be very high. People simply don’t know much about mental illnesses. They only know the concept of ‘lunatics’. Our hospital only locked up these lunatics so that they wouldn’t run out and harm society and cause trouble for their family. That’s all.

“One can only imagine how terrible the treatment environment is.

“I’ve been to that place three times. It’s—it’s simply not a place for human beings. When I think of my brother suffering in that place, I feel as if my heart has been cut by a knife. I want to cry but have no tears.

“Of course, the conditions of the mental hospital in the provincial capital are better, but I’ve read a lot of files and found that they don’t have any effective treatment for mental illnesses. They can only provide conservative treatment. At most, the environment is better, and the nursing care is more standard.

/ .

“If my brother had really gone mad, I wouldn’t have been able to say anything about such an outcome. However, the conversation with Chuan gave me a deep suspicion. This suspicion was the only reason I was willing to be honest with you when I got in touch with the ‘Ark Foundation’.

“After I returned from Tanchuan, I grew suspicious of everything. I couldn’t bear to see my brother locked in a mental hospital with a harsh environment any longer, but I had no other options. Therefore, I wrote down my experience and sent a request for help.

“Oh?”

Li Yao did not expect that ‘Dream Traveler’s’ sister would post something about it, too. But when he thought about it, he realized that 99% of the amazing incidents that popped up on the Internet every day would be buried in the invalid messages and therefore could not be seen by him.

“A few days later, a woman who called herself a worker of the ‘Ark Foundation’ contacted me and asked about my brother’s condition, especially the course of his illness.

The stranger continued, “After I told them everything, they said that it might not be schizophrenia but a new, special mental disease. The ‘psychotherapy center’ under the name of their foundation has a one in ten thousand chance that the disease can be cured. Even if the disease cannot be cured, their treatment environment will be much better than that of the county hospital or the provincial hospital. They will try to make sure that the patient is in a very comfortable environment and that the care is more standard and thorough.

“Is that so?”

Li Yao thought for a moment and said, “Sounds good. It must be very expensive. Besides, such a private medical center might not be reimbursed. Can your family afford long-term treatment?”

“No, they don’t want money.”

The other party said, “The staff said that the elder brother could be placed into a ‘charity medical program’. All the hospitalization fees would be paid for by the ‘Ark Foundation’, at the cost that they might try some untested, cutting-edge medical treatment on the elder brother. It’s quite understandable. How could they cure diseases such as schizophrenia without the most advanced and dangerous medical treatment?”

“So, you agree?”

Li Yao asked.

“We—we don’t have a solution.”

The doctor sobbed again. “I know that the treatment plan provided by the Ark Foundation is very risky and even treats my brother as a mouse. But—but my brother was already in a very bad condition at that time. He tried to kill himself three times when he was hospitalized. He bit his wrist so hard that it was full of bruises because he didn’t have any tools. The last time, he even broke the veins on his wrist in the middle of the night and stuffed his hand into the blanket so that nobody would notice. Otherwise, he would’ve almost succeeded in the ward rounds!

“When he was conscious, he told me that he did not want to die, nor did he want to kill himself. Something was controlling him. His shadow was trying to kill him!

“What can our local hospital do? If we don’t want him to commit suicide, we can only inject high-intensity tranquilizers into his body 24 hours a day. Such tranquilizers can cause irreversible damage to the brain, too. What’s the difference between my brother and death in three to five years?

“Through the previous owner of my brother’s company, a psychiatrist from a major hospital in the provincial capital has also come to give my brother a consultation. Although it is not explicitly stated, it can be seen that they don’t have any particularly effective targeted treatment plans. Even if they are sent to the provincial capital, it will still be the same. Wearing clothes and tranquilizers… My brother used to be such a smart and proud person. I don’t want him to become an idiot and lunatic who will commit suicide at the drop of a hat for the rest of his life. As long as there is a glimmer of hope, I am willing to give it a try!”

“Besides, I’ve investigated the ‘Ark Foundation’ through a lot of channels, and there are no problems. I’ve visited their office and a temporary medical center in person, too. The environment there is indeed much better than that of the county hospital and the mental hospital in the capital city. It is said that a lot of well-known mental and neurological experts will be consulting each other. What is there to worry about?”

“Yes, Miss Dong. You’ve done the best you can.”

Li Yao could tell that her voice was shaking. It was not easy for a girl who had just started her career to do so much for her brother. He hurried to comfort her and said, “Then, can you tell me where your brother is being treated right now? Can you visit him often?”

“I’m afraid that’s not possible.”

The stranger replied, “Brother might not be in the country right now.”

“Not in the country?”

Li Yao blinked quickly. “What do you mean?”

“The Ark Foundation has several offices and temporary medical centers in the country, but their real medical center is overseas. They also have a very advanced medical starship that seems to have been modified by a super tanker. It can accommodate more than ten thousand patients at the same time.

The other party said, “The staff of the Ark Foundation told me that many of their treatment methods and drugs, such as the intervention of the human brain with nanotechnology and the ‘neural restorative drugs’ retrieved from the brains of monkeys, were all approved by medical organizations around the world. Since many of the methods and drugs were highly controversial and even ethically risky like the cloned sheep in the old days, it was very difficult for them to be approved by the medical institutions of other countries within three to five years.

“It means that they cannot establish medical institutions in the territories of the major countries, nor can they engage in activities in the fields of medical treatment, drug sales, and so on. Therefore, they resorted to such a method and employed a starship registered in the Republic of Java as their mobile hospital to avoid legal problems.

“Those things were explained to us in advance, including the risks. We understood everything and respected our opinions to the best of our ability. Our family thought for a long time before we sent our brother to the ‘Ark Foundation’.

“Although I can’t see my brother often, we do video-call him every week. I can tell that my brother is much better after only a few weeks of treatment. He has put on weight, and his skin is darker than before. He is as cheerful as before again. What is there to worry about now that the treatment is so effective?”

“That explains a lot. A medical starship that comes and goes without a trace.”

Li Yao narrowed his eyes and asked, “Are you sure it was your brother who talked to you on the video call? Now that his illness has been relieved, did you say when he will be cured and return home?”

“Of course I’m sure. He is my brother!”

The stranger laughed. “Are you suspecting that somebody is pretending to be my brother? Why? My brother is not a big shot!”

“As for when we are going home, I’ve asked him before, but he said that the ‘Ark Foundation’ does not just treat mental illnesses. Their main research field is ‘the limit of the human brain’. They believe that there is only a thin line between a genius and a lunatic. Only by starting with a seemingly crazy brain can we break through the limits of human intelligence and the soul. In short, the Ark Foundation has gathered countless elites from all over the world. My brother has made many good friends there, and it is possible that he will stay and work at the Ark Foundation. My brother’s schizophrenia has been recorded in the hospital. It will be difficult for the major domestic companies to recruit such a person who has gone ‘mad’, right? If it is possible for him to stay and work at the Ark Foundation after he recovers, my family will have no objections.”

“Understood. Thank you, Miss Dong.”

Li Yao thanked him solemnly. Then, he asked him about the workers of the ‘Ark Foundation’. However, it sounded no different from regular medical facilities. In the end, Li Yao simply recorded the addresses of several offices of the Ark Foundation and temporary medical centers. After thanking him again, he hung up the phone.

Chapter 3196: Prepare For Action!

Li Yao and Zhang Daniu stared at each other.

What should I do next?

Zhang Daniu asked.

“First of all, let’s be sure of three things. First, this is a world where superpowers exist. Whatever seems illogical and scientific can happen. Do you agree?”

Li Yao asked.

“Agreed.”

Zhang Daniu nodded solemnly. He pointed at the empty plastic bag and said, “You’ve eaten 100 bucks of my bread!”

“Don’t be so petty. I’ve tried my best to keep my stomach in. Secondly, it is definitely not a coincidence that the Ark Foundation found you and ‘Traveler Dream’ at the same time, is it? It is very likely that this is an organization that specializes in searching for superpowered people. As to whether they are kind or malicious, and what they will do when they find the superpowered people, there is no telling.

Li Yao said, “But we must prepare for the worst, mustn’t we?”

“…Yes.”

Zhang Daniu thought for a long time but still could not think of any other possibilities. He covered his face and lamented, “I just wrote a novel. Why am I so unlucky?”

“Thirdly, you are very likely to be the next target of the ‘Ark’.

“This is just my speculation,” Li Yao said. “I don’t have any direct proof. But even if the possibility is only one in ten thousand, it is still worth it to try your best to find the answer. You don’t want to be dissected and studied in a laboratory, do you?”

“Nonsense.”

“But the evidence right now is not enough to prove that the ‘Ark Foundation’ is malicious. Chances are that they are good people. Don’t you see that ‘Dream Traveler’ is living a good life under the protection of the Ark?”

“Yes. This is what we are going to figure out next.

Li Yao snapped his fingers and said, “Alright. Now that we’ve reached a consensus on the three points, let me give you my suggestion—I suggest you contact the ‘Ark Foundation’.”

“What?”

Zhang Daniu jumped up. “Are you crazy or am I? I’m not going to contact them!”

“Assuming that the Ark Foundation is malicious, there is no way that you and I can get away from them. I guarantee that, as time goes by, the people of the Ark will gather more intelligence about you and even me and devise a ‘perfect plan of action’ against us. By then, we will have no secrets at all and will be at their mercy—this is the disadvantage of being in the dark while they are in the light.

Li Yao opened his hands and said, “But right now, the day after I contacted you, the Ark Foundation should still be unaware of my existence. As long as you contact them or even enter the secret base of the Ark Foundation, I will have a way to sneak in and protect you in secret. Even if you are chopped into pieces and dissected for research, I will definitely avenge you, Master Niu!”

Zhang Daniu glared at Li Yao for a long time. “This is not funny at all.”

“All in all, a blessing is not a curse. A curse cannot be avoided. This is our only chance.

“I can’t keep my identity a secret for long,” Li Yao said. “If you want to take action when the Ark Foundation knows everything about me, it will be too late.

“Wait.”

Zhang Daniu asked, “What if the Ark Foundation is friendly?”

“If the Ark Foundation was kind, how did the creators of the fantasy worlds die?

Li Yao said, “Even if some of them were suddenly ill and some died of accidents, at the very least, ‘Father of the Ring’ Galiot was killed by a shooting. The shooter admitted that they were from an organization named ‘Apocalypse’. The organization must’ve been malicious! In order to avoid serious harm from the ‘Apocalypse’, it is better to seek the protection of the Ark Foundation as soon as possible!”

“Sounds reasonable.”

Zhang Daniu said, “Hey, why do I feel that your thinking is getting sharper and sharper and your will is very firm? Your two eyes are shining like two light bulbs?”

“This is all because of you, Master Niu. How should I put it? I feel that you are a star, and I am a planet. There is an inexplicable attraction between us. The more I get to know you, the more I remember many things. Incredible ‘instincts’ even burst out from the depths of my every cell.

Li Yao warmed up his hands and feet at a super high speed that was invisible to the naked eye. His fists pierced through the air and even drew obvious white lights.

“Stop.”

Zhang Daniu said, “I will never have any ‘inexplicable attraction’ with a muscular man in my life. Just tell me what I should do next.”

“Let’s do it exactly.”

Li Yao’s mind became incomparably clear. He spoke with complete confidence, “Next, I need to spend the entire day to purchase a few things. Mainly electronic components. I need to use them to make miniature detectaphones and trackers. I also need to purchase weapons. Rest assured. Of course, I won’t purchase those particularly eye-catching, murderous-looking controlled knives. But I need to have the necessary self-defense ability. Oh, that’s right, there’s also high-energy food. I’m still in the state of rebirth, cell reconstruction, and ‘super metabolism’. I can’t always rely on bread and sugar water to survive. I have to go to the military goods store to purchase some true compressed military food. Rest assured, I feel that once my cells are completely reconstructed, I can eat much less food.”

“After purchasing everything I need for the operation, I need to go back to school and erase all the traces I left in the dormitory, especially the notebook in which I recorded my dream and the dumbbells that I tore apart.

“After all the traces are cleared, I will meet you here. I hope that you can stay at home in the meantime and make sure that the gas and the circuits are safe. Don’t ever go out. Can you do that?”

“Sure.”

Zhang Daniu also realized the seriousness of the problem and nodded vigorously.

“Buses, trains, and planes are too revealing. I’m planning to rent one and modify it myself. By the time we move, the odds of us being discovered will be much lower.

Li Yao was tapping his fingers, when he suddenly frowned, as if he had encountered an unprecedentedly complicated problem. He said, “Right. There’s another tricky problem.

“What is it?”

Zhang Daniu was shocked by Li Yao’s solemn attitude.

“I’m out of money.”

Li Yao extended his hand towards Zhang Daniu. “Master Niu, purchasing electronic components, weapons, and high-energy food is very costly. I also need to rent a car. How much savings do you have? Can you give it all to me?”

“Ah!”

Zhang Daniu was dumbfounded. He looked at Li Yao’s hands in disbelief. “You—you don’t have any money?”

“That’s right. I’m a college student, and I don’t have any income. Over the past week, I’ve already used up all my money.”

Li Yao said, “When my parents passed away, they left me a small house in my hometown, but I didn’t have much money to rent it. Every month, I would have to pay 1,000 credits in rent, and I would have to work part-time to study. My life was quite difficult. Over the past week, I overdrafted three credit cards and took out two online loans. I really don’t have any money left. Master Niu, you’ve been roaming the online literature world for so many years. You must have some savings. How much do you have?”

“I…”

Zhang Daniu’s eyes were filled with tears. His lips trembled for a long time. It was obvious that he wanted to curse. He held it in for a long time before saying in a trembling voice, “Nonsense. If I had money, would I still live in such a place? I don’t have money. I really don’t have money. I only have 20,000 bucks left. I haven’t paid the rent for the next quarter!”

“Twenty thousand is enough.”

Li Yao said, “You don’t have a wife anyway. Why do you need a wife?”

“…” Zhang Daniu.

“…” Li Yao.

“I’m going to kill you! I’m going to kill you in the novel! Really! I’m going to kill you in ‘Forty Thousand Years of Cultivation’!”

“Don’t be rash, Master Niu. Our operation is either a success or a failure.”

Li Yao said, “If you succeed, it is very likely that you will be able to crack the ultimate secrets about the ‘Vulture Plan’ and the ‘Origin of Earth’. You might even be able to save the entire planet! If so, 20,000 credits are nothing. It wouldn’t be unreasonable to offer you a hundred or even a thousand times more in return!”

“True.”

Zhang Daniu thought for a moment. “What if it fails?”

“If we fail, the two of us might be captured and dissected by the ‘Ark Foundation’ or the ‘Apocalyptic Organization’.

Li Yao said, “If that’s the case, what’s the point of you saving 20,000 credits for your wife?”

“…”

Zhang Daniu took a deep breath. “Let me see. I have to think very calmly. Are you really trying to cheat me of 20,000 bucks with such a lousy excuse?”

Li Yao smiled.

It was a smile that was filled with confidence and could suppress everything.

“Master Niu—”

He picked up the encyclopedia from the ground, which he hadn’t torn apart yet, and stabbed his fingers into it. The veins on his arms and neck bulged out, and he tore the dictionary, which was tougher than a brick, into two halves!

“Well—”

Zhang Daniu looked at the neatly cut dictionary. There were still ten deep indentations that still carried a burning smell. He was speechless for a long time.

“Master Niu, now you should be clear what kind of terrifying power you have awakened, right?”

Li Yao grinned hideously. “Give me the money now!”

“Is—is this considered robbery?”

Zhang Daniu shouted desperately, “This has to count as robbery!”

“Earth will remember you.”

Li Yao snatched the bank card from Zhang Daniu’s hand and said solemnly, “The six billion citizens of Earth will remember your 20,000 credits of rebellion funds.”

Leaving the author’s home, Li Yao went directly to the largest electronic components market in Jiangnan City.

With the awakening of his brain cells and his soul, countless information about refining and maintenance from the ‘Four Universities of Cultivators’ flooded into his brain every second. Although the world of Cultivators was different from that of Earth, magical equipment and electronic products shared similar principles. Li Yao did not intend to make any complicated electronic products. They were just simple detectaphones and trackers that were not difficult for him at all.

Chapter 3197: Unbelievable Strength!

After purchasing some of the most basic components in bulk, which was enough for him to produce a dozen or so detectaphones, trackers, and alarms, Li Yao went to the construction material hardware market not far away.

Although he had told Zhang Daniu that he was going to buy weapons, he did not intend to buy any controlled knives or guns.

Firstly, he did not have the relevant channels to find the controlled knives or guns. Although it was not difficult for him to find the so-called ‘underground black market’ with his current capability and computational ability, how could he solve the problem of transportation and usage?

In most of the countries in the world, firearms were still very sensitive. They would cause endless trouble both in possession and in use.

The mysterious ‘Ark Foundation’ and the even more mysterious ‘Apocalyptic Organization’ both involved enormous resources and forces. Even if Li Yao armed himself to the teeth and carried three to five heavy cannons, he would still be no match for them.

Guns were absolutely useless against superpowered people.

On the other hand, as long as he could further stimulate the “cultivation force” inside his body, even if it was an ordinary tool that wouldn’t raise any suspicion, it would still be able to display a destructive power even greater than a bullet.

Li Yao bought a batch of nails of different lengths and sizes from the construction material market. They were as heavy as bullets.

Then, he bought an imported special toolbox with screwdrivers and other tools. With his amazing strength, they were as good as military daggers.

Most importantly, these things were not afraid of inspection at all. Although they might not be able to send him to a train or a plane, he could store them in the trunk of the car in case he ran into a checkpoint.

After thinking for a bit, Li Yao bought a few sets of hard work clothes, labor gloves, and rubber boots for himself and Zhang Daniu. In the books, most of the techniques were released in the form of ‘fire balls’ or ‘electric arcs’. The heat-proof clothes, insulated clothes, and rubber boots could reduce the damage of the techniques to the minimum.

Then, Li Yao bought a few electrician vests, the kind with a lot of pockets, as well as a few very solid leather belts that could hang screwdrivers of various sizes and leather bags filled with nails. The pockets all over his body could also store a lot of high-energy food, which would help him recover during the gaps of combat and ensure his continuous combat ability.

In the end, Li Yao bought two extremely large canvas bags, stuffed everything inside, and left the construction material hardware market. Following the introduction of a famous sports forum, he went to a very famous outdoor sports club in Jiangnan City and purchased a large batch of energy bars and energy chews.

Energy bars were compressed from various nuts, chocolate, and high-energy materials. The heat contained in the same volume was several times more abundant than that of military compressed biscuits. Energy chews were also essential tonics for high-energy sports such as marathons and triathlons. A small stick was enough to keep an athlete burning for half an hour to an hour under extreme fatigue. Although it was expensive, it was Li Yao’s best choice when he didn’t have the “nutrition drugs and strengthening drugs” from .

The energy bars and the energy chews alone had cost him tens of thousands of dollars. Li Yao did not even bat an eye. To save the planet, he should spend what he needed. ‘Petty’ was never in his dictionary.

After purchasing everything, Li Yao reversed three times and went to an industrial area in the northern suburbs of Jiangnan City.

It used to be a very famous heavy industry district in Jiangnan City. There was a large-scale steel smelting base there. However, with the establishment of the development concept of “financial establishment city, real estate strength city”, the northern suburbs industrial zone had declined. Right now, heavy industry companies such as steel-making factories and affiliated processing factories had all moved and closed down. All that was left was an empty factory and a labyrinth of intricate pipes waiting to be demolished and auctioned.

Li Yao had seen this place on the news, and it had left a deep impression on him.

/ .

It was the most suitable place for his training.

Although the abandoned steel factory was patrolled by a few old guards and security guards, the vast factory district was like a small town and simply couldn’t be managed. In this day and age, scrap iron and copper weren’t worth much. Few people came here to steal things, so they simply gathered in the reception room to play cards. Li Yao was like a transparent leaf as he silently floated into the abandoned steel factory. No one noticed him.

Deep inside the steel factory, Li Yao found an area where tubes and blast furnaces were everywhere. He extended his senses to the maximum and made sure that nobody was following or spying on him. Then, he put on rubber boots, an insulated suit, and an electrician’s vest, filling his pockets with iron nails and energy gel.

At first, he jumped up and down on the ground, sensing the effects of the heavy load on his body. After warming up, he squatted slightly and sprinted a few steps forward. With a burst of strength in his legs, the cells inside his body exploded with “pa pa pa pa pa” sounds. His entire body soared into the air and actually rushed towards a tube that was 5-6 meters in the air.

Seeing that he was only half a meter away from the tube and that his momentum had run out and that he was about to fall, Li Yao waved his right arm without any hesitation and caught a hat with his index finger.

Huchi. Huchi. Huchi…

Li Yao swayed in the air as he breathed in large mouthfuls of the air filled with the smell of rust. The familiar memories spread out like an intense ink. The smell reminded him of the magical equipment graveyard a long time ago. It was the same pungent smell of rust from the depths of the garbage heap.

“This is me.”

Li Yao mumbled to himself, “This is the real me!”

As far as his eyes could see, his eyes seemed to have turned into X-ray machines. Everything within his sight became clear and transparent. The entire abandoned steel factory had turned into a 3D map. He could easily distinguish the most vulnerable points and the tubes that were suitable for crawling.

Moreover, at the periphery of the abandoned steel factory, the laughter of the security guards and the gatekeepers, as well as the footsteps and splashing sounds of a security guard peeing, could be heard clearly.

With a vague smile on his face, inexplicable strength surged out of the bulging veins all over Li Yao’s body and transmitted to his index finger that was supporting his body.

He curled his index finger and stretched out his arm. He was blown more than ten meters away and jumped to another tube.

“How relaxing! It’s even more natural than walking and running!”

Li Yao was jumping up and down between the tubes, the steel structure, and the blast furnace like a giant monkey. He was jumping up and down, jumping up and down, and performing all kinds of thrilling movements that even extreme athletes would not be able to do. Sometimes, he crossed a distance of thirty to fifty meters and landed lightly on the ground from the blast furnace. Other times, he relied on the strength of one or two fingers to swing himself through extremely dangerous angles. He even relied on the strength of his toes to hang himself in midair.

Finally, he crossed his arms and touched his leather belt. Two screwdrivers appeared between his fingers.

Hiu! Hiu Hiu Hiu Hiu!

The screwdriver tore through the air like a real military dagger, leaving gray blurred shadows.

In the beginning, Li Yao’s movements were clumsy, as if he had never learned close combat before. When he waved his saber, it was redundant and cumbersome. He didn’t even know how to control his strength. His body swayed violently, and he almost fell off the tube several times.

Very soon, he tried to reduce the unnecessary movements and strength while he practiced and recalled. Like a viper that was slowly waking up from hibernation, he resumed his deadly attack.

Hiss! Hiss hiss hiss!

Vipers were hissing between his fingers, which was a sign that the screwdriver was about to break the sonic barrier.

Li Yao felt that he could still increase his speed by 30%, but there was no need to try it here. It was not easy for him to choose a suitable training ground. It would be terrible if the security guards were summoned.

Then, it was the practice of absolute strength and speed as well as remote weapons.

Li Yao found an abandoned factory and happened to find a bundle of steel bars that had not been moved away yet.

The steel bars that had not been processed yet might not be as tough and durable as the standard ones, but they were good enough to test his strength.

Li Yao bent one, two, and three iron bars easily. Then he bent four, five, and six iron bars that were tied together.

It was not until he tied up the iron bars that were as thick as nine fingers that he reached the limit of his strength. His arms seemed to have been corroded by venom, and his shoulder blades seemed to have been burnt and could not be bent or broken. The physical strength that he had just gathered was running out, too.

That was good.

Li Yao took out the energy bars and the energy gel and started testing his recovery speed. He calculated the rhythm of his strength that he should maintain during the battle and the likelihood that his combat ability would rise again.

“That’s the limit of my current strength. There’s no need to pursue the matter further. The strength of an individual is limited. It’s impossible for me to fight them recklessly.

Li Yao narrowed his eyes and looked at the factory, which was more than twenty meters tall. Without any warning, he accelerated, sprinted, and jumped, leaving insignificant traces on the walls and the steel beams above his head.

“What an inhuman motor force. If I go all out, I can even dodge bullets.”

Li Yao hung upside down on the steel beam and took out three nails of different lengths from his belt.

He was not in a hurry to fire it. Instead, he held the nail in his palm and placed it horizontally and vertically on his fingertip. He sensed the weight, center of gravity, material, and shape of the nail and calculated the rotation, offset, and fall of the nail when it was breaking the air, as well as the perfect mode of movement of every muscle fiber and every bone in his arm.

Although he hadn’t made a move yet, he had thrown the nail in his brain thirty to fifty times, three hundred to five hundred times, three to five thousand times, calculated and corrected time and time again. Countless data were crashing inside his brain cells.

“Found it.”

Li Yao’s half-closed eyes suddenly opened, emitting brightness that was sharper than nails. His arm was like a weird boa that had just molted and was more flexible and stronger than before. “This is the perfect plan!”

Chapter 3198: Mutation

In the blink of an eye, Li Yao’s right arm turned from a monster python into a whip. With a crisp and melodious “Bang!” sound, the three nails on the palm of his hand shot out like the fragments of three meteoroids. The nail in the middle walked in a straight line, letting out a whistle that was sharper than a bullet. The two nails on the left and the right drew a mournful arc, sealing the imaginary escape route of the enemy. 0.1 seconds later, the three nails shot one after another into the wall of the factory. It was as easy as chopsticks stabbing into tofu.

Li Yao did not stop. Instead, he took out nails from his pocket and shot them at the wall in front of him. While he was shooting, he corrected the errors between his muscles and his fascia and adjusted his shooting trajectory. He also imagined all kinds of emergencies and emergencies to train his speed and accuracy in the wrong posture.

In the end, he emptied all the nails in his pocket, which were evenly distributed on the wall, forming a dense cross.

Li Yao clapped his hands in satisfaction. He jumped off the beam and walked to the wall to check the status of the nail.

Almost all the nails sank into the wall, leaving only the last nail cap exposed to the air—and that was when Li Yao had restrained two thirds of his strength.

He estimated that, if he went all out, he could easily shoot through the composite ceramic armor of ordinary rebar, concrete walls, or body armor. Of course, shooting the moving civilian vehicles was not a problem, either. Different from many movies and TV shows, civilian vehicles were the most fragile things. Whoever dared to dodge the bullets with the non-engine and tire parts of the civilian vehicles would be committing suicide.

It took Li Yao a lot of effort to dig out all the nails from the wall.

He had tried the techniques of controlling objects remotely, or rubbing his hands against each other at a high speed, hoping to summon a fireball or a bolt of lightning from his palm. However, it was a pity that his ‘strength of Cultivation’ had only just awakened. It was possible that there was some sort of ‘encryption’, ‘suppression’, ‘spiritual energy lock’, ‘genetic lock’, ‘true energy lock’, and the like on Earth. His capability was far from that of the ‘cultivator Li Yao’ in the novels. Even with all his strength, he could not make the nail in his palm float. There was not even a shadow of the fireball or the electric arcs. His hands were almost scratched to the skin, but all he could do was turn his palms into an abnormal crimson color.

Thankfully, he felt that he was still far from reaching his limit. He estimated that, as Zhang Daniu wrote more chapters of , his “Power of Cultivation” still had the potential to be greatly improved.

On the other hand, the enemies on Earth should not be as insane as the ‘Four Universities of Cultivation’, should they?

Li Yao packed everything up and left the abandoned steel factory along a winding road paved with fallen leaves. It was not until he walked for a long distance that he hailed a taxi and returned to Jiangnan University Town.

On the way, the car radio was still broadcasting the news of the super earthquake in the Western Pacific Ocean. The rumors that were flying everywhere in the beginning had been confirmed. Several nuclear power plants on the east coast of the island of Fusang had been hit by both an earthquake and a tsunami. Several units had suffered serious accidents due to the leakage of radioactive materials. Although the authorities had admitted the accident and guaranteed that everything was under their control, several international authorities had listed the accident as the most serious ‘level-seven nuclear accident’ and asserted that the situation could get worse. It was impossible to control the situation without sacrificing tens of thousands of lives.

It was a pity that the islands today were not as fearless as they had been decades ago. It was impossible for the authorities to fill a nuclear power plant with tens of thousands of lives.

The news further validated the prophecy of the Dream Traveler. Li Yao felt that a storm was brewing.

If the ‘Ultimate Earthquake in the Western Pacific’ was not over yet, an even more intense earthquake that could sink all the islands was coming. It was not just the Pacific Rim that was going to be affected, but also… the entire Earth.

Li Yao returned to his dormitory with a heavy heart. He discovered that the door was ajar. Zhao Kai’s laughter came from inside.

His heart could not help but tighten as he thought of how he could explain to Zhao Kai the two large bags he was carrying.

However, when he pushed the door open and entered, he discovered that Zhao Kai was on the phone facing the wall. There was no telling what kind of joke the other party had said, but it made him burst into laughter. He did not notice Li Yao’s strange behavior at all.

Li Yao was somewhat relieved. He quickly packed up his belongings, especially those that could potentially expose his uniqueness, and packed up his laptop.

Now that everything was done, he was somewhat relieved. He took out his phone and looked at the time. It was 5 p.m.

He had accomplished so many things in one day. Everything was going well.

However…

Li Yao looked at the notification that popped up on the top of his phone. Another chapter of had been updated.

A few days ago, he had deleted the entire reading app. However, after talking things over with Zhang Daniu in the morning, he had installed the reading app again. He had only collected and subscribed to one book, , and had even opened an update notification.

Li Yao opened the reading interface and carefully checked the directory. He discovered that not only had Zhang Daniu posted all the chapters he had written during his sleepwalking last night, he had also written two new chapters while his brain, fingers, and ass were all in excruciating pain.

“It can’t be that this guy is sleepwalking again in the middle of the day, right? Or are his thoughts really flooding?”

Li Yao tilted his head and thought for a moment. “Or maybe, there really is some kind of mysterious… connection between us. We are like magnets or quantum entanglement. Once the two of us get in touch with each other, it is easy for us to activate each other’s superpowers. When I read his novels, the ‘power of Cultivation’ inside my body gradually awakens, and when he meets me, he will be inspired and receive more ‘revelations’.

“Crap. I forgot to tell him that he shouldn’t have uploaded the chapter.

“If we do that, he might be in even more danger, just like the creators of the fantasy worlds who died miserably.

“The problem is, if he doesn’t write a new chapter, I won’t be able to awaken a stronger ‘power of Cultivation’. My body of flesh and blood, which is as tough as a monster’s, is not enough to fight against organizations such as ‘Ark’ or ‘Apocalypse’.

While pondering, Li Yao skimmed over the two chapters that Zhang Daniu had just published.

Very good. It was still the same as before. Every detail had to be described in detail. The style of the long conversation between the two proved that it was indeed written by Zhang Daniu. If he was not drunk, sleeping, or sleepwalking during the day, it meant that he could still receive the mysterious ‘information’ when he was sober.

However, when Li Yao flipped to the end of the latest chapter, he saw this paragraph:

“Hello everyone, I am Master Snail. I’m very sorry that I haven’t updated recently, mainly because I have been involved in a soul-stirring, bizarre, and absolutely unbelievable mysterious event. My time is limited, so I can’t describe the full picture of the event to you for the time being. I can only say that it has to start from a female college student with fluttering long hair and a swaying skirt…”

“In short, in the coming period of time, this old bull’s updates may not be very stable. I hope that my dear brothers and sisters can forgive me. In addition, this old bull is carrying a very sacred and important mission. I am in dire need of an activity fund. I earnestly ask for all the rich and powerful to extend a helping hand. This old bull’s bank account is… This old bull will make a solemn promise to everyone here. After the matter has been successfully resolved, not only will I write down the story that the female university student and I had to tell, I will also give you a hundredfold in return for your support today. At that time, each of you will be given a villa!”

“It’s true. Believe in me and support me. I won’t be at a disadvantage. I won’t be fooled. I’m a man of integrity!”

“What the hell is this?”

Li Yao stared at his phone in bewilderment for a long time. “This—this—this is too—”

Right then, Li Yao heard movement behind him. It was the sound of Zhao Kai climbing out of bed.

Zhao Kai seemed to have said something to him.

“Zhao Kai, what did you say?”

Li Yao hurriedly put away his phone and checked the things on his desk again. There were no loopholes.

“Clang! Clang! Clang! Clang! Clang! Clang! Clang!”

Zhao Kai said.

“What?”

Li Yao’s pupils suddenly constricted. In an instant, the pores, muscles, and bones on his back all shrank to the limit. It was as if the person standing behind him was not his university classmate but a wolf that had been starved for three days and three nights.

Before his brain realized what was going on, his muscles, bones, and joints had already contracted under his control and dodged to the side like a crooked spring. The moment he dodged, Zhao Kai had already lunged to the place where he was just a moment ago and slashed at his neck with an extra-large utility knife in his hand. If he hadn’t dodged, it was very likely that the knife would have opened the artery on his neck!

“Zhao Kai…”

Li Yao looked at his university classmate in disbelief.

Zhao Kai’s hair was glistening with oil, and it was covered in hot sweat. Every muscle on his face was twitching, but it was condensed into an incomparably cold expression. His pair of eyes were clearly darting in two directions, making him look a bit like a cross-eyed clown. Yet, he had clearly scanned every detail in his surroundings.

Seeing that his first attack had failed, he seemed to have realized how awesome Li Yao was. He didn’t launch a second wave of attacks in a hurry, but he rolled his eyes and swallowed rapidly, uttering a series of rapid and rhythmic syllables.

It was a foreign language that was full of rapid tongue twisters. It was definitely not English.

Li Yao knew that Zhao Kai was the same as him. Zhao Kai was a “long live 60 points” slacker. Even after learning English for more than ten years, he was still unable to open his mouth, let alone speak a second foreign language… He was certain that Zhao Kai had never learned a second foreign language. It was even more impossible for him to speak as fluently as… his mother tongue!

Chapter 3199: Remote Control Assassin

That’s right. Although Li Yao didn’t understand what Zhao Kai was talking about, human emotions were always interlinked. Zhao Kai spat out a series of rapid and rapid words that were full of retroflex and retroflex sounds. The words flowed like water without any obstruction. The sharp syllables perfectly blended with the hideous expression on his face. It was as if he was speaking the language from his mother’s womb. What else could it be if not his mother tongue?

The corner of his mouth twitched uncontrollably. Ruthlessness and indifference gradually spread out in his eyes. He did not care about his own life, nor did he care about the lives of other people.

Li Yao didn’t know how to describe Zhao Kai’s temperament. He was like a well-trained professional assassin, or a government agent who specialized in dirty work, or even a drug lord from the jungles of South America. That’s right, he was grimacing, and his canine teeth blossomed with a faint cold light. He was truly like a piranha from the Amazon River!

“He is not Zhao Kai.”

A thought suddenly occurred to Li Yao.

It was like the soul of a professional assassin, agent, or even a drug lord had been stuffed into the body of his roommate. Or maybe, Zhao Kai had awakened the memories of his previous life and turned into a different person.

Shua!

While Li Yao was pondering, Zhao Kai had already grasped the large penknife in his palm and, as if holding a dagger, slashed at Li Yao’s throat again.

“Ding!”

The screwdriver on Li Yao’s waist moved out of instinct. The two sharp blades drew a sharp arc at the same time and collided perfectly. The screwdriver was much harder than the utility knife after all. It broke the blade at the front end of the utility knife and shot out, hitting the bed frame first before bouncing back and embedding itself into the wall.

Zhao Kai’s expression didn’t change in the slightest. A bubbling sound came from the depths of his throat. He lowered his head and bent his knees, using all his strength to ruthlessly crash towards Li Yao.

His movements were clean and precise, full of the brutality of perishing together with the target, like a precise and fatal machine.

He was using a very ruthless indoor close combat technique. Li Yao had seen it in some action movies and knew that it was a fatal technique practiced by the elite special forces of the major countries.

Zhao Kai’s hands and feet seemed to have turned into four boas. They stuck to Li Yao’s skin and ruthlessly coiled upwards. If Li Yao was still himself from a week ago, he would have suffocated within 3-5 seconds.

Unfortunately, his opponent was Li Yao, who had already awakened!

“Zhao Kai!”

Li Yao shouted. His limbs and torso were suddenly filled with blood. His muscles expanded in an instant and forcibly pushed away Zhao Kai’s arm that was wrapped around him. Then, a vicious headbutt hit Zhao Kai’s nose. There was no telling whether or not the nose was broken, but at the very least, Zhao Kai’s head was pushed back.

Li Yao took the opportunity to use both his elbows to ruthlessly strike Zhao Kai’s chest, causing him to fall to the ground.

“Zhao Kai, wake up! You’ve been controlled by a certain force!”

Li Yao discovered that Zhao Kai was still wearing the Bluetooth earpiece. Beside the earpiece, a small blue dot was blinking, indicating that he was still on the phone. When he thought of Zhao Kai’s strange laughter, how could he not understand what had happened?

Zhao Kai was his roommate for four years. Unless it was absolutely necessary, Li Yao was absolutely unwilling to harm Zhao Kai’s life. Instead, he took out a nail from his waist, bent his finger, and flicked it towards the Bluetooth earpiece in Zhao Kai’s ear.

/ .

He was almost sure that he would win, but Zhao Kai suddenly tilted his head. A mysterious and weird smile appeared on his crying face again. The syllables in his mouth suddenly changed from what seemed to be the native language of South America to a simpler, ancient, and rough language.

It could not even be called a language; it was purely a whistle for primitive people to call their companions when they were hunting.

“Moo! Moo! Moo! Moo! Moo! Moo! Moo!”

Zhao Kai said.

His eyeballs, which had been covered in brutality and coldness, seemed to have been broken by an invisible membrane. Brutality and hunger were leaking out. His eyeballs turned as brown as those of wolves, tigers, and leopards. Blood streaks that were almost black exploded, and the corners of his mouth were torn to an unbelievable degree. His canine teeth were growing at a visible speed, and a disgusting stench was spurting out of his throat, as if his stomach was not his internal organs but full of corrosive liquids.

Like a primitive man, or a furious gorilla, Zhao Kai lunged at Li Yao again.

His strength was as terrifying as that of a gorilla.

Li Yao could bend three to five rebars at once, but it was difficult for him to completely control Zhao Kai without hurting him.

“Bang! Huala!”

The two of them hugged each other and crashed into the desk below Li Yao’s bed. The desk made of hardwood and steel was as fragile as a matchbox.

“Zhao Kai, wake up. I don’t want to fight with you!”

Li Yao reached out to grab the Bluetooth earpiece in Zhao Kai’s ear, but the other party shook his head violently and opened his mouth as if he was going to bite him. Li Yao had no idea where to start.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

The tip of the penknife was pushed an inch away by Zhao Kai, drawing streaks of silver brilliance in the air. The steel pipe supporting the bed frame was easily cut in half.

There was no doubt that Zhao Kai’s strength far exceeded the limits of human beings.

After three to five seconds, Li Yao’s hands went limp, as if he had lost all his strength.

A mixture of ruthlessness and ruthlessness appeared on Zhao Kai’s face as he stabbed his saber at Li Yao’s throat.

Suddenly, Li Yao’s Adam’s apple moved. He spat out a nail from below his tongue and pierced it into his Bluetooth earpiece.

Zhao Kai was so focused on Li Yao’s hands that he didn’t realize there was a nail hidden in Li Yao’s mouth. In a moment of carelessness, the Bluetooth earpiece was shot into pieces.

Zhao Kai’s expression suddenly turned blank.

Now, it was Li Yao’s turn to play the role of an anaconda. He flipped over to Zhao Kai’s back in the blink of an eye. His two thumbs pierced deeply three inches below Zhao Kai’s ear, cutting off the carotid artery from supplying blood and oxygen to the brain.

Three seconds later, Zhao Kai passed out. His mouth was foaming, and his limbs were still cramping.

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao went to the bathroom and took out Zhao Kai’s towel. He folded the towel and stuffed it into Zhao Kai’s mouth to prevent him from suffocating or biting his own tongue.

There was no time to clean up the room. He first snatched Zhao Kai’s phone and looked at it. He discovered that the other party had already ended the call, and the other party’s number was simply not displayed on the screen.

Licking the blood from the corner of his mouth, Li Yao turned on the speakerphone and called back. Then he threw Zhao Kai’s phone to the ground. His fingers were full of nails, and he was extremely vigilant, as if an invisible devil would jump out of the phone at any moment.

“I’m sorry. The number you have dialed is not available. I’m sorry. The number you have dialed is not available…”

A hollow female voice repeated itself.

At this moment, footsteps echoed in the corridor outside.

It seemed that his fight with Zhao Kai had attracted the attention of his fellow students or the dormitory manager. At least five or six people were walking towards their dormitory.

Li Yao cursed silently. He grabbed his two packages and jumped out of the window.

The conditions of the male dormitory were always a bit worse than those of the female dormitory. Their dormitory was located at the back of the sixth floor. Fortunately, there weren’t many pedestrians coming and going outside. Li Yao jumped from the sixth floor to the fourth floor’s window ledge, then from the fourth floor to the second floor’s balcony. In the end, he silently landed from the second floor, brushed off the dust on his body, and calmly walked out of the school.

Under the setting sun, the campus appeared exceptionally tranquil. The lackluster sunlight was like a layer of pink mist. The football field and basketball court were filled with students who were drenched in sweat. The boulevard and small garden next to them were filled with couples who were so sweet that they could not be melted.

But no amount of tranquility and sweetness could extinguish Li Yao’s anxiety.

“How did Zhao Kai become like this? Just through the control of the Bluetooth earpiece, he was able to turn a person into a monster with boundless strength like a gorilla. He even grasped so many fatal combat skills?”

Is the ‘Ark Foundation’ behind this? Or is it some other superpower organization or anti-superpower organization?

“The enemy has his eyes on me. What did I do wrong? The drunk racing incident? Or the contact with Zhang Daniu?

“Not good. Now that I have made my move, Zhang Daniu’s side will certainly not be peaceful. He is in great danger. I have to go there immediately!”

Half of Li Yao’s brain was filled with an ocean of ice and half with flames. One side of his mind was burning with anxiety, while the other side was reflecting on all the possible plans calmly.

Li Yao took out his phone and resumed the factory settings.

At this moment, a garbage truck that recycled slops slowly drove out from the back of the cafeteria. Seeing that there was no one around, Li Yao directly threw his phone onto the garbage truck.

Thankfully, he had anticipated that his cell phone would be followed and eavesdropped on. Therefore, he had redeemed more than 3,000 credits of cash in advance and brought it with him. Otherwise, in a city where mobile payments were scarce, it would have been a major nuisance not to have a cell phone.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

With every step he took, endless information would pour out from the depths of his mind, including the major traffic routes from Jiangnan University Town to downtown, the detailed topographic map of Zhang Daniu’s residential area, all the ‘weapons’ that could be used along the way, and so on. Li Yao had calculated every detail clearly.

“It’s time to unleash the full power of my racing prince!”

Li Yao calculated in his mind, “First, I’ll go to a random car in a shabby alley next to the school. Then, I’ll rush there at the limit of 180 km/h. With my amazing driving skills, I should be able to arrive at my destination in half an hour!”

While thinking, Li Yao walked out of the school gate.

Then, he saw the surging traffic at 5:30 p.m.

The steel dragons seemed to have extended from the ancient past to the unreachable future.

“…”

Li Yao pondered for a moment. “Forget it. Let’s take the subway. It’s quite fast to take the No. 1 train to the No. 4 train and ride a shared bicycle.”

Chapter 3200: Secret War Begins

The subway station at night was packed with people, too. Everyone’s face was as gloomy as a solar eclipse, like a bunch of burnt-out zombies.

Maybe it was because Li Yao was dressed like a construction worker, but the tools in his pocket did not cause much trouble when he went through the security check.

Li Yao bought a one-off subway ticket with cash and waited in front of the platform.

When the first subway arrived, there were too many passengers. He did not find a suitable candidate and had to wait for the next one.

When the second subway entered the station, a suitable candidate appeared.

It was a tired, groggy, and staggering office worker.

Yawning, he walked out of the subway and put his phone back into his pocket. It seemed that he had just finished browsing a webpage and his phone was not locked yet.

Li Yao walked towards the office worker and quietly bumped into a young girl who was dressed very avant-garde next to him. The young girl was pushed into the arms of the office worker. While the office worker was in a flurry or secretly happy, Li Yao passed by and took the still-shining mobile phone.

When the doors were closed and the subway slowly opened, the office worker was still in a hurry to apologize or strike up a conversation with the avant-garde girl. This was probably a rarely-seen bright color in his boring and boring life. Through the glass window, he could see that his neck had become thicker from excitement. He didn’t even notice that his phone was missing.

Li Yao was somewhat relieved. He dialed Zhang Daniu’s number without changing his expression.

“Pick up the phone! Pick up the phone now!”

Li Yao screamed in his heart and roared at the deities, hoping that he would not see Zhang Daniu’s cold body when he arrived.

Five seconds was as unbearable as five days and five nights. Finally, the call was connected!

“Cough cough.”

Li Yao coughed twice first.

“Cough cough, cough cough cough cough cough!”

The guy was dazed for a moment. Then he coughed, too.

It was a code that the two of them had agreed upon. If they both coughed, it meant that everything was normal, and if they both coughed, it meant that one of them was in danger.

So far, nothing had happened to Zhang Daniu, but the alarm deep inside Li Yao’s brain was getting louder and louder.

“Listen, we are being watched. My roommate has just been hypnotized into a monster with immense physical strength who is adept at assassination techniques. He is trying to kill me.”

“I estimate that the root of the problem lies with you,” Li Yao said. “The people who are targeting you should be on their way and will find you soon.

“What?”

Zhang Daniu was shocked. “Then—then what about me? How long until you arrive?”

“It will take me half an hour to forty-five minutes to reach you.”

Li Yao said, “In the meantime, you must calm down and protect yourself in order to survive the disaster.

“Calm down? How can I calm down?”

Zhang Daniu screamed, “This morning, you just told me that this is a world with superpowers. Then, you ate an entire sack of bread right in front of me. Then, you tore open a dictionary that was thicker than a brick and snatched away the 20,000 bucks that I had painstakingly saved. At night, at night, you will tell me that I’ve been targeted by a certain ‘evil superpower organization’. How am I supposed to calm down? This won’t do. Call the police. There’s a police station in our neighborhood and a community police station in the neighborhood across the street. At the crucial moment, I still have to believe in the government!”

“No. It is not a question of whether or not to believe in the government, but how to make the government believe in you.

Li Yao said, “Think about it. If you call the police, what are you going to say? That you have discovered the existence of superpowers in this world and an evil organization of superpowers is hunting you?”

Zhang Daniu said, “Well…”

“If you really say that, you will definitely be controlled as a drug user.”

Li Yao said, “The community police can’t resist the superpowered people, but they can control your movements and reduce your only advantage in mobility. Such a warning is tantamount to walking into a trap.

“Then—then what do we do?”

Zhang Daniu sounded like he was about to cry. “I’m coming out now to meet you?”

“That won’t work, either. I’m not sure whether or not somebody is spying on you. Maybe they will be more than happy to see you leave the house. A fully-enclosed van will take you away from the gate of the neighborhood within one second.”

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao said, “Listen, what you are going to do next is to follow my instructions strictly for every step. Maybe you can make it to the next hour.

“Firstly, do you have any flammable materials in your house? Books. Oh, that’s right. You have so many books. Look for a few books that are old, have crisp paper, and are easier to burn. Then look for some pillowcases, clothes, or even plastic bags.

“Secondly, you have a pressure cooker in your house, right? If you have one, then that’s good. Throw the flammable material into the pressure cooker, ignite it, and then throw in pillow covers, clothes, cotton wool, plastic bags, and the like to burn. Remember, of course, I don’t want you to really start a fire, but I want you to create intense black smoke.

“Thirdly, when the black smoke comes out of the pressure cooker, bring the cooker to the balcony and let the black smoke drift out of the window. It would be best if everyone in the neighborhood could see and smell it.

“Fourthly, you know what to do, right? Call the fire alarm and let the fire department handle it.

“Fifthly, after you’re done, leave the room immediately. But don’t go downstairs. Turn around and go to the rooftop. I’ve observed the structure of your residential building. It’s an old-fashioned water supply system. There’s a very large water tank on the rooftop, so you can definitely go to the rooftop from the stairs, right?

“It doesn’t matter what method you use to open the lock from the corridor to the rooftop. If you want to go up and hide, it would be best to hide in the water tank and make sure that the high vantage points in all directions can’t directly aim at you… Don’t be nervous. This is just in case. The domestic firearms control is so strict. I estimate that the other party won’t be as brazen as the ‘T3 Game Show Shooting’. If they really want to make a move on you, it would be through an ‘accident’. They wouldn’t be so arrogant as to get a few sniper rifles, right?”

“Right, before that, leave your phone at home and don’t bring any communication equipment. I will look for you on the rooftop within the next hour. Do you understand?”

“Ah, ah ah!”

Zhang Daniu shouted a few times. He seemed to be frightened by Li Yao’s words. “Then, then what if someone else besides you comes to the rooftop to find me and drag me out of the tank?”

“Whoever is pretending to be a police officer or a firefighter, don’t trust them.

Li Yao said solemnly, “Activate your courage and fight him bravely!”

“…Fight?”

Zhang Daniu said, “Are you joking?”

“Of course not. I know that you are no match for him, but what can I do?”

Li Yao said, “I’m just a superhuman who has just awakened the ‘power of Cultivation’. I’m not an omnipotent god. My enemy is so mysterious and strong. I’m not confident that I can defeat him. I can only try my best to save you if I can.

“At the very least, one piece of good news is certain.

“Oh?”

Zhang Daniu said, “There’s more good news?”

“Judging from the experiences of Gilson, the author of Blood and Sand, Takaya Ibo, and Garriott, the ‘Father of the Ring of Stars’, the guy is a well-trained professional. He is decisive and will not make his target suffer for long.

Li Yao said, “Therefore, you don’t need to worry that you will be captured, interrogated, or chopped into pieces. Chances are that everything will be over before you know it.

“…”

Zhang Daniu said, “Are you really f*cking here to help me? You can’t be sent by my mortal enemy to play with me!”

“Alright, I’m not joking. I have a situation here that I can’t talk about.”

Li Yao said, “I’m dead. Remember, save your own life!”

“Wait, wait!”

Zhang Daniu suddenly panicked. “Hey, what’s wrong with you? You—you—you haven’t been attacked by the superpowered people again, have you? Li Yao, Li Yao!”

“No.”

Li Yao was pinned to the glass window by the crowd and could not move at all. He struggled to breathe as he said, “I was on the subway and just passed by a big station. All of a sudden, more than 10,000 people rushed in. I—I was completely suppressed. Huhu. Many people. Huhuhu. I can’t even move my hands and feet. Don’t push. Can’t you have some manners… Hey, where’s my phone? Where did I drop my phone? Who stepped on my phone? Can you please move your feet? My phone!”

…

Li Yao brought his super power to maximum and finally made his way out of the subway. Of course, the stolen phone was still nowhere to be found. Maybe it had been stomped into pieces.

Li Yao apologized to the innocent office worker in his heart and left the subway to find a row of shared bicycles.

The latest model of shared bicycles had an anti-stealing alarm system. Li Yao didn’t have a cell phone, so naturally, he couldn’t scan the QR code to unlock it.

Fortunately, nowadays, the competition for shared bicycles was fierce. Many first-generation, old-fashioned bicycles had been left behind. The rusted locks were just a decoration for the deaf.

With a light pinch and a twist of two fingers, one of the locks broke. Li Yao flew up and rode the shared bicycle with the imposing aura of a racing motorcycle. In less than five minutes, he arrived at the entrance of Zhang Daniu’s neighborhood.

At this time, the urgent cry of a fire engine could already be heard coming from the residential area. A small support car had just entered, but a large fire engine had been blocked at the entrance of the residential area because it had occupied the road and was parked illegally. The local people raised their spirits to watch the show and gathered in twos and threes inside and outside the residential area. They waved their fans while pointing at the residential building rented by Zhang Daniu and whispering to each other.

It was almost seven o’clock in the evening, but the summer days were particularly long. The sky was still full of gray brightness. Hundreds of eyes around the residential building saw everything clearly.

Since the Ark Foundation had chosen to disguise itself as a charity, it was apparently wary of the consequences. There was no way that it would attack somebody in public, was there?
